<Project>
  <!-- ============================================================= -->
  <!-- GLOBAL PATH DEFINITIONS                                       -->
  <!-- Source & Development root: C:\Users\<user>\Documents\codes\SKSB_App -->
  <!-- Deployment Release target: C:\Users\<user>\Documents\SKSB_App -->
  <!-- ============================================================= -->
  <PropertyGroup>
    <AIExportDir>$(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)AI_Source_Export</AIExportDir>
    <SingleBundleFile>$(AIExportDir)\AI_Context_AllCode.txt</SingleBundleFile>
    <SourceAddInsDir>$(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)AddIns</SourceAddInsDir>
    <ReleaseDeploymentDir>$(UserProfile)\Documents\SKSB_App</ReleaseDeploymentDir>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <!-- ============================================================= -->
  <!-- 1. TOTAL CLEAN (RUNS ONLY ON 'CLEAN' OR 'REBUILD' BUTTON)    -->
  <!-- Will NEVER run during normal 'Build Solution' or 'F5' Debug   -->
  <!-- ============================================================= -->
  <Target Name="DeepCleanAllArtifactsOnClean"
          BeforeTargets="Clean"
          Condition="'$(_DeepCleanExecuted)' != 'true'">

    <PropertyGroup>
      <_DeepCleanExecuted>true</_DeepCleanExecuted>
    </PropertyGroup>

    <Message Importance="high" Text="[Clean] Wiping easy-access AddIns, AI export, and Release directories..." />

    <!-- 1a. Completely wipe everything inside Codes\SKSB_App\AddIns -->
    <Exec Command="powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -Command &quot;if (Test-Path '$(SourceAddInsDir)') { Get-ChildItem -Path '$(SourceAddInsDir)' -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Remove-Item -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }&quot;" IgnoreExitCode="true" />

    <!-- 1b. Wipe AI export folder and any loose AI zips -->
    <Exec Command="powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -Command &quot;Remove-Item -Path '$(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)*AI*.zip' -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue; if (Test-Path '$(AIExportDir)') { Remove-Item -Path '$(AIExportDir)' -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }&quot;" IgnoreExitCode="true" />

    <!-- 1c. Only wipe Documents\SKSB_App when cleaning in RELEASE mode -->
    <Exec Condition="'$(Configuration)' == 'Release'"
          Command="powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -Command &quot;if (Test-Path '$(ReleaseDeploymentDir)') { Get-ChildItem -Path '$(ReleaseDeploymentDir)' -Recurse -File -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Where-Object { $_.FullName -notmatch '\\Data\\' } | Remove-Item -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }&quot;"
          IgnoreExitCode="true" />
  </Target>

  <!-- ============================================================= -->
  <!-- 2. COPY BUILD ARTIFACTS TO CODES\ADDINS ON BUILD              -->
  <!-- Safe wildcard copy: Never fails on optional/missing files     -->
  <!-- ============================================================= -->
  <Target Name="SyncAddInsToCodesFolderOnBuild"
          AfterTargets="Build"
          Condition="'$(MSBuildProjectName)' == 'Core' or '@(PackageReference->WithMetadataValue(&quot;Identity&quot;, &quot;ExcelDna.AddIn&quot;))' != ''">

    <MakeDir Directories="$(SourceAddInsDir)" Condition="!Exists('$(SourceAddInsDir)')" />

    <ItemGroup>
      <!-- Deploy project's DLL, config, and symbols (using wildcards so missing items won't break MSB3030) -->
      <ModuleArtifacts Include="$(TargetDir)$(TargetName).dll" />
      <ModuleArtifacts Include="$(TargetDir)$(TargetName).pdb" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)$(TargetName).pdb')" />
      <ModuleArtifacts Include="$(TargetDir)$(TargetName).deps.json" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)$(TargetName).deps.json')" />
      <ModuleArtifacts Include="$(TargetDir)$(TargetName).runtimeconfig.json" Condition="Exists('$(TargetDir)$(TargetName).runtimeconfig.json')" />

      <!-- Wildcards automatically capture Core-AddIn.dna, Core-AddIn64.dna, and all XLLs without hardcoding -->
      <ModuleArtifacts Include="$(TargetDir)*-AddIn*.xll" />
      <ModuleArtifacts Include="$(TargetDir)*-AddIn*.dna" />
      <ModuleArtifacts Include="$(TargetDir)*.dna" />
      <PackedArtifacts Include="$(TargetDir)publish\*-packed.xll" />
    </ItemGroup>

    <Copy SourceFiles="@(ModuleArtifacts)"
          DestinationFolder="$(SourceAddInsDir)"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="false" />

    <Copy SourceFiles="@(PackedArtifacts)"
          DestinationFolder="$(SourceAddInsDir)"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="false"
          Condition="'@(PackedArtifacts)' != ''" />

    <Message Importance="high" Text="[AddIn Sync] Deployed $(MSBuildProjectName) artifacts to $(SourceAddInsDir)" />
  </Target>

  <!-- ============================================================= -->
  <!-- 3. AUTO-GENERATE AI CHAT EXPORT BUNDLE                        -->
  <!-- Triggers once per build cycle on Launcher                     -->
  <!-- ============================================================= -->
	<Target Name="GenerateAIChatCodeBundle"
		  AfterTargets="Build"
		  Condition="'$(_AIChatBundleRan)' != 'true' and ('$(MSBuildProjectName)' == 'Launcher' or '$(MSBuildProjectName)' == 'SKSB_App')">

    <PropertyGroup>
      <_AIChatBundleRan>true</_AIChatBundleRan>
    </PropertyGroup>

    <MakeDir Directories="$(AIExportDir)" Condition="!Exists('$(AIExportDir)')" />

    <ItemGroup>
      <CleanSourceFiles Include="$(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\*.cs;
                                 $(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\*.csproj;
                                 $(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\*.dna;
                                 $(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\*.props;
                                 $(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\*.targets;
                                 $(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\*.sql"
                        Exclude="$(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\bin\**;
                                 $(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\obj\**;
                                 $(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\.vs\**;
                                 $(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\.git\**;
                                 $(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\packages\**;
                                 $(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\publish\**;
                                 $(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\publish_staging\**;
                                 $(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\AI_Source_Export\**;
                                 $(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)**\*AI*\**;
                                 $(AIExportDir)\**" />
    </ItemGroup>

    <Copy SourceFiles="@(CleanSourceFiles)"
          DestinationFolder="$(AIExportDir)\%(RecursiveDir)"
          SkipUnchangedFiles="false" />

    <!-- Consolidate into AI_Context_AllCode.txt using atomic write -->
    <Exec Command="powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -Command &quot;$dest = '$(SingleBundleFile)'; $tmp = $dest + '.tmp'; New-Item -ItemType File -Path $tmp -Force | Out-Null; Get-ChildItem -Path '$(AIExportDir)' -Recurse -File | Where-Object { $_.FullName -ne $dest -and $_.FullName -ne $tmp -and $_.Extension -match '\.(cs|csproj|dna|props|targets|sql)$' } | ForEach-Object { '============================================================' | Out-File $tmp -Append -Encoding utf8; ('FILE: ' + $_.FullName.Replace('$(AIExportDir)\', '')) | Out-File $tmp -Append -Encoding utf8; '============================================================' | Out-File $tmp -Append -Encoding utf8; Get-Content $_.FullName -Encoding utf8 | Out-File $tmp -Append -Encoding utf8; '`n' | Out-File $tmp -Append -Encoding utf8 }; Move-Item -Path $tmp -Destination $dest -Force;&quot;" IgnoreExitCode="true" />

    <Message Importance="high" Text="[AI Export Ready] Unified bundle created at: $(SingleBundleFile)" />
  </Target>

	<!-- ============================================================= -->
	<!-- 4. AUTO-DEPLOY RELEASE PACKAGE TO SERVER (PUBLISH ONLY)       -->
	<!-- ============================================================= -->
	<Target Name="AutoDeployReleaseToServer"
			AfterTargets="Publish"
			Condition="'$(Configuration)' == 'Release' and ('$(MSBuildProjectName)' == 'Launcher' or '$(MSBuildProjectName)' == 'SKSB_App')">
		<Message Importance="high" Text="[Release Deploy] Building and deploying SKSB package to server..." />
		<Exec Command="powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File &quot;$(MSBuildThisFileDirectory)publish.ps1&quot;" />
	</Target>
</Project>

FILE: Core\01_Bootstrap\Environment\Environment.BinaryInspector.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Environment;

using System;
using System.IO;

public enum Bitness
{
    Unknown,
    Win32,
    Win64
}

public static class BinaryInspector
{
    private const ushort PE_SIGNATURE_OFFSET = 0x3C;
    private const uint PE_SIGNATURE = 0x00004550; // "PE\0\0"
    private const ushort IMAGE_FILE_MACHINE_I386 = 0x014C;
    private const ushort IMAGE_FILE_MACHINE_AMD64 = 0x8664;

    /// <summary>
    /// Inspects the Portable Executable (PE) header of EXCEL.EXE directly.
    /// Eliminates registry lookup inaccuracies and side-by-side Click-to-Run false positives.
    /// </summary>
    public static Bitness GetExcelBitness(string excelExePath)
    {
        try
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(excelExePath) || !File.Exists(excelExePath))
            {
                return Bitness.Unknown;
            }

            using var stream = new FileStream(excelExePath, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.ReadWrite);
            using var reader = new BinaryReader(stream);

            // Read DOS Header to locate the PE Header pointer
            if (stream.Length < 64) return Bitness.Unknown;

            stream.Seek(PE_SIGNATURE_OFFSET, SeekOrigin.Begin);
            int peHeaderOffset = reader.ReadInt32();

            if (peHeaderOffset + 24 > stream.Length) return Bitness.Unknown;

            // Verify PE Signature
            stream.Seek(peHeaderOffset, SeekOrigin.Begin);
            uint signature = reader.ReadUInt32();
            if (signature != PE_SIGNATURE) return Bitness.Unknown;

            // Read Machine field from COFF File Header (immediately after signature)
            ushort machine = reader.ReadUInt16();
            return machine switch
            {
                IMAGE_FILE_MACHINE_AMD64 => Bitness.Win64,
                IMAGE_FILE_MACHINE_I386 => Bitness.Win32,
                _ => Bitness.Unknown
            };
        }
        catch
        {
            return Bitness.Unknown;
        }
    }
}

FILE: Core\01_Bootstrap\Environment\Environment.ExcelPathFinder.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Environment;

using System.IO;
using Microsoft.Win32;

public static class ExcelPathFinder
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Locates the EXCEL.EXE binary via App Paths registry key or standard Office install directories.
    /// </summary>
    public static string ResolveExcelExecutable()
    {
        using var key = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey(@"SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\App Paths\excel.exe");
        if (key?.GetValue(null) is string appPath && File.Exists(appPath))
        {
            return appPath;
        }

        string[] standardLocations =
        {
            @"C:\Program Files\Microsoft Office\root\Office16\EXCEL.EXE",
            @"C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft Office\root\Office16\EXCEL.EXE",
            @"C:\Program Files\Microsoft Office\Office16\EXCEL.EXE",
            @"C:\Program Files\Microsoft Office\Office15\EXCEL.EXE"
        };

        foreach (var path in standardLocations)
        {
            if (File.Exists(path)) return path;
        }

        return "excel.exe";
    }
}

FILE: Core\01_Bootstrap\Environment\Environment.ExcelRegistry.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Environment;

using System;

public static class ExcelRegistry
{
    private static string _excelPath;
    private static Bitness _officeBitness;

    static ExcelRegistry()
    {
        _excelPath = ExcelPathFinder.ResolveExcelExecutable();
        _officeBitness = BinaryInspector.GetExcelBitness(_excelPath);
    }

    public static string ExcelPath
    {
        get => _excelPath;
        set
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value))
                throw new ArgumentException("Excel path cannot be null or empty.", nameof(value));

            _excelPath = value;
            _officeBitness = BinaryInspector.GetExcelBitness(value);
        }
    }

    public static Bitness OfficeBitness => _officeBitness;
}

FILE: Core\01_Bootstrap\Storage\Storage.FolderPathHelper.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Storage;

using System;
using System.IO;
using SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Environment;

public sealed class AppFolder
{
    private readonly string _path;

    public AppFolder(string path)
    {
        _path = path ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(path));
    }

    public string GetPath() => _path;
    public bool Exists() => Directory.Exists(_path);

    public string EnsureExists()
    {
        if (!Directory.Exists(_path))
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(_path);
        }
        return _path;
    }

    public string GetFilePath(string fileName, bool mustExist = false)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fileName))
            throw new ArgumentException("Filename cannot be empty.", nameof(fileName));

        string fullPath = Path.Combine(_path, fileName);
        if (mustExist && !File.Exists(fullPath))
        {
            throw new FileNotFoundException($"Required operational file was not found at '{fullPath}'.", fullPath);
        }
        return fullPath;
    }

    public override string ToString() => _path;
    public static implicit operator string(AppFolder folder) => folder?._path ?? string.Empty;
}

public sealed class TemplateAddInPair
{
    private readonly string _template;
    private readonly string _addIn;

    public TemplateAddInPair(string template, string addIn)
    {
        _template = template ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(template));
        _addIn = addIn ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(addIn));
    }

    public string Template() => _template;

    public string AddIn()
    {
        return ExcelRegistry.OfficeBitness.Equals(Bitness.Win32)
            ? $"{_addIn}.xll"
            : $"{_addIn}64.xll";
    }

    public static string ResolveAddInPath(string addInFileName)
    {
#if DEBUG
        // 1. Current executing project base directory
        string localBinPath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, addInFileName);
        if (File.Exists(localBinPath)) return localBinPath;

        // 2. Sibling module build directory: codes\SKSB_App\<Module>\bin\Debug\net8.0-windows\<addInFileName>
        string moduleName = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(addInFileName)
                                .Replace("-AddIn64", "")
                                .Replace("-AddIn", "");

        string siblingPath = Path.Combine(
            FolderRegistry.SKSB_App.GetPath(),
            moduleName,
            "bin", "Debug", "net8.0-windows",
            addInFileName
        );
        if (File.Exists(siblingPath)) return siblingPath;

        // 3. Packed fallback: publish\<addIn>-packed.xll
        string baseName = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(addInFileName);
        string packedPath = Path.Combine(
            FolderRegistry.SKSB_App.GetPath(),
            moduleName,
            "bin", "Debug", "net8.0-windows",
            "publish",
            $"{baseName}-packed.xll"
        );
        if (File.Exists(packedPath)) return packedPath;

        return siblingPath;
#else
        // In Release: Strictly use the deployed Documents\SKSB_App\AddIns folder
        return Path.Combine(FolderRegistry.AddIns.GetPath(), addInFileName);
#endif
    }

    public static implicit operator string(TemplateAddInPair pair) => nameof(pair) ?? string.Empty;
}

FILE: Core\01_Bootstrap\Storage\Storage.FolderRegistry.cs

namespace SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Storage;

using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

public static class FolderRegistry
{
    // Canonical deployed runtime path for clients / Release
    private static readonly string ReleaseRootPath = Path.Combine(
        Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.MyDocuments),
        "SKSB_App"
    );

    // Development source repository root for Debug
    private static readonly string CodesRootPath = Path.Combine(
        Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.MyDocuments),
        "codes",
        "SKSB_App"
    );

#if DEBUG
    // In Debug: Root is strictly the active solution codebase root (Documents\codes\SKSB_App)
    private static readonly string SolutionRoot = ResolveDebugSolutionRoot();
    public static AppFolder SKSB_App { get; } = new AppFolder(SolutionRoot);
    public static AppFolder Templates { get; } = new AppFolder(ResolveFolder("Templates"));
    public static AppFolder AddIns { get; } = new AppFolder(ResolveFolder("AddIns"));
    public static AppFolder Data { get; } = new AppFolder(ResolveFolder("Data"));
#else
    // In Release: Root is strictly the client deployment directory (Documents\SKSB_App)
    public static AppFolder SKSB_App { get; } = new AppFolder(ReleaseRootPath);
    public static AppFolder AddIns { get; } = new AppFolder(Path.Combine(ReleaseRootPath, "AddIns"));
    public static AppFolder Templates { get; } = new AppFolder(Path.Combine(ReleaseRootPath, "Templates"));
    public static AppFolder Data { get; } = new AppFolder(Path.Combine(ReleaseRootPath, "Data"));
#endif

    private static string ResolveFolder(string subFolder)
    {
#if DEBUG
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SolutionRoot) && Directory.Exists(SolutionRoot))
        {
            string slnPath = Path.Combine(SolutionRoot, subFolder);
            if (Directory.Exists(slnPath)) return slnPath;
        }

        string directCodesPath = Path.Combine(CodesRootPath, subFolder);
        if (Directory.Exists(directCodesPath)) return directCodesPath;

        return Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, subFolder);
#else
        return Path.Combine(ReleaseRootPath, subFolder);
#endif
    }

#if DEBUG
    private static string ResolveDebugSolutionRoot()
    {
        try
        {
            string? startDir = AppContext.BaseDirectory;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(startDir))
            {
                var dir = new DirectoryInfo(startDir);
                while (dir != null)
                {
                    if (dir.GetFiles("*.sln").Length > 0 || dir.GetDirectories(".git").Length > 0)
                    {
                        return dir.FullName;
                    }
                    dir = dir.Parent;
                }
            }
        }
        catch { }

        // Hard fallback to codes repository root
        if (Directory.Exists(CodesRootPath))
        {
            return CodesRootPath;
        }

        return AppContext.BaseDirectory;
    }
#endif
}

public static class ModuleRegistry
{
    public static TemplateAddInPair DailyScheduler { get; } = CreateDefault("DailyScheduler");
    public static TemplateAddInPair StaffManager { get; } = CreateDefault("StaffManager");
    public static TemplateAddInPair ProjectManager { get; } = CreateDefault("ProjectManager");
    public static TemplateAddInPair VehicleManager { get; } = CreateDefault("VehicleManager");

    public static TemplateAddInPair CreateDefault(string moduleName)
    {
        string snake = Regex.Replace(moduleName, "([a-z])([A-Z])", "$1_$2");
        return new TemplateAddInPair(
            template: $"{snake}_Template.xltx",
            addIn: $"{moduleName}-AddIn"
        );
    }
}

FILE: Core\02_WorkbookOpen\Entry\Entry.EventsAutoOpen.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Entry;

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.RuleEngine;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.TableRowManager;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sync;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.SheetMapper;
using DropDownCommands = SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown.Commands;

public class EventsAutoOpen : IExcelAddIn
{
    private static dynamic? _xlAppRoot;
    private delegate void SheetDoubleClickDelegate(dynamic sh, dynamic target, ref bool cancel);
    private delegate void SheetSelectionChangeDelegate(dynamic sh, dynamic target);
    private delegate void SheetChangeDelegate(dynamic sh, dynamic target);
    private delegate void SheetActivateDelegate(dynamic sh);

    private static SheetDoubleClickDelegate? _doubleClickHandler;
    private static SheetSelectionChangeDelegate? _selectionHandler;
    private static SheetChangeDelegate? _changeHandler;
    private static SheetActivateDelegate? _activateHandler;

    private static string HookedPidKey => $"SKSB_EVENTS_HOOKED_{Environment.ProcessId}";
    private static string LastCheckedWorkbookKey => $"SKSB_LAST_CHECKED_WB_{Environment.ProcessId}";
    private static string LastTemplateModeKey => $"SKSB_TEMPLATE_MODE_{Environment.ProcessId}";

    public void AutoOpen()
    {
        Dapper.DefaultTypeMap.MatchNamesWithUnderscores = true;

        RuleRegistry.EnsureInitialized();

        if (Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(HookedPidKey) == "1")
        {
            AppLogger.Info($"[Coexistence] Core COM events already active in PID {Environment.ProcessId} (XLL: {ExcelDnaUtil.XllPath}).");
            return;
        }

        Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(HookedPidKey, "1");
        AppLogger.ResetSessionLog("Workbook Startup");
        AppLogger.Info($"EventsAutoOpen.AutoOpen primary hook in PID {Environment.ProcessId} (XLL: {ExcelDnaUtil.XllPath}).");

        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            using var _ = AppLogger.Scope("AutoOpen Queued Macro");
            EventHooks();

            try
            {
                dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
                xlApp.WindowActivate += new Action<dynamic, dynamic>((wb, wn) => OnWindowActivatedMacro());
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                AppLogger.Error("Failed hooking WindowActivate", ex);
            }

            try
            {
                double nowSerial = Convert.ToDouble(XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlfNow));
                XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnTime, nowSerial + (3.0 / 86400.0), "DelayedTemplateCheckMacro");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                AppLogger.Error("Failed scheduling DelayedTemplateCheckMacro", ex);
            }
        });
    }

    public void AutoClose()
    {
        AppLogger.Info("EventsAutoOpen.AutoClose triggered.");
        Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(HookedPidKey, null);
        Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(LastCheckedWorkbookKey, null);
        Arrow.Teardown();
        UnEventHooks();
    }

    [ExcelCommand(Name = "DelayedTemplateCheckMacro")]
    public static void DelayedTemplateCheckMacro()
    {
        try
        {
            RuleRegistry.EnsureInitialized();
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic? wb = null;
            try { wb = xlApp?.ActiveWorkbook; } catch { }
            if (wb == null) return;

            string wbName = wb.Name?.ToString() ?? "Workbook";
            string? alreadyCheckedWb = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(LastCheckedWorkbookKey);
            if (string.Equals(alreadyCheckedWb, wbName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                AppLogger.Info($"[Coexistence] DelayedTemplateCheckMacro already completed for '{wbName}', skipping duplicate run.");
                return;
            }
            Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(LastCheckedWorkbookKey, wbName);

            using var _ = AppLogger.Scope($"DelayedTemplateCheckMacro({wbName})");

            dynamic? activeSheetBefore = xlApp.ActiveSheet;
            string activeSheetName = activeSheetBefore?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;

            bool isTemplate = SheetOperations.IsMasterTemplateFile();
            SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode = isTemplate;
            Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(LastTemplateModeKey, isTemplate ? "1" : "0");
            AppLogger.Info($"Workbook='{wbName}', IsMasterTemplateFile={isTemplate}");

            bool origUpdating = true;
            bool origEvents = true;
            try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }
            try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }

            try
            {
                SheetMapperHelper.ClampAllTablesToActualData(wb);
                RuleRegistry.ApplyDropdownStylesToWorkbook(wb);

                if (isTemplate)
                {
                    SheetOperations.SetAuxiliarySheetsVisibility(visible: true);
                    EnumList.SyncAllCoreEnums();
                    DataList.SyncAllTemplateData();
                    SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.TemplateEditor.RibbonController.RefreshRibbonUI();
                }
                else
                {
                    SheetOperations.SetAuxiliarySheetsVisibility(visible: false);
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                if (activeSheetBefore != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(activeSheetName))
                {
                    if (!activeSheetName.Equals(SheetOperations.LookupsSheetName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                        !activeSheetName.Equals(SheetOperations.DataSheetName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        try { activeSheetBefore.Activate(); } catch { }
                    }
                }

                try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
                try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error("DelayedTemplateCheckMacro failed", ex);
        }
    }

    [ExcelCommand(Name = "OnWindowActivatedMacro")]
    public static void OnWindowActivatedMacro()
    {
        try
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(LastCheckedWorkbookKey)))
                return;

            bool isTemplate = SheetOperations.IsMasterTemplateFile();
            bool prevMode = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(LastTemplateModeKey) == "1";
            SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode = isTemplate;

            if (prevMode != isTemplate)
            {
                Environment.SetEnvironmentVariable(LastTemplateModeKey, isTemplate ? "1" : "0");
                AppLogger.Info($"WindowActivate mode switch: IsTemplateEditMode -> {isTemplate}");
                SheetOperations.SetAuxiliarySheetsVisibility(visible: isTemplate);
                SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.TemplateEditor.RibbonController.RefreshRibbonUI();
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error("OnWindowActivatedMacro error", ex);
        }
    }

    private static void EventHooks()
    {
        try
        {
            if (_xlAppRoot != null) return;
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            _xlAppRoot = xlApp;

#if DEBUG
            // DIAGNOSTIC 1: Intercept all sheet activations with stack trace
            _activateHandler = new SheetActivateDelegate(sh =>
            {
                string sName = sh?.Name?.ToString() ?? "Unknown";
                AppLogger.Warn($"[SHEET ACTIVATION DETECTED] Switched active tab to: '{sName}'\nStack:\n{new StackTrace(1, true)}");
            });
            _xlAppRoot.SheetActivate += _activateHandler;
#endif

            // DIAGNOSTIC 2: Intercept all selection changes to pinpoint horizontal viewport scrolling
            _selectionHandler = new SheetSelectionChangeDelegate(OnSheetSelectionChange);
            _xlAppRoot.SheetSelectionChange += _selectionHandler;

            _doubleClickHandler = new SheetDoubleClickDelegate(OnSheetBeforeDoubleClick);
            _xlAppRoot.SheetBeforeDoubleClick += _doubleClickHandler;

            _changeHandler = new SheetChangeDelegate(OnSheetChange);
            _xlAppRoot.SheetChange += _changeHandler;

            AppLogger.Info("Application Sheet Events hooked successfully with full navigation diagnostics.");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error("EventHooks failed", ex);
        }
    }

    private static void UnEventHooks()
    {
        try
        {
            if (_xlAppRoot != null)
            {
                if (_activateHandler != null) { _xlAppRoot.SheetActivate -= _activateHandler; _activateHandler = null; }
                if (_doubleClickHandler != null) { _xlAppRoot.SheetBeforeDoubleClick -= _doubleClickHandler; _doubleClickHandler = null; }
                if (_selectionHandler != null) { _xlAppRoot.SheetSelectionChange -= _selectionHandler; _selectionHandler = null; }
                if (_changeHandler != null) { _xlAppRoot.SheetChange -= _changeHandler; _changeHandler = null; }
                _xlAppRoot = null;
            }
        }
        catch { }
    }

    private static void OnSheetSelectionChange(dynamic sh, dynamic target)
    {
        if (target == null)
        {
            Arrow.HideAll();
            return;
        }

        string sheetName = sh?.Name?.ToString() ?? "Sheet";
        string addr = target.Address?.ToString() ?? "Unknown";
        int col = (int)target.Column;
        int row = (int)target.Row;

#if DEBUG
        // Log selection changes to capture horizontal panning
        AppLogger.Info($"[SELECTION CHANGE] Sheet='{sheetName}', Range={addr}, Col={col}, Row={row}");
#endif

        dynamic singleCell = target.Cells[1, 1];
        if (DropDownCommands.IsDropdownCell(singleCell))
        {
            Arrow.ShowArrowForCell(singleCell);
            return;
        }

        Arrow.HideAll();
    }

    private static void OnSheetChange(dynamic sh, dynamic target)
    {
        try
        {
            if (target == null) return;

            if (SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode)
            {
                return;
            }

            // 1. Guard Table Headers from modification
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            bool hitProtectedStructure = false;
            try
            {
                foreach (dynamic tbl in sh.ListObjects)
                {
                    if (xlApp.Intersect(target, tbl.HeaderRowRange) != null)
                    {
                        hitProtectedStructure = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
            catch { }

            if (hitProtectedStructure)
            {
                bool origEvents = true;
                try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }
                try { xlApp.Undo(); } catch { }
                try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
                SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation("Table headers are protected to allow sorting and cannot be modified.");
                return;
            }

            // 2. Handle Multi-Cell Deletes (e.g., selecting an entire row and hitting Delete)
            if (target.Cells.Count > 1)
            {
                TableRowManager.HandleSheetChange(target);
                return;
            }

            dynamic singleCell = target.Cells[1, 1];
            string sheetName = sh?.Name?.ToString() ?? "Sheet";
            string addr = singleCell.Address?.ToString() ?? "";
            AppLogger.Info($"OnSheetChange: {sheetName}!{addr}");

            dynamic? table = null;
            try { table = singleCell.ListObject; } catch { }

            if (table != null)
            {
                RuleEvaluator.EvaluateAndHealRow(
                    worksheet: sh,
                    table: table,
                    excelRow: (int)singleCell.Row,
                    editedAbsoluteCol: (int)singleCell.Column
                );
            }
            else
            {
                ExecuteLegacyCellSyncOnType(singleCell);
            }

            if (DropDownCommands.IsDropdownCell(singleCell))
            {
                Arrow.ShowArrowForCell(singleCell);
            }

            TableRowManager.HandleSheetChange(singleCell);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error("OnSheetChange error", ex);
        }
    }

    private static void ExecuteLegacyCellSyncOnType(dynamic singleCell)
    {
        var config = Metadata.ReadConfig(singleCell);
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.SyncLeft)) return;

        string cellVal = singleCell.Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(cellVal)) return;

        SyncEngine.ExecuteSync(singleCell, config.SyncLeft, cellVal, cellVal);
    }

    private static void OnSheetBeforeDoubleClick(dynamic sh, dynamic target, ref bool cancel)
    {
        try
        {
            if (!DropDownCommands.IsDropdownCell(target))
                return;

            bool isTemplate = SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode ||
                              Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable(LastTemplateModeKey) == "1";

            if (isTemplate)
            {
                cancel = true;
                Arrow.HideAll();
                DropDownCommands.InspectAndConfigure(target);
            }
            else
            {
                Arrow.HideAll();
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error("OnSheetBeforeDoubleClick error", ex);
        }
    }
}

FILE: Core\02_WorkbookOpen\Sheet\Sheet.Operations.cs

namespace SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;

using System;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;

public static class SheetOperations
{
    public static bool IsTemplateEditMode { get; set; } = false;
    public const string LookupsSheetName = "Lookups";
    public const string DataSheetName = "Data";

    public static string ResolveSheetName(string[] candidates, string defaultFallback)
    {
        if (candidates == null || candidates.Length == 0)
            return defaultFallback;

        try
        {
            object sheetsObj = XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlfGetWorkbook, 1);
            if (sheetsObj is object[,] sheetArray)
            {
                foreach (object item in sheetArray)
                {
                    string rawName = item?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                    int bracketIdx = rawName.LastIndexOf(']');
                    string cleanName = bracketIdx >= 0 ? rawName.Substring(bracketIdx + 1) : rawName;
                    foreach (var candidate in candidates)
                    {
                        if (string.Equals(cleanName, candidate, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        {
                            return cleanName;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        catch { }

        return defaultFallback;
    }

    public static bool IsMasterTemplateFile()
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic? wb = xlApp?.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (wb == null) return false;

            string fileName = wb.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            return fileName.EndsWith(".xltx", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                   fileName.EndsWith(".xltm", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        }
        catch
        {
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static void ShowStatusConfirmation(string message)
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            if (xlApp != null)
            {
                xlApp.StatusBar = $"[SKSB System] {message}";
            }
        }
        catch { }
    }

    public static void SetAuxiliarySheetsVisibility(bool visible)
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            using var _ = AppLogger.Scope($"SetAuxiliarySheetsVisibility(visible={visible})");
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic wb = xlApp?.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (wb == null) return;

            bool origUpdating = true;
            bool origEvents = true;
            try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }
            try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }

            string activeSheetBefore = xlApp.ActiveSheet?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;

            try
            {
                SetSingleSheetVisibility(LookupsSheetName, visible);
                SetSingleSheetVisibility(DataSheetName, visible);
                MoveAuxiliarySheetsToEnd();

                // Restore original active sheet if Excel shifted focus
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(activeSheetBefore))
                {
                    try { wb.Sheets[activeSheetBefore].Activate(); } catch { }
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
                try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
            }
        });
    }

    private static void SetSingleSheetVisibility(string sheetName, bool visible)
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (wb == null) return;

            dynamic? ws = null;
            try { ws = wb.Sheets[sheetName]; } catch { }
            if (ws == null) return;

            int targetVis = visible ? -1 /* xlSheetVisible */ : 0 /* xlSheetHidden */;
            if ((int)ws.Visible == targetVis)
            {
                return; // Already in desired state, do not trigger UI repaint
            }

            string currentActive = wb.ActiveSheet?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;

            if (!visible && string.Equals(currentActive, sheetName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                foreach (dynamic candidate in wb.Sheets)
                {
                    string cName = candidate.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                    if (!cName.Equals(LookupsSheetName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                        !cName.Equals(DataSheetName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        candidate.Activate();
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            ws.Visible = targetVis;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error($"SetSingleSheetVisibility('{sheetName}', visible={visible}) failed", ex);
        }
    }

    public static void MoveAuxiliarySheetsToEnd()
    {
        MoveSingleSheetToEnd(LookupsSheetName);
        MoveSingleSheetToEnd(DataSheetName);
    }

    private static void MoveSingleSheetToEnd(string targetSheetName)
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (wb == null) return;

            dynamic sheets = wb.Sheets;
            dynamic? targetSheet = null;
            try { targetSheet = sheets[targetSheetName]; } catch { }
            if (targetSheet == null) return;

            int totalSheets = (int)sheets.Count;
            int currentIdx = (int)targetSheet.Index;

            // Lookups and Data together occupy the final two slots (totalSheets - 1 and totalSheets).
            // If the sheet is already in the final 2 positions, do NOT move it!
            if (currentIdx >= totalSheets - 1)
            {
                return;
            }

            dynamic? activeSheetBeforeMove = wb.ActiveSheet;

            targetSheet.Move(After: sheets[totalSheets]);
            AppLogger.Info($"[MoveSingleSheetToEnd] Moved '{targetSheetName}' from {currentIdx} to end ({totalSheets}).");

            if (activeSheetBeforeMove != null)
            {
                try { activeSheetBeforeMove.Activate(); } catch { }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error($"MoveSingleSheetToEnd('{targetSheetName}') failed", ex);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Safely purges all operational data rows across all sheets, preserving:
    /// 1. Headers on any row (Row 1, Row 2, Row 3, etc.)
    /// 2. Exactly 1 clean archetype row per table (keeping calculated formulas and styles intact)
    /// 3. Non-table sheet parameter anchor cells (e.g., Dates, Range Filters in rows 1-2)
    /// 4. System sheets ('Lookups' and 'Data')
    /// </summary>
    public static void PurgeAllSheetsData()
    {
        dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
        dynamic wb = xlApp?.ActiveWorkbook;
        if (wb == null) return;

        bool origEvents = true;
        bool origUpdating = true;
        try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }
        try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }

        try
        {
            foreach (dynamic ws in wb.Sheets)
            {
                string sheetName = ws.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;

                // Never purge Lookups or Data sheets
                if (sheetName.Equals("Lookups", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                    sheetName.Equals("Data", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)ws, new Action(() =>
                {
                    int tableCount = 0;
                    try { tableCount = (int)ws.ListObjects.Count; } catch { }

                    for (int t = 1; t <= tableCount; t++)
                    {
                        dynamic table = ws.ListObjects[t];
                        PurgeSingleTable(ws, table);
                    }
                }));
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error("PurgeAllSheetsData failed", ex);
        }
        finally
        {
            try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
            try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
        }
    }

    private static void PurgeSingleTable(dynamic ws, dynamic table)
    {
        if (table == null) return;

        bool hadTotals = false;
        try { hadTotals = (bool)table.ShowTotals; } catch { }

        try
        {
            // 1. Dynamically read the table's actual coordinates from Excel COM
            dynamic headerRange = table.HeaderRowRange;
            int headerRow = (int)headerRange.Row;       // e.g. Row 1, 2, or 3!
            int startCol = (int)headerRange.Column;
            int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
            int archetypeRow = headerRow + 1;           // First data row directly beneath header
            int endCol = startCol + colCount - 1;

            // 2. Temporarily disable total row (restored in finally)
            if (hadTotals) { try { table.ShowTotals = false; } catch { } }

            // 3. Clear data from all rows BELOW the archetype row (if any exist)
            dynamic? body = table.DataBodyRange;
            int currentBodyRows = body != null ? (int)body.Rows.Count : 0;

            if (currentBodyRows > 1)
            {
                int clearStartRow = archetypeRow + 1;
                int clearEndRow = headerRow + currentBodyRows;

                // Resize table back down to 1 row first
                dynamic singleRowRange = ws.Range[ws.Cells[headerRow, startCol], ws.Cells[archetypeRow, endCol]];
                table.Resize(singleRowRange);

                // Clear any lingering cells outside the table
                dynamic staleRange = ws.Range[ws.Cells[clearStartRow, startCol], ws.Cells[clearEndRow, endCol]];
                try { staleRange.Clear(); } catch { }
            }
            else if (currentBodyRows == 0)
            {
                // If the table was shrunk to 0 body rows, re-expand to exactly 1 archetype row
                dynamic singleRowRange = ws.Range[ws.Cells[headerRow, startCol], ws.Cells[archetypeRow, endCol]];
                table.Resize(singleRowRange);
            }

            // 4. Clean the remaining single archetype row (archetypeRow)
            // CRITICAL: Preserve cell formulas, clear only user data values
            for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
            {
                int absCol = startCol + c - 1;
                dynamic cell = ws.Cells[archetypeRow, absCol];

                string formula = cell.Formula2?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(formula))
                    formula = cell.Formula?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;

                // If the cell contains a formula (e.g. =XLOOKUP, =IF, =AND), DO NOT clear it!
                if (!formula.StartsWith("="))
                {
                    cell.Value2 = null;
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            string tName = table?.Name?.ToString() ?? "Table";
            AppLogger.Error($"PurgeSingleTable failed for '{tName}'", ex);
        }
        finally
        {
            if (hadTotals) { try { table.ShowTotals = true; } catch { } }
        }
    }
}

FILE: Core\02_WorkbookOpen\Sheet\Sheet.Protections.cs

namespace SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using ExcelDna.Integration;

public static class SheetProtections
{
    private const string ProtectionPassword = "SKSB_Internal_Guard";

    // Whitelist of styles that represent user-editable inputs
    public static readonly HashSet<string> UnlockedStyles = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
    {
        "DropDown",
        "Unlocked",
        "StandardInput",
        "DisplayDate",
        "DisplayRange",
        "Comma",        // Standard Excel Comma style (#,##0.00)
        "Comma [0]",     // Comma style without decimals (#,##0)
        "Cost"
    };

    public static bool IsUnlockedStyle(string? styleName) =>
        !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(styleName) && UnlockedStyles.Contains(styleName.Trim());

    public static bool IsProtected(dynamic worksheet)
    {
        try { return (bool)worksheet.ProtectContents; } catch { return false; }
    }

    public static void Unprotect(dynamic worksheet)
    {
        try { worksheet.Unprotect(ProtectionPassword); } catch { }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Executes an action that modifies table structure by temporarily lifting protection.
    /// </summary>
    public static void ExecuteWithBypass(dynamic worksheet, Action action)
    {
        if (worksheet == null || action == null) return;
        // Sheet protection is no longer used; lift any legacy protection and never re-apply it.
        if (IsProtected(worksheet)) Unprotect(worksheet);
        action();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Unlocks table structure for native sorting, while locking non-editable columns
    /// using Excel Custom Data Validation (=FALSE) with a custom alert popup.
    /// </summary>
    public static void ApplyStyleDrivenProtection(dynamic worksheet)
    {
        if (SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode)
        {
            try { worksheet.Unprotect(ProtectionPassword); } catch { }
            try
            {
                worksheet.Cells.Locked = false;
                foreach (dynamic table in worksheet.ListObjects)
                {
                    table.Range.Locked = false;
                    foreach (dynamic col in table.ListColumns)
                    {
                        try { col.Range.Validation.Delete(); } catch { }
                    }
                }
            }
            catch { }
            return;
        }

        dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
        bool origUpdating = true;
        try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }

        try
        {
            try { worksheet.Unprotect(ProtectionPassword); } catch { }

            // Configure all tables on the worksheet (no sheet protection is applied)
            foreach (dynamic table in worksheet.ListObjects)
            {
                dynamic bodyRange = table.DataBodyRange;
                int headerRow = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row;
                int startCol = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Column;
                int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
                int bodyRows = bodyRange != null ? (int)bodyRange.Rows.Count : 1;

                for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                {
                    int currentCol = startCol + c - 1;
                    dynamic archetypeCell = worksheet.Cells[headerRow + 1, currentCol];
                    string styleName = archetypeCell.Style?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;

                    dynamic colBody = worksheet.Range[
                        worksheet.Cells[headerRow + 1, currentCol],
                        worksheet.Cells[headerRow + bodyRows, currentCol]
                    ];

                    if (!UnlockedStyles.Contains(styleName))
                    {
                        // Apply custom =FALSE Stop alert to calculated / read-only columns
                        try
                        {
                            colBody.Validation.Delete();
                            colBody.Validation.Add(
                                Type: 7,       // xlValidateCustom
                                AlertStyle: 1, // xlValidAlertStop
                                Formula1: "=FALSE"
                            );
                            colBody.Validation.IgnoreBlank = true;
                            colBody.Validation.ErrorMessage = "This column is auto-calculated and cannot be modified directly.";
                            colBody.Validation.ErrorTitle = "Protected Field";
                        }
                        catch { }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        // Clean up any stale =FALSE validation if the column style was changed to editable
                        try
                        {
                            if (colBody.Validation?.Formula1 == "=FALSE")
                            {
                                colBody.Validation.Delete();
                            }
                        }
                        catch { }
                    }
                }
            }

        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[ApplyStyleDrivenProtection Error] {ex.Message}");
        }
        finally
        {
            try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
        }
    }

    private static void UnlockIfStylePermits(dynamic cell)
    {
        try
        {
            string styleName = cell.Style?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            if (UnlockedStyles.Contains(styleName))
            {
                cell.Locked = false;
            }
        }
        catch { }
    }
}

FILE: Core\02_WorkbookOpen\Sync\Sync.DataList.cs

namespace SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sync;

using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Linq.Expressions;
using System.Reflection;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types.Attributes;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Storage;

public static class DataList
{
    public const string TargetSheet = SheetOperations.DataSheetName;

    private class PropertyDescriptor
    {
        public string ListName { get; init; } = string.Empty;
        public Func<object, object?> Getter { get; init; } = null!;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Synchronizes specific entity models requested by an operational manager.
    /// Preserves column row-alignment across compound fields (e.g., StaffID and StaffName).
    /// </summary>
    public static int SyncEntities(params Type[] entityTypes)
    {
        if (entityTypes == null || entityTypes.Length == 0) return 0;
        return ExecuteEntityBlockSync(headersOnly: false, entityTypes);
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Discovers and synchronizes all public entities decorated with [DataList] across all assemblies.
    /// Dedicated strictly for Template Edit Mode.
    /// </summary>
    public static int SyncAllTemplateData(bool headersOnly = false)
    {
        var targetAssemblies = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies()
            .Where(a => !a.IsDynamic && (a.FullName?.StartsWith("SKSB_App", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ?? false))
            .Distinct()
            .ToList();

        var allClassTypes = new List<Type>();
        foreach (var asm in targetAssemblies)
        {
            try
            {
                var types = asm.GetTypes().Where(t => t.IsClass && t.IsPublic && !t.IsAbstract && !t.IsEnum);
                allClassTypes.AddRange(types);
            }
            catch (ReflectionTypeLoadException ex)
            {
                var loadable = ex.Types.Where(t => t != null && t.IsClass && t.IsPublic && !t.IsAbstract && !t.IsEnum);
                allClassTypes.AddRange(loadable!);
            }
            catch { }
        }

        return ExecuteEntityBlockSync(headersOnly, allClassTypes.ToArray());
    }

    private static int ExecuteEntityBlockSync(bool headersOnly, Type[] targetTypes)
    {
        var childDetailTypes = new HashSet<Type>();
        foreach (var type in targetTypes)
        {
            foreach (var prop in type.GetProperties(BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Instance))
            {
                if (prop.GetCustomAttribute<DataDetailAttribute>() != null ||
                    (prop.PropertyType.IsClass && prop.PropertyType != typeof(string) && !prop.PropertyType.IsArray && !typeof(IEnumerable).IsAssignableFrom(prop.PropertyType)))
                {
                    childDetailTypes.Add(prop.PropertyType);
                }
            }
        }

        var rootEntityTypes = targetTypes.Where(t => !childDetailTypes.Contains(t)).Distinct().ToList();

        var loadMethod = typeof(OfflineSnapshot).GetMethod(
            nameof(OfflineSnapshot.Load),
            BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static,
            Type.EmptyTypes
        );

        var entityBlocks = new List<EntityTableBlock>();
        var registeredListNames = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        foreach (var type in rootEntityTypes)
        {
            var descriptors = DiscoverDecoratedProperties(type);
            descriptors = descriptors.Where(d => !registeredListNames.Contains(d.ListName)).ToList();
            if (descriptors.Count == 0) continue;

            IList? cachedRecords = null;
            if (!headersOnly && loadMethod != null)
            {
                try
                {
                    var genericLoad = loadMethod.MakeGenericMethod(typeof(List<>).MakeGenericType(type));
                    cachedRecords = genericLoad.Invoke(null, null) as IList;
                }
                catch { }
            }

            var block = BuildEntityBlock(type.Name, descriptors, cachedRecords, injectPlaceholderIfEmpty: SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode);
            if (block != null && block.ColumnNames.Count > 0)
            {
                entityBlocks.Add(block);
                foreach (var col in block.ColumnNames)
                {
                    registeredListNames.Add(col);
                }
            }
        }

        SyncHelper.SyncEntityBlocks(TargetSheet, entityBlocks);
        return entityBlocks.Sum(b => b.ColumnNames.Count);
    }

    public static bool SyncData<TEntity>(IEnumerable<TEntity>? entities = null) where TEntity : class
    {
        var records = entities?.ToList() ?? OfflineSnapshot.Load<List<TEntity>>();
        if (records == null) return false;

        var descriptors = DiscoverDecoratedProperties(typeof(TEntity));
        if (descriptors.Count == 0) return false;

        var block = BuildEntityBlock(typeof(TEntity).Name, descriptors, records, injectPlaceholderIfEmpty: SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode);
        if (block == null) return false;

        SyncHelper.SyncEntityBlocks(TargetSheet, new List<EntityTableBlock> { block });
        return true;
    }

    public static bool SyncProperty<TEntity, TProp>(
        Expression<Func<TEntity, TProp>> propertyExpression,
        IEnumerable<TEntity>? entities = null,
        string? customListName = null) where TEntity : class
    {
        var records = entities?.ToList() ?? OfflineSnapshot.Load<List<TEntity>>();
        if (records == null || records.Count == 0) return false;

        string propName = (propertyExpression.Body as MemberExpression)?.Member.Name ?? "Item";
        string listName = customListName ?? $"{propName}List";

        var compiled = propertyExpression.Compile();
        var values = records
            .Select(r => compiled(r)?.ToString()?.Trim())
            .Where(v => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v))
            .ToList();

        SyncValues(listName, values!);
        return true;
    }

    public static void SyncValues(string listName, IEnumerable<string> values)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(listName)) return;

        var dict = new Dictionary<string, IEnumerable<string>?>
        {
            { listName, values }
        };

        SyncHelper.SyncDictionary(
            TargetSheet,
            dict,
            WriteMode.SmartOrganize,
            injectPlaceholderIfEmpty: SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode
        );
    }

    private static EntityTableBlock? BuildEntityBlock(
        string entityName,
        List<PropertyDescriptor> descriptors,
        IList? cachedRecords,
        bool injectPlaceholderIfEmpty)
    {
        if (descriptors == null || descriptors.Count == 0) return null;

        var colNames = descriptors.Select(d => d.ListName.EndsWith("List", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            ? d.ListName
            : $"{d.ListName}List").ToList();

        var validRecords = new List<object>();
        if (cachedRecords != null)
        {
            foreach (var rec in cachedRecords)
            {
                if (rec == null) continue;
                object? primaryVal = descriptors[0].Getter(rec);
                string primaryStr = primaryVal?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(primaryStr) && primaryStr != "0")
                {
                    validRecords.Add(rec);
                }
            }
        }

        // Numeric primary key sort (e.g., StaffID 10, 15, 100, 101)
        if (validRecords.Count > 1)
        {
            validRecords = validRecords.OrderBy(r =>
            {
                object? val = descriptors[0].Getter(r);
                if (val == null) return (object)0L;
                if (val is long l) return l;
                if (val is int i) return (long)i;
                if (long.TryParse(val.ToString(), out long parsedL)) return parsedL;
                return val.ToString()!;
            }).ToList();
        }

        int rowCount = validRecords.Count;
        int colCount = descriptors.Count;

        if (rowCount == 0)
        {
            if (!injectPlaceholderIfEmpty) return null;

            object[,] placeholderGrid = new object[1, colCount];
            for (int c = 0; c < colCount; c++)
            {
                placeholderGrid[0, c] = $"[{colNames[c].Replace("List", "")} Placeholder]";
            }

            return new EntityTableBlock
            {
                EntityName = entityName,
                ColumnNames = colNames,
                Grid = placeholderGrid
            };
        }

        object[,] dataGrid = new object[rowCount, colCount];
        for (int r = 0; r < rowCount; r++)
        {
            var record = validRecords[r];
            for (int c = 0; c < colCount; c++)
            {
                object? rawVal = descriptors[c].Getter(record);
                if (rawVal == null)
                {
                    dataGrid[r, c] = string.Empty;
                }
                else if (rawVal is long l)
                {
                    dataGrid[r, c] = (double)l;
                }
                else if (rawVal is int i)
                {
                    dataGrid[r, c] = (double)i;
                }
                else if (rawVal is double d)
                {
                    dataGrid[r, c] = d;
                }
                else
                {
                    string str = rawVal.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                    if (double.TryParse(str, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double parsedD) && str.Length < 10 && !str.StartsWith("0"))
                    {
                        dataGrid[r, c] = parsedD;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        dataGrid[r, c] = str;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return new EntityTableBlock
        {
            EntityName = entityName,
            ColumnNames = colNames,
            Grid = dataGrid
        };
    }

    private static List<PropertyDescriptor> DiscoverDecoratedProperties(Type rootType)
    {
        var results = new List<PropertyDescriptor>();

        void Scan(Type targetType, Func<object, object?> parentGetter)
        {
            var props = targetType.GetProperties(BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Instance);
            foreach (var p in props)
            {
                var attr = p.GetCustomAttribute<DataListAttribute>();
                if (attr != null)
                {
                    string listName = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(attr.CustomListName)
                        ? attr.CustomListName!
                        : (p.Name.EndsWith("List", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? p.Name : $"{p.Name}List");

                    results.Add(new PropertyDescriptor
                    {
                        ListName = listName,
                        Getter = obj =>
                        {
                            var parent = parentGetter(obj);
                            return parent != null ? p.GetValue(parent) : null;
                        }
                    });
                }
                else if (p.GetCustomAttribute<DataDetailAttribute>() != null ||
                         (p.PropertyType.IsClass && p.PropertyType != typeof(string) && !p.PropertyType.IsArray && !typeof(IEnumerable).IsAssignableFrom(p.PropertyType)))
                {
                    Scan(p.PropertyType, obj =>
                    {
                        var parent = parentGetter(obj);
                        return parent != null ? p.GetValue(parent) : null;
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        Scan(rootType, obj => obj);
        return results;
    }
}

FILE: Core\02_WorkbookOpen\Sync\Sync.EnumList.cs

namespace SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sync;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;

public static class EnumList
{
    public const string TargetSheet = SheetOperations.LookupsSheetName;

    /// <summary>
    /// Auto-discovers and syncs public Enums across Core and domain assemblies into the 'Lookups' tab.
    /// </summary>
    public static void SyncAllCoreEnums(params Type[] excludeTypes)
    {
        var excludeSet = new HashSet<Type>(excludeTypes ?? Array.Empty<Type>());
        var targetAssemblies = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies()
            .Where(a => !a.IsDynamic && (a.FullName?.StartsWith("SKSB_App", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ?? false))
            .Distinct()
            .ToList();

        var coreAssembly = typeof(EnumList).Assembly;
        if (!targetAssemblies.Contains(coreAssembly))
        {
            targetAssemblies.Add(coreAssembly);
        }

        var allEnums = new List<Type>();
        foreach (var asm in targetAssemblies)
        {
            try
            {
                var enums = asm.GetTypes().Where(t => t.IsEnum && t.IsPublic && !excludeSet.Contains(t));
                allEnums.AddRange(enums);
            }
            catch (ReflectionTypeLoadException ex)
            {
                var loadable = ex.Types.Where(t => t != null && t.IsEnum && t.IsPublic && !excludeSet.Contains(t!));
                allEnums.AddRange(loadable!);
            }
            catch { }
        }

        var uniqueEnums = allEnums
            .GroupBy(t => t.Name)
            .Select(g => g.First())
            .OrderBy(t => t.Name)
            .ToArray();

        if (uniqueEnums.Length == 0) return;
        Sync(uniqueEnums);
    }

    public static void Sync(params Type[] enumTypes)
    {
        if (enumTypes == null || enumTypes.Length == 0) return;

        var mappings = new Dictionary<string, IEnumerable<string>?>();
        foreach (var type in enumTypes)
        {
            if (!type.IsEnum) continue;
            string[] values = Enum.GetNames(type)
                                  .Where(n => !string.Equals(n, "None", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                  .ToArray();
            mappings[type.Name] = values;
        }

        SyncHelper.SyncDictionary(TargetSheet, mappings, WriteMode.SequentialReset, injectPlaceholderIfEmpty: false);
    }

    public static void SyncDictionary(Dictionary<string, string[]> enumMappings)
    {
        var dict = enumMappings.ToDictionary(k => k.Key, v => (IEnumerable<string>?)v.Value);
        SyncHelper.SyncDictionary(TargetSheet, dict, WriteMode.SequentialReset, injectPlaceholderIfEmpty: false);
    }
}

FILE: Core\02_WorkbookOpen\Sync\Sync.Helper.cs
