namespace SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sync;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;

public enum WriteMode
{
    SequentialReset,
    SmartOrganize
}

public class EntityTableBlock
{
    public string EntityName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public List<string> ColumnNames { get; set; } = new();
    public List<string> Columns => ColumnNames;
    public object[,] Grid { get; set; } = new object[0, 0];
    public int RowCount => Grid.GetLength(0);
    public int ColCount => Grid.GetLength(1);
}

public static class SyncHelper
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Writes entity table blocks directly to targetSheet via COM without activating the sheet or moving the screen.
    /// Performs live verification immediately after write.
    /// </summary>
    public static void SyncEntityBlocks(string targetSheet, List<EntityTableBlock> blocks)
    {
        if (blocks == null || blocks.Count == 0) return;

        using var _ = AppLogger.Scope($"SyncEntityBlocks(targetSheet='{targetSheet}', blocks={blocks.Count})");

        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
                dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
                if (wb == null) return;

                string activeSheetBefore = xlApp.ActiveSheet?.Name?.ToString() ?? "Unknown";
                AppLogger.Info($"[SyncEntityBlocks Start] Target: '{targetSheet}' | User ActiveSheet: '{activeSheetBefore}'");

                dynamic ws = EnsureSheetExistsCom(wb, targetSheet);
                if (ws == null)
                {
                    AppLogger.Error($"[SYNC FAILED] Could not locate or create '{targetSheet}'");
                    return;
                }

                // 1. Clear operational area on targetSheet (Cols A..CW, Rows 1..2500) without activating or selecting
                dynamic clearRange = ws.Range[ws.Cells[1, 1], ws.Cells[2500, 100]];
                clearRange.ClearContents();

                int currentCol = 1; // 1-based COM index
                int totalBlocksWritten = 0;
                int totalColumnsWritten = 0;

                foreach (var block in blocks)
                {
                    int rowCount = block.RowCount;
                    int colCount = block.ColCount;
                    if (rowCount == 0 || colCount == 0) continue;

                    AppLogger.Info($"Writing EntityBlock '{block.EntityName}' ({rowCount} rows x {colCount} cols) at Col {currentCol} on '{targetSheet}' (Silent COM)");

                    // A. Write Headers (Row 1)
                    object[,] headerGrid = new object[1, colCount];
                    for (int c = 0; c < colCount; c++)
                    {
                        headerGrid[0, c] = block.ColumnNames[c];
                    }
                    dynamic headerRange = ws.Range[ws.Cells[1, currentCol], ws.Cells[1, currentCol + colCount - 1]];
                    headerRange.Value2 = headerGrid;

                    // B. Write 2D Data Grid (Rows 2..rowCount+1)
                    dynamic dataRange = ws.Range[ws.Cells[2, currentCol], ws.Cells[rowCount + 1, currentCol + colCount - 1]];
                    dataRange.Value2 = block.Grid;

                    // C. Register Defined Names
                    for (int c = 0; c < colCount; c++)
                    {
                        string listName = block.ColumnNames[c];
                        try { wb.Names.Item(listName).Delete(); } catch { }

                        int r1 = 2;
                        int r2 = rowCount + 1;
                        int cPos = currentCol + c;
                        string refersTo = $"='{targetSheet}'!R{r1}C{cPos}:R{r2}C{cPos}";
                        wb.Names.Add(Name: listName, RefersToR1C1: refersTo, Visible: true);
                    }

                    currentCol += colCount + 1;
                    totalBlocksWritten++;
                    totalColumnsWritten += colCount;
                }

                // =========================================================================
                // VERIFICATION: Check whether data was written and Named Ranges are valid
                // =========================================================================
                bool verifySuccess = VerifyEntityBlocks(wb, ws, blocks);
                AppLogger.Info($"[SYNC VERIFICATION RESULT] EntityBlocks on '{targetSheet}': {(verifySuccess ? "SUCCESS" : "FAILED / PARTIAL")} (Blocks={totalBlocksWritten}, Cols={totalColumnsWritten})");

                // Confirm active sheet never shifted
                string activeSheetAfter = xlApp.ActiveSheet?.Name?.ToString() ?? "Unknown";
                if (!string.Equals(activeSheetBefore, activeSheetAfter, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    AppLogger.Warn($"[VIEWPORT SHIFT DETECTED] ActiveSheet changed from '{activeSheetBefore}' to '{activeSheetAfter}'. Restoring focus...");
                    try { wb.Sheets[activeSheetBefore].Activate(); } catch { }
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                AppLogger.Error($"SyncEntityBlocks on '{targetSheet}' threw exception", ex);
            }
        });
    }

    public static void SyncDictionary(
        string targetSheet,
        Dictionary<string, IEnumerable<string>?> lists,
        WriteMode writeMode,
        bool injectPlaceholderIfEmpty = false)
    {
        if (lists == null || lists.Count == 0) return;

        var sanitized = new Dictionary<string, string[]>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        foreach (var (rawName, items) in lists)
        {
            string listName = rawName.EndsWith("List", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                ? rawName
                : $"{rawName}List";

            var distinctValues = (items ?? Enumerable.Empty<string>())
                .Where(v => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v))
                .Select(v => v.Trim())
                .Where(v => v != "0" && !v.StartsWith("#"))
                .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                .OrderBy(v => v)
                .ToArray();

            if (distinctValues.Length > 0)
            {
                sanitized[listName] = distinctValues;
            }
            else if (injectPlaceholderIfEmpty)
            {
                string stub = $"[{listName.Replace("List", "")} Placeholder]";
                sanitized[listName] = new[] { stub };
            }
        }

        if (sanitized.Count > 0)
        {
            SyncNamedLists(targetSheet, sanitized, writeMode);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Writes list mappings directly to targetSheet via COM in the background.
    /// Avoids activating the auxiliary sheet and runs verification immediately afterwards.
    /// </summary>
    public static void SyncNamedLists(
        string targetSheet,
        Dictionary<string, string[]> mappings,
        WriteMode writeMode)
    {
        if (mappings == null || mappings.Count == 0) return;

        using var _ = AppLogger.Scope($"SyncNamedLists(targetSheet='{targetSheet}', keys={mappings.Count}, mode={writeMode})");

        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
                dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
                if (wb == null) return;

                string activeSheetBefore = xlApp.ActiveSheet?.Name?.ToString() ?? "Unknown";
                AppLogger.Info($"[SyncNamedLists Start] Target: '{targetSheet}' | User ActiveSheet: '{activeSheetBefore}'");

                dynamic ws = EnsureSheetExistsCom(wb, targetSheet);
                if (ws == null)
                {
                    AppLogger.Error($"[SYNC FAILED] Could not locate or create '{targetSheet}'");
                    return;
                }

                // Inside SyncHelper.SyncNamedLists (Core\02_WorkbookOpen\Sync\Sync.Helper.cs),
                // replace the foreach loop (lines 166-222) with:
                int maxColsToScan = 200;
                object[,]? headerRowGrid = null;
                if (writeMode == WriteMode.SmartOrganize)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        headerRowGrid = ws.Range[ws.Cells[1, 1], ws.Cells[1, maxColsToScan]].Value2 as object[,];
                    }
                    catch { }
                }

                int colOneBased = 1;
                foreach (var (listName, values) in mappings)
                {
                    int targetCol = colOneBased;

                    if (writeMode == WriteMode.SmartOrganize)
                    {
                        targetCol = 0;
                        for (int c = 1; c <= maxColsToScan; c += 2)
                        {
                            string existingHeader = headerRowGrid != null
                                ? (headerRowGrid[1, c]?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty)
                                : (ws.Cells[1, c].Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty);

                            if (string.Equals(existingHeader, listName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            {
                                targetCol = c;
                                break;
                            }
                            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(existingHeader) && targetCol == 0)
                            {
                                targetCol = c;
                            }
                        }
                        if (targetCol == 0) targetCol = 1;
                        if (headerRowGrid != null && targetCol <= maxColsToScan)
                        {
                            headerRowGrid[1, targetCol] = listName;
                        }
                    }

                    int rowCount = values.Length;

                    // Clear target column and write header + values in a single contiguous 2D array write
                    dynamic wipeRange = ws.Range[ws.Cells[1, targetCol], ws.Cells[Math.Max(2000, rowCount + 10), targetCol]];
                    wipeRange.ClearContents();

                    object[,] colPayload = new object[rowCount + 1, 1];
                    colPayload[0, 0] = listName;
                    for (int i = 0; i < rowCount; i++)
                    {
                        string v = values[i];
                        colPayload[i + 1, 0] = double.TryParse(v, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double dVal)
                            ? dVal
                            : v;
                    }

                    dynamic writeRange = ws.Range[ws.Cells[1, targetCol], ws.Cells[rowCount + 1, targetCol]];
                    writeRange.Value2 = colPayload;

                    if (rowCount > 0)
                    {
                        try { wb.Names.Item(listName).Delete(); } catch { }
                        string refersTo = $"='{targetSheet}'!R2C{targetCol}:R{rowCount + 1}C{targetCol}";
                        wb.Names.Add(Name: listName, RefersToR1C1: refersTo, Visible: true);
                    }

                    if (writeMode == WriteMode.SequentialReset)
                    {
                        colOneBased += 2;
                    }
                }

                // =========================================================================
                // VERIFICATION: Check whether columns and Defined Names exist
                // =========================================================================
                bool verifySuccess = VerifyNamedLists(wb, ws, mappings);
                AppLogger.Info($"[SYNC VERIFICATION RESULT] NamedLists on '{targetSheet}': {(verifySuccess ? "SUCCESS" : "FAILED / PARTIAL")} (Total Lists={mappings.Count})");

                // Confirm active sheet was not shifted
                string activeSheetAfter = xlApp.ActiveSheet?.Name?.ToString() ?? "Unknown";
                if (!string.Equals(activeSheetBefore, activeSheetAfter, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    AppLogger.Warn($"[VIEWPORT SHIFT DETECTED] ActiveSheet changed from '{activeSheetBefore}' to '{activeSheetAfter}'. Restoring focus...");
                    try { wb.Sheets[activeSheetBefore].Activate(); } catch { }
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                AppLogger.Error($"SyncNamedLists on '{targetSheet}' threw exception", ex);
            }
        });
    }

    private static dynamic EnsureSheetExistsCom(dynamic wb, string sheetName)
    {
        try
        {
            foreach (dynamic ws in wb.Sheets)
            {
                if (string.Equals(ws.Name?.ToString(), sheetName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    return ws;
                }
            }

            AppLogger.Info($"Sheet '{sheetName}' not found. Adding silently at the end...");
            int count = (int)wb.Sheets.Count;
            dynamic newWs = wb.Sheets.Add(After: wb.Sheets[count]);
            newWs.Name = sheetName;
            return newWs;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error($"EnsureSheetExistsCom('{sheetName}') error", ex);
            return null!;
        }
    }

    private static bool VerifyEntityBlocks(dynamic wb, dynamic ws, List<EntityTableBlock> blocks)
    {
        try
        {
            foreach (var block in blocks)
            {
                if (block.RowCount == 0 || block.ColCount == 0) continue;

                foreach (var colName in block.ColumnNames)
                {
                    dynamic? nameObj = null;
                    try { nameObj = wb.Names.Item(colName); } catch { }
                    if (nameObj == null)
                    {
                        AppLogger.Error($"[VERIFY FAILURE] Defined Name '{colName}' was not found in Workbook Names!");
                        return false;
                    }

                    dynamic range = nameObj.RefersToRange;
                    if (range == null || (int)range.Rows.Count != block.RowCount)
                    {
                        AppLogger.Error($"[VERIFY FAILURE] Name '{colName}' points to invalid range rows: Expected {block.RowCount}");
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error("VerifyEntityBlocks exception", ex);
            return false;
        }
    }

    private static bool VerifyNamedLists(dynamic wb, dynamic ws, Dictionary<string, string[]> mappings)
    {
        try
        {
            foreach (var (listName, values) in mappings)
            {
                if (values.Length == 0) continue;

                dynamic? nameObj = null;
                try { nameObj = wb.Names.Item(listName); } catch { }
                if (nameObj == null)
                {
                    AppLogger.Error($"[VERIFY FAILURE] Defined Name '{listName}' was not created!");
                    return false;
                }

                dynamic range = nameObj.RefersToRange;
                if (range == null || (int)range.Rows.Count != values.Length)
                {
                    AppLogger.Error($"[VERIFY FAILURE] Defined Name '{listName}' row count mismatch: Expected {values.Length}");
                    return false;
                }
            }
            return true;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error("VerifyNamedLists exception", ex);
            return false;
        }
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sync;

using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Entry;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Storage;
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Windows.Forms;

public static class OfflineSave
{
    public static void HandleStartup<T>(SaveContract<T> contract)
    {
        using var _ = AppLogger.Scope($"OfflineSave.HandleStartup<{typeof(T).Name}>({contract.ModuleName})");
        try
        {
            if (SheetOperations.IsMasterTemplateFile() || SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode)
            {
                AppLogger.Info("Design Mode / Master Template File detected: Auto-load bypassed.");
                SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation("Design Mode: Auto-load bypassed.");
                return;
            }

            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic wb = xlApp?.ActiveWorkbook;
            dynamic? activeSheetBefore = null;
            try { activeSheetBefore = xlApp?.ActiveSheet; } catch { }

            AppLogger.Info("Calling contract.SyncLookups...");
            contract.SyncLookups?.Invoke(false);

            bool hasPending = OfflineSnapshot.HasPendingSnapshot<T>();
            AppLogger.Info($"HasPendingSnapshot<{typeof(T).Name}> = {hasPending}");

            if (hasPending)
            {
                string pendingFilePath = OfflineSnapshot.GetPendingUploadPath<T>();
                var pendingData = OfflineSnapshot.LoadPendingSnapshot<T>();
                bool isValid = pendingData != null && (contract.ValidateOfflinePayload?.Invoke(pendingData) ?? true);
                AppLogger.Info($"Pending snapshot loaded. Valid = {isValid}");

                if (!isValid)
                {
                    AppLogger.Warn("Found a corrupt or invalid offline pending file. Aborting upload.");
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        "Found a corrupt or invalid offline pending file. The upload has been aborted to protect the database.",
                        "Upload Rejected",
                        MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Warning
                    );
                    OfflineSnapshot.ClearPendingSnapshot<T>();
                }
                else
                {
                    DateTime localOfflineUtc = File.GetLastWriteTimeUtc(pendingFilePath);
                    DateTime? serverLastUpdateRaw = string.IsNullOrEmpty(contract.ServerTableName)
                        ? null
                        : OfflineSnapshot.GetServerLatestTimestamp(contract.ServerTableName);

                    DateTime? serverLastUpdateUtc = serverLastUpdateRaw.HasValue
                        ? (serverLastUpdateRaw.Value.Kind == DateTimeKind.Utc
                            ? serverLastUpdateRaw.Value
                            : serverLastUpdateRaw.Value.ToUniversalTime())
                        : null;

                    AppLogger.Info($"LocalPendingUtc={localOfflineUtc:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss}, ServerLatestUtc={serverLastUpdateUtc:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss}");

                    bool canCommit = true;
#if DEBUG
                    var serverDataDebug = contract.FetchFromServer != null ? contract.FetchFromServer() : default;
                    string diffMessage = contract.FormatConflictSummary != null && serverDataDebug != null
                        ? contract.FormatConflictSummary(pendingData!, serverDataDebug)
                        : string.Empty;

                    string promptMessage =
                        $"[DEBUG MODE] PENDING OFFLINE SAVE DETECTED\n" +
                        $"Module: {contract.ModuleName}\n\n" +
                        $"  Local Offline Save: {localOfflineUtc.ToLocalTime():yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss}\n" +
                        $"  Last Server Commit: {(serverLastUpdateUtc.HasValue ? serverLastUpdateUtc.Value.ToLocalTime().ToString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss") : "Unknown / Server Offline")}\n\n" +
                        (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(diffMessage) ? string.Empty : $"Changes:\n{diffMessage}\n\n") +
                        $"Choose action:\n" +
                        $"  [Yes]    Commit Offline Save to PostgreSQL Server\n" +
                        $"  [No]     Delete Offline Save (Discard local and load Server)\n" +
                        $"  [Cancel] Keep Offline Save on disk (Skip without uploading)";

                    var debugChoice = MessageBox.Show(
                        promptMessage,
                        "[DEBUG] Offline Sync Decision",
                        MessageBoxButtons.YesNoCancel,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Question
                    );

                    if (debugChoice == DialogResult.Cancel) canCommit = false;
                    else if (debugChoice == DialogResult.No)
                    {
                        OfflineSnapshot.ClearPendingSnapshot<T>();
                        canCommit = false;
                        AppLogger.Info("User discarded offline pending file.");
                    }
                    else canCommit = true;
#else
                    if (serverLastUpdateUtc.HasValue && serverLastUpdateUtc.Value > localOfflineUtc)
                    {
                        var serverData = contract.FetchFromServer != null ? contract.FetchFromServer() : default;
                        string diffMessage = contract.FormatConflictSummary != null && serverData != null
                            ? contract.FormatConflictSummary(pendingData!, serverData)
                            : $"  Your Offline Save: {localOfflineUtc.ToLocalTime():yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss}\n  Current Server:     {serverLastUpdateUtc.Value.ToLocalTime():yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss}\n";

                        var dialogChoice = MessageBox.Show(
                            $"=== DATA VERSION CONFLICT DETECTED ===\n" +
                            $"The server was modified after your last offline save.\n\n" +
                            $"{diffMessage}\n\n" +
                            $"Would you like to OVERWRITE the server with your local offline version?\n" +
                            $"  Yes: Overwrite server database with your local offline records.\n" +
                            $"  No:  Discard your offline file and load the latest server copy.",
                            "Synchronization Conflict",
                            MessageBoxButtons.YesNo,
                            MessageBoxIcon.Question
                        );

                        canCommit = (dialogChoice == DialogResult.Yes);
                        if (!canCommit) OfflineSnapshot.ClearPendingSnapshot<T>();
                    }
#endif
                    if (canCommit)
                    {
                        AppLogger.Info("Committing offline pending save to server...");
                        var (success, dbError) = contract.SaveToServer(pendingData!);
                        if (success)
                        {
                            OfflineSnapshot.ClearPendingSnapshot<T>();
                            AppLogger.Info("Committed pending snapshot to server successfully. Calling PopulateWorksheet...");

                            // FREEZE SCREEN DURING POPULATION
                            bool origUpdating = true;
                            bool origEvents = true;
                            int origCalculation = -4105;
                            try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }
                            try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }
                            try { origCalculation = (int)xlApp.Calculation; xlApp.Calculation = -4135; /* xlCalculationManual */ } catch { }
                            try
                            {
                                contract.PopulateWorksheet?.Invoke(pendingData!);
                                if (activeSheetBefore != null)
                                {
                                    try { activeSheetBefore.Activate(); } catch { }
                                }
                            }
                            finally
                            {
                                try { xlApp.Calculation = origCalculation; } catch { }
                                try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
                                try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
                            }

                            SaveNotificationDialog.ShowSuccess(
                                contract.ModuleName,
                                "Offline changes uploaded successfully!\n\n  Committed records to PostgreSQL server.\n  Local pending upload stash has been cleared."
                            );
                            return;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            AppLogger.Error($"Could not commit offline file to database: {dbError}");
                            MessageBox.Show(
                                $"Could not commit offline file to database:\n\n{dbError}\n\nThe offline file has been retained.",
                                "Server Upload Failed",
                                MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                                MessageBoxIcon.Error
                            );
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            if (contract.FetchFromServer != null)
            {
                AppLogger.Info("Calling contract.FetchFromServer()...");
                var serverData = contract.FetchFromServer();
                bool isEmpty = contract.IsDatasetEmpty(serverData);
                AppLogger.Info($"FetchFromServer returned: DataIsNull={serverData == null}, IsEmpty={isEmpty}");

                if (serverData != null && !isEmpty)
                {
                    AppLogger.Info("Calling contract.PopulateWorksheet with server records...");

                    // TOP-LEVEL FREEZE FOR THE ENTIRE POPULATE PASS
                    bool origUpdating = true;
                    bool origEvents = true;
                    int origCalculation = -4105; // xlCalculationAutomatic
                    try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }
                    try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }
                    try { origCalculation = (int)xlApp.Calculation; xlApp.Calculation = -4135; /* xlCalculationManual */ } catch { }

                    try
                    {
                        contract.PopulateWorksheet?.Invoke(serverData);

                        if (activeSheetBefore != null)
                        {
                            try { activeSheetBefore.Activate(); } catch { }
                        }
                    }
                    finally
                    {
                        try { xlApp.Calculation = origCalculation; } catch { }
                        try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
                        try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
                    }

                    SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation($"{contract.ModuleName} records loaded from server.");
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error("OfflineSave.HandleStartup failed", ex);
        }
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DateFilter;

/// <summary>
/// Universal temporal filter scope for operational queries and worksheet views.
/// Automatically discovered by Sync.EnumList.SyncAllCoreEnums() into '=DateFilterScopeList'.
/// </summary>
public enum DateFilterScope
{
    All_Time,
    Annual,
    Month,
    Day,
    None
}
`n

FILE: Core\03_RuntimeUI\DateFilter\DateFilter.Helper.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DateFilter;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;

public static class DateFilterHelper
{
    public static bool IsWithinScope(this DateFilterScope scope, DateOnly targetDate, DateOnly anchorDate)
    {
        return scope switch
        {
            DateFilterScope.All_Time or DateFilterScope.None => true,
            DateFilterScope.Annual => targetDate.Year == anchorDate.Year,
            DateFilterScope.Month => targetDate.Year == anchorDate.Year && targetDate.Month == anchorDate.Month,
            DateFilterScope.Day => targetDate == anchorDate,
            _ => true
        };
    }

    public static DateFilterScope ParseScope(string? scopeStr)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(scopeStr))
            return DateFilterScope.All_Time;

        string clean = scopeStr.Trim().Replace(" ", "_");
        if (Enum.TryParse<DateFilterScope>(clean, true, out var scope))
            return scope == DateFilterScope.None ? DateFilterScope.All_Time : scope;

        return clean.ToLowerInvariant() switch
        {
            "year" or "yearly" => DateFilterScope.Annual,
            "monthly" => DateFilterScope.Month,
            "daily" or "today" => DateFilterScope.Day,
            _ => DateFilterScope.All_Time
        };
    }

    public static IEnumerable<T> FilterByDateScope<T>(
        this IEnumerable<T>? items,
        Func<T, DateOnly> dateSelector,
        DateOnly anchorDate,
        DateFilterScope scope)
    {
        if (items == null) return Enumerable.Empty<T>();
        return items.Where(item => scope.IsWithinScope(dateSelector(item), anchorDate));
    }

    public static decimal SumByDateScope<T>(
        this IEnumerable<T>? items,
        Func<T, DateOnly> dateSelector,
        Func<T, decimal> amountSelector,
        DateOnly anchorDate,
        DateFilterScope scope)
    {
        if (items == null) return 0.00m;
        return items
            .Where(item => scope.IsWithinScope(dateSelector(item), anchorDate))
            .Sum(amountSelector);
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Environment;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.RuleEngine;

public static class Arrow
{
    private static ArrowHostForm? _arrowHost;
    private static Button? _arrowButton;
    private static PopupHostForm? _popupHost;
    private static ListBox? _listBox;
    private static dynamic? _activeCell;
    private static Action<string>? _onItemSelected;
    private static bool _isEventsHooked = false;

    private class DropdownItem
    {
        public string Display { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string Raw { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public int Section { get; set; } = 0;
        public bool IsValid { get; set; } = true;
    }

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    private static extern bool SetWindowPos(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr hWndInsertAfter, int X, int Y, int cx, int cy, uint uFlags);

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr hWnd);

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool ReleaseCapture();

    [DllImport("user32.dll", EntryPoint = "SetWindowLongPtr")]
    private static extern IntPtr SetWindowLongPtr64(IntPtr hWnd, int nIndex, IntPtr dwNewLong);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", EntryPoint = "SetWindowLong")]
    private static extern int SetWindowLong32(IntPtr hWnd, int nIndex, int dwNewLong);

    private static IntPtr SetWindowLongPtr(IntPtr hWnd, int nIndex, IntPtr dwNewLong)
    {
        bool is64Bit = ExcelRegistry.OfficeBitness switch
        {
            Bitness.Win64 => true,
            Bitness.Win32 => false,
            _ => IntPtr.Size == 8
        };

        if (is64Bit) return SetWindowLongPtr64(hWnd, nIndex, dwNewLong);
        return new IntPtr(SetWindowLong32(hWnd, nIndex, dwNewLong.ToInt32()));
    }

    public static IntPtr GetExcelHwnd(dynamic xlApp)
    {
        try
        {
            object rawHwnd = xlApp.Hwnd;
            if (rawHwnd is long l) return new IntPtr(l);
            if (rawHwnd is int i) return new IntPtr(i);
            if (rawHwnd is IntPtr p) return p;
            return new IntPtr(Convert.ToInt64(rawHwnd));
        }
        catch { return IntPtr.Zero; }
    }

    private const int GWL_HWNDPARENT = -8;
    private static readonly IntPtr HWND_TOP = new(0);
    private const uint SWP_NOACTIVATE = 0x0010;
    private const uint SWP_SHOWWINDOW = 0x0040;

    private class ArrowHostForm : Form
    {
        public ArrowHostForm()
        {
            FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.None;
            ShowInTaskbar = false;
            StartPosition = FormStartPosition.Manual;
            AutoScaleMode = AutoScaleMode.None;
        }
        protected override CreateParams CreateParams
        {
            get
            {
                var cp = base.CreateParams;
                cp.ExStyle |= 0x00000080;
                cp.ExStyle |= 0x08000000;
                return cp;
            }
        }
        protected override bool ShowWithoutActivation => true;
    }

    private class PopupHostForm : Form
    {
        public PopupHostForm()
        {
            FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.None;
            ShowInTaskbar = false;
            StartPosition = FormStartPosition.Manual;
            AutoScaleMode = AutoScaleMode.None;
        }
        protected override CreateParams CreateParams
        {
            get
            {
                var cp = base.CreateParams;
                cp.ExStyle |= 0x00000080;
                cp.ExStyle |= 0x08000000;
                return cp;
            }
        }
        protected override bool ShowWithoutActivation => true;
    }

    static Arrow()
    {
        InitializeArrowHost();
        InitializePopupHost();
    }

    private static void InitializeArrowHost()
    {
        _arrowHost = new ArrowHostForm { BackColor = Color.FromArgb(218, 220, 224) };
        _arrowButton = new Button
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
            BackColor = Color.FromArgb(242, 244, 246),
            Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            Margin = Padding.Empty,
            Padding = Padding.Empty
        };
        _arrowButton.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 1;
        _arrowButton.FlatAppearance.BorderColor = Color.FromArgb(170, 175, 182);
        _arrowButton.Paint += (s, e) =>
        {
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            int cx = _arrowButton.ClientSize.Width / 2;
            int cy = _arrowButton.ClientSize.Height / 2;
            Point[] triangle =
            {
                new(cx - 4, cy - 2),
                new(cx + 4, cy - 2),
                new(cx, cy + 3)
            };
            using var brush = new SolidBrush(Color.FromArgb(70, 70, 70));
            e.Graphics.FillPolygon(brush, triangle);
        };
        _arrowButton.Click += (_, _) => ToggleDropdown();
        _arrowHost.Controls.Add(_arrowButton);
    }

    private static void InitializePopupHost()
    {
        _popupHost = new PopupHostForm
        {
            BackColor = Color.FromArgb(160, 165, 175),
            Padding = new Padding(1)
        };
        _listBox = new ListBox
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None,
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9.5f),
            DrawMode = DrawMode.OwnerDrawFixed,
            ItemHeight = 22,
            IntegralHeight = false
        };
        _listBox.DrawItem += OnListBoxDrawItem;
        _listBox.Click += (s, e) =>
        {
            if (e is MouseEventArgs me)
            {
                int index = _listBox.IndexFromPoint(me.Location);
                if (index != ListBox.NoMatches && index >= 0)
                {
                    CommitSelection();
                }
            }
            else
            {
                CommitSelection();
            }
        };
        _listBox.KeyDown += (s, e) =>
        {
            if (e.KeyCode == Keys.Enter) CommitSelection();
            else if (e.KeyCode == Keys.Escape) DismissAndReturnFocus();
        };
        _popupHost.Controls.Add(_listBox);
        _popupHost.Deactivate += (_, _) => DismissAndReturnFocus();
    }

    public static void EnsureExcelEventsHooked()
    {
        if (_isEventsHooked) return;
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            if (xlApp == null) return;
            xlApp.WindowDeactivate += new Action<dynamic, dynamic>((wb, wn) => HideAll());
            xlApp.SheetDeactivate += new Action<dynamic>(ws => HideAll());
            _isEventsHooked = true;
            if (_activeCell != null && _arrowHost != null && _arrowHost.Visible)
            {
                ShowArrowForCell(_activeCell);
            }
        }
        catch { }
    }

    private static void OnListBoxDrawItem(object? sender, DrawItemEventArgs e)
    {
        if (e.Index < 0 || e.Index >= _listBox!.Items.Count) return;
        object rawObj = _listBox.Items[e.Index];
        if (rawObj is not DropdownItem item)
        {
            e.DrawBackground();
            using var defBrush = new SolidBrush(Color.Gray);
            e.Graphics.DrawString(rawObj.ToString(), e.Font!, defBrush, e.Bounds.X + 6, e.Bounds.Y + 3);
            return;
        }

        bool isSelected = (e.State & DrawItemState.Selected) == DrawItemState.Selected;
        Color backColor = !item.IsValid
            ? (isSelected ? Color.FromArgb(242, 242, 242) : Color.White)
            : (isSelected ? Color.FromArgb(217, 235, 255) : Color.White);
        Color foreColor = !item.IsValid ? Color.FromArgb(170, 170, 175) : Color.Black;

        using var bgBrush = new SolidBrush(backColor);
        e.Graphics.FillRectangle(bgBrush, e.Bounds);

        using var fgBrush = new SolidBrush(foreColor);
        var textBounds = new Rectangle(e.Bounds.X + 6, e.Bounds.Y + 2, e.Bounds.Width - 8, e.Bounds.Height - 4);
        var sf = new StringFormat { LineAlignment = StringAlignment.Center };
        e.Graphics.DrawString(item.Display, e.Font!, fgBrush, textBounds, sf);
    }

    public static void ShowArrowForCell(dynamic cell)
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            EnsureExcelEventsHooked();
            if (xlApp.WindowState == -4140)
            {
                HideAll();
                return;
            }

            _activeCell = cell.Cells[1, 1];
            dynamic window = xlApp.ActiveWindow;
            IntPtr excelHwnd = GetExcelHwnd(xlApp);
            if (excelHwnd != IntPtr.Zero)
            {
                SetWindowLongPtr(_arrowHost!.Handle, GWL_HWNDPARENT, excelHwnd);
                SetWindowLongPtr(_popupHost!.Handle, GWL_HWNDPARENT, excelHwnd);
            }

            int targetRow = (int)_activeCell.Row;
            int targetCol = (int)_activeCell.Column;
            dynamic targetPane = ResolveTargetPane(window, targetRow, targetCol);

            int cellTopPx = targetPane.PointsToScreenPixelsY((double)_activeCell.Top);
            int cellRightPx = targetPane.PointsToScreenPixelsX((double)_activeCell.Left + (double)_activeCell.Width);
            int cellBottomPx = targetPane.PointsToScreenPixelsY((double)_activeCell.Top + (double)_activeCell.Height);

            if (cellRightPx <= 0 || cellBottomPx <= 0)
            {
                HideAll();
                return;
            }

            int cellHeightPx = cellBottomPx - cellTopPx;
            int arrowWidthPx = 18;
            int arrowHeightPx = cellHeightPx;
            int arrowXPx = cellRightPx + 1;
            int arrowYPx = cellTopPx;

            if (!_arrowHost!.Visible) _arrowHost.Show();
            SetWindowPos(_arrowHost.Handle, HWND_TOP, arrowXPx, arrowYPx, arrowWidthPx, arrowHeightPx, SWP_SHOWWINDOW | SWP_NOACTIVATE);
            HidePopup();
        }
        catch
        {
            HideAll();
        }
    }

    public static void HideAll()
    {
        HidePopup();
        if (_arrowHost != null && _arrowHost.Visible) _arrowHost.Hide();
        _activeCell = null;
    }

    public static void HidePopup()
    {
        if (_popupHost != null && _popupHost.Visible) _popupHost.Hide();
    }

    public static void DismissAndReturnFocus()
    {
        ReleaseCapture();
        HidePopup();
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            IntPtr excelHwnd = GetExcelHwnd(xlApp);
            if (excelHwnd != IntPtr.Zero) SetForegroundWindow(excelHwnd);
        }
        catch { }
    }

    public static void ToggleDropdown()
    {
        if (_popupHost!.Visible)
        {
            DismissAndReturnFocus();
            return;
        }
        if (_activeCell == null) return;
        PopulateAndOpenMenu(_activeCell);
    }

    private static void PopulateAndOpenMenu(dynamic singleCell)
    {
        var swTotal = Stopwatch.StartNew();
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
            dynamic sheet = singleCell.Worksheet;
            int row = (int)singleCell.Row;
            int col = (int)singleCell.Column;

            string inCellText = string.Empty;
            try
            {
                inCellText = singleCell.Text?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(inCellText))
                    inCellText = singleCell.Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
            }
            catch { }

            dynamic? table = null;
            try { table = singleCell.ListObject; } catch { }

            var rule = RuleRegistry.GetRuleForCell(singleCell);
            var processedItems = new List<DropdownItem>();

            // =========================================================================
            // PATH A: Unified C# Attribute Rule Engine (Zero COM formula evaluations)
            // =========================================================================
            if (rule != null && rule.HasDropdown)
            {
                var evaluatedOptions = RuleEvaluator.GetEvaluatedDropdownOptions(wb, table, row, rule);
                foreach (var opt in evaluatedOptions)
                {
                    processedItems.Add(new DropdownItem
                    {
                        Display = opt.Display,
                        Raw = opt.Raw,
                        Section = opt.Section,
                        IsValid = opt.IsValid
                    });
                }

                _onItemSelected = chosenDisplay =>
                {
                    var targetItem = processedItems.FirstOrDefault(i => i.Display.Equals(chosenDisplay, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
                    if (targetItem == null) return;

                    bool origEvents = true;
                    try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }
                    try
                    {
                        WriteSelectedValueToCell(singleCell, targetItem, rule, inCellText);
                    }
                    finally
                    {
                        try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
                    }

                    // Immediately run self-healing row pass (executes [ColumnSync], [ColumnDefault], [ColumnStateGuard])
                    if (table != null)
                    {
                        RuleEvaluator.EvaluateAndHealRow(sheet, table, row, col);
                    }
                };
            }
            // =========================================================================
            // PATH B: Legacy Fallback (For top sheet parameter cells outside tables)
            // =========================================================================
            else
            {
                var config = Metadata.ReadConfig(singleCell);
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.Source))
                {
                    var items = RuleDataCache.GetSourceItems(wb, config.Source, preserveDuplicates: false);
                    foreach (var itm in items)
                    {
                        string disp = FormatIfDate(itm);
                        processedItems.Add(new DropdownItem { Display = disp, Raw = itm, Section = 0, IsValid = true });
                    }
                }

                _onItemSelected = chosenDisplay =>
                {
                    var targetItem = processedItems.FirstOrDefault(i => i.Display.Equals(chosenDisplay, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
                    if (targetItem == null) return;

                    bool origEvents = true;
                    try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }
                    try
                    {
                        singleCell.Value2 = targetItem.Display;
                    }
                    finally
                    {
                        try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
                    }

                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.SyncLeft) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(targetItem.Raw))
                    {
                        SyncEngine.ExecuteSync(singleCell, config.SyncLeft, targetItem.Raw, targetItem.Display);
                    }
                };
            }

            // =========================================================================
            // RENDER & SORT MENU ITEMS
            // =========================================================================
            _listBox!.Items.Clear();
            if (processedItems.Count > 0)
            {
                var validItems = processedItems.Where(i => i.IsValid);
                var invalidItems = processedItems.Where(i => !i.IsValid);

                var sortedValid = validItems.OrderBy(i => i.Section).ThenBy(i => i.Display);
                var sortedInvalid = invalidItems.OrderBy(i => i.Section).ThenBy(i => i.Display);

                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(inCellText) && (rule == null || !rule.IsMultiSelect))
                {
                    string q = inCellText.Trim();
                    Func<DropdownItem, int> ranker = i =>
                    {
                        if (i.Display.Equals(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return 0;
                        if (i.Display.StartsWith(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return 1;
                        if (i.Display.IndexOf(q, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0) return 2;
                        return 3;
                    };
                    sortedValid = validItems.OrderBy(ranker).ThenBy(i => i.Section).ThenBy(i => i.Display);
                    sortedInvalid = invalidItems.OrderBy(ranker).ThenBy(i => i.Section).ThenBy(i => i.Display);
                }

                foreach (var item in sortedValid) _listBox.Items.Add(item);
                foreach (var item in sortedInvalid) _listBox.Items.Add(item);

                int firstValid = _listBox.Items.Cast<object>().ToList().FindIndex(o => o is DropdownItem d && d.IsValid);
                if (firstValid >= 0) _listBox.SelectedIndex = firstValid;
            }
            else
            {
                _listBox.Items.Add(new DropdownItem { Display = "(No options available)", IsValid = false });
            }

            dynamic window = xlApp.ActiveWindow;
            dynamic targetPane = ResolveTargetPane(window, (int)singleCell.Row, (int)singleCell.Column);

            int cellLeftPx = targetPane.PointsToScreenPixelsX((double)singleCell.Left);
            int cellRightPx = targetPane.PointsToScreenPixelsX((double)singleCell.Left + (double)singleCell.Width);
            int cellBottomPx = targetPane.PointsToScreenPixelsY((double)singleCell.Top + (double)singleCell.Height);

            int popupWidthPx = Math.Max((cellRightPx - cellLeftPx) + 19, 160);
            int calculatedHeightPx = Math.Min(240, Math.Max(38, (_listBox.Items.Count * _listBox.ItemHeight) + 4));

            if (!_popupHost!.Visible) _popupHost.Show();
            SetWindowPos(_popupHost.Handle, HWND_TOP, cellLeftPx, cellBottomPx, popupWidthPx, calculatedHeightPx, SWP_SHOWWINDOW | SWP_NOACTIVATE);

            swTotal.Stop();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[Arrow.PopulateAndOpenMenu Error] {ex.Message}");
        }
    }

    private static void WriteSelectedValueToCell(
        dynamic singleCell,
        DropdownItem targetItem,
        PropertyRuleDescriptor rule,
        string existingCellText)
    {
        if (rule.IsMultiSelect && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(existingCellText))
        {
            var tokens = existingCellText
                .Split(new[] { ',' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)
                .Select(t => t.Trim())
                .Where(t => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(t))
                .ToList();

            int existingIdx = tokens.FindIndex(t => t.Equals(targetItem.Display, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
            if (existingIdx >= 0)
                tokens.RemoveAt(existingIdx);
            else
                tokens.Add(targetItem.Display);

            singleCell.Value2 = string.Join(", ", tokens);
            return;
        }

        if (rule.IsDateColumn && RuleDataCache.TryParseDate(targetItem.Raw, out DateOnly dOnly))
        {
            singleCell.Value2 = dOnly.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue).ToOADate();
            return;
        }

        if (rule.IsNumericColumn &&
            double.TryParse(targetItem.Raw, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double dNum))
        {
            singleCell.Value2 = dNum;
            return;
        }

        singleCell.Value2 = targetItem.Display;
    }

    private static dynamic ResolveTargetPane(dynamic window, int targetRow, int targetCol)
    {
        dynamic? targetPane = null;
        int panesCount = 0;
        try { panesCount = (int)window.Panes.Count; } catch { }

        if (panesCount > 1)
        {
            for (int i = 1; i <= panesCount; i++)
            {
                try
                {
                    dynamic p = window.Panes[i];
                    dynamic vr = p.VisibleRange;
                    int r1 = (int)vr.Row;
                    int r2 = r1 + (int)vr.Rows.Count - 1;
                    int c1 = (int)vr.Column;
                    int c2 = c1 + (int)vr.Columns.Count - 1;
                    if (targetRow >= r1 && targetRow <= r2 && targetCol >= c1 && targetCol <= c2)
                    {
                        targetPane = p;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                catch { }
            }
        }
        return targetPane ?? window.ActivePane;
    }

    private static readonly string[] RecognizedDatePatterns =
    {
        "yyyy-MM-dd", "yyyy/MM/dd", "dd/MM/yyyy", "d/M/yyyy", "dd-MM-yyyy", "d-M-yyyy"
    };

    public static string FormatIfDate(string input)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input)) return input;
        if (DateTime.TryParseExact(input.Trim(), RecognizedDatePatterns, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DateTimeStyles.None, out DateTime exactDt))
        {
            return exactDt.ToString("dd/MM/yyyy");
        }
        if (double.TryParse(input, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double d))
        {
            if (d >= 30000 && d <= 80000)
            {
                return DateTime.FromOADate(d).ToString("dd/MM/yyyy");
            }
        }
        return input;
    }

    private static void CommitSelection()
    {
        if (_listBox!.SelectedItem is DropdownItem item && _onItemSelected != null)
        {
            _onItemSelected(item.Display);
        }
        DismissAndReturnFocus();
    }

    public static void Teardown()
    {
        try
        {
            HideAll();
            _arrowHost?.Dispose();
            _arrowHost = null;
            _popupHost?.Dispose();
            _popupHost = null;
        }
        catch { }
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown;

using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.RuleEngine;

public static class Commands
{
    public const string DropDownStyleName = "DropDown";

    public static void ApplyDropdownStyle()
    {
        dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
        dynamic selection = xlApp.Selection;
        if (selection == null) return;

        EnsureNamedStyleExists(xlApp, DropDownStyleName);
        selection.Style = DropDownStyleName;

        try
        {
            selection.Validation.Delete();
        }
        catch { }
    }

    public static void InspectAndConfigure(dynamic cell)
    {
        try
        {
            using var form = new ConfiguratorForm(cell);
            form.ShowDialog();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show($"Failed opening DropDown configurator: {ex.Message}", "Dropdown Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
        }
    }

    public static bool IsDropdownCell(dynamic cell)
    {
        if (cell == null) return false;

        try
        {
            dynamic singleCell = cell.Cells[1, 1];

            // 1. Primary path: Check if cell belongs to a ListObject table governed by C# RuleRegistry
            dynamic? cellTable = null;
            try { cellTable = singleCell.ListObject; } catch { }

            if (cellTable != null)
            {
                if (!IsInsideTableDataBody(singleCell, cellTable))
                    return false;

                var rule = RuleRegistry.GetRuleForCell(singleCell);
                if (rule != null && rule.HasDropdown)
                {
                    return true;
                }
            }

            // 2. Fallback path: Legacy style + cell config check (for non-mapped tables or top parameter cells)
            string currentStyleName = singleCell.Style?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            if (!string.Equals(currentStyleName, DropDownStyleName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                return false;
            }

            var cfg = Metadata.ReadConfig(singleCell);
            return !cfg.IsEmpty;
        }
        catch
        {
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static bool IsInsideTableDataBody(dynamic singleCell, dynamic cellTable)
    {
        try
        {
            int row = (int)singleCell.Row;
            int headerRow = (int)cellTable.HeaderRowRange.Row;
            if (row <= headerRow) return false;

            dynamic? bodyRange = cellTable.DataBodyRange;
            if (bodyRange == null)
            {
                return row == headerRow + 1;
            }

            int bodyStartRow = (int)bodyRange.Row;
            int bodyEndRow = bodyStartRow + (int)bodyRange.Rows.Count - 1;
            return row >= bodyStartRow && row <= bodyEndRow;
        }
        catch
        {
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static bool IsInsideSameTableDataBody(dynamic cell)
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic? cellTable = null;
            try { cellTable = cell.ListObject; } catch { }
            if (cellTable == null) return false;

            return IsInsideTableDataBody(cell, cellTable);
        }
        catch
        {
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static string ResolveContextListFormula(dynamic targetCell)
    {
        try
        {
            var rule = RuleRegistry.GetRuleForCell(targetCell);
            if (rule != null && rule.Sources.Length > 0)
            {
                return "=" + string.Join(", ", rule.Sources);
            }

            dynamic sheet = targetCell.Worksheet;
            dynamic wb = sheet.Parent;
            int col = (int)targetCell.Column;

            dynamic? listObj = null;
            try { listObj = targetCell.ListObject; } catch { }
            if (listObj != null)
            {
                string tableName = listObj.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                int headerRow = (int)listObj.HeaderRowRange.Row;
                string colHeader = sheet.Cells[headerRow, col].Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(tableName) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(colHeader))
                {
                    return $"={tableName}[{colHeader}]";
                }
            }

            string header = sheet.Cells[1, col].Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(header))
            {
                string cleanHeader = Regex.Replace(header, @"[^\w]", "");
                string candidateListName = cleanHeader.EndsWith("List", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                    ? cleanHeader
                    : $"{cleanHeader}List";

                try
                {
                    dynamic match = wb.Names.Item(candidateListName);
                    if (match != null) return $"={candidateListName}";
                }
                catch { }
            }

            return string.Empty;
        }
        catch { return string.Empty; }
    }

    public static void EnsureNamedStyleExists(dynamic xlApp, string styleName)
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic styles = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook.Styles;
            foreach (dynamic s in styles)
            {
                if (string.Equals(s.Name?.ToString(), styleName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    return;
            }

            dynamic newStyle = styles.Add(styleName);
            newStyle.IncludeNumber = false;
            newStyle.IncludeFont = true;
            newStyle.IncludeAlignment = true;
            newStyle.IncludeBorder = true;
            newStyle.IncludePatterns = true;

            newStyle.Font.Name = "Segoe UI";
            newStyle.Font.Size = 10;
            newStyle.Interior.Color = ColorTranslator.ToOle(Color.FromArgb(240, 244, 250));
        }
        catch { }
    }

    private static Config? _copiedConfig;

    public static void CopySelectedCellMetadata()
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic activeCell = xlApp.ActiveCell;
            if (activeCell == null) return;

            var rule = RuleRegistry.GetRuleForCell(activeCell);
            if (rule != null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    $"Column '{rule.HeaderName}' on table '{rule.TableName}' is managed automatically by C# [ColumnRule] attributes.\n\n" +
                    $"  Sources: {(rule.Sources.Length > 0 ? string.Join(", ", rule.Sources) : "(None / Default Only)")}\n" +
                    $"  Default: {rule.DefaultRule?.Kind.ToString() ?? "None"}\n" +
                    $"  Syncs: {rule.SyncRules.Count}\n" +
                    $"  State Guards: {rule.StateGuards.Count}",
                    "C# Attribute Rule Active",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                    MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                return;
            }

            var cfg = Metadata.ReadConfig(activeCell);
            if (cfg.IsEmpty)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Selected cell has no custom dropdown configuration to copy.",
                                "Copy Dropdown Metadata", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            _copiedConfig = cfg;
            MessageBox.Show($"Copied dropdown configuration from {activeCell.Address}!",
                            "Metadata Copied", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show($"Failed to copy metadata: {ex.Message}", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
        }
    }

    public static void PasteSelectedCellMetadata()
    {
        if (_copiedConfig == null || _copiedConfig.IsEmpty)
        {
            MessageBox.Show("No legacy dropdown configuration has been copied yet.",
                            "Paste Dropdown Metadata", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
            return;
        }

        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic selection = xlApp.Selection;
            if (selection == null) return;

            EnsureNamedStyleExists(xlApp, DropDownStyleName);

            foreach (dynamic cell in selection.Cells)
            {
                cell.Style = DropDownStyleName;
                Metadata.WriteConfig(cell, _copiedConfig);
                try { cell.Validation.Delete(); } catch { }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show($"Failed to paste metadata: {ex.Message}", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
        }
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown;

using System;

using ExcelDna.Integration;

public partial class ConfiguratorForm : Form
{

    private readonly TextBox _txtSource;
    private readonly TextBox _txtTransform;
    private readonly TextBox _txtFilter;
    private readonly TextBox _txtSync;
    private readonly CheckBox _chkMultiSelect;
    private readonly CheckBox _chkIsWarning;
    private readonly Button _btnConfirm;
    private readonly Button _btnCancel;

    public ConfiguratorForm(dynamic singleCell)
    {
        _singleCell = singleCell;
        string cellAddress = _singleCell.Address?.ToString() ?? "Selected Cell";

        Text = $"Cell Rule & Dropdown Configurator - {cellAddress}";
        FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterScreen;
        MaximizeBox = false;
        MinimizeBox = false;
        TopMost = true;
        AutoScaleMode = AutoScaleMode.Dpi;
        Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9.5f, FontStyle.Regular);
        AutoSize = true;
        AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink;
        Padding = new Padding(18);

        var config = Metadata.ReadConfig(_singleCell);
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.Source))
        {
            config.Source = Commands.ResolveContextListFormula(_singleCell);
        }

        var layout = new TableLayoutPanel
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            ColumnCount = 1,
            AutoSize = true,
            AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink
        };

        var lblSource = new Label
        {
            Text = "1. Source List / Default Formula:\n   (e.g. =AllStaffsTable[Staff Name], =IF(@left<>\"\", @left+365, TODAY()+365))",
            AutoSize = true,
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f, FontStyle.Bold),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 4)
        };

        _txtSource = new TextBox
        {
            Text = config.Source,
            Width = 460,
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9.5f),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 10)
        };

        var lblTransform = new Label
        {
            Text = "2. Item Transform Formula (Optional. Use @item as placeholder):\n   (e.g. LET(p, TEXTSPLIT(TRIM(@item), \" \"), INDEX(p,1) & \" \" & INDEX(p,COUNTA(p))))",
            AutoSize = true,
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f, FontStyle.Bold),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 4)
        };

        _txtTransform = new TextBox
        {
            Text = config.Transform,
            Width = 460,
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9.5f),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 10)
        };

        var lblFilter = new Label
        {
            Text = "3. Extra Filter / Condition (Optional. Use @item, @left, @right):\n   (e.g. IF(@left<>\"\", XLOOKUP(@left, AllStaffsTable[Staff ID], AllStaffsTable[Staff Name], \"\") = @item, TRUE))",
            AutoSize = true,
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f, FontStyle.Bold),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 4)
        };

        _txtFilter = new TextBox
        {
            Text = config.Filter,
            Width = 460,
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9.5f),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 10)
        };

        var lblSync = new Label
        {
            Text = "4. Auto-Fill Adjacent Cell Formula (Optional. Resolves when item is picked):\n   (e.g. @right XLOOKUP(@item, AllStaffsTable[Staff ID], AllStaffsTable[Staff Name], \"\"))",
            AutoSize = true,
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f, FontStyle.Bold),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 4)

        _txtSync = new TextBox

            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 10)
        };

        _chkMultiSelect = new CheckBox
        {
            Text = "Allow Multi-Selections (Checklist popup)",
            Checked = config.IsMultiSelect,
            AutoSize = true,
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 6)
        };

        _chkIsWarning = new CheckBox
        {
            Text = "Treat as Warning / Auto-Default (Apply formula if left empty, do not block save)",
            Checked = config.IsWarningOnly,
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f, FontStyle.Bold),
            ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(180, 83, 9),
            AutoSize = true,
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 14)
        };

        var pnlButtons = new FlowLayoutPanel
        {
            FlowDirection = FlowDirection.RightToLeft,
            AutoSize = true,
            AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink,
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            Margin = new Padding(0)
        };

        _btnCancel = new Button
        {
            Text = "Cancel",
            AutoSize = true,
            Padding = new Padding(16, 6, 16, 6),
            DialogResult = DialogResult.Cancel
        };

        _btnConfirm = new Button
        {
            Text = "Confirm / Apply",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f, FontStyle.Bold),
            AutoSize = true,
            Padding = new Padding(18, 6, 18, 6),
            DialogResult = DialogResult.OK,
            BackColor = Color.FromArgb(240, 244, 250)
        };
        _btnConfirm.Click += OnConfirmClicked;
        _btnCancel.Click += (_, _) => Close();

        pnlButtons.Controls.Add(_btnCancel);
        pnlButtons.Controls.Add(_btnConfirm);

        layout.Controls.Add(lblSource);
        layout.Controls.Add(_txtSource);
        layout.Controls.Add(lblTransform);
        layout.Controls.Add(_txtTransform);
        layout.Controls.Add(lblFilter);
        layout.Controls.Add(_txtFilter);
        layout.Controls.Add(lblSync);
        layout.Controls.Add(_txtSync);
        layout.Controls.Add(_chkMultiSelect);
        layout.Controls.Add(_chkIsWarning);
        layout.Controls.Add(pnlButtons);

        Controls.Add(layout);
        AcceptButton = _btnConfirm;
        CancelButton = _btnCancel;
    }

    private void OnConfirmClicked(object? sender, EventArgs e)
    {
        string rawSrc = _txtSource.Text.Trim();
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(rawSrc))
        {
            MessageBox.Show("Source List / Default Formula cannot be empty.", "Validation", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
            return;
        }

        var newConfig = new Config
        {
            Source = rawSrc,
            Transform = _txtTransform.Text.Trim(),
            Filter = _txtFilter.Text.Trim(),
            SyncLeft = _txtSync.Text.Trim(),
            IsMultiSelect = _chkMultiSelect.Checked,
            IsWarningOnly = _chkIsWarning.Checked
        };

        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
                Commands.EnsureNamedStyleExists(xlApp, Commands.DropDownStyleName);
                _singleCell.Style = Commands.DropDownStyleName;
                Metadata.WriteConfig(_singleCell, newConfig);
                try { _singleCell.Validation.Delete(); } catch { }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"Excel rejected validation setup: {ex.Message}", "Config Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
        });

        Close();
    }
}

FILE: Core\03_RuntimeUI\DropDown\DropDown.Metadata.cs
