namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown;

using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

public record Config
{
    public string Source { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string Transform { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string Filter { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string SyncLeft { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public bool IsMultiSelect { get; set; } = false;
    public bool IsWarningOnly { get; set; } = false;

    public bool IsEmpty =>
        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Source) &&
        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Transform) &&
        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Filter) &&
        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(SyncLeft);
}

public static class Metadata
{
    private static readonly HashSet<string> ModernFunctions = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
    {
        "XLOOKUP", "XMATCH", "VSTACK", "HSTACK", "FILTER", "UNIQUE",
        "SORT", "SORTBY", "LET", "LAMBDA", "TEXTSPLIT", "CHOOSEROWS", "CHOOSECOLS"
    };

    public static string GetCellConfigKey(dynamic singleCell)
    {
        try
        {
            string sheetName = singleCell.Worksheet.Name?.ToString() ?? "Sheet";
            string cleanSheet = Regex.Replace(sheetName, @"[^\w]", "_");
            return $"CFG_{cleanSheet}_R{singleCell.Row}C{singleCell.Column}";
        }
        catch { return string.Empty; }
    }

    public static void PurgeConfigIfPresent(dynamic singleCell)
    {
        try
        {
            string key = GetCellConfigKey(singleCell);
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(key)) return;
            dynamic wb = singleCell.Worksheet.Parent;
            try { wb.Names.Item(key).Delete(); } catch { }
        }
        catch { }
    }

    public static void WriteConfig(dynamic singleCell, Config config)
    {
        try
        {
            string key = GetCellConfigKey(singleCell);
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(key)) return;
            dynamic wb = singleCell.Worksheet.Parent;
            try { wb.Names.Item(key).Delete(); } catch { }

            var parts = new List<string> { config.Source };
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.Transform))
                parts.Add($"TRANSFORM: {config.Transform}");
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.Filter))
                parts.Add($"FILTER: {config.Filter}");
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(config.SyncLeft))
                parts.Add($"SYNC_LEFT: {config.SyncLeft}");
            parts.Add($"MULTI: {config.IsMultiSelect}");
            parts.Add($"WARN: {config.IsWarningOnly}");

            string composite = string.Join(" | ", parts);
            string safeConfig = composite.Replace("\"", "\"\"");
            wb.Names.Add(Name: key, RefersTo: $"=\"{safeConfig}\"", Visible: false);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[Metadata.WriteConfig Error] {ex.Message}");
        }
    }

    public static Config ReadConfig(dynamic singleCell)
    {
        string styleName = singleCell.Style?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
        if (!string.Equals(styleName, Commands.DropDownStyleName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            return new Config();
        }

        var config = ReadConfigInternal(singleCell);
        if (!config.IsEmpty) return config;

        try
        {
            int row = (int)singleCell.Row;
            int col = (int)singleCell.Column;
            dynamic sheet = singleCell.Worksheet;

            if (row > 2 && Commands.IsInsideSameTableDataBody(singleCell))
            {
                dynamic archetypeCell = sheet.Cells[2, col];
                string archStyle = archetypeCell.Style?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                if (string.Equals(archStyle, Commands.DropDownStyleName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    var inherited = ReadConfigInternal(archetypeCell);
                    if (!inherited.IsEmpty) return inherited;
                }
            }
        }
        catch { }

        return config;
    }

    private static Config ReadConfigInternal(dynamic singleCell)
    {
        var config = new Config();
        string raw = string.Empty;
        try
        {
            string key = GetCellConfigKey(singleCell);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(key))
            {
                dynamic wb = singleCell.Worksheet.Parent;
                dynamic nameObj = null;
                try { nameObj = wb.Names.Item(key); } catch { }
                if (nameObj != null)
                {
                    string refersTo = nameObj.RefersTo?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                    if (refersTo.StartsWith("=\"") && refersTo.EndsWith("\""))
                    {
                        raw = refersTo.Substring(2, refersTo.Length - 3).Replace("\"\"", "\"");
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        raw = refersTo.TrimStart('=');
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        catch { }

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(raw)) return config;

        var parts = raw.Split(new[] { " | " }, StringSplitOptions.None);
        config.Source = parts[0].Trim();

        foreach (var p in parts)
        {
            if (p.StartsWith("TRANSFORM:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                config.Transform = p.Substring("TRANSFORM:".Length).Trim();
            else if (p.StartsWith("FILTER:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                config.Filter = p.Substring("FILTER:".Length).Trim();
            else if (p.StartsWith("SYNC_LEFT:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                config.SyncLeft = p.Substring("SYNC_LEFT:".Length).Trim();
            else if (p.StartsWith("MULTI:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                if (bool.TryParse(p.Substring("MULTI:".Length).Trim(), out bool m))
                    config.IsMultiSelect = m;
            }
            else if (p.StartsWith("WARN:", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                if (bool.TryParse(p.Substring("WARN:".Length).Trim(), out bool w))
                    config.IsWarningOnly = w;
            }
        }

        return config;
    }

    public static object? EvaluateFormulaSafely(dynamic xlApp, string formula)
    {
        try
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(formula)) return null;
            string clean = formula.Trim();
            if (clean.StartsWith("=", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                clean = clean.Substring(1).Trim();

            clean = ResolveTableReferences(xlApp, clean);

            // Detect if expression relies on modern Excel 365 calculation engine
            bool requiresModernEngine = ContainsModernFunction(clean);

            // Fast-path: xlApp.Evaluate only for simple/legacy formulas under 250 characters
            if (!requiresModernEngine && clean.Length <= 250)
            {
                object? res = xlApp.Evaluate(clean);
                if (res != null && !(res is int iErr && iErr < -2000000000))
                {
                    return res;
                }
            }

            // Reliable Worksheet Evaluation for modern 365 formulas (XLOOKUP, VSTACK, cross-sheet array joins)
            dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
            dynamic dataWs = null;
            try { dataWs = wb.Sheets[SheetOperations.DataSheetName]; } catch { }
            if (dataWs == null && wb.Sheets.Count > 0) dataWs = wb.Sheets[1];

            if (dataWs != null)
            {
                dynamic scratchCell = dataWs.Cells[1, 101]; // CW1
                try
                {
                    scratchCell.Formula2 = "=" + clean;
                    object? val = scratchCell.Value2;
                    // CRITICAL FIX: Extract full dynamic array if the formula spills
                    try { val = scratchCell.SpillingToRange.Value2 ?? val; } catch { }
                    return val;
                }
                catch
                {
                    try
                    {
                        scratchCell.Formula = "=" + clean;
                        object? val = scratchCell.Value2;
                        try { val = scratchCell.SpillingToRange.Value2 ?? val; } catch { }
                        return val;
                    }
                    catch { return null; }
                }
                finally
                {
                    try { scratchCell.Value2 = null; } catch { }
                }
            }

            return xlApp.Evaluate(clean);
        }
        catch
        {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private static bool ContainsModernFunction(string formula)
    {
        foreach (var func in ModernFunctions)
        {
            if (formula.IndexOf(func, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    public static string ResolveTableReferences(dynamic xlApp, string formula)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(formula) || !formula.Contains("["))
            return formula;

        var regex = new Regex(@"\b([A-Za-z0-9_]+)\[([^\]@\r\n]+)\]");
        return regex.Replace(formula, match =>
        {
            string tableName = match.Groups[1].Value;
            string colName = match.Groups[2].Value.Trim('[', ']');

            try
            {
                dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
                if (wb == null) return match.Value;

                foreach (dynamic ws in wb.Sheets)
                {
                    dynamic tbl = null;
                    try { tbl = ws.ListObjects[tableName]; } catch { }
                    if (tbl == null)
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            foreach (dynamic t in ws.ListObjects)
                            {
                                if (string.Equals(t.Name?.ToString(), tableName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                {
                                    tbl = t;
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        catch { }
                    }

                    if (tbl != null)
                    {
                        dynamic colObj = null;
                        try { colObj = tbl.ListColumns[colName]; } catch { }
                        if (colObj == null)
                        {
                            try
                            {
                                foreach (dynamic c in tbl.ListColumns)
                                {
                                    if (string.Equals(c.Name?.ToString(), colName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                    {
                                        colObj = c;
                                        break;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            catch { }
                        }

                        if (colObj != null)
                        {
                            dynamic colRange = colObj.DataBodyRange ?? colObj.Range;
                            string sName = ws.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                            string cleanSheet = sName.Replace("'", "''");

                            string addr = string.Empty;
                            try { addr = colRange.Address?.ToString() ?? string.Empty; } catch { }
                            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(addr))
                            {
                                return $"'{cleanSheet}'!{addr}";
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            catch { }

            return match.Value;
        });
    }
}

FILE: Core\03_RuntimeUI\DropDown\DropDown.SyncEngine.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown;

using System;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;

public static class SyncEngine
{
    public static void ExecuteSync(dynamic singleCell, string syncFormula, string rawVal, string displayVal)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(syncFormula)) return;
        string cellVal = !string.IsNullOrEmpty(rawVal) ? rawVal : (singleCell.Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty);
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(cellVal)) return;

        dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
        dynamic sheet = singleCell.Worksheet;
        int row = (int)singleCell.Row;
        int col = (int)singleCell.Column;

        var statements = syncFormula.Split(new[] { ';' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
        bool origEvents = true;
        try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }

        var debugAudit = new StringBuilder();
        debugAudit.AppendLine("==========================================================================");
        debugAudit.AppendLine(" AUTO-FILL SYNC ENGINE AUDIT");
        debugAudit.AppendLine("==========================================================================");
        debugAudit.AppendLine($"Trigger Cell : {singleCell.Address} (Row {row}, Col {col}) on '{sheet.Name}'");
        debugAudit.AppendLine($"Input Value  : '{cellVal}' (Display: '{displayVal}')");
        debugAudit.AppendLine();

        int executedCount = 0;

        try
        {
            foreach (var rawStmt in statements)
            {
                string stmt = rawStmt.Trim();
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(stmt)) continue;

                int targetCol = col - 1;
                string targetColLabel = "Left (Col - 1)";

                if (stmt.StartsWith("@target[", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    int closeBracket = stmt.IndexOf(']');
                    if (closeBracket > 8)
                    {
                        string targetHeader = stmt.Substring(8, closeBracket - 8).Trim();
                        stmt = stmt.Substring(closeBracket + 1).Trim();
                        try
                        {
                            dynamic listObj = singleCell.ListObject;
                            if (listObj != null)
                            {
                                targetCol = (int)listObj.ListColumns[targetHeader].Range.Column;
                                targetColLabel = $"Target [{targetHeader}] -> Col {targetCol}";
                            }
                        }
                        catch { }
                    }
                }
                else if (stmt.Contains("@to_right", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) || stmt.Contains("@right", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    targetCol = col + 1;
                    targetColLabel = $"Right (Col + 1) -> Col {targetCol}";
                    stmt = stmt.Replace("@to_right", "", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                               .Replace("@right", "", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                }
                else if (stmt.Contains("@to_left", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) || stmt.Contains("@left", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    targetCol = col - 1;
                    targetColLabel = $"Left (Col - 1)";
                    stmt = stmt.Replace("@to_left", "", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                               .Replace("@left", "", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                }

                bool isNum = double.TryParse(cellVal, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out _) && cellVal.Length < 10;
                string rawToken = isNum ? cellVal : $"\"{cellVal.Replace("\"", "\"\"")}\"";
                string displayToken = $"\"{displayVal.Replace("\"", "\"\"")}\"";

                string preparedStmt = stmt.Replace("@item", rawToken)
                                          .Replace("@display", displayToken)
                                          .Trim().TrimStart('=');

                // CRITICAL FIX: Resolve cross-sheet table references before evaluating
                preparedStmt = Metadata.ResolveTableReferences(xlApp, preparedStmt);

                debugAudit.AppendLine($"--- Statement {++executedCount} ---");
                debugAudit.AppendLine($"Direction / Target : {targetColLabel}");
                debugAudit.AppendLine($"Formula Evaluated  : {preparedStmt}");
                object? resolvedVal = Metadata.EvaluateFormulaSafely(xlApp, preparedStmt);

                // Type-tolerant retry: If unquoted lookup against text range returned empty, try quoted
                if ((resolvedVal == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(resolvedVal.ToString())) && isNum)
                {
                    string quotedStmt = stmt.Replace("@item", $"\"{cellVal.Replace("\"", "\"\"")}\"")
                                            .Replace("@display", displayToken)
                                            .Trim().TrimStart('=');

                    quotedStmt = Metadata.ResolveTableReferences(xlApp, quotedStmt);
                    resolvedVal = Metadata.EvaluateFormulaSafely(xlApp, quotedStmt);
                }

                if (resolvedVal == null)
                {
                    debugAudit.AppendLine("Result             : <NULL>");
                    continue;
                }

                string resStr = resolvedVal.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                bool isComError = (resolvedVal is int iErr && iErr < -2000000000) ||
                                  (resolvedVal is long lErr && lErr < -2000000000) ||
                                  resStr.StartsWith("#") ||
                                  (long.TryParse(resStr, out long num) && num < -2000000000);

                debugAudit.AppendLine($"Result Type        : {resolvedVal.GetType().Name}");
                debugAudit.AppendLine($"Result Value       : '{resStr}'");
                debugAudit.AppendLine($"Is COM Error?      : {isComError}");

                if (!isComError && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(resStr))
                {
                    try
                    {
                        sheet.Cells[row, targetCol].Value2 = resolvedVal;
                        debugAudit.AppendLine($"Writeback Status   : SUCCESS -> Wrote '{resStr}' to Cell R{row}C{targetCol}");
                    }
                    catch (Exception exWrite)
                    {
                        debugAudit.AppendLine($"Writeback Status   : FAILED -> {exWrite.Message}");
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    debugAudit.AppendLine("Writeback Status   : SKIPPED (Result was empty or evaluated to an Excel Error)");
                }
                debugAudit.AppendLine();
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            debugAudit.AppendLine($"Global Execution Exception: {ex.Message}");
        }
        finally
        {
            try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
#if DEBUG
            if (DebugDialog.DebugEnabled && !DebugDialog.SuppressForSession && executedCount > 0)
            {
                DebugDialog.ShowLog("Auto-Fill Sync Audit", debugAudit.ToString());
            }
#endif
        }
    }
}
`n

FILE: Core\03_RuntimeUI\RuleEngine\RuleEngine.DataCache.cs

namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.RuleEngine;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.SheetMapper;

public static class RuleDataCache
{
    private static readonly string[] RecognizedDatePatterns =
{
    "yyyy-MM-dd", "yyyy/MM/dd", "dd/MM/yyyy", "d/M/yyyy", "dd-MM-yyyy", "d-M-yyyy", "MM/dd/yyyy", "M/d/yyyy"
};

    /// <summary>
    /// Extracts clean string values from a source expression (either "TableName[ColName]" or a Defined Name like "VehicleTypeList").
    /// Zero xlApp.Evaluate COM calls.
    /// </summary>
    public static List<string> GetSourceItems(dynamic wb, string sourceExpr, bool preserveDuplicates = false)
    {
        var results = new List<string>();
        if (wb == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sourceExpr))
            return results;

        string clean = sourceExpr.Trim().TrimStart('=');

        // 1. COMPOUND / CONCAT EXPRESSION: e.g. "AllVehiclesTable[Brand] + ' ' + AllVehiclesTable[Model]"
        if (clean.Contains("[") && (clean.Contains("+") || clean.Contains("&")))
        {
            var compoundList = EvaluateCompoundExpression(wb, clean);
            return FilterList(compoundList, preserveDuplicates);
        }

        // 2. SINGLE Table[Column] Reference
        if (!clean.Contains("(") && clean.EndsWith("]") && clean.IndexOf('[') > 0)
        {
            int openBracket = clean.IndexOf('[');
            int closeBracket = clean.LastIndexOf(']');
            if (openBracket > 0 && closeBracket > openBracket)
            {
                string tableName = clean.Substring(0, openBracket).Trim();
                string colName = clean.Substring(openBracket + 1, closeBracket - openBracket - 1).Trim('[', ']', ' ');
                var colValues = ReadTableColumnValues(wb, tableName, colName);
                return FilterList(colValues, preserveDuplicates);
            }
        }

        // 3. DIRECT DEFINED NAME LOOKUP (Zero Evaluate / Zero Scratch-Cell Mutation!)
        if (!clean.Contains("(") && !clean.Contains("["))
        {
            try
            {
                dynamic? nameObj = null;
                try { nameObj = wb.Names.Item(clean); } catch { }
                if (nameObj != null)
                {
                    dynamic? targetRange = null;
                    try { targetRange = nameObj.RefersToRange; } catch { }
                    if (targetRange != null)
                    {
                        object? rawVal = targetRange.Value2;
                        var rawList = ExtractRawArrayToList(rawVal);
                        var filtered = FilterList(rawList, preserveDuplicates);
                        if (filtered.Count > 0) return filtered;
                    }
                }
            }
            catch { }
        }

        // 4. Try as C# Enum Name directly in memory (Fastest, zero COM)
        if (clean.EndsWith("List", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            string enumCandidate = clean.Substring(0, clean.Length - 4);
            var enumVals = TryGetEnumNames(enumCandidate);
            if (enumVals.Count > 0) return enumVals;
        }

        return results;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Performs an in-memory XLOOKUP between LookupSource and ResultSource with type-tolerant matching.
    /// </summary>
    public static string? LookupValue(dynamic wb, string searchKey, string lookupSource, string resultSource)
    {
        if (wb == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(searchKey)) return null;

        var keys = GetSourceItems(wb, lookupSource, preserveDuplicates: true);
        var vals = GetSourceItems(wb, resultSource, preserveDuplicates: true);

        int count = Math.Min(keys.Count, vals.Count);
        if (count == 0) return null;

        string cleanSearch = searchKey.Trim();

        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
        {
            if (ValuesMatch(keys[i], cleanSearch))
            {
                return vals[i];
            }
        }

        return null;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Reads all raw values in a specific table column directly from its ListObject DataBodyRange.
    /// </summary>
    public static List<string> ReadTableColumnValues(dynamic wb, string tableName, string colHeader)
    {
        var list = new List<string>();
        try
        {
            dynamic? targetTable = FindTableInWorkbook(wb, tableName);
            if (targetTable == null) return list;

            dynamic? bodyRange = targetTable.DataBodyRange;
            if (bodyRange == null) return list;

            int colIndex = FindRelativeColumnIndex(targetTable, colHeader);
            if (colIndex < 1) return list;

            dynamic colRange = targetTable.ListColumns[colIndex].DataBodyRange;
            if (colRange == null) return list;

            object? rawVal = colRange.Value2;
            return ExtractRawArrayToList(rawVal);
        }
        catch
        {
            return list;
        }
    }

    public static dynamic? FindTableInWorkbook(dynamic wb, string tableName)
    {
        if (wb == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(tableName)) return null;
        string normTarget = RuleRegistry.NormalizeIdentifier(tableName);

        foreach (dynamic ws in wb.Sheets)
        {
            try
            {
                dynamic direct = ws.ListObjects[tableName];
                if (direct != null) return direct;
            }
            catch { }

            try
            {
                foreach (dynamic tbl in ws.ListObjects)
                {
                    string tName = tbl.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                    if (RuleRegistry.NormalizeIdentifier(tName).Equals(normTarget, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        return tbl;
                }
            }
            catch { }
        }
        return null;
    }

    public static int FindRelativeColumnIndex(dynamic table, string targetHeader)
    {
        if (table == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(targetHeader)) return -1;
        string normTarget = RuleRegistry.NormalizeIdentifier(targetHeader);

        try
        {
            int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
            for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
            {
                string cName = table.ListColumns[c].Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                string normCol = RuleRegistry.NormalizeIdentifier(cName);
                if (normCol.Equals(normTarget, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                    normCol.TrimEnd('s').Equals(normTarget.TrimEnd('s'), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    return c;
                }
            }
        }
        catch { }

        return -1;
    }

    public static bool ValuesMatch(string? a, string? b)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(a) && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(b)) return true;
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(a) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(b)) return false;

        string sa = a.Trim();
        string sb = b.Trim();

        if (string.Equals(sa, sb, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return true;

        // Enum underscore/space tolerance ("Regular Service" == "Regular_Service")
        if (string.Equals(sa.Replace(" ", "_"), sb.Replace(" ", "_"), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return true;

        // Numeric tolerance ("100" == "100.0")
        if (double.TryParse(sa, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double da) &&
            double.TryParse(sb, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double db))
        {
            if (Math.Abs(da - db) < 0.0001) return true;
        }

        // Date tolerance
        if (TryParseDate(sa, out DateOnly dta) && TryParseDate(sb, out DateOnly dtb))
        {
            return dta == dtb;
        }

        return false;
    }

    public static bool TryParseDate(object? rawVal, out DateOnly result)
    {
        result = default;
        if (rawVal == null || SheetMapperHelper.IsComError(rawVal)) return false;

        if (rawVal is DateOnly dOnly) { result = dOnly; return true; }
        if (rawVal is DateTime dt) { result = DateOnly.FromDateTime(dt); return true; }
        if (rawVal is double oa && oa >= 15000 && oa <= 80000)
        {
            result = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.FromOADate(oa));
            return true;
        }

        string s = rawVal.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s) || s == "—") return false;

        if (double.TryParse(s, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double parsedOa) &&
            parsedOa >= 15000 && parsedOa <= 80000)
        {
            result = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.FromOADate(parsedOa));
            return true;
        }

        if (DateTime.TryParseExact(s, RecognizedDatePatterns, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DateTimeStyles.None, out DateTime exactDt))
        {
            result = DateOnly.FromDateTime(exactDt);
            return true;
        }

        if (DateTime.TryParse(s, new CultureInfo("en-GB"), DateTimeStyles.None, out DateTime gbDt))
        {
            result = DateOnly.FromDateTime(gbDt);
            return true;
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static List<string> ExtractRawArrayToList(object? raw)
    {
        var list = new List<string>();
        if (raw == null) return list;

        if (raw is object[,] matrix)
        {
            int rStart = matrix.GetLowerBound(0);
            int rEnd = matrix.GetUpperBound(0);
            int cStart = matrix.GetLowerBound(1);
            int cEnd = matrix.GetUpperBound(1);

            for (int r = rStart; r <= rEnd; r++)
            {
                for (int c = cStart; c <= cEnd; c++)
                {
                    list.Add(NormalizeCellToString(matrix[r, c]));
                }
            }
        }
        else if (raw is object[] vec)
        {
            foreach (var item in vec)
                list.Add(NormalizeCellToString(item));
        }
        else
        {
            list.Add(NormalizeCellToString(raw));
        }

        return list;
    }

    private static string NormalizeCellToString(object? val)
    {
        if (val == null || SheetMapperHelper.IsComError(val)) return string.Empty;
        if (val is double d)
        {
            if (Math.Abs(d - Math.Round(d)) < 0.00001 && d < 1e15)
                return ((long)Math.Round(d)).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            return d.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        }
        string s = val.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
        if (s.StartsWith("#") || s.Equals("System.__ComObject", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return string.Empty;
        return s;
    }

    private static List<string> FilterList(List<string> raw, bool preserveDuplicates)
    {
        if (preserveDuplicates)
            return raw;

        return raw
            .Where(s => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s) && !s.StartsWith("[") && s != "0" && s != "-" && s != "\u2014")
            .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            .ToList();
    }

    private static List<string> TryGetEnumNames(string enumName)
    {
        var coreAsm = typeof(RuleDataCache).Assembly;
        var enumType = coreAsm.GetTypes().FirstOrDefault(t =>
            t.IsEnum &&
            (t.Name.Equals(enumName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
             t.Name.TrimEnd('s').Equals(enumName.TrimEnd('s'), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)));

        if (enumType == null) return new List<string>();

        return Enum.GetNames(enumType)
            .Where(n => !n.Equals("None", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            .ToList();
    }

    private static List<string> EvaluateCompoundExpression(dynamic wb, string expr)
    {
        // Split on '+' or '&'
        var parts = expr.Split(new[] { '+', '&' }, StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries);
        var tokenColumns = new List<(bool IsColumn, string Table, string Col, string Literal)>();
        int maxRows = 0;

        foreach (var part in parts)
        {
            string token = part.Trim();
            if (token.StartsWith("[") || token.Contains("["))
            {
                int ob = token.IndexOf('[');
                int cb = token.LastIndexOf(']');
                string tName = ob > 0 ? token.Substring(0, ob).Trim() : string.Empty;
                string cName = token.Substring(ob + 1, cb - ob - 1).Trim('[', ']', ' ');

                var vals = ReadTableColumnValues(wb, tName, cName);
                if (vals.Count > maxRows) maxRows = vals.Count;
                tokenColumns.Add((true, tName, cName, string.Empty));
            }
            else
            {
                // Literal string token like ' ' or "-"
                string literal = token.Trim('\'', '\"');
                tokenColumns.Add((false, string.Empty, string.Empty, literal));
            }
        }

        if (maxRows == 0) return new List<string>();

        // Cache each distinct column's rows in memory
        var columnData = new Dictionary<string, List<string>>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        foreach (var (isCol, tbl, col, _) in tokenColumns)
        {
            if (isCol)
            {
                string key = $"{tbl}[{col}]";
                if (!columnData.ContainsKey(key))
                {
                    columnData[key] = ReadTableColumnValues(wb, tbl, col);
                }
            }
        }

        var combined = new List<string>(maxRows);
        for (int r = 0; r < maxRows; r++)
        {
            var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            foreach (var (isCol, tbl, col, literal) in tokenColumns)
            {
                if (isCol)
                {
                    var list = columnData[$"{tbl}[{col}]"];
                    if (r < list.Count && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(list[r]))
                    {
                        sb.Append(list[r]);
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    sb.Append(literal);
                }
            }

            string resultStr = sb.ToString().Trim();
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(resultStr))
            {
                combined.Add(resultStr);
            }
        }

        return combined;
    }
}
`n
============================================================
FILE: Core\03_RuntimeUI\RuleEngine\RuleEngine.Evaluator.cs

namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.RuleEngine;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types.Attributes;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.SheetMapper;

public sealed class DropdownOption
{
    public string Display { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string Raw { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public int Section { get; init; } = 0;
    public bool IsValid { get; init; } = true;
}

public static class RuleEvaluator
{
    private static bool _isEvaluating = false;

    /// <summary>
    /// Live self-healing row evaluation: runs on SheetChange and Dropdown selection.
    /// Executes:
    /// 1) Transform on edited cell (e.g. UpperCase plate/VIN, ShortName formatting)
    /// 2) Two-way [ColumnSync] lookups into partner columns
    /// 3) [ColumnDefault] auto-defaults on blank cells (once the row has user input)
    /// 4) [ColumnStateGuard] state enforcement (e.g. forcing "Expired" if date < Today, or reverting "Expired" to "Active" if dates are valid)
    /// </summary>
    /// <summary>
    /// Live self-healing row evaluation: runs on SheetChange and Dropdown selection.
    /// </summary>
    public static List<string> EvaluateAndHealRow(
        dynamic worksheet,
        dynamic table,
        int excelRow,
        int? editedAbsoluteCol = null,
        bool isAlreadyUnprotected = false) // <-- Added parameter to prevent redundant unprotecting
    {
        var notices = new List<string>();
        if (_isEvaluating || worksheet == null || table == null) return notices;

        try
        {
            _isEvaluating = true;
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic wb = worksheet.Parent;

            bool origEvents = true;
            try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }

            try
            {
                string tableName = table.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                int headerRow = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row;
                int startCol = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Column;
                int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;

                if (excelRow <= headerRow) return notices;

                // Ignore anything outside the DataBodyRange (e.g. the Total Row)
                dynamic? bodyRange = table.DataBodyRange;
                if (bodyRange == null) return notices;
                int bodyFirstRow = (int)bodyRange.Row;
                int bodyLastRow = bodyFirstRow + (int)bodyRange.Rows.Count - 1;
                if (excelRow < bodyFirstRow || excelRow > bodyLastRow) return notices;

                var colHeaders = new string[colCount + 1];
                var colRules = new PropertyRuleDescriptor?[colCount + 1];
                var rowValues = new string[colCount + 1];
                var rawObjects = new object?[colCount + 1];

                for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                {
                    int absCol = startCol + c - 1;
                    string h = worksheet.Cells[headerRow, absCol].Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                    colHeaders[c] = h;
                    colRules[c] = RuleRegistry.GetRule(tableName, h);

                    object? valObj = worksheet.Cells[excelRow, absCol].Value2;
                    rawObjects[c] = valObj;
                    rowValues[c] = CleanCellText(valObj);
                }

                bool rowHasUserInput = false;
                for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                {
                    int absCol = startCol + c - 1;
                    string f = worksheet.Cells[excelRow, absCol].Formula?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                    if (f.StartsWith("=")) continue;

                    string clean = rowValues[c];
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(clean)) continue;
                    if (clean == "0" || clean == "0.00" || clean == "-" || clean == "\u2014" ||
                        clean.Equals("None", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        continue;

                    var rule = colRules[c];
                    if (rule?.DefaultRule?.Kind == DefaultKind.Literal &&
                        string.Equals(clean, rule.DefaultRule.LiteralValue, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        continue;

                    rowHasUserInput = true;
                    break;
                }

                if (!rowHasUserInput) return notices;

                // Extract the heal logic into an Action
                Action healAction = new Action(() =>
                {
                    // STEP 1: Apply Transform & Explicit Sync from the Edited Column
                    if (editedAbsoluteCol.HasValue)
                    {
                        int relEdited = editedAbsoluteCol.Value - startCol + 1;
                        if (relEdited >= 1 && relEdited <= colCount)
                        {
                            var editedRule = colRules[relEdited];
                            string currentText = rowValues[relEdited];
                            if (editedRule != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(currentText))
                            {
                                string transformed = ApplyTextTransform(currentText, editedRule.Transform);
                                if (!string.Equals(transformed, currentText, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                                {
                                    WriteCellSafe(worksheet.Cells[excelRow, editedAbsoluteCol.Value], transformed, editedRule);
                                    rowValues[relEdited] = transformed;
                                    currentText = transformed;
                                }
                                if (editedRule.SyncRules.Count > 0)
                                {
                                    ExecuteSyncRulesForColumn(wb, worksheet, table, excelRow, startCol, colCount, colHeaders, colRules, rowValues, rawObjects, editedRule, currentText);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // STEP 2: Fill Missing Partner Syncs Across the Row
                    for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                    {
                        var rule = colRules[c];
                        string val = rowValues[c];
                        if (rule == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(val) || rule.SyncRules.Count == 0) continue;

                        foreach (var sync in rule.SyncRules)
                        {
                            int targetRelCol = FindColumnByHeader(colHeaders, sync.TargetHeader);
                            if (targetRelCol < 1) continue;

                            var targetRule = colRules[targetRelCol];
                            string currentTargetVal = rowValues[targetRelCol];
                            string? lookedUp = RuleDataCache.LookupValue(wb, val, sync.LookupSource, sync.ResultSource);

                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(lookedUp))
                            {
                                bool isMismatch = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(currentTargetVal) && !RuleDataCache.ValuesMatch(currentTargetVal, lookedUp);
                                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(currentTargetVal) || (isMismatch && (targetRule?.IsWarningOnly ?? false)))
                                {
                                    int targetAbsCol = startCol + targetRelCol - 1;
                                    WriteCellSafe(worksheet.Cells[excelRow, targetAbsCol], lookedUp, targetRule);
                                    rowValues[targetRelCol] = lookedUp;
                                    rawObjects[targetRelCol] = lookedUp;
                                    if (isMismatch) notices.Add($"Row {excelRow} [{colHeaders[targetRelCol]}]: Auto-corrected '{currentTargetVal}' to '{lookedUp}' based on {colHeaders[c]} '{val}'.");
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // STEP 3: Apply [ColumnDefault]
                    for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                    {
                        int absCol = startCol + c - 1;
                        var rule = colRules[c];
                        if (rule?.DefaultRule == null || rule.DefaultRule.Kind == DefaultKind.None) continue;
                        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rowValues[c])) continue;

                        string cellFormula = worksheet.Cells[excelRow, absCol].Formula?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                        if (cellFormula.StartsWith("=")) continue;

                        object? defaultVal = ComputeDefaultValue(wb, table, c, colHeaders, rawObjects, rowValues, rule.DefaultRule);
                        if (defaultVal != null)
                        {
                            WriteCellSafe(worksheet.Cells[excelRow, absCol], defaultVal, rule);
                            rawObjects[c] = defaultVal;
                            rowValues[c] = FormatDisplayValue(defaultVal, rule);
                            notices.Add($"Row {excelRow} [{colHeaders[c]}]: Defaulted to '{rowValues[c]}'.");
                        }
                    }

                    // STEP 4: Enforce [ColumnStateGuard]
                    for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                    {
                        int absCol = startCol + c - 1;
                        var rule = colRules[c];
                        if (rule == null || rule.StateGuards.Count == 0) continue;

                        string currentVal = rowValues[c];
                        string? enforcedValue = EvaluateStateGuardsForCell(colHeaders, rawObjects, rowValues, rule, currentVal, out string? guardReason);
                        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(enforcedValue) && !RuleDataCache.ValuesMatch(currentVal, enforcedValue))
                        {
                            WriteCellSafe(worksheet.Cells[excelRow, absCol], enforcedValue, rule);
                            rowValues[c] = enforcedValue;
                            rawObjects[c] = enforcedValue;
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(guardReason))
                            {
                                SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation(guardReason);
                                notices.Add($"Row {excelRow} [{colHeaders[c]}]: {guardReason}");
                            }
                        }
                    }
                });

                // EXECUTION: Use existing bypass if batching, otherwise unprotect single row
                if (isAlreadyUnprotected)
                {
                    healAction();
                }
                else
                {
                    SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)worksheet, healAction);
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[RuleEvaluator.EvaluateAndHealRow Error] {ex.Message}");
        }
        finally
        {
            _isEvaluating = false;
        }

        return notices;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Bulk in-memory table healing: reads headers, formulas, and body values in 3 COM calls total,
    /// evaluates all rules in RAM with O(1) dictionary lookups, and writes back ONLY modified cells.
    /// </summary>
    public static List<string> EvaluateAndHealTable(dynamic worksheet, dynamic table)
    {
        var allNotices = new List<string>();
        if (_isEvaluating || worksheet == null || table == null) return allNotices;

        try
        {
            _isEvaluating = true;
            dynamic? body = table.DataBodyRange;
            if (body == null) return allNotices;

            int startRow = (int)body.Row;
            int rowCount = (int)body.Rows.Count;
            int headerRow = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row;
            int startCol = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Column;
            int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
            if (rowCount <= 0 || colCount <= 0) return allNotices;

            string tableName = table.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            dynamic wb = worksheet.Parent;

            // 1. Bulk-read headers (1 COM call)
            object[,] headerGrid = TableMapper.GetRange2DValues(table.HeaderRowRange, 1, colCount);
            var colHeaders = new string[colCount + 1];
            var colRules = new PropertyRuleDescriptor?[colCount + 1];

            for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
            {
                string h = headerGrid[1, c]?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                colHeaders[c] = h;
                colRules[c] = RuleRegistry.GetRule(tableName, h);
            }

            // 2. Bulk-read archetype formulas on first data row to identify formula columns (1 COM call)
            var isFormulaCol = new bool[colCount + 1];
            try
            {
                dynamic archRange = worksheet.Range[
                    worksheet.Cells[startRow, startCol],
                    worksheet.Cells[startRow, startCol + colCount - 1]
                ];
                object? rawFormulas = archRange.Formula;
                if (rawFormulas is object[,] fMat)
                {
                    for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                    {
                        string f = fMat[1, c]?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                        isFormulaCol[c] = f.StartsWith("=");
                    }
                }
                else if (rawFormulas is string singleF)
                {
                    isFormulaCol[1] = singleF.StartsWith("=");
                }
            }
            catch { }

            // 3. Bulk-read entire DataBodyRange into memory (1 COM call)
            object[,] dataGrid = TableMapper.GetRange2DValues(body, rowCount, colCount);

            // 4. Pre-build O(1) lookup dictionaries for all SyncRules on this table
            var lookupDictCache = new Dictionary<string, Dictionary<string, string>>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            Dictionary<string, string> GetLookupMap(string lookupSrc, string resultSrc)
            {
                string cacheKey = $"{lookupSrc}||{resultSrc}";
                if (!lookupDictCache.TryGetValue(cacheKey, out var map))
                {
                    map = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                    var keys = RuleDataCache.GetSourceItems(wb, lookupSrc, preserveDuplicates: true);
                    var vals = RuleDataCache.GetSourceItems(wb, resultSrc, preserveDuplicates: true);
                    int len = Math.Min(keys.Count, vals.Count);
                    for (int i = 0; i < len; i++)
                    {
                        string k = keys[i].Trim();
                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(k) && !map.ContainsKey(k))
                        {
                            map[k] = vals[i];
                            string normKey = k.Replace(" ", "_");
                            if (!map.ContainsKey(normKey)) map[normKey] = vals[i];
                        }
                    }
                    lookupDictCache[cacheKey] = map;
                }
                return map;
            }

            // Pre-compute max AutoIncrement ID per column in memory (0 COM calls)
            var maxAutoIdByCol = new long[colCount + 1];
            for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
            {
                if (colRules[c]?.DefaultRule?.Kind == DefaultKind.AutoIncrement)
                {
                    long maxId = 0;
                    for (int r = 1; r <= rowCount; r++)
                    {
                        string s = CleanCellText(dataGrid[r, c]);
                        if (long.TryParse(s, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out long val) && val > maxId)
                            maxId = val;
                    }
                    maxAutoIdByCol[c] = maxId;
                }
            }

            // Track dirty cells to write back only what changed
            var dirtyWrites = new List<(int ExcelRow, int AbsCol, object Value, PropertyRuleDescriptor? Rule)>();

            // 5. Pure In-Memory Row Healing Loop
            for (int r = 1; r <= rowCount; r++)
            {
                int excelRow = startRow + r - 1;
                var rowValues = new string[colCount + 1];
                var rawObjects = new object?[colCount + 1];
                bool rowHasUserInput = false;
                for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                {
                    object? valObj = dataGrid[r, c];
                    rawObjects[c] = valObj;
                    string clean = CleanCellText(valObj);
                    rowValues[c] = clean;

                    if (isFormulaCol[c] || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(clean)) continue;
                    if (clean == "0" || clean == "0.00" || clean == "-" || clean == "\u2014" ||
                        clean.Equals("None", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        continue;

                    var rule = colRules[c];
                    if (rule?.DefaultRule?.Kind == DefaultKind.Literal &&
                        string.Equals(clean, rule.DefaultRule.LiteralValue, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        continue;

                    rowHasUserInput = true;
                }

                if (!rowHasUserInput) continue;

                // STEP 0: Apply [ColumnRule(Transform = ...)] in memory
                for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                {
                    var rule = colRules[c];
                    string val = rowValues[c];
                    if (isFormulaCol[c] || rule == null || rule.Transform == TransformKind.None || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(val))
                        continue;

                    string transformed = ApplyTextTransform(val, rule.Transform);
                    if (!string.Equals(val, transformed, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                    {
                        dirtyWrites.Add((excelRow, startCol + c - 1, transformed, rule));
                        rowValues[c] = transformed;
                        rawObjects[c] = transformed;
                    }
                }

                // STEP A: Fill Missing Partner Syncs Across the Row
                for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                {
                    var rule = colRules[c];
                    string val = rowValues[c];
                    if (rule == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(val) || rule.SyncRules.Count == 0) continue;

                    foreach (var sync in rule.SyncRules)
                    {
                        int targetRelCol = FindColumnByHeader(colHeaders, sync.TargetHeader);
                        if (targetRelCol < 1 || isFormulaCol[targetRelCol]) continue;

                        var targetRule = colRules[targetRelCol];
                        string currentTargetVal = rowValues[targetRelCol];

                        var map = GetLookupMap(sync.LookupSource, sync.ResultSource);
                        if (!map.TryGetValue(val.Trim(), out string? lookedUp))
                            map.TryGetValue(val.Trim().Replace(" ", "_"), out lookedUp);

                        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(lookedUp))
                        {
                            bool isMismatch = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(currentTargetVal) &&
                                              !RuleDataCache.ValuesMatch(currentTargetVal, lookedUp);

                            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(currentTargetVal) || (isMismatch && (targetRule?.IsWarningOnly ?? false)))
                            {
                                dirtyWrites.Add((excelRow, startCol + targetRelCol - 1, lookedUp, targetRule));
                                rowValues[targetRelCol] = lookedUp;
                                rawObjects[targetRelCol] = lookedUp;
                                if (isMismatch)
                                {
                                    allNotices.Add($"Row {excelRow} [{colHeaders[targetRelCol]}]: Auto-corrected '{currentTargetVal}' to '{lookedUp}' based on {colHeaders[c]} '{val}'.");
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // STEP B: Apply [ColumnDefault] in memory
                for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                {
                    var rule = colRules[c];
                    if (isFormulaCol[c] || rule?.DefaultRule == null || rule.DefaultRule.Kind == DefaultKind.None) continue;
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rowValues[c])) continue;

                    object? defaultVal = rule.DefaultRule.Kind == DefaultKind.AutoIncrement
                        ? ++maxAutoIdByCol[c]
                        : ComputeDefaultValue(wb, table, c, colHeaders, rawObjects, rowValues, rule.DefaultRule);

                    if (defaultVal != null)
                    {
                        dirtyWrites.Add((excelRow, startCol + c - 1, defaultVal, rule));
                        rawObjects[c] = defaultVal;
                        rowValues[c] = FormatDisplayValue(defaultVal, rule);
                        allNotices.Add($"Row {excelRow} [{colHeaders[c]}]: Defaulted to '{rowValues[c]}'.");
                    }
                }

                // STEP C: Enforce [ColumnStateGuard] in memory
                for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                {
                    var rule = colRules[c];
                    if (isFormulaCol[c] || rule == null || rule.StateGuards.Count == 0) continue;

                    string currentVal = rowValues[c];
                    string? enforcedValue = EvaluateStateGuardsForCell(colHeaders, rawObjects, rowValues, rule, currentVal, out string? guardReason);

                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(enforcedValue) && !RuleDataCache.ValuesMatch(currentVal, enforcedValue))
                    {
                        dirtyWrites.Add((excelRow, startCol + c - 1, enforcedValue, rule));
                        rowValues[c] = enforcedValue;
                        rawObjects[c] = enforcedValue;
                        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(guardReason))
                        {
                            allNotices.Add($"Row {excelRow} [{colHeaders[c]}]: {guardReason}");
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // 6. Write to Excel ONLY if at least one cell was actually healed
            if (dirtyWrites.Count > 0)
            {
                dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
                bool origEvents = true;
                try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }

                // Lift any legacy sheet protection; it is never re-applied.
                if (SheetProtections.IsProtected(worksheet)) SheetProtections.Unprotect(worksheet);

                try
                {
                    foreach (var (excelRow, absCol, val, rule) in dirtyWrites)
                    {
                        WriteCellSafe(worksheet.Cells[excelRow, absCol], val, rule);
                    }
                }
                finally
                {
                    try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[RuleEvaluator.EvaluateAndHealTable Error] {ex.Message}");
        }
        finally
        {
            _isEvaluating = false;
        }

        return allNotices;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Builds the interactive dropdown menu options for a cell, including section grouping and gray-out validation.
    /// </summary>
    public static List<DropdownOption> GetEvaluatedDropdownOptions(
        dynamic wb,
        dynamic? table,
        int excelRow,
        PropertyRuleDescriptor? rule)
    {
        var options = new List<DropdownOption>();
        if (wb == null || rule == null) return options;

        // 1. If the column has explicit list Sources, load them by section
        if (rule.Sources.Length > 0)
        {
            for (int sec = 0; sec < rule.Sources.Length; sec++)
            {
                var items = RuleDataCache.GetSourceItems(wb, rule.Sources[sec], preserveDuplicates: false);
                foreach (var rawItem in items)
                {
                    string display = ApplyTextTransform(FormatIfDate(rawItem), rule.Transform);
                    options.Add(new DropdownOption
                    {
                        Raw = rawItem,
                        Display = display,
                        Section = sec,
                        IsValid = true
                    });
                }
            }
        }
        // 2. If the column has no list Source, but has a [ColumnDefault] (e.g. AutoIncrement ID or Date offset), show the computed default as a 1-click option
        else if (rule.DefaultRule != null && rule.DefaultRule.Kind != DefaultKind.None && table != null)
        {
            int startCol = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Column;
            int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
            int headerRow = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row;
            dynamic ws = table.Parent;

            var colHeaders = new string[colCount + 1];
            var rawObjects = new object?[colCount + 1];
            var rowValues = new string[colCount + 1];
            int targetRelCol = 1;

            for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
            {
                int absCol = startCol + c - 1;
                colHeaders[c] = ws.Cells[headerRow, absCol].Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                rawObjects[c] = ws.Cells[excelRow, absCol].Value2;
                rowValues[c] = CleanCellText(rawObjects[c]);
                if (RuleRegistry.NormalizeIdentifier(colHeaders[c]).Equals(rule.NormalizedHeader, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    targetRelCol = c;
            }

            object? computed = ComputeDefaultValue(wb, table, targetRelCol, colHeaders, rawObjects, rowValues, rule.DefaultRule);
            if (computed != null)
            {
                string disp = FormatDisplayValue(computed, rule);
                options.Add(new DropdownOption { Raw = disp, Display = disp, Section = 0, IsValid = true });
            }
        }

        if (options.Count == 0 || table == null) return options;

        // 3. Evaluate relational gray-out (Partner Sync & StateGuards) on the current row
        try
        {
            dynamic ws = table.Parent;
            int startCol = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Column;
            int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
            int headerRow = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row;

            var colHeaders = new string[colCount + 1];
            var rawObjects = new object?[colCount + 1];
            var rowValues = new string[colCount + 1];

            for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
            {
                int absCol = startCol + c - 1;
                colHeaders[c] = ws.Cells[headerRow, absCol].Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                rawObjects[c] = ws.Cells[excelRow, absCol].Value2;
                rowValues[c] = CleanCellText(rawObjects[c]);
            }

            // A) Check if a partner column on this row already has a value that constrains this dropdown
            HashSet<string>? allowedByPartner = null;
            foreach (var sync in rule.SyncRules)
            {
                int partnerCol = FindColumnByHeader(colHeaders, sync.TargetHeader);
                if (partnerCol >= 1 && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rowValues[partnerCol]))
                {
                    string partnerVal = rowValues[partnerCol];
                    // Find all items in LookupSource whose corresponding ResultSource equals partnerVal
                    var keys = RuleDataCache.GetSourceItems(wb, sync.LookupSource, preserveDuplicates: true);
                    var vals = RuleDataCache.GetSourceItems(wb, sync.ResultSource, preserveDuplicates: true);
                    int len = Math.Min(keys.Count, vals.Count);
                    allowedByPartner ??= new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

                    for (int i = 0; i < len; i++)
                    {
                        if (RuleDataCache.ValuesMatch(vals[i], partnerVal))
                        {
                            allowedByPartner.Add(keys[i]);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // B) Check StateGuards on this column
            var forbiddenStates = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            string? forcedState = null;

            foreach (var guard in rule.StateGuards)
            {
                bool condTrue = EvaluateCondition(guard.Condition, guard.ReferenceHeaders, guard.ExpectedValue, colHeaders, rawObjects, rowValues);
                if (condTrue)
                {
                    forcedState = guard.ForcedValueWhenTrue;
                    break;
                }
                else if (guard.DisallowForcedValueWhenFalse)
                {
                    forbiddenStates.Add(guard.ForcedValueWhenTrue);
                }
            }

            // Update IsValid flags on options
            var updated = new List<DropdownOption>(options.Count);
            foreach (var opt in options)
            {
                bool valid = true;

                if (allowedByPartner != null && allowedByPartner.Count > 0)
                {
                    valid = allowedByPartner.Any(a => RuleDataCache.ValuesMatch(a, opt.Raw) || RuleDataCache.ValuesMatch(a, opt.Display));
                }

                if (valid && forcedState != null)
                {
                    valid = RuleDataCache.ValuesMatch(opt.Raw, forcedState);
                }
                else if (valid && forbiddenStates.Count > 0)
                {
                    if (forbiddenStates.Any(f => RuleDataCache.ValuesMatch(opt.Raw, f)))
                        valid = false;
                }

                updated.Add(new DropdownOption
                {
                    Raw = opt.Raw,
                    Display = opt.Display,
                    Section = opt.Section,
                    IsValid = valid
                });
            }

            return updated;
        }
        catch
        {
            return options;
        }
    }

    private static void ExecuteSyncRulesForColumn(
        dynamic wb,
        dynamic worksheet,
        dynamic table,
        int excelRow,
        int startCol,
        int colCount,
        string[] colHeaders,
        PropertyRuleDescriptor?[] colRules,
        string[] rowValues,
        object?[] rawObjects,
        PropertyRuleDescriptor sourceRule,
        string sourceVal)
    {
        var filledTargets = new HashSet<int>();

        foreach (var sync in sourceRule.SyncRules)
        {
            int targetRelCol = FindColumnByHeader(colHeaders, sync.TargetHeader);
            if (targetRelCol < 1 || filledTargets.Contains(targetRelCol))
                continue;

            string? resolved = RuleDataCache.LookupValue(wb, sourceVal, sync.LookupSource, sync.ResultSource);
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(resolved))
            {
                int targetAbsCol = startCol + targetRelCol - 1;
                var targetRule = colRules[targetRelCol];
                string finalVal = targetRule != null
                    ? ApplyTextTransform(resolved, targetRule.Transform)
                    : resolved;

                WriteCellSafe(worksheet.Cells[excelRow, targetAbsCol], finalVal, targetRule);
                rowValues[targetRelCol] = finalVal;
                rawObjects[targetRelCol] = finalVal;
                filledTargets.Add(targetRelCol);
            }
        }
    }

    private static object? ComputeDefaultValue(
        dynamic wb,
        dynamic table,
        int targetRelCol,
        string[] colHeaders,
        object?[] rawObjects,
        string[] rowValues,
        ColumnDefaultAttribute defaultRule)
    {
        switch (defaultRule.Kind)
        {
            case DefaultKind.Literal:
                return defaultRule.LiteralValue;

            case DefaultKind.Today:
                return DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today);

            case DefaultKind.DateOffsetDays:
                {
                    DateOnly anchor = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today);
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(defaultRule.ReferenceHeader))
                    {
                        int refCol = FindColumnByHeader(colHeaders, defaultRule.ReferenceHeader);
                        if (refCol >= 1 && RuleDataCache.TryParseDate(rawObjects[refCol], out DateOnly parsedRef))
                        {
                            anchor = parsedRef;
                        }
                    }
                    return anchor.AddDays(defaultRule.Offset);
                }

            case DefaultKind.DateOffsetMonths:
                {
                    DateOnly anchor = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today);
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(defaultRule.ReferenceHeader))
                    {
                        int refCol = FindColumnByHeader(colHeaders, defaultRule.ReferenceHeader);
                        if (refCol >= 1 && RuleDataCache.TryParseDate(rawObjects[refCol], out DateOnly parsedRef))
                        {
                            anchor = parsedRef;
                        }
                    }
                    return anchor.AddMonths(defaultRule.Offset);
                }

            case DefaultKind.AutoIncrement:
                {
                    long maxId = 0;
                    try
                    {
                        dynamic? colBody = table.ListColumns[targetRelCol].DataBodyRange;
                        if (colBody != null)
                        {
                            object? rawCol = colBody.Value2;
                            if (rawCol is object[,] mat)
                            {
                                int rStart = mat.GetLowerBound(0);
                                int rEnd = mat.GetUpperBound(0);
                                int cStart = mat.GetLowerBound(1);
                                for (int r = rStart; r <= rEnd; r++)
                                {
                                    string s = CleanCellText(mat[r, cStart]);
                                    if (long.TryParse(s, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out long val) && val > maxId)
                                        maxId = val;
                                }
                            }
                            else if (rawCol != null)
                            {
                                string s = CleanCellText(rawCol);
                                if (long.TryParse(s, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out long val) && val > maxId)
                                    maxId = val;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    catch { }
                    return maxId + 1;
                }

            default:
                return null;
        }
    }

    internal static string? EvaluateStateGuardsForCell(
        string[] colHeaders,
        object?[] rawObjects,
        string[] rowValues,
        PropertyRuleDescriptor rule,
        string currentVal,
        out string? reason)
    {
        reason = null;
        if (rule.StateGuards.Count == 0) return null;

        foreach (var guard in rule.StateGuards)
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(guard.SkipIfCurrentEquals) &&
            RuleDataCache.ValuesMatch(currentVal, guard.SkipIfCurrentEquals))
            {
                return null; // Keeps currentVal exactly as it is (e.g., "Reserved" or "Decommissioned")
            }

            bool condTrue = EvaluateCondition(guard.Condition, guard.ReferenceHeaders, guard.ExpectedValue, colHeaders, rawObjects, rowValues);
            if (condTrue)
            {
                if (!RuleDataCache.ValuesMatch(currentVal, guard.ForcedValueWhenTrue))
                {
                    reason = $"{rule.HeaderName} auto-set to '{guard.ForcedValueWhenTrue}' based on row date/state.";
                    return guard.ForcedValueWhenTrue;
                }
                return guard.ForcedValueWhenTrue;
            }
            else if (guard.DisallowForcedValueWhenFalse && RuleDataCache.ValuesMatch(currentVal, guard.ForcedValueWhenTrue))
            {
                reason = $"'{guard.ForcedValueWhenTrue}' cannot be selected when all dates/conditions are valid. Auto-reverted to '{guard.FallbackValueWhenFalse}'.";
                return guard.FallbackValueWhenFalse;
            }
        }

        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(currentVal) && rule.DefaultRule?.Kind == DefaultKind.Literal)
        {
            return rule.DefaultRule.LiteralValue;
        }

        return null;
    }

    internal static bool EvaluateCondition(
        StateConditionKind condition,
        string[] referenceHeaders,
        string? expectedValue,
        string[] colHeaders,
        object?[] rawObjects,
        string[] rowValues,
        string? currentCellVal = null)
    {
        if (referenceHeaders == null || referenceHeaders.Length == 0) return false;
        DateOnly today = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today);

        foreach (var refHeader in referenceHeaders)
        {
            int c = FindColumnByHeader(colHeaders, refHeader);
            if (c < 1) continue;

            object? raw = rawObjects[c];
            string str = rowValues[c];

            switch (condition)
            {
                case StateConditionKind.DateExpired:
                    if (RuleDataCache.TryParseDate(raw, out DateOnly d) && d < today)
                        return true;
                    break;

                case StateConditionKind.ColumnNotEmpty:
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(str) && str != "—" && str != "0")
                        return true;
                    break;

                case StateConditionKind.ColumnEquals:
                    if (RuleDataCache.ValuesMatch(str, expectedValue))
                        return true;
                    break;

                case StateConditionKind.DateGreaterThan:
                    if (RuleDataCache.TryParseDate(currentCellVal, out DateOnly thisDate) &&
                        RuleDataCache.TryParseDate(str, out DateOnly refDate))
                    {
                        if (refDate >= thisDate)
                            return true;
                    }
                    break;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    internal static int FindColumnByHeader(string[] colHeaders, string targetHeader)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(targetHeader)) return -1;
        string normTarget = RuleRegistry.NormalizeIdentifier(targetHeader);

        for (int c = 1; c < colHeaders.Length; c++)
        {
            string normCol = RuleRegistry.NormalizeIdentifier(colHeaders[c]);
            if (normCol.Equals(normTarget, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                normCol.TrimEnd('s').Equals(normTarget.TrimEnd('s'), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                return c;
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }

    public static string ApplyTextTransform(string input, TransformKind transform)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input)) return string.Empty;
        string trimmed = input.Trim();

        return transform switch
        {
            TransformKind.Trim => trimmed,
            TransformKind.UpperCase => trimmed.ToUpperInvariant(),
            TransformKind.ShortNameFirstLast => FormatShortName(trimmed, maxWords: 2),
            TransformKind.ShortNameThreeWords => FormatShortName(trimmed, maxWords: 3),
            _ => trimmed
        };
    }

    private static string FormatShortName(string fullName, int maxWords)
    {
        var parts = fullName.Split(new[] { ' ' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
        if (parts.Length <= maxWords) return string.Join(" ", parts);
        if (maxWords == 2) return $"{parts[0]} {parts[^1]}";
        return $"{parts[0]} {parts[1]} {parts[^1]}";
    }

    private static void WriteCellSafe(dynamic cell, object value, PropertyRuleDescriptor? rule)
    {
        try
        {
            if (value is DateOnly dOnly)
            {
                cell.Value2 = dOnly.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue).ToOADate();
                return;
            }
            if (value is DateTime dt)
            {
                cell.Value2 = dt.ToOADate();
                return;
            }
            if (rule != null && rule.IsDateColumn && RuleDataCache.TryParseDate(value, out DateOnly parsedDate))
            {
                cell.Value2 = parsedDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue).ToOADate();
                return;
            }
            if (rule != null && rule.IsNumericColumn &&
                double.TryParse(value.ToString(), NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double dNum))
            {
                cell.Value2 = dNum;
                return;
            }

            cell.Value2 = value.ToString();
        }
        catch { }
    }

    public static string FormatDisplayValue(object? val, PropertyRuleDescriptor? rule)
    {
        if (val == null) return string.Empty;
        if (val is DateOnly d) return d.ToString("dd/MM/yyyy", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        if (val is DateTime dt) return dt.ToString("dd/MM/yyyy", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        if (rule != null && rule.IsDateColumn && RuleDataCache.TryParseDate(val, out DateOnly parsed))
            return parsed.ToString("dd/MM/yyyy", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        return CleanCellText(val);
    }

    private static string FormatIfDate(string input)
    {
        if (RuleDataCache.TryParseDate(input, out DateOnly d) &&
            (input.Contains('/') || input.Contains('-') || (double.TryParse(input, out double oa) && oa > 30000)))
        {
            return d.ToString("dd/MM/yyyy", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        }
        return input;
    }

    private static string CleanCellText(object? val)
    {
        if (val == null || SheetMapperHelper.IsComError(val)) return string.Empty;
        if (val is double d && Math.Abs(d - Math.Round(d)) < 0.00001 && d < 1e15)
            return ((long)Math.Round(d)).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        string s = val.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
        if (s.StartsWith("#") || s == "—") return string.Empty;
        return s;
    }
}
`n

FILE: Core\03_RuntimeUI\RuleEngine\RuleEngine.Registry.cs
