namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.RuleEngine;

using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types.Attributes;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;

public sealed class PropertyRuleDescriptor
{
    public string TableName { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string HeaderName { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string NormalizedHeader { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public PropertyInfo Property { get; init; } = null!;
    public Type PropertyType { get; init; } = typeof(string);

    public string[] Sources { get; init; } = Array.Empty<string>();
    public bool IsRequired { get; init; }
    public bool IsWarningOnly { get; init; }
    public bool IsMultiSelect { get; init; }
    public bool AllowNonNegativeZero { get; init; } = true;
    public TransformKind Transform { get; init; } = TransformKind.None;

    public List<ColumnSyncAttribute> SyncRules { get; init; } = new();
    public ColumnDefaultAttribute? DefaultRule { get; init; }
    public List<ColumnStateGuardAttribute> StateGuards { get; init; } = new();
    public string? DateMustBeGreaterThanHeader { get; init; }

    public bool HasDropdown =>
        Sources.Length > 0 ||
        (DefaultRule != null && DefaultRule.Kind != DefaultKind.None);

    public bool IsDateColumn =>
        (Nullable.GetUnderlyingType(PropertyType) ?? PropertyType) == typeof(DateOnly) ||
        (Nullable.GetUnderlyingType(PropertyType) ?? PropertyType) == typeof(DateTime);

    public bool IsNumericColumn
    {
        get
        {
            Type t = Nullable.GetUnderlyingType(PropertyType) ?? PropertyType;
            return t == typeof(int) || t == typeof(long) || t == typeof(decimal) || t == typeof(double);
        }
    }
}

public static class RuleRegistry
{
    private static bool _isInitialized = false;
    private static readonly object _initLock = new();

    // Key: NormalizedTableName -> (NormalizedHeader -> PropertyRuleDescriptor)
    private static readonly Dictionary<string, Dictionary<string, PropertyRuleDescriptor>> _tableMap =
        new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    public static void EnsureInitialized()
    {
        if (_isInitialized) return;

        lock (_initLock)
        {
            if (_isInitialized) return;

            var coreAssembly = typeof(ExcelTableAttribute).Assembly;
            var types = coreAssembly.GetTypes()
                .Where(t => t.IsClass && !t.IsAbstract && t.GetCustomAttributes<ExcelTableAttribute>(true).Any())
                .ToList();

            foreach (var rootType in types)
            {
                var tableAttrs = rootType.GetCustomAttributes<ExcelTableAttribute>(true);
                foreach (var tableAttr in tableAttrs)
                {
                    string normTable = NormalizeIdentifier(tableAttr.TableName);
                    if (!_tableMap.TryGetValue(normTable, out var colDict))
                    {
                        colDict = new Dictionary<string, PropertyRuleDescriptor>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                        _tableMap[normTable] = colDict;
                    }

                    ScanTypeProperties(rootType, tableAttr.TableName, colDict);
                }
            }

            _isInitialized = true;
        }
    }

    private static void ScanTypeProperties(
    Type targetType,
    string tableName,
    Dictionary<string, PropertyRuleDescriptor> colDict)
    {
        foreach (var prop in targetType.GetProperties(BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Instance))
        {
            if (prop.GetIndexParameters().Length > 0) continue;

            // Recurse into [DataDetail] child objects (e.g., VehicleDetail, StaffDetails, ProjectDetails)
            if (prop.GetCustomAttribute<DataDetailAttribute>() != null)
            {
                ScanTypeProperties(prop.PropertyType, tableName, colDict);
                continue;
            }

            // Skip child entity collections (e.g., List<Maintenance>), but KEEP List<string> (e.g., Workers/Vehicles multi-select)
            if (typeof(IEnumerable).IsAssignableFrom(prop.PropertyType) &&
                prop.PropertyType != typeof(string) &&
                prop.PropertyType != typeof(List<string>) &&
                prop.PropertyType != typeof(string[]))
            {
                continue;
            }

            var excelColAttr = prop.GetCustomAttribute<ExcelColumnAttribute>();
            var ruleAttr = prop.GetCustomAttribute<ColumnRuleAttribute>();
            var defaultAttr = prop.GetCustomAttribute<ColumnDefaultAttribute>();
            var dateGreaterAttr = prop.GetCustomAttribute<ColumnDateGreaterThanAttribute>();
            var syncAttrs = prop.GetCustomAttributes<ColumnSyncAttribute>().ToList();
            var guardAttrs = prop.GetCustomAttributes<ColumnStateGuardAttribute>()
                                 .OrderBy(g => g.Priority)
                                 .ToList();

            // If no attributes exist at all, skip the property
            if (excelColAttr == null && ruleAttr == null && defaultAttr == null && syncAttrs.Count == 0 && guardAttrs.Count == 0)
                continue;

            // Prefer ExcelColumn header; fallback to the property name
            string headerName = excelColAttr != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(excelColAttr.HeaderName)
                ? excelColAttr.HeaderName
                : prop.Name;

            string normHeader = NormalizeIdentifier(headerName);

            // If there's no ExcelColumn attribute, it is a UI-only column (never required to save)
            bool isMappedToStorage = excelColAttr != null;
            bool isRequired = isMappedToStorage && (ruleAttr?.IsRequired ?? excelColAttr.IsRequired);
            bool isWarningOnly = !isMappedToStorage || (ruleAttr?.IsWarningOnly ?? false);

            var descriptor = new PropertyRuleDescriptor
            {
                TableName = tableName,
                HeaderName = headerName,
                NormalizedHeader = normHeader,
                Property = prop,
                PropertyType = prop.PropertyType,
                Sources = ruleAttr?.Sources ?? Array.Empty<string>(),
                IsRequired = isRequired,
                IsWarningOnly = isWarningOnly,
                IsMultiSelect = ruleAttr?.IsMultiSelect ?? false,
                AllowNonNegativeZero = ruleAttr?.AllowNonNegativeZero ?? true,
                Transform = ruleAttr?.Transform ?? TransformKind.None,
                SyncRules = syncAttrs,
                DefaultRule = defaultAttr,
                StateGuards = guardAttrs,
                DateMustBeGreaterThanHeader = dateGreaterAttr?.ReferenceHeader
            };

            colDict[normHeader] = descriptor;
        }
    }

    public static IReadOnlyList<PropertyRuleDescriptor> GetTableRules(string tableName)
    {
        EnsureInitialized();
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(tableName)) return Array.Empty<PropertyRuleDescriptor>();

        if (TryResolveTableDictionary(tableName, out var colDict))
        {
            return colDict.Values.ToList();
        }

        return Array.Empty<PropertyRuleDescriptor>();
    }

    public static PropertyRuleDescriptor? GetRule(string tableName, string columnHeader)
    {
        EnsureInitialized();
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(tableName) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(columnHeader))
            return null;

        if (!TryResolveTableDictionary(tableName, out var colDict))
            return null;

        return ResolveHeaderInDictionary(colDict, columnHeader);
    }

    public static PropertyRuleDescriptor? GetRuleForCell(dynamic singleCell)
    {
        if (singleCell == null) return null;
        try
        {
            dynamic? table = null;
            try { table = singleCell.ListObject; } catch { }
            if (table == null) return null;

            string tableName = table.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            int headerRow = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row;
            int col = (int)singleCell.Column;
            dynamic sheet = singleCell.Worksheet;

            string colHeader = sheet.Cells[headerRow, col].Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
            return GetRule(tableName, colHeader);
        }
        catch
        {
            return null;
        }
    }

    public static bool HasDropdown(string tableName, string columnHeader)
    {
        var rule = GetRule(tableName, columnHeader);
        return rule != null && rule.HasDropdown;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Automatically applies the "DropDown" named style and clears legacy native validation
    /// on all table columns governed by C# [ColumnRule] or [ColumnDefault] attributes,
    /// while also disabling legacy ShowTotals rows across all tables.
    /// </summary>
    public static void ApplyDropdownStylesToWorkbook(dynamic wb)
    {
        if (wb == null) return;
        EnsureInitialized();

        dynamic xlApp = wb.Application;
        bool origUpdating = true;
        bool origEvents = true;

        try
        {
            try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }
            try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }

            dynamic? activeSheet = null;
            try { activeSheet = wb.ActiveSheet; } catch { }

            Commands.EnsureNamedStyleExists(xlApp, Commands.DropDownStyleName);

            foreach (dynamic ws in wb.Sheets)
            {
                string sName = ws.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                if (sName.Equals(SheetOperations.LookupsSheetName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                    sName.Equals(SheetOperations.DataSheetName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                int tableCount = 0;
                try { tableCount = (int)ws.ListObjects.Count; } catch { }
                if (tableCount == 0) continue;

                SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)ws, new Action(() =>
                {
                    for (int t = 1; t <= tableCount; t++)
                    {
                        dynamic table = ws.ListObjects[t];
                        string tableName = table.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;

                        int headerRow = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row;
                        int startCol = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Column;
                        int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
                        dynamic? bodyRange = table.DataBodyRange;

                        for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                        {
                            int absCol = startCol + c - 1;
                            string headerText = ws.Cells[headerRow, absCol].Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                            var rule = GetRule(tableName, headerText);
                            if (rule == null) continue;

                            dynamic archCell = ws.Cells[headerRow + 1, absCol];
                            string archFormula = archCell.Formula?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                            if (archFormula.StartsWith("=")) continue;

                            // Inside RuleRegistry.ApplyDropdownStylesToWorkbook:
                            if (rule.HasDropdown)
                            {
                                try
                                {
                                    // Pre-read the template's original format & style on the archetype cell
                                    string origStyle = archCell.Style?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                                    string origFmt = archCell.NumberFormat?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                                    dynamic? origFmtLocal = null;
                                    try { origFmtLocal = archCell.NumberFormatLocal; } catch { }

                                    dynamic targetRange = (bodyRange != null) ? table.ListColumns[c].DataBodyRange : archCell;
                                    if (targetRange != null)
                                    {
                                        // Only set DropDown style if the cell doesn't already have a custom unlocked style
                                        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(origStyle) || origStyle.Equals("Normal", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                        {
                                            targetRange.Style = Commands.DropDownStyleName;
                                        }
                                        try { targetRange.Validation.Delete(); } catch { }

                                        // Restore the template's cell-level NumberFormat (e.g. Date format) if the style reset it
                                        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(origFmt) && !origFmt.Equals("General", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                        {
                                            try { targetRange.NumberFormat = origFmt; } catch { }
                                            try { if (origFmtLocal != null) targetRange.NumberFormatLocal = origFmtLocal; } catch { }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                                catch { }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }));

                // Ensure operational protection (and =FALSE lock on calculated columns) is active on open
                SheetProtections.ApplyStyleDrivenProtection(ws);
            }

            // Restore original sheet view so Excel doesn't jump
            if (activeSheet != null)
            {
                try { activeSheet.Activate(); } catch { }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[RuleRegistry.ApplyDropdownStylesToWorkbook Error] {ex.Message}");
        }
        finally
        {
            try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
            try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
        }
    }

    internal static string NormalizeIdentifier(string raw)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(raw)) return string.Empty;
        string clean = raw.Trim()
            .Replace("'s", "", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            .Replace("’s", "", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            .Replace(" s ", " ", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        // Common header alias normalization across templates
        clean = Regex.Replace(clean, @"[\s_\-\.'`]+", string.Empty).ToLowerInvariant();
        if (clean == "workerid") return "staffid";
        if (clean == "workername") return "staffname";
        if (clean == "drivenby") return "staffname";
        if (clean == "cost" || clean == "transactionamount") return "amount";
        if (clean == "insuracerenewalid") return "insurancerenewalid";
        if (clean == "recieptnumber") return "receiptnumber";
        if (clean == "cardnumber") return "fuelcardnumber";
        return clean;
    }

    private static bool TryResolveTableDictionary(
        string tableName,
        out Dictionary<string, PropertyRuleDescriptor> colDict)
    {
        string norm = NormalizeIdentifier(tableName);
        if (_tableMap.TryGetValue(norm, out colDict!))
            return true;

        string stripped = norm.TrimEnd('s');
        foreach (var (k, v) in _tableMap)
        {
            if (k.TrimEnd('s').Equals(stripped, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                colDict = v;
                return true;
            }
        }

        colDict = null!;
        return false;
    }

    private static PropertyRuleDescriptor? ResolveHeaderInDictionary(
        Dictionary<string, PropertyRuleDescriptor> colDict,
        string header)
    {
        string norm = NormalizeIdentifier(header);
        if (colDict.TryGetValue(norm, out var exact))
            return exact;

        string stripped = norm.TrimEnd('s');
        foreach (var (k, v) in colDict)
        {
            if (k.TrimEnd('s').Equals(stripped, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return v;
        }

        // Tolerant fallback for ID/Cost/Contact variations
        if (norm.EndsWith("id") && colDict.Values.Count(d => d.NormalizedHeader.EndsWith("id") && d.DefaultRule?.Kind == DefaultKind.AutoIncrement) == 1)
        {
            return colDict.Values.First(d => d.NormalizedHeader.EndsWith("id") && d.DefaultRule?.Kind == DefaultKind.AutoIncrement);
        }

        if (norm.EndsWith("cost") && colDict.TryGetValue(norm.Replace("cost", ""), out var costMatch))
            return costMatch;

        if (!norm.EndsWith("cost") && colDict.TryGetValue(norm + "cost", out var addCostMatch))
            return addCostMatch;

        return null;
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.TableRowManager;

using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.RuleEngine;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types.Attributes;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.SheetMapper;
using System;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows.Forms;

public enum TableInsertMode
{
    InsertAtBottom,
    InsertAtTop
}

public enum TableNature
{
    Absolute,
    Additive
}

public static partial class TableRowManager
{
    private static bool _isManagingRows = false;
    private const string InsertAtTopDefinedName = "__SKSB_INSERT_AT_TOP";
    private const string DebounceSigDefinedName = "__SKSB_ROW_SIG";
    private static bool _inMemoryFallback = false;

    private static readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, TableInsertMode> TableModes =
        new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    private static readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, TableNature> TableNatures =
        new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    static TableRowManager()
    {
        // =========================================================================
        // PRE-CONFIGURED TABLE NATURE CLASSIFICATIONS
        // =========================================================================
        // Absolute Tables (Master Entities / Registries - Full Set Reconciliation)
        TableNatures["AllVehiclesTable"] = TableNature.Absolute;
        TableNatures["WorkshopsTable"] = TableNature.Absolute;
        TableNatures["FuelCardsTable"] = TableNature.Absolute;
        TableNatures["FuelCompanyNamesTable"] = TableNature.Absolute;
        TableNatures["InsuranceCompaniesTable"] = TableNature.Absolute;
        TableNatures["StaffMasterTable"] = TableNature.Absolute;
        TableNatures["AllStaffsTable"] = TableNature.Absolute;
        TableNatures["AllWorkersTable"] = TableNature.Absolute;
        TableNatures["SkillLicenseTypesTable"] = TableNature.Absolute;
        TableNatures["ProjectsTable"] = TableNature.Absolute;

        // Additive Tables (Append-Only Event Logs / Transaction Ledgers)
        TableNatures["OdometersTable"] = TableNature.Additive;
        TableNatures["MaintenancesTable"] = TableNature.Additive;
        TableNatures["RoadTaxesTable"] = TableNature.Additive;
        TableNatures["InspectionsTable"] = TableNature.Additive;
        TableNatures["PermitsTable"] = TableNature.Additive;
        TableNatures["InsurancesTable"] = TableNature.Additive;
        TableNatures["StaffLicensesTable"] = TableNature.Additive;
        TableNatures["StaffLeavesTable"] = TableNature.Additive;
    }

    public static void SetTableNature(string tableName, TableNature nature)
    {
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(tableName))
        {
            TableNatures[tableName.Trim()] = nature;
        }
    }

    public static TableNature GetTableNature(dynamic table)
    {
        try
        {
            string tableName = table?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(tableName))
            {
                if (TableNatures.TryGetValue(tableName, out var nature))
                    return nature;

                return ResolveDefaultTableNature(tableName);
            }
        }
        catch { }

        return TableNature.Absolute;
    }

    private static TableNature ResolveDefaultTableNature(string tableName)
    {
        string norm = tableName.Replace("_", "").Replace(" ", "").ToLowerInvariant();

        // Additive log heuristics
        if (norm.Contains("odometer") ||
            norm.Contains("maintenance") ||
            norm.Contains("inspection") ||
            norm.Contains("roadtax") ||
            norm.Contains("permit") ||
            (norm.Contains("insurance") && !norm.Contains("company") && !norm.Contains("companies")) ||
            norm.Contains("log") ||
            norm.Contains("transaction") ||
            norm.Contains("attendance") ||
            norm.Contains("history") ||
            norm.Contains("schedule") ||
            norm.Contains("timesheet"))
        {
            return TableNature.Additive;
        }

        return TableNature.Absolute;
    }

    public static bool IsInsertAtTopEnabled
    {
        get => GetWorkbookInsertAtTop();
        set => SetWorkbookInsertAtTop(value);
    }

    public static void SetTableInsertMode(string tableName, TableInsertMode mode)
    {
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(tableName))
        {
            TableModes[tableName.Trim()] = mode;
        }
    }

    public static TableInsertMode GetTableInsertMode(dynamic table)
    {
        try
        {
            string tableName = table?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(tableName) && TableModes.TryGetValue(tableName, out var mode))
            {
                return mode;
            }
        }
        catch { }

        return IsInsertAtTopEnabled ? TableInsertMode.InsertAtTop : TableInsertMode.InsertAtBottom;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Explicit row deletion command. Only executes on Absolute tables; blocks on Additive tables.
    /// Accessible via Manager Ribbon and Ctrl+Shift+D.
    /// </summary>
    [ExcelCommand(Name = "DeleteSelectedTableRow", ShortCut = "^+D")]
    public static void DeleteSelectedRowCommand()
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic selection = xlApp.Selection;
            if (selection == null) return;

            dynamic? table = null;
            try { table = selection.ListObject; } catch { }
            if (table == null)
            {
                try { table = selection.Cells[1, 1].ListObject; } catch { }
            }

            if (table == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("Please select a cell inside a table to delete a row.",
                    "No Table Selected", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                return;
            }

            string tableName = table.Name?.ToString() ?? "Table";
            var nature = GetTableNature(table);

            // GUARD: Block row deletion if table is Additive
            if (nature == TableNature.Additive)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    $"Row deletion is disabled for '{tableName}'.\n\n" +
                    $"This table is categorized as an Additive / Transaction Log table.\n" +
                    $"Individual rows cannot be deleted directly to safeguard historical operational records.",
                    "Deletion Blocked (Additive Table)",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                    MessageBoxIcon.Warning
                );
                SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation($"Deletion blocked: '{tableName}' is an Additive log table.");
                return;
            }

            dynamic worksheet = table.Parent;
            dynamic dataBody = table.DataBodyRange;
            if (dataBody == null) return;

            int bodyStartRow = (int)dataBody.Row;
            int totalBodyRows = (int)dataBody.Rows.Count;

            var rowIndicesToDelete = new HashSet<int>();
            foreach (dynamic cell in selection.Cells)
            {
                int r = (int)cell.Row;
                int listRowIdx = r - bodyStartRow + 1;
                if (listRowIdx >= 1 && listRowIdx <= totalBodyRows)
                {
                    rowIndicesToDelete.Add(listRowIdx);
                }
            }

            if (rowIndicesToDelete.Count == 0) return;

            var confirmResult = MessageBox.Show(
                $"Are you sure you want to delete {rowIndicesToDelete.Count} selected row(s) from '{tableName}'?\n\n" +
                "This change will be permanently committed to the database upon save.",
                "Confirm Delete Row",
                MessageBoxButtons.YesNo,
                MessageBoxIcon.Question
            );
            if (confirmResult != DialogResult.Yes) return;

            SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)worksheet, new Action(() =>
            {
                foreach (int idx in rowIndicesToDelete.OrderByDescending(x => x))
                {
                    if ((int)table.ListRows.Count > 1)
                    {
                        table.ListRows[idx].Delete();
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        // Keep 1 clean archetype row if deleting the final row
                        dynamic lastRowRange = table.ListRows[1].Range;
                        int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
                        int headerRow = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row;
                        int startCol = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Column;

                        for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                        {
                            dynamic archCell = worksheet.Cells[headerRow + 1, startCol + c - 1];
                            string archFormula = archCell.Formula?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                            if (!archFormula.StartsWith("="))
                            {
                                lastRowRange.Cells[1, c].Value2 = null;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }));

            SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation($"Deleted {rowIndicesToDelete.Count} row(s) from '{tableName}'.");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error("DeleteSelectedRowCommand failed", ex);
        }
    }

    public static void HandleSheetChange(dynamic target)
    {
        if (target == null || SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode || _isManagingRows) return;

        dynamic? table = null;
        try { table = target.ListObject; } catch { }
        if (table == null)
        {
            try { table = target.Cells[1, 1].ListObject; } catch { }
        }
        if (table == null) return;

        dynamic dataBody = table.DataBodyRange;

        // Ignore edits touching the Total Row or falling outside the DataBodyRange
        try
        {
            dynamic xlAppForBounds = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            if ((bool)table.ShowTotals && table.TotalsRowRange != null &&
                xlAppForBounds.Intersect(target, table.TotalsRowRange) != null)
            {
                return;
            }
            if (dataBody != null && xlAppForBounds.Intersect(target, dataBody) == null)
            {
                return;
            }
        }
        catch { }

        int bodyStartRow = dataBody != null ? (int)dataBody.Row : (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row + 1;
        int rowCount = dataBody != null ? (int)dataBody.Rows.Count : 0;
        int lastDataRow = bodyStartRow + Math.Max(1, rowCount) - 1;

        TableInsertMode insertMode = GetTableInsertMode(table);

        int targetTopRow = (int)target.Row;
        int targetRowCount = (int)target.Rows.Count;
        int targetBottomRow = targetTopRow + targetRowCount - 1;

        bool touchesBottom = (targetBottomRow >= lastDataRow);
        bool touchesTop = (targetTopRow <= bodyStartRow);
        bool hasEmptiedCells = false;

        try
        {
            foreach (dynamic cell in target.Cells)
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(cell.Value2?.ToString()?.Trim()))
                {
                    hasEmptiedCells = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        catch { }

        if (!touchesBottom && !touchesTop && !hasEmptiedCells) return;

        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            if (_isManagingRows) return;
            _isManagingRows = true;

            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            bool origEnableEvents = true;
            try { origEnableEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; } catch { }

            var auditTrail = new List<string>();

            try
            {
                xlApp.EnableEvents = false;
                dynamic worksheet = table.Parent;

                SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)worksheet, new Action(() =>
                {
                    dynamic currentBody = table.DataBodyRange;
                    int startCol = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Column;
                    int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
                    int headerRow = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row;

                    if (currentBody == null)
                    {
                        SpawnRowSafely(worksheet, table, startCol, colCount, headerRow, false, auditTrail);
                        return;
                    }

                    int curRowCount = (int)currentBody.Rows.Count;
                    int curBodyStartRow = (int)currentBody.Row;

                    if (insertMode == TableInsertMode.InsertAtBottom)
                    {
                        int lastRowIdx = curBodyStartRow + curRowCount - 1;
                        bool lastRowHasInput = CheckRowHasUserInput(worksheet, table, lastRowIdx, startCol, colCount, headerRow);
                        if (lastRowHasInput && touchesBottom)
                        {
                            SpawnRowSafely(worksheet, table, startCol, colCount, headerRow, false, auditTrail);
                        }
                    }
                    else if (insertMode == TableInsertMode.InsertAtTop)
                    {
                        int topRowIdx = curBodyStartRow;
                        bool topRowHasInput = CheckRowHasUserInput(worksheet, table, topRowIdx, startCol, colCount, headerRow);
                        if (topRowHasInput && touchesTop)
                        {
                            SpawnRowSafely(worksheet, table, startCol, colCount, headerRow, true, auditTrail);
                        }
                    }

                    // Auto-delete / compact empty buffer rows
                    CleanTableEmptyRows(table, insertMode, auditTrail);
                }));
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                AppLogger.Error("HandleSheetChange error", ex);
            }
            finally
            {
                try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEnableEvents; } catch { }
                _isManagingRows = false;
            }
        });
    }

    public static int CleanActiveSheetTables(dynamic worksheet)
    {
        if (worksheet == null) return 0;

        dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
        bool origEnableEvents = true;
        try { origEnableEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }

        int tablesCleaned = 0;
        try
        {
            SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)worksheet, new Action(() =>
            {
                foreach (dynamic table in worksheet.ListObjects)
                {
                    TableInsertMode mode = GetTableInsertMode(table);
                    CleanTableEmptyRows(table, mode, null);
                    tablesCleaned++;
                }
            }));
        }
        finally
        {
            try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEnableEvents; } catch { }
        }

        return tablesCleaned;
    }

    public static void CleanTableEmptyRows(dynamic table, TableInsertMode insertMode, List<string>? auditLog = null)
    {
        if (table == null) return;

        dynamic worksheet = table.Parent;
        dynamic currentBody = table.DataBodyRange;
        if (currentBody == null) return;

        int curRowCount = (int)currentBody.Rows.Count;
        int startCol = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Column;
        int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
        int headerRow = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row;
        int curBodyStartRow = (int)currentBody.Row;

        var emptyRowIndices = new List<int>();

        for (int r = 1; r <= curRowCount; r++)
        {
            int absRow = curBodyStartRow + r - 1;
            bool hasInput = CheckRowHasUserInput(worksheet, table, absRow, startCol, colCount, headerRow);
            if (!hasInput)
            {
                emptyRowIndices.Add(r);
            }
        }

        if (emptyRowIndices.Count > 1)
        {
            int toDeleteCount = emptyRowIndices.Count - 1;
            List<int> rowsToDelete = insertMode == TableInsertMode.InsertAtBottom
                ? emptyRowIndices.Take(toDeleteCount).OrderByDescending(x => x).ToList()
                : emptyRowIndices.Skip(1).OrderByDescending(x => x).ToList();

            foreach (int idx in rowsToDelete)
            {
                DeleteTableRowSafely(table, idx);
                auditLog?.Add($"[DELETE] Trimmed redundant empty row at index {idx}.");
            }
        }
        else if (emptyRowIndices.Count == 1 && curRowCount > 1)
        {
            int emptyIdx = emptyRowIndices[0];
            if (insertMode == TableInsertMode.InsertAtBottom && emptyIdx != curRowCount)
            {
                DeleteTableRowSafely(table, emptyIdx);
                SpawnRowSafely(worksheet, table, startCol, colCount, headerRow, false, auditLog);
            }
            else if (insertMode == TableInsertMode.InsertAtTop && emptyIdx != 1)
            {
                DeleteTableRowSafely(table, emptyIdx);
                SpawnRowSafely(worksheet, table, startCol, colCount, headerRow, true, auditLog);
            }
        }
        else if (emptyRowIndices.Count == 0)
        {
            SpawnRowSafely(worksheet, table, startCol, colCount, headerRow, insertMode == TableInsertMode.InsertAtTop, auditLog);
        }
    }

    public static bool CheckRowHasUserInput(dynamic worksheet, dynamic table, int rowIndex, int startCol, int colCount, int headerRow)
    {
        dynamic driverCell = worksheet.Cells[rowIndex, startCol];
        string driverVal = driverCell.Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(driverVal))
        {
            int nonDefaultCount = 0;
            int archetypeRow = headerRow + 1;

            for (int c = 2; c <= colCount; c++)
            {
                int absCol = startCol + c - 1;
                dynamic cell = worksheet.Cells[rowIndex, absCol];
                dynamic archCell = worksheet.Cells[archetypeRow, absCol];

                string archFormula = archCell.Formula?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                if (archFormula.StartsWith("=")) continue;

                string cellVal = cell.Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(cellVal)) continue;

                string headerText = worksheet.Cells[headerRow, absCol].Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                var rule = RuleRegistry.GetRule(table.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty, headerText);

                if (rule?.DefaultRule?.Kind == DefaultKind.Literal &&
                    string.Equals(cellVal, rule.DefaultRule.LiteralValue, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                nonDefaultCount++;
            }

            if (nonDefaultCount == 0) return false;
        }

        return true;
    }

    public static void SpawnRowSafely(
        dynamic worksheet,
        dynamic table,
        int startCol,
        int colCount,
        int headerRow,
        bool atTop,
        List<string>? auditLog = null)
    {
        string tableName = table.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
        dynamic newRow = atTop ? table.ListRows.Add(1) : table.ListRows.Add();
        dynamic newRowRange = newRow.Range;

        int sourceArchetypeRow = atTop ? headerRow + 2 : headerRow + 1;

        // Inside TableRowManager.SpawnRowSafely:

        for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
        {
            int absCol = startCol + c - 1;
            dynamic archetypeCell = worksheet.Cells[sourceArchetypeRow, absCol];
            dynamic newCell = newRowRange.Cells[1, c];

            string headerText = worksheet.Cells[headerRow, absCol].Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
            var rule = RuleRegistry.GetRule(tableName, headerText);

            if (rule != null && rule.HasDropdown)
            {
                newCell.Style = Commands.DropDownStyleName;
                try { newCell.Validation.Delete(); } catch { }
            }
            else
            {
                string styleName = archetypeCell.Style?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(styleName))
                {
                    try { newCell.Style = styleName; } catch { }
                }
            }

            // Explicitly mirror NumberFormat
            try
            {
                string archFormat = archetypeCell.NumberFormat?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(archFormat))
                {
                    newCell.NumberFormat = archFormat;
                }
                newCell.NumberFormatLocal = archetypeCell.NumberFormatLocal;
            }
            catch { }

            try
            {
                newCell.HorizontalAlignment = archetypeCell.HorizontalAlignment;
                newCell.VerticalAlignment = archetypeCell.VerticalAlignment;
            }
            catch { }

            // Ensure cell is unlocked if the style is whitelisted
            string appliedStyle = newCell.Style?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            newCell.Locked = !SheetProtections.IsUnlockedStyle(appliedStyle);

            var cfg = Metadata.ReadConfig(archetypeCell);
            if (!cfg.IsEmpty)
            {
                Metadata.WriteConfig(newCell, cfg);
            }

            string archFormula = archetypeCell.Formula?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            if (!archFormula.StartsWith("="))
            {
                newCell.Value2 = null;
            }
        }

        auditLog?.Add(atTop
            ? "[INSERT] Spawned new entry row at TOP."
            : "[INSERT] Spawned new entry row at BOTTOM.");
    }

    public static void DeleteTableRowSafely(dynamic table, int listRowIndex)
    {
        table.ListRows[listRowIndex].Delete();
    }

    private static bool GetWorkbookInsertAtTop()
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic wb = xlApp?.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (wb != null)
            {
                dynamic nameObj = null;
                try { nameObj = wb.Names.Item(InsertAtTopDefinedName); } catch { }
                if (nameObj != null)
                {
                    string refersTo = nameObj.RefersTo?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                    return refersTo.IndexOf("TRUE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0;
                }
            }
        }
        catch { }
        return _inMemoryFallback;
    }

    private static void SetWorkbookInsertAtTop(bool enabled)
    {
        _inMemoryFallback = enabled;
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic wb = xlApp?.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (wb != null)
            {
                try { wb.Names.Item(InsertAtTopDefinedName).Delete(); } catch { }
                wb.Names.Add(
                    Name: InsertAtTopDefinedName,
                    RefersTo: enabled ? "=\"TRUE\"" : "=\"FALSE\"",
                    Visible: false
                );
            }
        }
        catch { }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// High-speed bulk deduplication: reads the table body in 1 COM call into a 2D memory array,
    /// detects duplicates in RAM, and deletes redundant rows with calculation and screen updating suspended.
    /// </summary>
    public static int CleanTableDuplicateRows(dynamic table, List<string> warnings, int[]? keyColumnIndices = null)
    {
        if (table == null) return 0;

        dynamic? currentBody = null;
        try { currentBody = table.DataBodyRange; } catch { }
        if (currentBody == null) return 0;

        int curRowCount = 0;
        try { curRowCount = (int)currentBody.Rows.Count; } catch { }
        int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
        if (curRowCount <= 1 || colCount <= 0) return 0;

        int startCol = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Column;
        int headerRow = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row;
        int curBodyStartRow = (int)currentBody.Row;
        string tableName = table.Name?.ToString() ?? "Table";
        dynamic ws = table.Parent;
        dynamic xlApp = ws.Application;

        // 1. FREEZE COM ENVIRONMENT (Eliminates repaint & formula cascade lag)
        bool origUpdating = true;
        bool origEvents = true;
        int origCalc = -4105; // xlCalculationAutomatic
        try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }
        try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }
        try { origCalc = (int)xlApp.Calculation; xlApp.Calculation = -4135; /* xlCalculationManual */ } catch { }

        try
        {
            // 2. Identify formula columns in 1 COM call across archetype row
            int archetypeRow = headerRow + 1;
            var isFormulaCol = new bool[colCount + 1];
            try
            {
                dynamic archRange = ws.Range[ws.Cells[archetypeRow, startCol], ws.Cells[archetypeRow, startCol + colCount - 1]];
                object? rawFormulas = archRange.Formula;
                if (rawFormulas is object[,] fMat)
                {
                    for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                    {
                        string f = fMat[1, c]?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                        isFormulaCol[c] = f.StartsWith("=");
                    }
                }
                else if (rawFormulas is string singleF)
                {
                    isFormulaCol[1] = singleF.StartsWith("=");
                }
            }
            catch { }

            // 3. Bulk-read entire table body in 1 COM call
            object[,] dataGrid = TableMapper.GetRange2DValues(currentBody, curRowCount, colCount);

            var seenSignatures = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            var duplicateIndices = new List<int>();

            // 4. In-Memory Analysis (0 COM calls inside the loop)
            for (int r = 1; r <= curRowCount; r++)
            {
                // Fast check: verify row contains actual user data
                bool rowHasInput = false;
                for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                {
                    if (isFormulaCol[c]) continue;
                    string cleanVal = CleanCellText(dataGrid[r, c]);
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cleanVal) || cleanVal == "0" || cleanVal == "0.00" ||
                        cleanVal == "-" || cleanVal == "\u2014" || cleanVal.Equals("None", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        continue;
                    }
                    rowHasInput = true;
                    break;
                }

                if (!rowHasInput) continue;

                // Build unique signature using data values in memory
                var sigParts = new List<string>(colCount);
                for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                {
                    if (keyColumnIndices != null && !keyColumnIndices.Contains(c)) continue;
                    if (isFormulaCol[c]) continue; // Skip calculated columns

                    string cleanVal = CleanCellText(dataGrid[r, c]);
                    sigParts.Add(cleanVal);
                }

                string signature = string.Join("|", sigParts);
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(signature) || signature.All(ch => ch == '|'))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                int absRow = curBodyStartRow + r - 1;
                if (!seenSignatures.Add(signature))
                {
                    duplicateIndices.Add(r);
                    warnings.Add($"Table '{tableName}' Row {absRow}: Duplicate entry removed ({signature.Trim('|')}).");
                }
            }

            // 5. Delete duplicate rows from bottom up under protection bypass
            if (duplicateIndices.Count > 0)
            {
                SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)ws, new Action(() =>
                {
                    foreach (int idx in duplicateIndices.OrderByDescending(x => x))
                    {
                        table.ListRows[idx].Delete();
                    }
                }));
            }

            return duplicateIndices.Count;
        }
        finally
        {
            // 6. Restore Excel environment in correct sequence
            try { xlApp.Calculation = origCalc; } catch { }
            try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
            try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
        }
    }

    private static string CleanCellText(object? val)
    {
        if (val == null || SheetMapperHelper.IsComError(val)) return string.Empty;
        if (val is double d && Math.Abs(d - Math.Round(d)) < 0.00001 && d < 1e15)
            return ((long)Math.Round(d)).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        string s = val.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
        if (s.StartsWith("#") || s == " ") return string.Empty;
        return s;
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.TableRowManager;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Drawing.Drawing2D;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;

public static partial class TableRowManager
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Isolates and skips duplicate records while preserving and returning clean unique records.
    /// If duplicates are found, displays a warning dialog showing what was skipped.
    /// Returns the list of non-duplicate records to import (or null if the user clicks Cancel).
    /// </summary>
    public static List<T>? FilterDuplicates<T>(
        IEnumerable<T> incomingRecords,
        Func<T, string> keySelector,
        string entityLabel,
        out List<string> duplicateWarnings,
        Func<T, string>? displaySelector = null,
        IEnumerable<T>? existingRecords = null)
    {
        duplicateWarnings = new List<string>();
        if (incomingRecords == null) return new List<T>();

        var incomingList = incomingRecords.ToList();
        if (incomingList.Count == 0) return incomingList;

        displaySelector ??= keySelector;

        // 1. Build fast O(1) set of existing table signatures
        var existingKeys = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        if (existingRecords != null)
        {
            foreach (var existing in existingRecords)
            {
                if (existing == null) continue;
                string k = keySelector(existing)?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(k))
                {
                    existingKeys.Add(k);
                }
            }
        }

        // 2. Separate records in RAM
        var seenBatchKeys = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        var cleanRecords = new List<T>();
        var duplicateRecords = new List<T>();

        foreach (var item in incomingList)
        {
            if (item == null) continue;
            string key = keySelector(item)?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
            string display = displaySelector(item)?.Trim() ?? key;

            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(key))
            {
                cleanRecords.Add(item);
                continue;
            }

            bool isExistingDuplicate = existingKeys.Contains(key);
            bool isFileDuplicate = !seenBatchKeys.Add(key);

            if (isExistingDuplicate || isFileDuplicate)
            {
                duplicateRecords.Add(item);
                string reason = isExistingDuplicate
                    ? "already exists in destination table"
                    : "appears more than once in import file";

                string notice = $"[Duplicate Skipped] {entityLabel} '{display}' {reason}.";
                duplicateWarnings.Add(notice);
                AppLogger.Warn(notice);
            }
            else
            {
                cleanRecords.Add(item);
            }
        }

        // 3. If zero duplicates found, return clean records immediately
        if (duplicateRecords.Count == 0)
        {
            return cleanRecords;
        }

        // 4. If all records are duplicates, notify and return empty list
        if (cleanRecords.Count == 0)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                $"All {duplicateRecords.Count} {entityLabel} records in this file are duplicates that already exist in the system.\n\nNo new records to import.",
                $"{entityLabel} Import - All Duplicates",
                MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                MessageBoxIcon.Information
            );
            return cleanRecords;
        }

        // 5. Present notification dialog allowing user to import clean records or cancel
        using var dialog = new DuplicateSkippedSummaryDialog(
            entityLabel: entityLabel,
            duplicateCount: duplicateRecords.Count,
            cleanCount: cleanRecords.Count,
            skippedWarnings: duplicateWarnings
        );

        var result = dialog.ShowDialog();
        return result == DialogResult.OK ? cleanRecords : null;
    }
}

public class DuplicateSkippedSummaryDialog : Form
{
    public DuplicateSkippedSummaryDialog(
        string entityLabel,
        int duplicateCount,
        int cleanCount,
        List<string> skippedWarnings)
    {
        Text = $"{entityLabel} Import - Duplicates Excluded";
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterScreen;
        FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
        MaximizeBox = false;
        MinimizeBox = false;
        AutoScaleMode = AutoScaleMode.Dpi;
        AutoSize = true;
        AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink;
        Padding = new Padding(24, 20, 24, 20);
        BackColor = Color.White;

        var layout = new TableLayoutPanel
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            ColumnCount = 2,
            AutoSize = true,
            AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink
        };
        layout.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        layout.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100f));

        var pnlIndicator = new Panel
        {
            Size = new Size(38, 38),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 2, 16, 0)
        };
        pnlIndicator.Paint += (s, e) =>
        {
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            using var brush = new SolidBrush(Color.FromArgb(217, 119, 6)); // Amber Warning
            e.Graphics.FillEllipse(brush, 2, 2, 34, 34);

            using var pen = new Pen(Color.White, 2.5f);
            e.Graphics.DrawLine(pen, 19, 10, 19, 21);
            e.Graphics.FillEllipse(Brushes.White, 17.5f, 25f, 3, 3);
        };

        var pnlContent = new TableLayoutPanel
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            ColumnCount = 1,
            AutoSize = true,
            AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink
        };

        var lblTitle = new Label
        {
            Text = "Duplicates Skipped During Import",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 12f, FontStyle.Bold),
            ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(30, 41, 59),
            AutoSize = true,
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 6)
        };

        var sbMessage = new StringBuilder();
        sbMessage.AppendLine($"Duplicate entries were detected and excluded to protect existing data.");
        sbMessage.AppendLine();
        sbMessage.AppendLine($"  • Duplicate Records Excluded : {duplicateCount}");
        sbMessage.AppendLine($"  • Clean Records to Import    : {cleanCount}");
        sbMessage.AppendLine();
        sbMessage.AppendLine($"Would you like to proceed with importing the {cleanCount} clean record(s)?");

        var lblMessage = new Label
        {
            Text = sbMessage.ToString(),
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9.5f),
            ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(71, 85, 105),
            AutoSize = true,
            MaximumSize = new Size(520, 0),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 8)
        };

        var txtLog = new TextBox
        {
            Multiline = true,
            ReadOnly = true,
            ScrollBars = ScrollBars.Vertical,
            Width = 520,
            Height = 130,
            Font = new Font("Consolas", 8.8f),
            BackColor = Color.FromArgb(254, 252, 232),
            ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(146, 64, 14),
            BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 16),
            Text = string.Join(Environment.NewLine, skippedWarnings)
        };

        var flowActions = new FlowLayoutPanel
        {
            FlowDirection = FlowDirection.RightToLeft,
            AutoSize = true,
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            Margin = Padding.Empty
        };

        var btnCancel = new Button
        {
            Text = "Cancel Import",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f),
            AutoSize = true,
            Padding = new Padding(14, 6, 14, 6),
            DialogResult = DialogResult.Cancel
        };
        btnCancel.Click += (_, _) => { DialogResult = DialogResult.Cancel; Close(); };
        flowActions.Controls.Add(btnCancel);

        var btnProceed = new Button
        {
            Text = $"Import {cleanCount} Clean Record(s)",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9.5f, FontStyle.Bold),
            ForeColor = Color.White,
            BackColor = Color.FromArgb(79, 70, 229),
            FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
            AutoSize = true,
            Padding = new Padding(16, 6, 16, 6),
            DialogResult = DialogResult.OK
        };
        btnProceed.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
        btnProceed.Click += (_, _) => { DialogResult = DialogResult.OK; Close(); };
        flowActions.Controls.Add(btnProceed);

        pnlContent.Controls.Add(lblTitle);
        pnlContent.Controls.Add(lblMessage);
        pnlContent.Controls.Add(txtLog);
        pnlContent.Controls.Add(flowActions);

        layout.Controls.Add(pnlIndicator, 0, 0);
        layout.Controls.Add(pnlContent, 1, 0);
        Controls.Add(layout);

        AcceptButton = btnProceed;
        CancelButton = btnCancel;
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.TemplateEditor;

using System;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using ExcelDna.Integration.CustomUI;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.TemplateEditor.RulesAudit;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sync;

[ComVisible(true)]
public class RibbonController : ExcelRibbon
{
    private static IRibbonUI? _ribbonUi;

    public override string GetCustomUI(string ribbonId)
    {
#if DEBUG
        string debugGroupXml = @"
                <group id='grpGlobalDebug' label='Diagnostics Control'>
                  <toggleButton id='btnToggleMasterDebug'
                                label='Debug Logs Active'
                                size='large'
                                imageMso='VisualBasic'
                                getPressed='GetMasterDebugPressed'
                                onAction='OnToggleMasterDebugClicked' />
                </group>";
#else
        string debugGroupXml = string.Empty;
#endif

        return $@"
        <customUI xmlns='http://schemas.microsoft.com/office/2009/07/customui' onLoad='OnRibbonLoad'>
          <ribbon>
            <tabs>
              <tab id='tabTemplateEditor' label='Template Editor'>
                <group id='grpTemplateDesign' label='Template Design &amp; Validation'>
                  <toggleButton id='btnToggleTemplateMode'
                                label='Template Edit Mode'
                                size='large'
                                imageMso='DesignMode'
                                getPressed='GetTemplateModePressed'
                                onAction='OnToggleTemplateModeClicked' />
                  <separator id='sep1' />
                  <button id='btnFormatDropdown'
                            label='Make Dropdown Cell'
                            size='large'
                            imageMso='DataValidation'
                            onAction='OnFormatDropdownClicked' />
                  <button id='btnCopyMetadata'
                            label='Copy Config'
                            size='normal'
                            imageMso='Copy'
                            onAction='OnCopyMetadataClicked' />
                  <button id='btnPasteMetadata'
                            label='Paste Config'
                            size='normal'
                            imageMso='Paste'
                            onAction='OnPasteMetadataClicked' />
                  <separator id='sep2' />
                  <button id='btnExportRules'
                            label='Export Rules Audit'
                            size='large'
                            imageMso='ExportExcel'
                            onAction='OnExportRulesClicked' />
                  <button id='btnImportRules'
                            label='Import Rules Audit'
                            size='large'
                            imageMso='ImportExcel'
                            onAction='OnImportRulesClicked' />
                  <button id='btnPurgeTemplateData'
                            label='Purge Template Data'
                            size='large'
                            imageMso='TableDeleteRows'
                            getVisible='GetPurgeVisible'
                            onAction='OnPurgeTemplateDataClicked' />
                </group>
                <group id='grpSync' label='Core Lookups &amp; Data'>
                  <button id='btnSyncCoreEnums'
                            label='Sync All Enums'
                            size='large'
                            imageMso='Refresh'
                            onAction='OnSyncEnumsClicked' />
                  <button id='btnSyncCoreData'
                            label='Sync All Data'
                            size='large'
                            imageMso='AccessRefreshAllLists'
                            onAction='OnSyncDataClicked' />
                </group>
                {debugGroupXml}
              </tab>
            </tabs>
          </ribbon>
        </customUI>";
    }

    public void OnRibbonLoad(IRibbonUI ribbon)
    {
        _ribbonUi = ribbon;
    }

    public static void RefreshRibbonUI()
    {
        try
        {
            _ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl("btnToggleTemplateMode");
            _ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl("btnPurgeTemplateData");
#if DEBUG
            _ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl("btnToggleMasterDebug");
#endif
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[RibbonController.RefreshRibbonUI Error] {ex.Message}");
        }
    }

    public bool GetTemplateModePressed(IRibbonControl control) => SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode;
    public bool GetPurgeVisible(IRibbonControl control) => SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode;

#if DEBUG
    public bool GetMasterDebugPressed(IRibbonControl control) => DebugDialog.DebugEnabled;

    public void OnToggleMasterDebugClicked(IRibbonControl control, bool isPressed)
    {
        DebugDialog.DebugEnabled = isPressed;
        _ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl("btnToggleMasterDebug");
        MessageBox.Show(
            isPressed
                ? "Debug diagnostics are now ENABLED across all operations."
                : "Debug diagnostics are now MUTED. No popup inspection logs will appear.",
            "Debug Log Switch",
            MessageBoxButtons.OK,
            isPressed ? MessageBoxIcon.Information : MessageBoxIcon.Warning
        );
    }
#endif

    public void OnToggleTemplateModeClicked(IRibbonControl control, bool isPressed)
    {
        SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode = isPressed;
        SheetOperations.SetAuxiliarySheetsVisibility(isPressed);

        if (isPressed)
        {
            EnumList.SyncAllCoreEnums();
            DataList.SyncAllTemplateData();
        }

        RefreshRibbonUI();
    }

    public void OnFormatDropdownClicked(IRibbonControl control) => ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(Commands.ApplyDropdownStyle);
    public void OnCopyMetadataClicked(IRibbonControl control) => Commands.CopySelectedCellMetadata();
    public void OnPasteMetadataClicked(IRibbonControl control) => ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(Commands.PasteSelectedCellMetadata);
    public void OnExportRulesClicked(IRibbonControl control) => ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(RulesAuditExporter.ExportAuditReport);
    public void OnImportRulesClicked(IRibbonControl control) => ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(RulesAuditImporter.ImportAuditReport);
    public void OnPurgeTemplateDataClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        var confirm = MessageBox.Show(
            "Clear all operational data rows on this template?\n\n(Headers, styles, and the 'Lookups' and 'Data' sheets will be preserved)",
            "Template Editor - Purge Template Data",
            MessageBoxButtons.YesNo,
            MessageBoxIcon.Warning
        );

        if (confirm == DialogResult.Yes)
        {
            ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
            {
                SheetOperations.PurgeAllSheetsData();
                MessageBox.Show("All sheet data has been purged. Template is now clean.",
                                "Template Editor", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
            });
        }
    }

    public void OnSyncEnumsClicked(IRibbonControl control) => EnumList.SyncAllCoreEnums();
    public void OnSyncDataClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        DataList.SyncAllTemplateData();
        MessageBox.Show("All entity data synchronized to 'Data' sheet.",
                        "Template Editor", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
    }
}
