FILE: Core\03_RuntimeUI\TemplateEditor\RulesAudit\RulesAudit.Exporter.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.TemplateEditor.RulesAudit;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Storage;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.RuleEngine;

public static class RulesAuditExporter
{
    [ExcelCommand(Name = "ExportTemplateRulesToMarkdown")]
    public static void ExportAuditReport()
    {
        try
        {
            RuleRegistry.EnsureInitialized();
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (wb == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("No active workbook found.", "Export Rules Audit", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            string rawWbName = wb.Name?.ToString() ?? "Template";
            string baseName = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(rawWbName);
            foreach (char c in Path.GetInvalidFileNameChars())
            {
                baseName = baseName.Replace(c, '_');
            }

            string targetFileName = $"{baseName}_Rules.md";
            var sb = new StringBuilder();

            sb.AppendLine($"# {baseName} Rules Audit");
            sb.AppendLine($"**Workbook:** `{rawWbName}`");
            sb.AppendLine($"**Generated:** {DateTime.Now:dd-MM-yyyy HH:mm:ss}");
            sb.AppendLine();

            int tableCount = 0;
            int sheetHeaderCellCount = 0;

            foreach (dynamic ws in wb.Sheets)
            {
                string sheetName = ws.Name?.ToString() ?? "Sheet";

                if (sheetName.Equals("Lookups", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                    sheetName.Equals("Data", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                int minTableStartRow = int.MaxValue;
                if (ws.ListObjects.Count > 0)
                {
                    foreach (dynamic t in ws.ListObjects)
                    {
                        int hRow = (int)t.HeaderRowRange.Row;
                        if (hRow < minTableStartRow) minTableStartRow = hRow;
                    }
                }

                // 1. Scan Top Parameter Cells
                var topCells = ScanTopAnchorCells(ws, minTableStartRow);
                if (topCells.Count > 0)
                {
                    sb.AppendLine("---");
                    sb.AppendLine($"## SHEET_HEADER_CELLS: Sheet=`{sheetName}`");
                    sb.AppendLine();
                    sb.AppendLine("| CellAddress | Row | Col | Style | NumberFormat | Formula | SourceList / NativeFormula | NativeType | NativeAlert | NativeIgnoreBlank |");
                    sb.AppendLine("| :---: | :---: | :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :---: |");

                    foreach (var tc in topCells)
                    {
                        sheetHeaderCellCount++;
                        sb.AppendLine(
                            $"| {tc.Address} " +
                            $"| {tc.Row} " +
                            $"| {tc.Col} " +
                            $"| {tc.Style} " +
                            $"| {RulesAuditHelper.EscapePipe(tc.NumberFormat)} " +
                            $"| {RulesAuditHelper.EscapePipe(tc.Formula)} " +
                            $"| {RulesAuditHelper.EscapePipe(tc.SourceOrFormula)} " +
                            $"| {tc.NativeType} " +
                            $"| {tc.NativeAlert} " +
                            $"| {(tc.NativeIgnoreBlank ? "TRUE" : "FALSE")} |"
                        );
                    }
                    sb.AppendLine();
                }

                if (ws.ListObjects.Count == 0) continue;

                // 2. Scan Tables
                foreach (dynamic table in ws.ListObjects)
                {
                    tableCount++;
                    string tableName = table.Name?.ToString() ?? "Table";
                    dynamic headerRange = table.HeaderRowRange;
                    int headerRow = (int)headerRange.Row;
                    int startCol = (int)headerRange.Column;
                    int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
                    int archetypeRow = headerRow + 1;

                    sb.AppendLine("---");
                    sb.AppendLine($"## TABLE: Sheet=`{sheetName}` | Table=`{tableName}` | HeaderRow={headerRow} | ArchetypeRow={archetypeRow} | StartCol={startCol} | Columns={colCount}");
                    sb.AppendLine();
                    sb.AppendLine("### Column Rules");
                    sb.AppendLine("| Col | Row | Address | Header | Style | NumberFormat | EngineType | InCellFormula | Sources | Syncs | Transform | DefaultRule | StateGuards | IsMulti | IsWarning | NativeType | NativeFormula | NativeAlert | NativeIgnoreBlank |");
                    sb.AppendLine("| :---: | :---: | :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- | :--- | :---: | :---: |");

                    for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                    {
                        int absCol = startCol + c - 1;
                        dynamic headerCell = ws.Cells[headerRow, absCol];
                        dynamic archetypeCell = ws.Cells[archetypeRow, absCol];

                        string headerText = headerCell.Value2?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? $"Col_{c}";
                        string colLetter = RulesAuditHelper.GetExcelColumnLetter(absCol);
                        string cellAddress = $"{colLetter}{archetypeRow}";
                        string styleName = RulesAuditHelper.GetStyleName(archetypeCell);
                        string numFormat = RulesAuditHelper.GetCellNumberFormat(archetypeCell);

                        string cellFormula = archetypeCell.Formula2?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cellFormula))
                            cellFormula = archetypeCell.Formula?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;

                        // Identify C# Attribute-Driven Rule
                        var csharpRule = RuleRegistry.GetRule(tableName, headerText);
                        bool hasCSharp = csharpRule != null;

                        var cfg = Metadata.ReadConfig(archetypeCell);
                        bool isCustomDropdown = !cfg.IsEmpty;

                        bool hasNativeValidation = false;
                        string nativeType = string.Empty;
                        string nativeFormula = string.Empty;
                        string nativeAlert = string.Empty;
                        bool nativeIgnoreBlank = true;

                        try
                        {
                            dynamic val = archetypeCell.Validation;
                            if (val != null)
                            {
                                int vt = (int)val.Type;
                                hasNativeValidation = true;
                                nativeType = RulesAuditHelper.GetValidationTypeName(vt);
                                nativeFormula = val.Formula1?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                                nativeAlert = ((int)val.AlertStyle) switch { 1 => "Stop", 2 => "Warning", _ => "Info" };
                                nativeIgnoreBlank = (bool)val.IgnoreBlank;
                            }
                        }
                        catch { }

                        string engineType = hasCSharp ? "CSharpAttribute"
                                          : isCustomDropdown ? "LegacyDropdown"
                                          : hasNativeValidation ? "NativeValidation"
                                          : (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cellFormula) && cellFormula.StartsWith("=")) ? "Formula"
                                          : "StandardInput";

                        // Populate payload either from C# Attributes or Legacy Metadata
                        string sources = hasCSharp ? string.Join(", ", csharpRule!.Sources) : cfg.Source;
                        string syncs = hasCSharp ? string.Join(" ; ", csharpRule!.SyncRules.Select(s => $"{s.TargetHeader}<-{s.LookupSource}")) : cfg.SyncLeft;
                        string transform = hasCSharp ? csharpRule!.Transform.ToString() : cfg.Transform;
                        string defaultRule = hasCSharp && csharpRule!.DefaultRule != null ? csharpRule.DefaultRule.Kind.ToString() : cfg.Filter;
                        string guards = hasCSharp && csharpRule!.StateGuards.Count > 0 ? $"{csharpRule.StateGuards.Count} Guard(s)" : "";
                        bool isMulti = hasCSharp ? csharpRule!.IsMultiSelect : cfg.IsMultiSelect;
                        bool isWarning = hasCSharp ? csharpRule!.IsWarningOnly : cfg.IsWarningOnly;

                        sb.AppendLine(
                            $"| {colLetter} " +
                            $"| {archetypeRow} " +
                            $"| {cellAddress} " +
                            $"| {RulesAuditHelper.EscapePipe(headerText)} " +
                            $"| {styleName} " +
                            $"| {RulesAuditHelper.EscapePipe(numFormat)} " +
                            $"| {engineType} " +
                            $"| {RulesAuditHelper.EscapePipe(cellFormula)} " +
                            $"| {RulesAuditHelper.EscapePipe(sources)} " +
                            $"| {RulesAuditHelper.EscapePipe(syncs)} " +
                            $"| {transform} " +
                            $"| {RulesAuditHelper.EscapePipe(defaultRule)} " +
                            $"| {guards} " +
                            $"| {(isMulti ? "TRUE" : "FALSE")} " +
                            $"| {(isWarning ? "TRUE" : "FALSE")} " +
                            $"| {nativeType} " +
                            $"| {RulesAuditHelper.EscapePipe(nativeFormula)} " +
                            $"| {nativeAlert} " +
                            $"| {(nativeIgnoreBlank ? "TRUE" : "FALSE")} |"
                        );
                    }
                    sb.AppendLine();

                    // 3. Scan Conditional Formatting
                    try
                    {
                        dynamic bodyRange = table.DataBodyRange;
                        dynamic targetRange = bodyRange ?? ws.Range[ws.Cells[archetypeRow, startCol], ws.Cells[archetypeRow + 500, startCol + colCount - 1]];
                        dynamic formatConditions = targetRange.FormatConditions;
                        int cfCount = (int)formatConditions.Count;

                        if (cfCount > 0)
                        {
                            sb.AppendLine("### Conditional Formatting");
                            sb.AppendLine("| AppliesTo | RuleType | Operator | Formula1 | Formula2 | Severity | StopIfTrue |");
                            sb.AppendLine("| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :---: | :---: |");

                            for (int i = 1; i <= cfCount; i++)
                            {
                                dynamic fc = formatConditions[i];
                                int cfType = (int)fc.Type;
                                string ruleType = cfType == 2 ? "Expression" : cfType == 1 ? "CellValue" : $"Type_{cfType}";

                                string op = string.Empty;
                                try { op = ((int)fc.Operator).ToString(); } catch { }

                                string f1 = string.Empty;
                                try { f1 = fc.Formula1?.ToString() ?? string.Empty; } catch { }

                                string f2 = string.Empty;
                                try { f2 = fc.Formula2?.ToString() ?? string.Empty; } catch { }

                                string severity = "None";
                                try
                                {
                                    int bgOle = Convert.ToInt32(fc.Interior.Color);
                                    int r = bgOle & 0xFF;
                                    int g = (bgOle >> 8) & 0xFF;
                                    int b = (bgOle >> 16) & 0xFF;
                                    if ((r > 200 && g < 150 && b < 150) || (r > 230 && g > 200 && b > 200 && r > g && r > b))
                                        severity = "Error";
                                    else if (r > 200 && g > 180 && b < 180)
                                        severity = "Warning";
                                }
                                catch { }

                                string appliesTo = string.Empty;
                                try { appliesTo = fc.AppliesTo?.Address?.ToString() ?? string.Empty; } catch { }

                                bool stopIfTrue = false;
                                try { stopIfTrue = (bool)fc.StopIfTrue; } catch { }

                                sb.AppendLine(
                                    $"| {RulesAuditHelper.EscapePipe(appliesTo)} " +
                                    $"| {ruleType} " +
                                    $"| {op} " +
                                    $"| {RulesAuditHelper.EscapePipe(f1)} " +
                                    $"| {RulesAuditHelper.EscapePipe(f2)} " +
                                    $"| {severity} " +
                                    $"| {(stopIfTrue ? "TRUE" : "FALSE")} |"
                                );
                            }
                            sb.AppendLine();
                        }
                    }
                    catch { }
                }
            }

            if (tableCount == 0 && sheetHeaderCellCount == 0)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("No ListObjects or configured template cells found.", "Export Rules", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                return;
            }

            string exportPath = FolderRegistry.Data.GetFilePath(targetFileName);
            FolderRegistry.Data.EnsureExists();
            File.WriteAllText(exportPath, sb.ToString(), Encoding.UTF8);
            Clipboard.SetText(sb.ToString());
            MessageBox.Show(
                $"Audit export complete!\n\n  Tables: {tableCount}\n  Header/Date Parameter Cells: {sheetHeaderCellCount}\n  Saved file: {exportPath}\n  Copied to Clipboard.",
                "Export Complete", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information
            );
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show($"Failed to export audit report:\n{ex.Message}", "Export Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
        }
    }

    private static List<RulesAuditHelper.HeaderCellInfo> ScanTopAnchorCells(dynamic ws, int maxRowLimit)
    {
        var list = new List<RulesAuditHelper.HeaderCellInfo>();
        try
        {
            int scanRows = Math.Min(3, maxRowLimit - 1);
            if (scanRows < 1) return list;

            for (int r = 1; r <= scanRows; r++)
            {
                for (int c = 1; c <= 7; c++)
                {
                    dynamic cell = ws.Cells[r, c];
                    string sName = RulesAuditHelper.GetStyleName(cell);
                    string numFormat = RulesAuditHelper.GetCellNumberFormat(cell);
                    string formula = cell.Formula?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;

                    bool isSpecialStyle = sName.Equals("DisplayDate", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                          sName.Equals("DisplayRange", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

                    bool hasVal = false;
                    string valType = string.Empty;
                    string f1 = string.Empty;
                    string alert = string.Empty;
                    bool ignoreBlank = true;

                    try
                    {
                        dynamic v = cell.Validation;
                        if (v != null)
                        {
                            int vt = (int)v.Type;
                            hasVal = true;
                            valType = RulesAuditHelper.GetValidationTypeName(vt);
                            f1 = v.Formula1?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                            alert = ((int)v.AlertStyle) == 2 ? "Warning" : "Stop";
                            ignoreBlank = (bool)v.IgnoreBlank;
                        }
                    }
                    catch { }

                    if (isSpecialStyle || hasVal || (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(formula) && formula.StartsWith("=")))
                    {
                        list.Add(new RulesAuditHelper.HeaderCellInfo
                        {
                            Address = cell.Address?.ToString() ?? $"R{r}C{c}",
                            Row = r,
                            Col = c,
                            Style = sName,
                            NumberFormat = numFormat,
                            Formula = formula,
                            SourceOrFormula = f1,
                            NativeType = valType,
                            NativeAlert = alert,
                            NativeIgnoreBlank = ignoreBlank
                        });
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        catch { }
        return list;
    }
}
`n
============================================================
FILE: Core\03_RuntimeUI\TemplateEditor\RulesAudit\RulesAudit.Helper.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.TemplateEditor.RulesAudit;

using System;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

public static class RulesAuditHelper
{
    public class HeaderCellInfo
    {
        public string Address { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public int Row { get; set; }
        public int Col { get; set; }
        public string Style { get; set; } = "Normal";
        public string NumberFormat { get; set; } = "@";
        public string Formula { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string SourceOrFormula { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string NativeType { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string NativeAlert { get; set; } = "Stop";
        public bool NativeIgnoreBlank { get; set; } = true;
    }

    public static dynamic? ResolveSheetSafely(dynamic wb, string rawName)
    {
        if (wb == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rawName)) return null;
        string cleanName = rawName.Trim().Trim('`', '"', '\'');

        try
        {
            dynamic s = wb.Sheets[cleanName];
            if (s != null) return s;
        }
        catch { }

        try
        {
            foreach (dynamic ws in wb.Sheets)
            {
                string sName = ws.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                if (string.Equals(sName.Trim(), cleanName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    return ws;
            }
        }
        catch { }

        try
        {
            string cleanTarget = Regex.Replace(cleanName, @"[\s_\-]+", "").ToLowerInvariant();
            foreach (dynamic ws in wb.Sheets)
            {
                string sName = ws.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                string cleanSheet = Regex.Replace(sName, @"[\s_\-]+", "").ToLowerInvariant();
                if (cleanSheet.Equals(cleanTarget, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    return ws;
                if (cleanSheet.TrimEnd('s').Equals(cleanTarget.TrimEnd('s'), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    return ws;
            }
        }
        catch { }

        return null;
    }

    public static string GetStyleName(dynamic cell)
    {
        try { return cell.Style?.Name?.ToString() ?? "Normal"; }
        catch { return "Normal"; }
    }

    public static string GetCellNumberFormat(dynamic cell)
    {
        try
        {
            string fmt = cell.NumberFormat?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fmt) || fmt.Equals("General", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return "General";
            return fmt;
        }
        catch
        {
            return "General";
        }
    }

    public static string ExtractHeaderVal(string line, string key)
    {
        var m = Regex.Match(line, $@"{key}=`?([^`|\r\n]+?)`?(?:\s*\||\s*$)");
        return m.Success ? m.Groups[1].Value.Trim().Trim('`', '\'') : string.Empty;
    }

    public static string EscapePipe(string input) =>
        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input) ? " " : input.Replace("|", "\\|").Replace("\r", "").Replace("\n", " ");

    public static string UnescapePipe(string input) =>
        input.Equals(" ") ? string.Empty : input.Replace("\\|", "|");

    public static string GetExcelColumnLetter(int colNumber)
    {
        string letter = string.Empty;
        while (colNumber > 0)
        {
            int rem = (colNumber - 1) % 26;
            letter = (char)('A' + rem) + letter;
            colNumber = (colNumber - rem) / 26;
        }
        return letter;
    }

    public static int ColumnLetterToIndex(string colLetter)
    {
        int sum = 0;
        foreach (char c in colLetter.ToUpperInvariant())
        {
            sum *= 26;
            sum += c - 'A' + 1;
        }
        return sum;
    }

    public static string GetValidationTypeName(int valType) => valType switch
    {
        1 => "WholeNumber",
        2 => "Decimal",
        3 => "List",
        4 => "Date",
        5 => "Time",
        6 => "TextLength",
        7 => "CustomFormula",
        _ => "Unknown"
    };

    public static int GetValidationTypeCode(string valType) => valType switch
    {
        "WholeNumber" => 1,
        "Decimal" => 2,
        "List" => 3,
        "Date" => 4,
        "Time" => 5,
        "TextLength" => 6,
        "CustomFormula" => 7,
        _ => 3
    };
}

FILE: Core\03_RuntimeUI\TemplateEditor\RulesAudit\RulesAudit.Importer.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.TemplateEditor.RulesAudit;

using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Storage;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using System.Windows.Forms;

public static class RulesAuditImporter
{
    [ExcelCommand(Name = "ImportTemplateRulesFromMarkdown")]
    public static void ImportAuditReport()
    {
        var diag = new StringBuilder();
        diag.AppendLine("=== RULES AUDIT IMPORT DIAGNOSTIC LOG ===");
        diag.AppendLine($"Started at: {DateTime.Now:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss}");
        diag.AppendLine();

        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (wb == null)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("No active workbook found.", "Import Rules Audit", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            string dataDir = FolderRegistry.Data.EnsureExists();
            using var openFileDialog = new OpenFileDialog
            {
                Title = "Select Rules Audit Markdown File to Import",
                InitialDirectory = dataDir,
                Filter = "Markdown Files (*.md)|*.md|Text Files (*.txt)|*.txt|All Files (*.*)|*.*",
                FilterIndex = 1,
                CheckFileExists = true,
                Multiselect = false,
                RestoreDirectory = true
            };

            if (openFileDialog.ShowDialog() != DialogResult.OK) return;
            string selectedFilePath = openFileDialog.FileName;
            diag.AppendLine($"Source File: {selectedFilePath}");

            string markdownContent = File.ReadAllText(selectedFilePath, Encoding.UTF8);
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(markdownContent))
            {
                MessageBox.Show("The selected file is empty.", "Import Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            Commands.EnsureNamedStyleExists(xlApp, Commands.DropDownStyleName);

            var lines = markdownContent.Split(new[] { "\r\n", "\r", "\n" }, StringSplitOptions.None);
            string currentSheet = string.Empty;
            dynamic? currentWs = null;
            int archetypeRow = 3;

            bool readingHeaderCells = false;
            bool readingCols = false;
            bool readingCf = false;

            var headerColMap = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            var tableColMap = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            var cfColMap = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            int appliedHeaderCells = 0;
            int appliedRulesCount = 0;
            int appliedCfCount = 0;
            var clearedSheetsCf = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            bool origAlerts = true;
            try { origAlerts = (bool)xlApp.DisplayAlerts; xlApp.DisplayAlerts = false; } catch { }

            try
            {
                foreach (var rawLine in lines)
                {
                    string line = rawLine.Replace(@"\vert{}", "|").Trim();
                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(line)) continue;

                    if (line.StartsWith("## SHEET_HEADER_CELLS:"))
                    {
                        currentSheet = RulesAuditHelper.ExtractHeaderVal(line, "Sheet");
                        currentWs = RulesAuditHelper.ResolveSheetSafely(wb, currentSheet);
                        if (currentWs != null) { try { currentWs.Activate(); } catch { } }

                        readingHeaderCells = true;
                        readingCols = false;
                        readingCf = false;
                        headerColMap.Clear();
                        diag.AppendLine($"\n--- SECTION: SHEET_HEADER_CELLS on [{currentSheet}] ---");
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (line.StartsWith("## TABLE:"))
                    {
                        currentSheet = RulesAuditHelper.ExtractHeaderVal(line, "Sheet");
                        currentWs = RulesAuditHelper.ResolveSheetSafely(wb, currentSheet);
                        if (currentWs != null) { try { currentWs.Activate(); } catch { } }

                        int.TryParse(RulesAuditHelper.ExtractHeaderVal(line, "ArchetypeRow"), out archetypeRow);
                        readingHeaderCells = false;
                        readingCols = false;
                        readingCf = false;
                        tableColMap.Clear();
                        diag.AppendLine($"\n--- SECTION: TABLE on [{currentSheet}], ArchetypeRow={archetypeRow} ---");
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (line.StartsWith("### Column Rules"))
                    {
                        readingCols = true;
                        readingHeaderCells = false;
                        readingCf = false;
                        tableColMap.Clear();
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (line.StartsWith("### Conditional Formatting"))
                    {
                        readingCf = true;
                        readingHeaderCells = false;
                        readingCols = false;
                        cfColMap.Clear();
                        continue;
                    }

                    if (readingHeaderCells && line.StartsWith("|") && line.IndexOf("CellAddress", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                    {
                        headerColMap = BuildHeaderMap(line);
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (readingCols && line.StartsWith("|") && line.IndexOf("Header", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0 && line.IndexOf("EngineType", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                    {
                        tableColMap = BuildHeaderMap(line);
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (readingCf && line.StartsWith("|") && line.IndexOf("AppliesTo", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                    {
                        cfColMap = BuildHeaderMap(line);
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (line.StartsWith("| :---") || line.StartsWith("| Col |") || line.StartsWith("| CellAddress |") || line.StartsWith("| AppliesTo |"))
                    {
                        continue;
                    }

                    var rawTokens = line.Split('|');
                    var cells = rawTokens.Select(c => RulesAuditHelper.UnescapePipe(c.Trim())).ToArray();
                    if (currentWs == null) continue;

                    // 1. Process Sheet Header Parameter Cells
                    if (readingHeaderCells && line.StartsWith("|") && cells.Length >= 5)
                    {
                        string cellAddress = GetField(cells, headerColMap, "CellAddress", 1);
                        int row = int.TryParse(GetField(cells, headerColMap, "Row", 2), out int r) ? r : 1;
                        int col = int.TryParse(GetField(cells, headerColMap, "Col", 3), out int cl) ? cl : RulesAuditHelper.ColumnLetterToIndex(Regex.Replace(cellAddress, @"[^\w]", ""));
                        string styleName = GetField(cells, headerColMap, "Style", 4);
                        string numFormat = GetField(cells, headerColMap, "NumberFormat", 5);
                        string inCellFormula = GetField(cells, headerColMap, "Formula", 6);
                        string sourceOrNative = GetField(cells, headerColMap, "SourceList / NativeFormula", 7);
                        string nativeType = GetField(cells, headerColMap, "NativeType", 8);
                        string nativeAlert = GetField(cells, headerColMap, "NativeAlert", 9);
                        bool nativeIgnoreBlank = GetField(cells, headerColMap, "NativeIgnoreBlank", 10).Equals("TRUE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

                        try
                        {
                            dynamic targetCell = currentWs.Cells[row, col];
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(styleName) && !styleName.Equals("Normal", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            {
                                Commands.EnsureNamedStyleExists(xlApp, styleName);
                                targetCell.Style = styleName;
                            }
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(inCellFormula) && inCellFormula.StartsWith("="))
                            {
                                try { targetCell.Formula2 = inCellFormula; }
                                catch { targetCell.Formula = inCellFormula; }
                            }
                            ApplyNumberFormatSafely(targetCell, numFormat);

                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(nativeType) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sourceOrNative))
                            {
                                ApplyNativeValidationSafely(wb, currentWs, targetCell, nativeType, nativeAlert, sourceOrNative, nativeIgnoreBlank, diag, cellAddress);
                            }
                            appliedHeaderCells++;
                        }
                        catch (Exception exHdr)
                        {
                            diag.AppendLine($"  -> Header Cell ERROR on {cellAddress}: {exHdr.Message}");
                        }
                    }

                    // 2. Process Table Column Rules
                    if (readingCols && line.StartsWith("|") && cells.Length >= 10)
                    {
                        string colLetter = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "Col", 1);
                        int explicitRow = int.TryParse(GetField(cells, tableColMap, "Row", 2), out int rNum) ? rNum : archetypeRow;
                        string styleName = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "Style", 4);
                        string numFormat = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "NumberFormat", 5);
                        string engineType = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "EngineType", 6);
                        string inCellFormula = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "InCellFormula", 7);
                        string sourceList = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "Sources", 8);
                        string syncFormula = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "Syncs", 9);
                        string transformExpr = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "Transform", 10);
                        string defaultRule = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "DefaultRule", 11);
                        bool isMulti = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "IsMulti", 13).Equals("TRUE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                        bool isWarning = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "IsWarning", 14).Equals("TRUE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                        string nativeType = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "NativeType", 15);
                        string nativeFormula = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "NativeFormula", 16);
                        string nativeAlert = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "NativeAlert", 17);
                        bool nativeIgnoreBlank = GetField(cells, tableColMap, "NativeIgnoreBlank", 18).Equals("TRUE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

                        try
                        {
                            int colIndex = RulesAuditHelper.ColumnLetterToIndex(colLetter);
                            dynamic archetypeCell = currentWs.Cells[explicitRow, colIndex];

                            try { archetypeCell.Validation.Delete(); } catch { }

                            // If this is a C# Attribute column, avoid injecting legacy metadata.
                            if (engineType.Equals("CSharpAttribute", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            {
                                Metadata.PurgeConfigIfPresent(archetypeCell);
                            }
                            else if (engineType.Equals("LegacyDropdown", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sourceList))
                            {
                                Commands.EnsureNamedStyleExists(xlApp, Commands.DropDownStyleName);
                                archetypeCell.Style = Commands.DropDownStyleName;
                                var cfg = new Config
                                {
                                    Source = sourceList,
                                    SyncLeft = syncFormula,
                                    Filter = defaultRule,
                                    Transform = transformExpr,
                                    IsMultiSelect = isMulti,
                                    IsWarningOnly = isWarning
                                };
                                Metadata.WriteConfig(archetypeCell, cfg);
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                Metadata.PurgeConfigIfPresent(archetypeCell);
                            }

                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(inCellFormula) && inCellFormula.StartsWith("="))
                            {
                                try { archetypeCell.Formula2 = inCellFormula; }
                                catch { archetypeCell.Formula = inCellFormula; }
                            }

                            ApplyNumberFormatSafely(currentWs.Columns[colIndex], numFormat);
                            ApplyNumberFormatSafely(archetypeCell, numFormat);

                            if (engineType.Equals("NativeValidation", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(nativeFormula))
                            {
                                ApplyNativeValidationSafely(wb, currentWs, archetypeCell, nativeType, nativeAlert, nativeFormula, nativeIgnoreBlank, diag, $"{colLetter}{explicitRow}");
                            }

                            appliedRulesCount++;
                        }
                        catch (Exception exCol)
                        {
                            diag.AppendLine($"  -> Col Error on {colLetter}{explicitRow}: {exCol.Message}");
                        }
                    }

                    // 3. Process Conditional Formatting
                    if (readingCf && line.StartsWith("|") && cells.Length >= 5)
                    {
                        string appliesTo = GetField(cells, cfColMap, "AppliesTo", 1);
                        string ruleType = GetField(cells, cfColMap, "RuleType", 2);
                        string op = GetField(cells, cfColMap, "Operator", 3);
                        string f1 = GetField(cells, cfColMap, "Formula1", 4);
                        string f2 = GetField(cells, cfColMap, "Formula2", 5);
                        string severity = GetField(cells, cfColMap, "Severity", 6);
                        bool stopIfTrue = GetField(cells, cfColMap, "StopIfTrue", 7).Equals("TRUE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(appliesTo) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(f1)) continue;

                        try
                        {
                            if (!clearedSheetsCf.Contains(currentSheet))
                            {
                                try { currentWs.Cells.FormatConditions.Delete(); } catch { }
                                clearedSheetsCf.Add(currentSheet);
                            }

                            dynamic targetRange = currentWs.Range[appliesTo];
                            int xlCfType = ruleType.Equals("CellValue", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? 1 : 2;
                            dynamic newRule = null;

                            if (xlCfType == 2)
                            {
                                newRule = targetRange.FormatConditions.Add(Type: 2, Formula1: f1);
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                int opCode = int.TryParse(op, out int o) ? o : 1;
                                newRule = targetRange.FormatConditions.Add(Type: 1, Operator: opCode, Formula1: f1, Formula2: f2);
                            }

                            if (newRule != null)
                            {
                                if (severity.Equals("Error", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                {
                                    newRule.Interior.Color = ColorTranslator.ToOle(Color.FromArgb(254, 226, 226));
                                }
                                else if (severity.Equals("Warning", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                                {
                                    newRule.Interior.Color = ColorTranslator.ToOle(Color.FromArgb(254, 243, 199));
                                }
                                newRule.StopIfTrue = stopIfTrue;
                                appliedCfCount++;
                            }
                        }
                        catch (Exception exCf)
                        {
                            diag.AppendLine($"  -> CF Error on {appliesTo}: {exCf.Message}");
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                try { xlApp.DisplayAlerts = origAlerts; } catch { }
            }

            diag.AppendLine();
            diag.AppendLine($"Summary: Parameter Cells={appliedHeaderCells}, Column Rules={appliedRulesCount}, CF Rules={appliedCfCount}");
            DebugDialog.ShowLog("Import Diagnostics Log", diag.ToString());

            MessageBox.Show(
                $"Import completed successfully from:\n{Path.GetFileName(selectedFilePath)}\n\n" +
                $"  Configured Parameter Cells : {appliedHeaderCells}\n" +
                $"  Applied Table Column Rules : {appliedRulesCount}\n" +
                $"  Applied Conditional Rules  : {appliedCfCount}\n\n" +
                "All rules, validations, formulas, and visual warnings are active.",
                "Import Complete",
                MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                MessageBoxIcon.Information
            );
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show($"Failed to import rules audit:\n{ex.Message}", "Import Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
        }
    }

    private static Dictionary<string, int> BuildHeaderMap(string headerLine)
    {
        var map = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        var tokens = headerLine.Split('|');
        for (int i = 0; i < tokens.Length; i++)
        {
            string clean = tokens[i].Trim();
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(clean) && !clean.StartsWith(":") && !clean.StartsWith("-"))
            {
                map[clean] = i;
            }
        }
        return map;
    }

    private static string GetField(string[] cells, Dictionary<string, int> map, string headerName, int fallbackIndex)
    {
        if (map.TryGetValue(headerName, out int index) && index >= 0 && index < cells.Length)
        {
            return cells[index]?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
        }
        return SafeGet(cells, fallbackIndex);
    }

    private static void ApplyNumberFormatSafely(dynamic rangeObj, string? explicitFormat)
    {
        try
        {
            string fmt = (explicitFormat ?? string.Empty).Trim();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fmt))
            {
                fmt = "General";
            }
            string currentFmt = rangeObj.NumberFormat?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            if (!string.Equals(currentFmt, fmt, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                rangeObj.NumberFormatLocal = fmt;
                rangeObj.NumberFormat = fmt;
            }
        }
        catch { }
    }

    private static string SafeGet(string[] arr, int index)
    {
        if (index < 0 || index >= arr.Length) return string.Empty;
        return arr[index] ?? string.Empty;
    }

    private static void ApplyNativeValidationSafely(
        dynamic wb,
        dynamic ws,
        dynamic cell,
        string nativeType,
        string nativeAlert,
        string formula1,
        bool ignoreBlank,
        StringBuilder diag,
        string cellLabel)
    {
        try
        {
            try { ws.Activate(); } catch { }
            try { cell.Select(); } catch { }
            try { cell.Validation.Delete(); } catch { }

            int xlValType = RulesAuditHelper.GetValidationTypeCode(nativeType);
            int xlAlert = string.Equals(nativeAlert, "Warning", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ? 2 : 1;
            string raw = (formula1 ?? string.Empty).Trim();

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(raw)) return;
            string unequals = raw.TrimStart('=').Trim();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(unequals)) return;

            if (xlValType == 3) // List
            {
                string listFormula = raw.StartsWith("=") ? ("=" + unequals) : raw;
                if (raw.StartsWith("="))
                {
                    bool exists = false;
                    try
                    {
                        dynamic nm = wb.Names.Item(unequals);
                        if (nm != null) exists = true;
                    }
                    catch { exists = false; }
                    if (!exists)
                    {
                        diag.AppendLine($"  [Validation Notice] {cellLabel}: Skipped List because Name '{unequals}' does not exist in Name Manager.");
                        return;
                    }
                }
                cell.Validation.Add(Type: 3, AlertStyle: xlAlert, Formula1: listFormula);
            }
            else if (xlValType == 7) // CustomFormula
            {
                cell.Validation.Add(Type: 7, AlertStyle: xlAlert, Formula1: "=" + unequals);
            }
            else if (xlValType == 1 || xlValType == 2) // WholeNumber, Decimal
            {
                string numStr = double.TryParse(unequals, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double dVal)
                     ? (dVal == 0 ? "0" : dVal.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture))
                     : "0";
                int op = (numStr == "0") ? 7 : 5;
                cell.Validation.Add(Type: xlValType, AlertStyle: xlAlert, Operator: op, Formula1: numStr);
            }
            else if (xlValType == 4) // Date
            {
                string dateStr;
                if (DateTime.TryParseExact(unequals, "yyyy-MM-dd", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DateTimeStyles.None, out DateTime dtIso))
                {
                    dateStr = $"=DATE({dtIso.Year},{dtIso.Month},{dtIso.Day})";
                }
                else if (raw.StartsWith("="))
                {
                    dateStr = "=" + unequals;
                }
                else
                {
                    dateStr = raw;
                }
                cell.Validation.Add(Type: 4, AlertStyle: xlAlert, Operator: 5, Formula1: dateStr);
            }
            else
            {
                cell.Validation.Add(Type: xlValType, AlertStyle: xlAlert, Formula1: raw);
            }

            cell.Validation.IgnoreBlank = ignoreBlank;
        }
        catch (Exception exVal)
        {
            diag.AppendLine($"  [Validation Exception] {cellLabel} (Type={nativeType}, Formula={formula1}): {exVal.Message}");
        }
    }
}

FILE: Core\04_WorkbookSave\Entry\Save.Contract.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Entry;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class SaveContract<T>
{
    public string ModuleName { get; set; } = "Manager";
    public string ServerTableName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    public Func<(T? Data, List<string> Errors, List<string> Warnings)> ExtractAndValidate { get; set; } = null!;
    public Predicate<T?> IsDatasetEmpty { get; set; } = data => data == null;
    public Func<T, (bool Success, string ErrorMessage)> SaveToServer { get; set; } = null!;

    public Action<T>? PopulateWorksheet { get; set; }
    public Func<T?>? FetchFromServer { get; set; }
    public Action<bool>? SyncLookups { get; set; }
    public Predicate<T>? ValidateOfflinePayload { get; set; }
    public Func<T, T, string>? FormatConflictSummary { get; set; }
}

FILE: Core\04_WorkbookSave\Entry\Save.Entry.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Entry;

using System;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Storage;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Validation;

public static class SaveEntry
{
    public static void Save<T>(SaveContract<T> contract)
    {
        if (SheetOperations.IsMasterTemplateFile() || SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode) return;

        dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
        bool origUpdating = true;
        try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; } catch { }

        try
        {
            // 1. FREEZE ENTIRE SCREEN BEFORE ANY EXTRACTION OR POPULATION
            try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }

            while (true)
            {
                var (data, validationErrors, validationWarnings) = contract.ExtractAndValidate();
                if (validationErrors.Count > 0 || validationWarnings.Count > 0)
                {
                    using var valDialog = new SummaryDialog(contract.ModuleName, validationErrors, validationWarnings);
                    valDialog.ShowDialog();
                    if (validationErrors.Count > 0 || valDialog.Action != ValidationDialogAction.ProceedAnyway)
                    {
                        return;
                    }
                }
#if DEBUG
                using var debugPrompt = new ExitConflictDialog(
                    contract.ModuleName,
                    string.Empty,
                    allowSaveOffline: true,
                    isClosing: false,
                    isDebugPrompt: true
                );
                debugPrompt.ShowDialog();
                switch (debugPrompt.Resolution)
                {
                    case ExitResolution.Retry:
                        var (dbSuccess, dbErr) = contract.SaveToServer(data!);
                        if (dbSuccess)
                        {
                            OfflineSnapshot.ClearPendingSnapshot<T>();
                            contract.PopulateWorksheet?.Invoke(data!);
                            try { ((dynamic)ExcelDnaUtil.Application).ActiveWorkbook.Saved = true; } catch { }
                            SaveNotificationDialog.ShowSuccess(contract.ModuleName, "Debug: Data committed to PostgreSQL server successfully.");
                            return;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            MessageBox.Show($"Server Save Failed:\n\n{dbErr}", "Debug Server Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                            continue;
                        }
                    case ExitResolution.SaveLocallyAndQuit:
                        OfflineSnapshot.SaveOfflineSnapshot(data);
                        contract.PopulateWorksheet?.Invoke(data!);
                        try { ((dynamic)ExcelDnaUtil.Application).ActiveWorkbook.Saved = true; } catch { }
                        SaveNotificationDialog.ShowOffline(contract.ModuleName, "Debug: Records stashed to offline JSON storage.");
                        return;
                    case ExitResolution.QuitAnyway:
                    case ExitResolution.Cancel:
                    default:
                        return;
                }
#else
                if (data == null || contract.IsDatasetEmpty(data))
                {
                    SaveNotificationDialog.ShowSuccess(contract.ModuleName, "The worksheet contains no records to save.");
                    return;
                }

                var (success, dbErrRelease) = contract.SaveToServer(data);
                if (success)
                {
                    OfflineSnapshot.ClearPendingSnapshot<T>();
                    contract.PopulateWorksheet?.Invoke(data);
                    try { ((dynamic)ExcelDnaUtil.Application).ActiveWorkbook.Saved = true; } catch { }
                    SaveNotificationDialog.ShowSuccess(contract.ModuleName, "Save completed successfully. All records persisted to database.");
                    return;
                }

                using var conflictDialog = new ExitConflictDialog(
                    contract.ModuleName,
                    string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dbErrRelease) ? "Could not establish a connection to PostgreSQL." : dbErrRelease,
                    allowSaveOffline: true,
                    isClosing: false,
                    isDebugPrompt: false
                );
                conflictDialog.ShowDialog();
                switch (conflictDialog.Resolution)
                {
                    case ExitResolution.Retry:
                        continue;
                    case ExitResolution.SaveLocallyAndQuit:
                        OfflineSnapshot.SaveOfflineSnapshot(data);
                        contract.PopulateWorksheet?.Invoke(data);
                        try { ((dynamic)ExcelDnaUtil.Application).ActiveWorkbook.Saved = true; } catch { }
                        SaveNotificationDialog.ShowOffline(contract.ModuleName, "Saved records to local offline storage.");
                        return;
                    case ExitResolution.Cancel:
                    default:
                        return;
                }
#endif
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            // 2. UNFREEZE SCREEN ONLY AT THE VERY END
            try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
        }
    }

    public static void InterceptClose<T>(
        dynamic wb,
        ref bool cancel,
        SaveContract<T> contract,
        ref bool isExiting,
        Action unEventHooks)
    {
        if (isExiting)
        {
            cancel = false;
            return;
        }
        if (SheetOperations.IsMasterTemplateFile() || SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode) return;

        // 1. Immediately hide any floating dropdown widgets to prevent Z-order fighting
        SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown.Arrow.HideAll();

        dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
        bool origUpdating = true;
        bool origEvents = true;
        try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; } catch { }
        try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; } catch { }
        dynamic? activeSheetBefore = null;
        try { activeSheetBefore = xlApp.ActiveSheet; } catch { }

        try
        {
            while (true)
            {
                // 2. Freeze screen updating and event processing during multi-sheet validation
                try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }
                try { xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }

                var (data, validationErrors, validationWarnings) = contract.ExtractAndValidate();

                // 3. Unfreeze before showing any modal dialogs or exiting
                try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = true; } catch { }
                try { xlApp.EnableEvents = true; } catch { }

                if (validationErrors.Count > 0 || validationWarnings.Count > 0)
                {
                    using var valDialog = new SummaryDialog(contract.ModuleName, validationErrors, validationWarnings);
                    valDialog.ShowDialog();

#if DEBUG
                    if (valDialog.Action == ValidationDialogAction.DebugQuitAnyway)
                    {
                        isExiting = true;
                        unEventHooks();
                        wb.Saved = true;
                        cancel = false;
                        QuitExcelIfLastWorkbook(wb);
                        return;
                    }
#endif
                    if (validationErrors.Count > 0 || valDialog.Action != ValidationDialogAction.ProceedAnyway)
                    {
                        cancel = true;
                        return;
                    }
                }

#if DEBUG
                using var closePrompt = new ExitConflictDialog(
                    contract.ModuleName,
                    string.Empty,
                    allowSaveOffline: true,
                    isClosing: true,
                    isDebugPrompt: true
                );
                closePrompt.ShowDialog();
                switch (closePrompt.Resolution)
                {
                    case ExitResolution.Retry:
                        try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }
                        try { xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }
                        var (dbSuccess, dbErr) = contract.SaveToServer(data!);
                        if (dbSuccess)
                        {
                            OfflineSnapshot.ClearPendingSnapshot<T>();
                            isExiting = true;
                            unEventHooks();
                            wb.Saved = true;
                            cancel = false;
                            QuitExcelIfLastWorkbook(wb);
                            return;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = true; } catch { }
                            try { xlApp.EnableEvents = true; } catch { }
                            MessageBox.Show($"Server Save Failed:\n\n{dbErr}", "Debug Server Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                            continue;
                        }
                    case ExitResolution.SaveLocallyAndQuit:
                        OfflineSnapshot.SaveOfflineSnapshot(data);
                        isExiting = true;
                        unEventHooks();
                        wb.Saved = true;
                        cancel = false;
                        QuitExcelIfLastWorkbook(wb);
                        return;
                    case ExitResolution.QuitAnyway:
                        isExiting = true;
                        unEventHooks();
                        wb.Saved = true;
                        cancel = false;
                        QuitExcelIfLastWorkbook(wb);
                        return;
                    case ExitResolution.Cancel:
                    default:
                        cancel = true;
                        return;
                }
#else
                if (data == null || contract.IsDatasetEmpty(data))
                {
                    isExiting = true;
                    wb.Saved = true;
                    cancel = false;
                    QuitExcelIfLastWorkbook(wb);
                    return;
                }

                try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }
                try { xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }

                var (success, dbError) = contract.SaveToServer(data);

                if (success)
                {
                    OfflineSnapshot.ClearPendingSnapshot<T>();
                    isExiting = true;
                    unEventHooks();
                    wb.Saved = true;
                    cancel = false;
                    QuitExcelIfLastWorkbook(wb);
                    return;
                }

                try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = true; } catch { }
                try { xlApp.EnableEvents = true; } catch { }

                using var dialog = new ExitConflictDialog(
                    contract.ModuleName,
                    string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dbError) ? "Could not persist records to the PostgreSQL server." : dbError,
                    allowSaveOffline: true,
                    isClosing: true,
                    isDebugPrompt: false
                );
                dialog.ShowDialog();

                switch (dialog.Resolution)
                {
                    case ExitResolution.Retry:
                        continue;
                    case ExitResolution.SaveLocallyAndQuit:
                        OfflineSnapshot.SaveOfflineSnapshot(data);
                        isExiting = true;
                        unEventHooks();
                        wb.Saved = true;
                        cancel = false;
                        QuitExcelIfLastWorkbook(wb);
                        return;
                    case ExitResolution.Cancel:
                    default:
                        cancel = true;
                        return;
                }
#endif
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show($"Error encountered during exit validation:\n\n{ex.Message}\n\n{ex.StackTrace}",
                "Exit Validation Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            cancel = true;
        }
        finally
        {
            // Restore active sheet if user cancelled out of the exit prompt
            if (activeSheetBefore != null && !isExiting && cancel)
            {
                try { activeSheetBefore.Activate(); } catch { }
            }
            try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
            try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
        }
    }

    private static void QuitExcelIfLastWorkbook(dynamic wb)
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic app = wb.Application;
            // Defer quit asynchronously so it executes after the WorkbookBeforeClose COM event returns cleanly
            ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
            {
                try
                {
                    if (app != null && (int)app.Workbooks.Count <= 1)
                    {
                        app.Quit();
                    }
                }
                catch { }
            });
        }
        catch { }
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Entry;

using System;

public enum ExitResolution
{
    Retry,
    SaveLocallyAndQuit,
    Cancel,
    QuitAnyway
}

public partial class ExitConflictDialog : Form
{
    public ExitResolution Resolution { get; private set; } = ExitResolution.Cancel;

    public ExitConflictDialog(
        string moduleName,
        string errorMessage,
        bool allowSaveOffline = true,
        bool isClosing = true,
        bool isDebugPrompt = false)
    {
        Text = isDebugPrompt
            ? $"[DEBUG MODE] {moduleName} - Save & Exit Options"
            : $"{moduleName} - Synchronization Alert";

        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterScreen;
        FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
        MaximizeBox = false;
        MinimizeBox = false;
        AutoScaleMode = AutoScaleMode.Dpi;
        AutoSize = true;
        AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink;
        Padding = new Padding(24, 20, 24, 20);
        BackColor = Color.White;

        var layout = new TableLayoutPanel
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            ColumnCount = 2,
            AutoSize = true,
            AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink
        };
        layout.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        layout.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100f));

        var pnlIndicator = new Panel
        {
            Size = new Size(36, 36),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 2, 16, 0)
        };

        pnlIndicator.Paint += (s, e) =>
        {
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            Color iconColor = isDebugPrompt ? Color.FromArgb(79, 70, 229) : Color.FromArgb(217, 119, 6);
            using var brush = new SolidBrush(iconColor);
            e.Graphics.FillEllipse(brush, 2, 2, 32, 32);

            using var pen = new Pen(Color.White, 2.5f);
            if (isDebugPrompt)
            {
                e.Graphics.DrawArc(pen, 12, 9, 12, 10, 180, 200);
                e.Graphics.DrawLine(pen, 18, 19, 18, 22);
                e.Graphics.FillEllipse(Brushes.White, 16.5f, 25f, 3, 3);
            }
            else
            {
                e.Graphics.DrawLine(pen, 18, 10, 18, 20);
                e.Graphics.FillEllipse(Brushes.White, 16.5f, 23.5f, 3, 3);
            }
        };

        var pnlContent = new TableLayoutPanel
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            ColumnCount = 1,
            AutoSize = true,
            AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink
        };

        var lblTitle = new Label
        {
            Text = isDebugPrompt ? "Debug Save Intercept" : "Server Database Offline",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 11.5f, FontStyle.Bold),
            ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(30, 41, 59),
            AutoSize = true,
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 6)
        };

        string actionPrompt = isDebugPrompt
            ? "Debug Mode is active. Select how to proceed with this record batch:"
            : (allowSaveOffline
                ? (isClosing
                    ? "Would you like to retry the server, save an offline stash and quit, or stay in Excel?"
                    : "Would you like to retry connecting, or save an offline stash to upload later?")
                : "Data validation failed. Fix errors before saving. Saving offline is disabled for invalid data.");

        var lblMessage = new Label
        {
            Text = isDebugPrompt
                ? $"Pending records ready to persist.\n\n{actionPrompt}"
                : $"Unable to persist records to the server database.\n\nDetails:\n{errorMessage}\n\n{actionPrompt}",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9.5f),
            ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(71, 85, 105),
            AutoSize = true,
            MaximumSize = new Size(480, 0),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 18)
        };

        var flowButtons = new FlowLayoutPanel
        {
            FlowDirection = FlowDirection.RightToLeft,
            AutoSize = true,
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            Margin = Padding.Empty
        };

        var btnCancel = new Button
        {
            Text = isClosing ? "Cancel (Stay in Excel)" : "Cancel",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f),
            AutoSize = true,
            Padding = new Padding(14, 6, 14, 6),
            BackColor = Color.FromArgb(241, 245, 249),
            FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
            DialogResult = DialogResult.Cancel
        };
        btnCancel.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 1;
        btnCancel.FlatAppearance.BorderColor = Color.FromArgb(203, 213, 225);
        btnCancel.Click += (_, _) => { Resolution = ExitResolution.Cancel; Close(); };
        flowButtons.Controls.Add(btnCancel);

#if DEBUG
        var btnQuitAnyway = new Button
        {
            Text = "Quit Anyway (Discard)",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f, FontStyle.Italic),

        };
        btnQuitAnyway.FlatAppearance.BorderColor = Color.FromArgb(252, 165, 165);

        if (allowSaveOffline)

                Text = isClosing ? "Save Offline & Quit" : "Save Offline",
                Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f, FontStyle.Bold),

        layout.Controls.Add(pnlContent, 1, 0);

    }

FILE: Core\04_WorkbookSave\SheetMapper\SheetMapper.SheetMapperHelper.cs

namespace SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.SheetMapper;

using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;

public static class SheetMapperHelper
{
    private static readonly string[] UniversalDateFormats =
    {
        "dd/MM/yyyy", "d/M/yyyy", "dd-MM-yyyy", "d-M-yyyy", "yyyy-MM-dd"
    };

    /// <summary>
    /// Resizes a ListObject table cleanly, logging its physical range and row count before and after.
    /// </summary>
    public static void ResizeTable(dynamic table, int dataRowCount, int? explicitColSpan = null)
    {
        if (table == null) return;
        try
        {
            dynamic ws = table.Parent;
            if (ws == null) return;

            // Lift any legacy sheet protection; it is never re-applied.
            try
            {
                if ((bool)ws.ProtectContents) ws.Unprotect("SKSB_Internal_Guard");
            }
            catch { }

            int headerRow = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row;
            int startCol = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Column;
            int finalColSpan = explicitColSpan ?? (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
            if (finalColSpan < 1) finalColSpan = 1;

            int endCol = startCol + finalColSpan - 1;
            dynamic? oldBody = table.DataBodyRange;
            int oldRowCount = oldBody != null ? (int)oldBody.Rows.Count : 0;

            int effectiveDataRows = Math.Max(1, dataRowCount);
            int endRow = headerRow + effectiveDataRows;

            // Skip COM Resize if dimensions are already identical
            if (oldRowCount == effectiveDataRows && (int)table.ListColumns.Count == finalColSpan)
            {
                return;
            }

            // Cache Total Row state; disable during resize to avoid COM collisions, then restore
            bool hadTotals = false;
            try { hadTotals = (bool)table.ShowTotals; } catch { }
            try
            {
                if (hadTotals) { try { table.ShowTotals = false; } catch { } }

                if (oldRowCount > effectiveDataRows)
                {
                    int clearStartRow = headerRow + effectiveDataRows + 1;
                    int clearEndRow = headerRow + oldRowCount;
                    if (clearStartRow <= clearEndRow)
                    {
                        dynamic staleRange = ws.Range[ws.Cells[clearStartRow, startCol], ws.Cells[clearEndRow, endCol]];
                        staleRange.Clear();
                    }
                }

                dynamic newRange = ws.Range[ws.Cells[headerRow, startCol], ws.Cells[endRow, endCol]];
                try
                {
                    table.Resize(newRange);
                }
                catch (Exception exResize)
                {
                    System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[{table.Name}] table.Resize threw COM error: {exResize.Message}");
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                if (hadTotals)
                {
                    try { table.ShowTotals = true; } catch { }
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"ResizeTable outer exception: {ex.Message}");
        }
    }

    public static void ResizeTable(
        string sheetName,
        int dataRowCount,
        int columnSpan,
        [CallerMemberName] string callerMember = "",
        [CallerFilePath] string callerFile = "",
        [CallerLineNumber] int callerLine = 0)
    {
        string sourceFile = System.IO.Path.GetFileName(callerFile);
        AppLogger.Warn($"[LEGACY OVERLOAD] ResizeTable('{sheetName}', targetRows={dataRowCount}, span={columnSpan}) called by [{sourceFile}:{callerLine} {callerMember}]");

        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic ws = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook.Sheets[sheetName];
            if (ws == null || ws.ListObjects.Count == 0) return;
            dynamic table = ws.ListObjects[1];
            ResizeTable(table, dataRowCount, columnSpan, callerMember, callerFile, callerLine);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error($"Legacy ResizeTable('{sheetName}') error", ex);
        }
    }

    public static void ClampAllTablesToActualData(dynamic wb)
    {
        if (wb == null) return;

        try
        {
            foreach (dynamic ws in wb.Sheets)
            {
                string sName = ws.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                if (sName.Equals(SheetOperations.LookupsSheetName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                    sName.Equals(SheetOperations.DataSheetName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                int tableCount = 0;
                try { tableCount = (int)ws.ListObjects.Count; } catch { }
                if (tableCount == 0) continue;

                for (int t = 1; t <= tableCount; t++)
                {
                    dynamic table = ws.ListObjects[t];
                    dynamic? body = table.DataBodyRange;
                    if (body == null) continue;

                    int currentRows = (int)body.Rows.Count;
                    if (currentRows <= 5) continue;

                    dynamic col1Range = table.ListColumns[1].DataBodyRange;
                    object? rawCol1 = col1Range.Value2;
                    int lastNonEmptyRow = 0;

                    if (rawCol1 is object[,] mat)
                    {
                        int rMax = mat.GetUpperBound(0);
                        int cMin = mat.GetLowerBound(1);
                        for (int r = rMax; r >= 1; r--)
                        {
                            string val = CleanString(mat[r, cMin]);
                            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(val))
                            {
                                lastNonEmptyRow = r;
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    int targetRows = Math.Max(1, lastNonEmptyRow);
                    if (currentRows > targetRows + 1)
                    {
                        AppLogger.Warn($"[UNINTENDED EXPANSION DETECTED] Table '{table.Name}' was inflated to {currentRows} rows! Clamping down to {targetRows} data rows.");
                        ResizeTable(table, targetRows);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error("ClampAllTablesToActualData error", ex);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Synchronizes a distinct list of strings into an Excel ListObject (Table) and registers a Named Range.
    /// Operates strictly on the target table's parent worksheet via COM to prevent active-sheet bleed-through.
    /// </summary>
    public static bool SyncReferenceTable(
        dynamic workbook,
        string tableName,
        string namedRange,
        IEnumerable<string> items)
    {
        if (workbook == null || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(tableName) || items == null) return false;

        try
        {
            var distinctList = items
                .Where(x => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(x))
                .Select(x => x.Trim())
                .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                .OrderBy(x => x)
                .ToList();

            dynamic? targetTable = null;
            foreach (dynamic ws in workbook.Sheets)
            {
                try
                {
                    targetTable = ws.ListObjects[tableName];
                    if (targetTable != null) break;
                }
                catch { }
            }

            if (targetTable == null)
            {
                AppLogger.Warn($"[SyncReferenceTable] Table '{tableName}' not found in workbook.");
                return false;
            }

            dynamic sheet = targetTable.Parent;
            int headerRow = (int)targetTable.HeaderRowRange.Row;
            int col = (int)targetTable.HeaderRowRange.Column;

            int rowCount = distinctList.Count;
            int effectiveRows = Math.Max(1, rowCount);

            // 1. Resize table boundary directly
            ResizeTable(targetTable, effectiveRows);

            dynamic dataRange = sheet.Range[
                sheet.Cells[headerRow + 1, col],
                sheet.Cells[headerRow + effectiveRows, col]
            ];

            // 2. Write data without touching any other worksheet
            if (rowCount > 0)
            {
                object[,] data = new object[rowCount, 1];
                for (int i = 0; i < rowCount; i++)
                {
                    data[i, 0] = distinctList[i];
                }

                try { dataRange.NumberFormat = "@"; } catch { }
                dataRange.Value2 = data;

                // 3. Register Excel Defined Name if requested
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(namedRange))
                {
                    try { XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcDeleteName, namedRange); } catch { }
                    string sName = (string)sheet.Name;
                    int rStart = headerRow + 1;
                    int rEnd = headerRow + rowCount;
                    string refersTo = $"='{sName}'!R{rStart}C{col}:R{rEnd}C{col}";
                    XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcDefineName, namedRange, refersTo, 3);
                }
            }
            else
            {
                dataRange.Value2 = null;
            }

            return true;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error($"[SyncReferenceTable] Failed on '{tableName}'", ex);
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static List<string> ExtractDistinctColumnValues(dynamic workbook, string tableName, int scanRowLimit = 100)
    {
        var distinctValues = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        dynamic? targetTable = null;

        foreach (dynamic ws in workbook.Sheets)
        {
            try
            {
                targetTable = ws.ListObjects[tableName];
                if (targetTable != null) break;
            }
            catch { }
        }

        if (targetTable == null) return distinctValues.ToList();

        dynamic sheet = targetTable.Parent;
        dynamic headerRange = targetTable.HeaderRowRange;
        int headerRow = (int)headerRange.Row;
        int col = (int)headerRange.Column;

        try
        {
            dynamic scanRange = sheet.Range[sheet.Cells[headerRow + 1, col], sheet.Cells[headerRow + scanRowLimit, col]];
            object[,] colGrid = scanRange.Value2;

            if (colGrid != null)
            {
                int rCount = colGrid.GetLength(0);
                for (int r = 1; r <= rCount; r++)
                {
                    string val = CleanString(colGrid[r, 1]);
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(val) &&
                        !val.StartsWith("#") &&
                        !val.Equals("System.__ComObject", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                    {
                        distinctValues.Add(val);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[SheetMapperHelper.ExtractDistinctColumnValues Error] {ex.Message}");
        }

        return distinctValues.OrderBy(x => x).ToList();
    }

    internal static bool IsComError(object? val) =>
        val == null ||
        (val is int i && i < -2000000000) ||
        (val is long l && l < -2000000000) ||
        val.ToString()?.StartsWith("#") == true;

    public static string CleanString(object? val)
    {
        if (val == null || IsComError(val)) return string.Empty;
        string s = val.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
        if (long.TryParse(s, out long num) && num < -2000000000) return string.Empty;
        return s;
    }

    public static DateOnly ParseDate(object? cell)
    {
        if (cell is double d) return DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.FromOADate(d));
        string clean = CleanString(cell);
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(clean)) return default;
        if (double.TryParse(clean, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double parsedD) && parsedD > 30000 && parsedD < 80000)
            return DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.FromOADate(parsedD));
        if (DateOnly.TryParseExact(clean, UniversalDateFormats, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DateTimeStyles.None, out DateOnly dOnly))
            return dOnly;
        if (DateTime.TryParse(clean, new CultureInfo("en-GB"), DateTimeStyles.None, out DateTime dt))
            return DateOnly.FromDateTime(dt);
        return default;
    }

    public static string FormatDate(DateOnly date) =>
        date == default ? string.Empty : date.ToString("dd/MM/yyyy");
}

============================================================
FILE: Core\04_WorkbookSave\SheetMapper\SheetMapper.TableMapper.cs
