namespace SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.SheetMapper;

using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.RuleEngine;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.TableRowManager;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types.Attributes;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

public static class TableMapper
{
    private static readonly string[] RecognizedDateFormats =
    {
        "yyyy-MM-dd", "dd/MM/yyyy", "d/M/yyyy", "dd-MM-yyyy", "d-M-yyyy", "yyyy/MM/dd"
    };

    public static dynamic? ResolveTable(dynamic worksheet, params string[] candidateNames)
    {
        if (worksheet == null || candidateNames == null || candidateNames.Length == 0) return null;

        foreach (var name in candidateNames)
        {
            try
            {
                dynamic t = worksheet.ListObjects[name];
                if (t != null) return t;
            }
            catch { }
        }

        try
        {
            foreach (dynamic t in worksheet.ListObjects)
            {
                string tName = NormalizeHeader(t.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty);
                foreach (var name in candidateNames)
                {
                    if (tName.Equals(NormalizeHeader(name), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        return t;
                }
            }
        }
        catch { }

        return null;
    }

    public static List<T> ExtractFromTable<T>(dynamic worksheet, string tableName) where T : new() =>
        ExtractFromTable<T>(worksheet, null, new[] { tableName });

    public static List<T> ExtractFromTable<T>(dynamic worksheet, params string[] candidateTableNames) where T : new() =>
        ExtractFromTable<T>(worksheet, null, candidateTableNames);

    public static List<T> ExtractFromTable<T>(dynamic worksheet, List<string>? warnings, params string[] candidateTableNames) where T : new()
    {
        var records = new List<T>();
        dynamic? table = ResolveTable(worksheet, candidateTableNames);
        if (table == null) return records;

        RuleEvaluator.EvaluateAndHealTable(worksheet, table);

        // VALIDATION DEDUPLICATION: Clean and delete duplicate rows before reading body
        var dedupeWarnings = new List<string>();
        int dupes = 0;
        SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)worksheet, new Action(() =>
        {
            dupes = TableRowManager.CleanTableDuplicateRows(table, dedupeWarnings);
        }));

        if (dupes > 0)
        {
            foreach (var w in dedupeWarnings)
            {
                warnings?.Add(w);
                AppLogger.Warn(w);
            }
        }

        dynamic dataBody = table.DataBodyRange;
        if (dataBody == null) return records;

        int rowCount = 0;
        try { rowCount = (int)dataBody.Rows.Count; } catch { }
        int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
        if (rowCount == 0 || colCount == 0) return records;

        object[,] dataGrid = GetRange2DValues(dataBody, rowCount, colCount);
        object[,] headerGrid = GetRange2DValues(table.HeaderRowRange, 1, colCount);

        var headerMap = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
        {
            string rawHeader = headerGrid[1, c]?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            string normalized = NormalizeHeader(rawHeader);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(normalized) && !headerMap.ContainsKey(normalized))
            {
                headerMap[normalized] = c;
            }
        }

        if (typeof(T) == typeof(string))
        {
            int targetCol = headerMap.Values.FirstOrDefault(1);
            for (int r = 1; r <= rowCount; r++)
            {
                object? rawVal = dataGrid[r, targetCol];
                if (rawVal == null || SheetMapperHelper.IsComError(rawVal)) continue;
                string s = rawVal.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s))
                {
                    records.Add((T)(object)s);
                }
            }
            return records;
        }

        var propBindings = ResolvePropertyBindings<T>(headerMap);
        if (propBindings.Count == 0) return records;

        for (int r = 1; r <= rowCount; r++)
        {
            if (IsRowEmpty(dataGrid, r, colCount))
                continue;

            T item = new();
            bool hasValidData = false;

            foreach (var binding in propBindings)
            {
                object? rawVal = dataGrid[r, binding.ColumnIndex];
                if (rawVal == null || SheetMapperHelper.IsComError(rawVal)) continue;

                object? converted = ConvertValue(rawVal, binding.Property.PropertyType);
                if (converted != null)
                {
                    object targetObj = binding.TargetInstance(item);
                    if (targetObj != null)
                    {
                        binding.Property.SetValue(targetObj, converted);
                        hasValidData = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            var plateProp = typeof(T).GetProperty("VehiclePlate");
            if (plateProp != null && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(plateProp.GetValue(item)?.ToString()))
            {
                continue;
            }

            var staffIdProp = typeof(T).GetProperty("StaffID");
            if (typeof(T).Name.StartsWith("Staff", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                staffIdProp != null &&
                (Convert.ToInt64(staffIdProp.GetValue(item) ?? 0) <= 0))
            {
                continue;
            }

            if (hasValidData)
            {
                records.Add(item);
            }
        }

        return records;
    }

    public static void PopulateTableSafe<T>(dynamic worksheet, string tableName, IList<T> records) =>
        PopulateTableSafe<T>(worksheet, records, tableName);

    public static void PopulateTableSafe<T>(dynamic worksheet, IList<T> records, params string[] candidateTableNames)
    {
        if (worksheet == null || records == null) return;
        dynamic? table = ResolveTable(worksheet, candidateTableNames);
        if (table == null) return;

        string tName = table.Name?.ToString() ?? "Table";
        int recordCount = records.Count;
        using var _ = AppLogger.Scope($"PopulateTableSafe<{typeof(T).Name}>({tName}, count={recordCount})");

        dynamic xlApp = worksheet.Application;
        bool origEvents = true;
        bool origUpdating = true;
        try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }
        try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }

        bool hadTotals = false;
        try { hadTotals = (bool)table.ShowTotals; } catch { }

        try
        {
            // 1. Temporarily disable Total Row (restored in finally)
            if (hadTotals) { try { table.ShowTotals = false; } catch { } }

            // 2. Empty dataset handling
            if (recordCount == 0)
            {
                SheetMapperHelper.ResizeTable(table, 1);
                dynamic? body = table.DataBodyRange;
                if (body != null)
                {
                    int cols = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
                    int hRow = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row;       // <-- Reads Row 3 correctly
                    int sCol = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Column;
                    for (int c = 1; c <= cols; c++)
                    {
                        dynamic cell = worksheet.Cells[hRow + 1, sCol + c - 1]; // <-- Cleans Row 4 (the archetype row)
                        string f = cell.Formula?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                        if (!f.StartsWith("="))
                        {
                            try { cell.Value2 = null; } catch { }
                        }
                    }
                }
                return;
            }

            // 3. Resize table to fit data
            int effectiveRows = recordCount;
            SheetMapperHelper.ResizeTable(table, effectiveRows);
            int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
            int headerRow = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Row;
            int startCol = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Column;

            // Simple 1-column string table
            if (typeof(T) == typeof(string))
            {
                dynamic colRange = worksheet.Range[
                    worksheet.Cells[headerRow + 1, startCol],
                    worksheet.Cells[headerRow + effectiveRows, startCol]
                ];
                object[,] colData = new object[effectiveRows, 1];
                for (int r = 0; r < recordCount; r++)
                {
                    colData[r, 0] = records[r]?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                }
                try { colRange.NumberFormat = "@"; } catch { }
                colRange.Value2 = colData;
                return;
            }

            // Complex typed entity table
            var columnMap = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
            {
                string name = table.ListColumns[c].Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                columnMap[NormalizeHeader(name)] = startCol + c - 1;
            }

            var propBindings = ResolvePropertyBindings<T>(columnMap);
            foreach (var binding in propBindings)
            {
                dynamic archCell = worksheet.Cells[headerRow + 1, binding.ColumnIndex];
                string archFormula = archCell.Formula?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                if (archFormula.StartsWith("="))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                // 0. PRE-READ FORMATS (Crucial: Read these BEFORE setting colRange.Style, 
                // because colRange includes archCell and applying a named style overwrites its custom decimals)
                string archStyleName = archCell.Style?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                string archFmt = string.Empty;
                dynamic? archFmtLocal = null;
                try
                {
                    archFmt = archCell.NumberFormat?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                    archFmtLocal = archCell.NumberFormatLocal;
                }
                catch { }

                // Inside TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe, right after reading archFmt:
                bool isTextFormattedCell = string.Equals(archFmt, "@", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                           binding.Property.PropertyType == typeof(string);

                object[,] colData = new object[effectiveRows, 1];
                for (int r = 0; r < recordCount; r++)
                {
                    object? target = binding.TargetInstance(records[r]);
                    object? val = target != null ? binding.Property.GetValue(target) : null;

                    if (isTextFormattedCell && val is long lVal)
                    {
                        // If the cell is formatted as Text ("@") in the template, write long as string (and hide 0)
                        colData[r, 0] = lVal > 0 ? lVal.ToString("D12") : string.Empty;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        colData[r, 0] = FormatForExcel(val);
                    }
                }

                dynamic colRange = worksheet.Range[
                    worksheet.Cells[headerRow + 1, binding.ColumnIndex],
                    worksheet.Cells[headerRow + effectiveRows, binding.ColumnIndex]
                ];

                // 1. Inherit Archetype Named Style across all populated rows
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(archStyleName) && !archStyleName.Equals("Normal", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    try { colRange.Style = archStyleName; } catch { }
                }

                // 2. Preserve explicit column NumberFormat using the pre-read values
                if (isTextFormattedCell)
                {
                    try { colRange.NumberFormat = "@"; } catch { }
                }
                else
                {
                    try
                    {
                        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(archFmt) && !archFmt.Equals("General", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        {
                            colRange.NumberFormat = archFmt;
                        }
                        if (archFmtLocal != null)
                        {
                            colRange.NumberFormatLocal = archFmtLocal;
                        }
                    }
                    catch { }
                }

                // 3. Write data
                colRange.Value2 = colData;
            }

            // 4. Pin table boundary
            int finalEndRow = headerRow + effectiveRows;
            int finalEndCol = startCol + colCount - 1;
            dynamic lockRange = worksheet.Range[worksheet.Cells[headerRow, startCol], worksheet.Cells[finalEndRow, finalEndCol]];

            // 5. Physical size verification
            dynamic? finalBody = table.DataBodyRange;
            int finalPhysicalRows = finalBody != null ? (int)finalBody.Rows.Count : 0;
            string finalRangeAddr = table.Range?.Address?.ToString() ?? "N/A";
            AppLogger.Info($"[{tName}] Physical verify after write: DataRows={finalPhysicalRows}, Range={finalRangeAddr}");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error($"PopulateTableSafe<{typeof(T).Name}> failed on '{tName}'", ex);
        }
        finally
        {
            if (hadTotals) { try { table.ShowTotals = true; } catch { } }
            try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
            try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
        }
    }

    public static object[,] GetRange2DValues(dynamic range, int expectedRows, int expectedCols)
    {
        object? raw = null;
        try { raw = range.Value2; } catch { }
        if (raw == null) return new object[Math.Max(2, expectedRows + 1), Math.Max(2, expectedCols + 1)];
        if (raw is object[,] matrix) return matrix;

        object[,] scalarGrid = new object[2, 2];
        scalarGrid[1, 1] = raw;
        return scalarGrid;
    }

    public static object FormatForExcel(object? val)
    {
        if (val == null) return string.Empty;
        if (val is DateOnly d) return d.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue).ToOADate();
        if (val is DateTime dt) return dt.ToOADate();
        if (val is decimal dec)
        {
            if (dec == 0.00m) return string.Empty;
            return (double)dec;
        }
        if (val is Enum e)
        {
            if (string.Equals(e.ToString(), "None", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return string.Empty;
            return e.ToString();
        }
        if (val is IEnumerable<string> strList && val is not string)
        {
            return string.Join(", ", strList);
        }
        return val;
    }

    public static object? ConvertValue(object? rawValue, Type targetType)
    {
        if (rawValue == null || SheetMapperHelper.IsComError(rawValue)) return null;

        Type underlyingType = Nullable.GetUnderlyingType(targetType) ?? targetType;
        string strVal = rawValue.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(strVal) || strVal == " " || strVal == "-") return null;

        if (underlyingType == typeof(List<string>))
        {
            return strVal.Split(new[] { ',' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)
                         .Select(s => s.Trim())
                         .Where(s => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(s))
                         .ToList();
        }

        if (underlyingType == typeof(string))
        {
            if (rawValue is double d && d >= 100000000)
            {
                return d.ToString("F0", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            }
            return strVal;
        }

        if (underlyingType == typeof(DateOnly))
            return ParseToDateOnly(rawValue, strVal);

        if (underlyingType == typeof(DateTime))
        {
            var dOnly = ParseToDateOnly(rawValue, strVal);
            return dOnly.HasValue ? dOnly.Value.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue) : null;
        }

        if (underlyingType.IsEnum)
        {
            string cleanEnumName = strVal.Replace(" ", "_").Replace("-", "_");
            if (Enum.TryParse(underlyingType, cleanEnumName, true, out var enumResult))
                return enumResult;
            return Enum.ToObject(underlyingType, 0);
        }

        if (underlyingType == typeof(decimal))
            return decimal.TryParse(strVal, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out decimal dec) ? dec : 0.00m;

        if (underlyingType == typeof(int))
        {
            if (rawValue is double d) return (int)Math.Round(d);
            return int.TryParse(strVal, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out int i) ? i : 0;
        }

        if (underlyingType == typeof(long))
        {
            if (rawValue is double d) return (long)Math.Round(d);
            string digitsOnly = Regex.Replace(strVal, @"[^\d\-]", "");
            return long.TryParse(digitsOnly, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out long l) ? l : 0L;
        }

        if (underlyingType == typeof(double))
            return double.TryParse(strVal, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double dbl) ? dbl : 0.0;

        if (underlyingType == typeof(bool))
        {
            if (bool.TryParse(strVal, out bool b)) return b;
            return strVal.Equals("1") || strVal.Equals("YES", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) || strVal.Equals("TRUE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        }

        return Convert.ChangeType(rawValue, underlyingType);
    }

    internal static string NormalizeHeader(string header) =>
        RuleRegistry.NormalizeIdentifier(header);

    private static DateOnly? ParseToDateOnly(object rawValue, string strVal)
    {
        if (RuleDataCache.TryParseDate(rawValue, out DateOnly parsed))
            return parsed;
        if (DateOnly.TryParseExact(strVal, RecognizedDateFormats, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DateTimeStyles.None, out DateOnly exactDate))
            return exactDate;
        return null;
    }

    private static bool IsRowEmpty(object[,] grid, int row, int colCount)
    {
        bool hasValue = false;
        for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
        {
            object? val = grid[row, c];
            if (val == null || SheetMapperHelper.IsComError(val)) continue;
            string s = val.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
            if (s == " " || s == "-" || s == "0" || s == "0.00" || s.Equals("None", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                continue;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(s)) hasValue = true;
        }
        return !hasValue;
    }

    private record PropertyBinding(
        PropertyInfo Property,
        Func<object, object> TargetInstance,
        int ColumnIndex
    );

    private static List<PropertyBinding> ResolvePropertyBindings<T>(Dictionary<string, int> headerMap)
    {
        var bindings = new List<PropertyBinding>();
        ScanType(typeof(T), root => root);

        void ScanType(Type type, Func<object, object> instanceResolver)
        {
            foreach (var prop in type.GetProperties(BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Instance))
            {
                if (!prop.CanWrite || prop.GetIndexParameters().Length > 0) continue;
                if (typeof(IEnumerable).IsAssignableFrom(prop.PropertyType) &&
                    prop.PropertyType != typeof(string) &&
                    prop.PropertyType != typeof(List<string>))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (prop.GetCustomAttribute<DataDetailAttribute>() != null ||
                    (prop.PropertyType.IsClass &&
                     prop.PropertyType != typeof(string) &&
                     prop.PropertyType != typeof(List<string>) &&
                     !prop.PropertyType.IsArray))
                {
                    ScanType(prop.PropertyType, root =>
                    {
                        var parent = instanceResolver(root);
                        if (parent == null) return null!;
                        var child = prop.GetValue(parent);
                        if (child == null)
                        {
                            child = Activator.CreateInstance(prop.PropertyType)!;
                            prop.SetValue(parent, child);
                        }
                        return child;
                    });
                    continue;
                }

                string candidateHeader = prop.Name;
                var colAttr = prop.GetCustomAttribute<ExcelColumnAttribute>();
                if (colAttr != null && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(colAttr.HeaderName))
                {
                    candidateHeader = colAttr.HeaderName;
                }

                string normalized = NormalizeHeader(candidateHeader);
                if (headerMap.TryGetValue(normalized, out int colIndex))
                {
                    bindings.Add(new PropertyBinding(prop, instanceResolver, colIndex));
                }
                else
                {
                    string stripped = normalized.TrimEnd('s');
                    bool found = false;

                    // 1. Plural match
                    foreach (var (key, val) in headerMap)
                    {
                        if (key.TrimEnd('s').Equals(stripped, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                        {
                            bindings.Add(new PropertyBinding(prop, instanceResolver, val));
                            found = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }

                    // 2. Cost suffix tolerance: matches "Tyre" <-> "Tyre Cost", "Brake" <-> "Brake Cost", etc.
                    if (!found)
                    {
                        foreach (var (key, val) in headerMap)
                        {
                            if (key.Equals(normalized + "cost", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                normalized.Equals(key + "cost", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                            {
                                bindings.Add(new PropertyBinding(prop, instanceResolver, val));
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (bindings.Count == 0 && headerMap.Count == 1)
        {
            var singleProp = typeof(T).GetProperties(BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Instance)
                .FirstOrDefault(p => p.CanWrite && p.PropertyType == typeof(string));
            if (singleProp != null)
            {
                bindings.Add(new PropertyBinding(singleProp, root => root, headerMap.Values.First()));
            }
        }

        return bindings;
    }
}

FILE: Core\04_WorkbookSave\Storage\Storage.OfflineSnapshot.cs

namespace SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Storage;

using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Serialization;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Storage;
using SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Database;

public class TolerantEnumConverter<T> : JsonConverter<T> where T : struct, Enum
{
    public override T Read(ref Utf8JsonReader reader, Type typeToConvert, JsonSerializerOptions options)
    {
        if (reader.TokenType == JsonTokenType.Number)
        {
            if (reader.TryGetInt32(out int intVal))
            {
                return (T)Enum.ToObject(typeof(T), intVal);
            }
            if (reader.TryGetInt64(out long longVal))
            {
                return (T)Enum.ToObject(typeof(T), (int)longVal);
            }
        }

        if (reader.TokenType == JsonTokenType.String)
        {
            string? str = reader.GetString();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(str))
                return default;

            string normalized = str.Trim().Replace(" ", "_").Replace("-", "_");
            if (Enum.TryParse<T>(normalized, true, out var result))
                return result;

            // Safe fallback to default (None / 0) for unrecognized strings
            return default;
        }

        return default;
    }

    public override void Write(Utf8JsonWriter writer, T value, JsonSerializerOptions options)
    {
        writer.WriteStringValue(value.ToString());
    }
}

public class TolerantEnumConverterFactory : JsonConverterFactory
{
    public override bool CanConvert(Type typeToConvert)
    {
        Type underlying = Nullable.GetUnderlyingType(typeToConvert) ?? typeToConvert;
        return underlying.IsEnum;
    }

    public override JsonConverter? CreateConverter(Type typeToConvert, JsonSerializerOptions options)
    {
        Type underlying = Nullable.GetUnderlyingType(typeToConvert) ?? typeToConvert;
        Type converterType = typeof(TolerantEnumConverter<>).MakeGenericType(underlying);
        return (JsonConverter)Activator.CreateInstance(converterType)!;
    }
}

public static class OfflineSnapshot
{
    public static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new()
    {
        WriteIndented = true,
        PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true,
        Converters =
        {
            new TolerantEnumConverterFactory()
        }
    };

    public static string Save<T>(T data, bool writeToDatabase = true)
    {
        if (data == null)
            throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(data), "Data payload cannot be null.");

        FolderRegistry.Data.EnsureExists();
        string typeName = ResolveTypeName(typeof(T));
        string targetFile = $"{typeName.ToLowerInvariant()}_data.json";
        string fullPath = FolderRegistry.Data.GetFilePath(targetFile);

        string json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(data, JsonOptions);
        File.WriteAllText(fullPath, json);

        if (writeToDatabase)
        {
            ServerDispatcher.Dispatch(data);
        }

        return fullPath;
    }

    public static async Task<string> SaveAsync<T>(T data, bool writeToDatabase = true)
    {
        return await Task.Run(() => Save(data, writeToDatabase));
    }

    public static T? Load<T>()
    {
        string typeName = ResolveTypeName(typeof(T));
        string targetFile = $"{typeName.ToLowerInvariant()}_data.json";

        if (!FolderRegistry.Data.Exists())
            return default;

        string fullPath = FolderRegistry.Data.GetFilePath(targetFile);
        if (!File.Exists(fullPath))
            return default;

        try
        {
            string json = File.ReadAllText(fullPath);
            return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<T>(json, JsonOptions);
        }
        catch
        {
            return default;
        }
    }

    private static string ResolveTypeName(Type type)
    {
        if (type.IsGenericType && type.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(System.Collections.Generic.List<>))
        {
            return type.GetGenericArguments()[0].Name;
        }
        if (type.IsArray)
        {
            return type.GetElementType()?.Name ?? "Object";
        }
        return type.Name;
    }

    public static string GetPendingUploadPath<T>() =>
        FolderRegistry.Data.GetFilePath($"{typeof(T).Name}_PendingUpload.json");

    public static string SaveOfflineSnapshot<T>(T data)
    {
        FolderRegistry.Data.EnsureExists();
        string filePath = GetPendingUploadPath<T>();
        string json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(data, JsonOptions);
        File.WriteAllText(filePath, json);
        return filePath;
    }

    public static bool HasPendingSnapshot<T>() =>
        File.Exists(GetPendingUploadPath<T>());

    public static T? LoadPendingSnapshot<T>()
    {
        string filePath = GetPendingUploadPath<T>();
        if (!File.Exists(filePath)) return default;

        try
        {
            string json = File.ReadAllText(filePath);
            return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<T>(json, JsonOptions);
        }
        catch
        {
            return default;
        }
    }

    public static void ClearPendingSnapshot<T>()
    {
        string filePath = GetPendingUploadPath<T>();
        if (File.Exists(filePath))
        {
            File.Delete(filePath);
        }
    }

    public static DateTime? GetServerLatestTimestamp(string tableName)
    {
        try
        {
            var db = new PostgreSqlService();
            string sql = $"SELECT MAX(updated_at) FROM {tableName};";
            return db.ExecuteScalar<DateTime?>(sql);
        }
        catch
        {
            return null;
        }
    }
}

FILE: Core\04_WorkbookSave\Validation\Validation.RulesExtractor.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Validation;

using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.RuleEngine;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.TableRowManager;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.SheetMapper;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;

public record ColumnRule(
    int ColumnIndex,
    string Header,
    bool IsRequired,
    int AlertStyle, // 1 = Stop (Error), 2 = Warning
    string ErrorMessage,
    Config DropDownConfig,
    bool IsFormula,
    PropertyRuleDescriptor? CSharpRule = null
);

public class SheetRulesCache
{
    public string CacheKey { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public List<ColumnRule> ColumnRules { get; set; } = new();
    public DateTime CachedAt { get; set; } = DateTime.UtcNow;
}

public static class RulesExtractor
{
    private static readonly Dictionary<string, SheetRulesCache> _cachedRules = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    public static SheetRulesCache InitializeRulesForSheet(object worksheetObj, int headerRow, int startCol, int columnSpan)
    {
        RuleRegistry.EnsureInitialized();
        dynamic worksheet = worksheetObj;

        string sheetName = "Sheet";
        try { sheetName = worksheet.Name?.ToString() ?? "Sheet"; } catch { }

        string tableName = string.Empty;
        try
        {
            dynamic? tbl = worksheet.Cells[headerRow, startCol].ListObject;
            tableName = tbl?.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
        }
        catch { }

        string cacheKey = $"{sheetName}_{tableName}_Col{startCol}_Span{columnSpan}";
        if (_cachedRules.TryGetValue(cacheKey, out var existingCache))
        {
            return existingCache;
        }

        AppLogger.Info($"[RulesExtractor] Initializing rule cache for '{tableName}' on '{sheetName}'...");
        var cache = new SheetRulesCache { CacheKey = cacheKey };

        // Bulk-read header row and archetype formulas in 2 COM calls instead of 3 * columnSpan calls
        int endCol = startCol + columnSpan - 1;
        object[,] headerGrid = TableMapper.GetRange2DValues(
            worksheet.Range[worksheet.Cells[headerRow, startCol], worksheet.Cells[headerRow, endCol]],
            1, columnSpan
        );

        var archFormulas = new string[columnSpan + 1];
        try
        {
            dynamic archRange = worksheet.Range[worksheet.Cells[headerRow + 1, startCol], worksheet.Cells[headerRow + 1, endCol]];
            object? rawFormulas = archRange.Formula;
            if (rawFormulas is object[,] fMat)
            {
                for (int c = 1; c <= columnSpan; c++)
                    archFormulas[c] = fMat[1, c]?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            }
            else if (rawFormulas is string singleF)
            {
                archFormulas[1] = singleF;
            }
        }
        catch { }

        for (int c = 0; c < columnSpan; c++)
        {
            int colIndex = startCol + c;
            string headerText = headerGrid[1, c + 1]?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? $"Col_{colIndex}";
            bool isFormula = archFormulas[c + 1].StartsWith("=");

            var csharpRule = RuleRegistry.GetRule(tableName, headerText);
            // Only query legacy COM metadata if no C# attribute rule governs this column
            var legacyConfig = (csharpRule != null || isFormula)
                ? new Config()
                : Metadata.ReadConfig(worksheet.Cells[headerRow + 1, colIndex]);

            bool isRequired = false;
            int alertStyle = 1;
            string errorMsg = string.Empty;

            if (!isFormula)
            {
                if (csharpRule != null)
                {
                    isRequired = csharpRule.IsRequired && !csharpRule.IsWarningOnly;
                    alertStyle = csharpRule.IsWarningOnly ? 2 : 1;
                    errorMsg = $"Field '{headerText}' has an invalid or missing value.";
                }
                else if (!legacyConfig.IsEmpty)
                {
                    isRequired = !legacyConfig.IsMultiSelect && !legacyConfig.IsWarningOnly;
                    alertStyle = legacyConfig.IsWarningOnly ? 2 : 1;
                }
            }

            cache.ColumnRules.Add(new ColumnRule(
                ColumnIndex: colIndex,
                Header: headerText,
                IsRequired: isRequired,
                AlertStyle: alertStyle,
                ErrorMessage: errorMsg,
                DropDownConfig: legacyConfig,
                IsFormula: isFormula,
                CSharpRule: csharpRule
            ));
        }

        _cachedRules[cacheKey] = cache;
        return cache;
    }

    public static (List<string> Errors, List<string> Warnings) ValidateAllWorkbookTables(
        object wbObj,
        params string[] excludeTableNames)
    {
        using var _ = AppLogger.Scope("ValidateAllWorkbookTables");
        var errors = new List<string>();
        var warnings = new List<string>();
        if (wbObj == null) return (errors, warnings);

        dynamic wb = wbObj;
        var excludeSet = new HashSet<string>(excludeTableNames ?? Array.Empty<string>(), StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        // Shared source cache across all tables during this validation pass
        var sharedSourceCache = new Dictionary<string, List<string>>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        foreach (dynamic ws in wb.Sheets)
        {
            string sName = ws.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            if (sName.Equals("Lookups", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                sName.Equals("Data", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                continue;
            }

            int tableCount = 0;
            try { tableCount = (int)ws.ListObjects.Count; } catch { }
            if (tableCount == 0) continue;

            for (int t = 1; t <= tableCount; t++)
            {
                dynamic table = ws.ListObjects[t];
                string tableName = table.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                if (excludeSet.Contains(tableName)) continue;

                dynamic? bodyRange = table.DataBodyRange;
                if (bodyRange == null) continue;

                int rowCount = (int)bodyRange.Rows.Count;
                int startRow = (int)bodyRange.Row;
                int startCol = (int)table.HeaderRowRange.Column;
                int colSpan = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;

                var (_, tblErrors, tblWarnings) = EvaluateAndExtractInternal(
                    worksheet: (object)ws,
                    tableObj: (object)table,
                    bodyRangeObj: (object)bodyRange,
                    startRow: startRow,
                    maxRows: rowCount,
                    startCol: startCol,
                    columnSpan: colSpan,
                    sharedSourceCache: sharedSourceCache
                );

                errors.AddRange(tblErrors);
                warnings.AddRange(tblWarnings);
            }
        }

        return (errors, warnings);
    }

    public static (List<Dictionary<string, object?>> Rows, List<string> Errors, List<string> Warnings) EvaluateAndExtract(
        object worksheet,
        int startRow,
        int maxRows,
        int startCol,
        int columnSpan,
        int driverColIndex = 1)
    {
        if (worksheet == null || maxRows <= 0 || columnSpan <= 0)
            return (new(), new(), new());

        dynamic wsDynamic = worksheet;
        dynamic? table = null;
        dynamic? bodyRange = null;
        try
        {
            table = wsDynamic.Cells[startRow, startCol].ListObject;
            bodyRange = table?.DataBodyRange;
        }
        catch { }

        return EvaluateAndExtractInternal(
            worksheet,
            table,
            bodyRange,
            startRow,
            maxRows,
            startCol,
            columnSpan,
            new Dictionary<string, List<string>>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
        );
    }

    private static (List<Dictionary<string, object?>> Rows, List<string> Errors, List<string> Warnings) EvaluateAndExtractInternal(
        object worksheet,
        object? tableObj,
        object? bodyRangeObj,
        int startRow,
        int maxRows,
        int startCol,
        int columnSpan,
        Dictionary<string, List<string>> sharedSourceCache)
    {
        var rows = new List<Dictionary<string, object?>>();
        var errors = new List<string>();
        var warnings = new List<string>();

        dynamic wsDynamic = worksheet;
        dynamic wb = wsDynamic.Parent;
        string sheetName = wsDynamic.Name?.ToString() ?? "Sheet";
        dynamic? table = tableObj;
        string tableName = table?.Name?.ToString() ?? "Table";

        if (table != null)
        {
            // 1. In-memory cell healing
            var healNotices = RuleEvaluator.EvaluateAndHealTable(wsDynamic, table);
            foreach (var notice in healNotices)
            {
                warnings.Add($"  [{sheetName}] {notice}");
            }

            // 2. VALIDATION DEDUPLICATION: Detect, Warn, Delete, and Skip
            var dedupeWarnings = new List<string>();
            int dupesRemoved = 0;

            SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)wsDynamic, new Action(() =>
            {
                dupesRemoved = TableRowManager.CleanTableDuplicateRows(table, dedupeWarnings);
            }));

            if (dupesRemoved > 0)
            {
                foreach (var w in dedupeWarnings)
                {
                    warnings.Add($"  [{sheetName}] {w}");
                }

                // Refresh DataBodyRange so subsequent array read skips the deleted rows
                dynamic? refreshedBody = table.DataBodyRange;
                if (refreshedBody == null)
                {
                    return (rows, errors, warnings);
                }

                bodyRangeObj = refreshedBody;
                startRow = (int)refreshedBody.Row;
                maxRows = (int)refreshedBody.Rows.Count;
            }
        }

        var rules = InitializeRulesForSheet(worksheet, startRow - 1, startCol, columnSpan);

        object[,] grid;
        try
        {
            dynamic range = bodyRangeObj ?? wsDynamic.Range[
                wsDynamic.Cells[startRow, startCol],
                wsDynamic.Cells[startRow + maxRows - 1, startCol + columnSpan - 1]
            ];
            grid = TableMapper.GetRange2DValues(range, maxRows, columnSpan);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error($"[Validation] Failed reading 2D Array for '{tableName}'", ex);
            return (rows, errors, warnings);
        }

        int rowCount = Math.Min(maxRows, grid.GetLength(0));

        List<string> GetCachedSource(string src, bool preserveDuplicates)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(src)) return new List<string>();
            string key = $"{src}|{preserveDuplicates}";
            if (!sharedSourceCache.TryGetValue(key, out var list))
            {
                list = RuleDataCache.GetSourceItems(wb, src, preserveDuplicates);
                sharedSourceCache[key] = list;
            }
            return list;
        }

        // Pre-build O(1) HashSet per column for allowed picklist options
        var allowedSetByCol = new HashSet<string>?[columnSpan];
        for (int c = 0; c < columnSpan; c++)
        {
            var cRule = rules.ColumnRules[c].CSharpRule;
            if (cRule != null && cRule.Sources.Length > 0 && !cRule.IsWarningOnly)
            {
                var set = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                foreach (var src in cRule.Sources)
                {
                    foreach (var item in GetCachedSource(src, preserveDuplicates: false))
                    {
                        AddNormalizedVariantsToSet(set, item);
                    }
                }
                allowedSetByCol[c] = set;
            }
        }

        // Pre-build O(1) lookup maps for SyncRules: Key -> Allowed Partner Values
        var syncLookupCache = new Dictionary<string, Dictionary<string, HashSet<string>>>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        Dictionary<string, HashSet<string>> GetSyncPairMap(string lookupSrc, string resultSrc)
        {
            string cacheKey = $"{lookupSrc}>>{resultSrc}";
            if (!syncLookupCache.TryGetValue(cacheKey, out var map))
            {
                map = new Dictionary<string, HashSet<string>>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                var keys = GetCachedSource(lookupSrc, preserveDuplicates: true);
                var vals = GetCachedSource(resultSrc, preserveDuplicates: true);
                int count = Math.Min(keys.Count, vals.Count);
                for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
                {
                    string k = NormalizeLookupToken(keys[i]);
                    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(k)) continue;
                    if (!map.TryGetValue(k, out var partnerSet))
                    {
                        partnerSet = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                        map[k] = partnerSet;
                    }
                    AddNormalizedVariantsToSet(partnerSet, vals[i]);
                }
                syncLookupCache[cacheKey] = map;
            }
            return map;
        }

        var colHeadersOneBased = new string[columnSpan + 1];
        for (int c = 0; c < columnSpan; c++)
        {
            colHeadersOneBased[c + 1] = rules.ColumnRules[c].Header;
        }

        for (int r = 1; r <= rowCount; r++)
        {
            int excelRow = startRow + r - 1;
            bool hasMeaningfulData = false;
            var rowStringsOneBased = new string[columnSpan + 1];
            var rowObjectsOneBased = new object?[columnSpan + 1];

            for (int c = 0; c < columnSpan; c++)
            {
                var rule = rules.ColumnRules[c];
                object? cellObj = grid[r, c + 1];
                rowObjectsOneBased[c + 1] = cellObj;
                string valStr = CleanString(cellObj);
                rowStringsOneBased[c + 1] = valStr;

                if (rule.IsFormula) continue;

                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(valStr) &&
                    valStr != "0" && valStr != "0.00" && valStr != "-" && valStr != " " &&
                    !valStr.Equals("None", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                    !valStr.Equals("Total", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    hasMeaningfulData = true;
                }
            }

            if (!hasMeaningfulData) continue;

            var rowDict = new Dictionary<string, object?>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            bool rowHasError = false;

            for (int c = 0; c < columnSpan; c++)
            {
                var rule = rules.ColumnRules[c];
                var cRule = rule.CSharpRule;
                object? rawVal = rowObjectsOneBased[c + 1];
                string strVal = rowStringsOneBased[c + 1];

                rowDict[rule.Header] = rawVal;
                if (rule.IsFormula) continue;

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(strVal))
                {
                    if (rule.IsRequired)
                    {
                        errors.Add($"  Row {excelRow} [{sheetName}]: Field '{rule.Header}' is required and cannot be empty.");
                        rowHasError = true;
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                if (cRule == null) continue;

                if (cRule.IsNumericColumn)
                {
                    if (!double.TryParse(strVal, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double dNum))
                    {
                        errors.Add($"  Row {excelRow} [{sheetName}]: Value '{strVal}' in '{rule.Header}' must be a valid number.");
                        rowHasError = true;
                        continue;
                    }
                    if (cRule.AllowNonNegativeZero && dNum < -0.0001)
                    {
                        errors.Add($"  Row {excelRow} [{sheetName}]: Value '{strVal}' in '{rule.Header}' cannot be negative.");
                        rowHasError = true;
                        continue;
                    }
                }

                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cRule.DateMustBeGreaterThanHeader))
                {
                    int refCol = RuleEvaluator.FindColumnByHeader(colHeadersOneBased, cRule.DateMustBeGreaterThanHeader);
                    if (refCol >= 1)
                    {
                        string refValStr = rowStringsOneBased[refCol];
                        if (RuleDataCache.TryParseDate(strVal, out DateOnly thisDate) &&
                            RuleDataCache.TryParseDate(refValStr, out DateOnly refDate))
                        {
                            if (thisDate <= refDate)
                            {
                                errors.Add($"  Row {excelRow} [{sheetName}]: '{rule.Header}' ({thisDate:dd/MM/yyyy}) must be strictly later than '{colHeadersOneBased[refCol]}' ({refDate:dd/MM/yyyy}).");
                                rowHasError = true;
                                continue;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // O(1) HashSet Picklist Lookup
                var allowedSet = allowedSetByCol[c];
                if (allowedSet != null && allowedSet.Count > 0 && !cRule.IsWarningOnly)
                {
                    if (cRule.IsMultiSelect)
                    {
                        var tokens = strVal.Split(new[] { ',' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Select(t => t.Trim());
                        foreach (var token in tokens)
                        {
                            if (!IsTokenInNormalizedSet(allowedSet, token))
                            {
                                errors.Add($"  Row {excelRow} [{sheetName}]: '{token}' is not a valid option for '{rule.Header}'.");
                                rowHasError = true;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        if (!IsTokenInNormalizedSet(allowedSet, strVal))
                        {
                            string displayVal = RuleEvaluator.FormatDisplayValue(rawVal, cRule);
                            errors.Add($"  Row {excelRow} [{sheetName}]: '{displayVal}' is not a valid option for '{rule.Header}'.");
                            rowHasError = true;
                        }
                    }
                }

                // O(1) Dictionary Sync Partner Validation
                if (cRule.SyncRules.Count > 0)
                {
                    var syncGroups = cRule.SyncRules.GroupBy(s => RuleRegistry.NormalizeIdentifier(s.TargetHeader));
                    string normKey = NormalizeLookupToken(strVal);

                    foreach (var group in syncGroups)
                    {
                        int partnerCol = RuleEvaluator.FindColumnByHeader(colHeadersOneBased, group.First().TargetHeader);
                        if (partnerCol < 1) continue;

                        string partnerVal = rowStringsOneBased[partnerCol];
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(partnerVal)) continue;

                        bool anyLookupFound = false;
                        bool anyLookupMatched = false;

                        foreach (var sync in group)
                        {
                            var pairMap = GetSyncPairMap(sync.LookupSource, sync.ResultSource);
                            if (pairMap.TryGetValue(normKey, out var validPartners) && validPartners.Count > 0)
                            {
                                anyLookupFound = true;
                                if (IsTokenInNormalizedSet(validPartners, partnerVal))
                                {
                                    anyLookupMatched = true;
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        if (anyLookupFound && !anyLookupMatched)
                        {
                            string conflictMsg = $"  Row {excelRow} [{sheetName}]: '{strVal}' in '{rule.Header}' conflicts with '{partnerVal}' in '{colHeadersOneBased[partnerCol]}'.";
                            if (cRule.IsWarningOnly)
                                warnings.Add(conflictMsg);
                            else
                            {
                                errors.Add(conflictMsg);
                                rowHasError = true;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            if (!rowHasError)
            {
                rows.Add(rowDict);
            }
        }

        return (rows, errors, warnings);
    }

    private static string NormalizeLookupToken(string? s)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s)) return string.Empty;
        string trimmed = s.Trim();
        if (double.TryParse(trimmed, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double d) &&
            Math.Abs(d - Math.Round(d)) < 0.0001 && d < 1e15)
        {
            return ((long)Math.Round(d)).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        }
        return trimmed.Replace(" ", "_");
    }

    private static void AddNormalizedVariantsToSet(HashSet<string> set, string? raw)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(raw)) return;
        string trimmed = raw.Trim();
        set.Add(trimmed);
        set.Add(trimmed.Replace(" ", "_"));
        set.Add(trimmed.Replace("_", " "));
        if (double.TryParse(trimmed, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double d) &&
            Math.Abs(d - Math.Round(d)) < 0.0001 && d < 1e15)
        {
            set.Add(((long)Math.Round(d)).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
        }
        if (RuleDataCache.TryParseDate(trimmed, out DateOnly dt))
        {
            set.Add(dt.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
        }
    }

    private static bool IsTokenInNormalizedSet(HashSet<string> set, string? token)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(token)) return false;
        string trimmed = token.Trim();
        if (set.Contains(trimmed) || set.Contains(trimmed.Replace(" ", "_")))
            return true;

        if (double.TryParse(trimmed, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double d) &&
            Math.Abs(d - Math.Round(d)) < 0.0001 && d < 1e15)
        {
            if (set.Contains(((long)Math.Round(d)).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)))
                return true;
        }

        if (RuleDataCache.TryParseDate(trimmed, out DateOnly dt))
        {
            if (set.Contains(dt.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)))
                return true;
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static string CleanString(object? val)
    {
        if (val == null || SheetMapperHelper.IsComError(val)) return string.Empty;
        if (val is double d && Math.Abs(d - Math.Round(d)) < 0.00001 && d < 1e15)
            return ((long)Math.Round(d)).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
        string s = val.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
        if (s.Equals("System.__ComObject", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return string.Empty;
        if (long.TryParse(s, out long num) && num < -2000000000) return string.Empty;
        return s;
    }
}

    public SummaryDialog(string moduleName, List<string> errors, List<string> warnings)
    {

            MaximumSize = new Size(440, 0),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 16)

            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 18)

            Close();
        };
        flowActions.Controls.Add(btnDebugQuit);
#endif

        pnlContent.Controls.Add(lblTitle);
        pnlContent.Controls.Add(lblMessage);
        pnlContent.Controls.Add(flowDrillDown);
        pnlContent.Controls.Add(flowActions);

        layout.Controls.Add(pnlIndicator, 0, 0);
        layout.Controls.Add(pnlContent, 1, 0);
        Controls.Add(layout);
    }

    private void ShowDetailsModal(string title, List<string> items, bool isError)
    {
        using var detailsForm = new Form
        {
            Text = title,
            Size = new Size(650, 420),
            StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent,
            FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.Sizable,
            MinimizeBox = false,
            MaximizeBox = true
        };

        var txtLog = new TextBox
        {
            Multiline = true,
            ReadOnly = true,
            ScrollBars = ScrollBars.Both,
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            Font = new Font("Consolas", 9.5f),
            BackColor = Color.White
        };

        var sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.AppendLine("==========================================================================");
        sb.AppendLine($" {title.ToUpperInvariant()} ({items.Count} items total)");
        sb.AppendLine("==========================================================================");
        sb.AppendLine();

        foreach (var item in items)
        {
            sb.AppendLine(item);
        }

        txtLog.Text = sb.ToString();
        detailsForm.Controls.Add(txtLog);
        detailsForm.ShowDialog(this);
    }
}

FILE: Core\Diagnostics\Diagnoestics.AppLogger.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading;
using SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Storage;

public static class AppLogger
{
    private static readonly object _lock = new();
    private static string? _logFilePath;
    private static int _indentLevel = 0;

    public static string LogFilePath
    {
        get
        {
            if (_logFilePath == null)
            {
                try
                {
                    string dir = FolderRegistry.Data.EnsureExists();
                    _logFilePath = Path.Combine(dir, "SKSB_Runtime_Trace.log");
                }
                catch
                {
                    _logFilePath = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "SKSB_Runtime_Trace.log");
                }
            }
            return _logFilePath;
        }
    }

    public static void ResetSessionLog(string sessionTitle)
    {
        lock (_lock)
        {
            try
            {
                _indentLevel = 0;
                var sb = new StringBuilder();
                sb.AppendLine("================================================================================");
                sb.AppendLine($" SKSB RUNTIME TRACE LOG - {sessionTitle}");
                sb.AppendLine($" Started : {DateTime.Now:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.fff}");
                sb.AppendLine($" Log Path: {LogFilePath}");
                sb.AppendLine("================================================================================");
                File.WriteAllText(LogFilePath, sb.ToString(), Encoding.UTF8);
            }
            catch { }
        }
    }

    public static void Info(
        string message,
        [CallerMemberName] string member = "",
        [CallerFilePath] string file = "",
        [CallerLineNumber] int line = 0)
    {
        WriteEntry("INFO ", message, member, file, line);
    }

    public static void Warn(
        string message,
        [CallerMemberName] string member = "",
        [CallerFilePath] string file = "",
        [CallerLineNumber] int line = 0)
    {
        WriteEntry("WARN ", message, member, file, line);
    }

    public static void Error(
        string message,
        Exception? ex = null,
        [CallerMemberName] string member = "",
        [CallerFilePath] string file = "",
        [CallerLineNumber] int line = 0)
    {
        string full = ex != null
            ? $"{message} | EX: {ex.GetType().Name}: {ex.Message}\n{ex.StackTrace}"
            : message;
        WriteEntry("ERROR", full, member, file, line);
    }

    public static IDisposable Scope(
        string scopeName,
        [CallerMemberName] string member = "",
        [CallerFilePath] string file = "",
        [CallerLineNumber] int line = 0)
    {
        return new LogScope(scopeName, member, file, line);
    }

    private static void WriteEntry(string level, string message, string member, string file, int line)
    {
        lock (_lock)
        {
            try
            {
                string shortFile = Path.GetFileName(file);
                string indent = new string(' ', Math.Max(0, _indentLevel * 2));
                int tid = Thread.CurrentThread.ManagedThreadId;
                string lineStr = $"[{DateTime.Now:HH:mm:ss.fff}] [T{tid:D2}] [{level}] [{shortFile}:{line} {member}] {indent}{message}{Environment.NewLine}";

                File.AppendAllText(LogFilePath, lineStr, Encoding.UTF8);
                Debug.Write(lineStr);
            }
            catch { }
        }
    }

    private sealed class LogScope : IDisposable
    {
        private readonly string _scopeName;
        private readonly string _member;
        private readonly string _file;
        private readonly int _line;
        private readonly Stopwatch _sw;

        public LogScope(string scopeName, string member, string file, int line)
        {
            _scopeName = scopeName;
            _member = member;
            _file = file;
            _line = line;
            _sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();

            WriteEntry("START", $"--> {_scopeName}", _member, _file, _line);
            Interlocked.Increment(ref _indentLevel);
        }

        public void Dispose()
        {
            Interlocked.Decrement(ref _indentLevel);
            _sw.Stop();
            WriteEntry("END  ", $"<-- {_scopeName} ({_sw.ElapsedMilliseconds} ms)", _member, _file, _line);
        }
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;

using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Windows.Forms;

public partial class DebugDialog : Form
{
    // Master switch: Toggled via Ribbon toggleButton. Defaults to true in Debug.
    public static bool DebugEnabled { get; set; } = true;

    // Session checkbox suppression inside the modal itself
    public static bool SuppressForSession { get; set; } = false;

    public DebugDialog(string title, string diagnosticLog)
    {
        Text = title;
        Size = new Size(680, 500);
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterScreen;
        FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.Sizable;
        TopMost = true;

        var txtLog = new TextBox
        {
            Multiline = true,
            ReadOnly = true,
            ScrollBars = ScrollBars.Both,
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            Font = new Font("Consolas", 9.5f),
            BackColor = Color.White,
            Text = diagnosticLog
        };

        var pnlBottom = new FlowLayoutPanel
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Bottom,
            Height = 44,
            FlowDirection = FlowDirection.LeftToRight,
            Padding = new Padding(10, 8, 10, 8)
        };

        var chkSuppress = new CheckBox
        {
            Text = "Don't show debugging for the rest of this session",
            AutoSize = true,
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 4, 20, 0)
        };
        chkSuppress.CheckedChanged += (s, e) => SuppressForSession = chkSuppress.Checked;

        var btnClose = new Button
        {
            Text = "Continue",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f, FontStyle.Bold),
            Width = 90,
            Height = 28,
            DialogResult = DialogResult.OK
        };
        btnClose.Click += (s, e) => Close();

        pnlBottom.Controls.Add(chkSuppress);
        pnlBottom.Controls.Add(btnClose);

        Controls.Add(txtLog);
        Controls.Add(pnlBottom);
        AcceptButton = btnClose;
    }

    public static void ShowLog(string title, string message)
    {
        // Universal gate: If the ribbon toggle is OFF or session is suppressed, silence completely
        if (!DebugEnabled || SuppressForSession) return;

        using var dlg = new DebugDialog(title, message);
        dlg.ShowDialog();
    }
}

FILE: Core\Integration\Database\Database.PostgreSqlService.cs

namespace SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Database;

using Dapper;
using Npgsql;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Data;
using System.Text;

public class PostgreSqlService
{
    private readonly string _connectionString;

    public PostgreSqlService(string? connectionString = null)
    {
        _connectionString = connectionString ??
            "Host=192.168.0.20;Port=5432;Database=sksb_db;Username=sksb_server;Password=sksb7811;";
    }

    private NpgsqlConnection CreateConnection()
    {
        ServerEnvironment.GuardConnection();
        return new NpgsqlConnection(_connectionString);
    }

    public IEnumerable<T> Query<T>(string sql, object? param = null)
    {
        using var db = CreateConnection();
        return db.Query<T>(sql, param);
    }

    public int Execute(string sql, object? param = null)
    {
        using var db = CreateConnection();
        return db.Execute(sql, param);
    }

    public void ExecuteTransaction<T>(string sql, IEnumerable<T> records)
    {
        using var db = CreateConnection();
        db.Open();
        using var trans = db.BeginTransaction();
        db.Execute(sql, records, transaction: trans);
        trans.Commit();
    }

    public void ExecuteInTransaction(Action<IDbConnection, IDbTransaction> action)
    {
        using var db = CreateConnection();
        db.Open();
        using var trans = db.BeginTransaction();
        try
        {
            action(db, trans);
            trans.Commit();
        }
        catch (PostgresException pgEx)
        {
            trans.Rollback();

            var errSb = new StringBuilder();
            errSb.AppendLine("==========================================================================");
            errSb.AppendLine($" POSTGRESQL TRANSACTION ERROR [{pgEx.SqlState}]");
            errSb.AppendLine("==========================================================================");
            errSb.AppendLine($"Message  : {pgEx.Message}");
            errSb.AppendLine($"Position : {pgEx.Position}");
            errSb.AppendLine($"Table    : {pgEx.TableName}");
            errSb.AppendLine($"Column   : {pgEx.ColumnName}");
            errSb.AppendLine($"Where    : {pgEx.Where}");
            errSb.AppendLine($"Routine  : {pgEx.Routine}");

            AppLogger.Error(errSb.ToString(), pgEx);
            throw;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            trans.Rollback();
            AppLogger.Error("ExecuteInTransaction general error", ex);
            throw;
        }
    }

    public T? ExecuteScalar<T>(string sql, object? param = null)
    {
        using var db = CreateConnection();
        db.Open();
        return db.ExecuteScalar<T>(sql, param);
    }
}
`n

FILE: Core\Integration\Database\Database.ServerDispatcher.cs
