namespace SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Database;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using Dapper;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;

public static class ServerDispatcher
{
    private static readonly PostgreSqlService Db = new();

    // =========================================================================
    // 1. UNIFIED DISPATCH ROUTER
    // =========================================================================
    public static void Dispatch<T>(T data)
    {
        switch (data)
        {
            // Staff Manager
            case IEnumerable<StaffRecord> staffBatch:
                PersistStaff(staffBatch.ToList(), isFullBatch: true);
                break;
            case StaffRecord singleStaff:
                PersistStaff(new[] { singleStaff }, isFullBatch: false);
                break;

            // Project Manager
            case IEnumerable<ProjectRecord> projectBatch:
                PersistProjects(projectBatch.ToList(), isFullBatch: true);
                break;
            case ProjectRecord singleProject:
                PersistProjects(new[] { singleProject }, isFullBatch: false);
                break;

            // Company Master
            case IEnumerable<CompanyRecord> companyBatch:
                PersistCompanies(companyBatch.ToList(), isFullBatch: true);
                break;
            case CompanyRecord singleCompany:
                PersistCompanies(new[] { singleCompany }, isFullBatch: false);
                break;

            // Vehicle Manager
            case IEnumerable<VehicleRecord> vehicleBatch:
                PersistVehicles(vehicleBatch.ToList(), isFullBatch: true);
                break;
            case VehicleRecord singleVehicle:
                PersistVehicles(new[] { singleVehicle }, isFullBatch: false);
                break;

            // Standalone Child Entity Collections
            case IEnumerable<VehicleLicense> vLicenses:
                PersistStaffVehicleLicenses(vLicenses);
                break;
            case IEnumerable<SkillLicense> sLicenses:
                PersistStaffSkillLicenses(sLicenses);
                break;
            case IEnumerable<SkillLicenseType> sTypes:
                PersistSkillLicenseTypes(sTypes);
                break;
        }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 2. VEHICLE MANAGER PERSISTENCE
    // =========================================================================
    public static void PersistVehicles(IList<VehicleRecord> vehicles, bool isFullBatch)
    {
        var validVehicles = vehicles.Where(v => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v.VehiclePlate)).ToList();
        if (!validVehicles.Any()) return;

        var activePlates = validVehicles.Select(v => v.VehiclePlate.Trim().ToUpperInvariant()).ToArray();

        var masterRecords = validVehicles.Select(v =>
        {
            var latestRoadTax = v.RoadTaxes.OrderByDescending(r => r.ExpiryDate).FirstOrDefault();
            var roadTaxExpiry = v.VehicleDetail.RoadTaxExpiry ?? latestRoadTax?.ExpiryDate;
            var latestInspection = v.Inspections.OrderByDescending(i => i.ExpiryDate).FirstOrDefault();
            var inspectionExpiry = v.VehicleDetail.InspectionExpiry ?? latestInspection?.ExpiryDate;
            return new
            {
                VehiclePlate = v.VehiclePlate.Trim().ToUpperInvariant(),
                Vin = v.VehicleDetail.VIN ?? string.Empty,
                VehicleType = v.VehicleDetail.Type.ToString(),
                Brand = v.VehicleDetail.Brand ?? string.Empty,
                Model = v.VehicleDetail.Model ?? string.Empty,
                RoadTaxExpiry = roadTaxExpiry?.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
                InspectionExpiry = inspectionExpiry?.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
                CurrentOdometer = v.VehicleDetail.CurrentOdometer,
                BoughtDate = v.VehicleDetail.BoughtDate.HasValue
                    ? (DateTime?)v.VehicleDetail.BoughtDate.Value.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue)
                    : null,
                BoughtPrice = v.VehicleDetail.BoughtPrice,
                SoldDate = v.VehicleDetail.SoldDate.HasValue
                    ? (DateTime?)v.VehicleDetail.SoldDate.Value.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue)
                    : null,
                SoldPrice = v.VehicleDetail.SoldPrice,
                Status = v.VehicleStatus.ToString()
            };
        }).ToList();

        var maintenanceRecords = validVehicles.SelectMany(v => v.Maintenances.Select(m =>
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(m.VehiclePlate)) m.VehiclePlate = v.VehiclePlate;
            return new
            {
                MaintenanceId = m.MaintenanceID,
                VehiclePlate = m.VehiclePlate.Trim().ToUpperInvariant(),
                ServiceType = m.ServiceType.ToString(),
                DateIn = m.DateIn.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
                EstimatedReturn = m.EstimatedReturn.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
                DateCompleted = m.DateCompleted.HasValue ? (DateTime?)m.DateCompleted.Value.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue) : null,
                OdometerIn = m.OdometerIn,
                OdometerOut = m.OdometerOut,
                RepairCost = m.RepairCost,
                WorkshopName = m.WorkshopName,
                Status = m.MaintenanceStatus.ToString(),
                Remarks = m.Remarks,
                TyreCost = m.TyreCost,
                BrakeCost = m.BrakeCost,
                ClutchCost = m.ClutchCost,
                ServiceCost = m.ServiceCost,
                FuelPumpCost = m.FuelPumpCost,
                AircondCost = m.AircondCost,
                WiringCost = m.WiringCost,
                BatteryCost = m.BatteryCost,
                TowingCost = m.TowingCost,
                CushionCost = m.CushionCost,
                PartsCost = m.PartsCost
            };
        })).ToList();

        var inspectionRecords = validVehicles.SelectMany(v => v.Inspections.Select(i =>
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(i.VehiclePlate)) i.VehiclePlate = v.VehiclePlate;
            return new
            {
                InspectionId = i.InspectionID,
                VehiclePlate = i.VehiclePlate.Trim().ToUpperInvariant(),
                ScheduleDateIn = i.ScheduleDateIn.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
                DateOut = i.DateOut.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
                ExpiryDate = i.ExpiryDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
                Amount = i.Amount,
                Status = i.Status.ToString(),
                Address = i.Address ?? string.Empty,
                Remarks = i.Remarks ?? string.Empty
            };
        })).ToList();

        var roadTaxRecords = validVehicles.SelectMany(v => v.RoadTaxes.Select(rt =>
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rt.VehiclePlate)) rt.VehiclePlate = v.VehiclePlate;
            return new
            {
                RoadTaxId = rt.RoadTaxID,
                VehiclePlate = rt.VehiclePlate.Trim().ToUpperInvariant(),
                VehicleType = rt.VehicleType.ToString(),
                RenewDate = rt.RenewDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
                ExpiryDate = rt.ExpiryDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
                Amount = rt.Amount,
                Status = rt.Status.ToString(),
                Remarks = rt.Remarks ?? string.Empty
            };
        })).ToList();

        var permitRecords = validVehicles.SelectMany(v => v.Permits.Select(p =>
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(p.VehiclePlate)) p.VehiclePlate = v.VehiclePlate;
            return new
            {
                PermitId = p.PermitID,
                VehiclePlate = p.VehiclePlate.Trim().ToUpperInvariant(),
                VehicleType = p.VehicleType.ToString(),
                PermitType = p.PermitType.ToString(),
                RenewDate = p.RenewDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
                ExpiryDate = p.ExpiryDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
                Amount = p.Amount,
                Status = p.Status.ToString(),
                Remarks = p.Remarks ?? string.Empty
            };
        })).ToList();

        var insuranceRecords = validVehicles.SelectMany(v => v.Insurances.Select(ins =>
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ins.VehiclePlate)) ins.VehiclePlate = v.VehiclePlate;
            return new
            {
                InsuranceId = ins.InsuranceID,
                VehiclePlate = ins.VehiclePlate.Trim().ToUpperInvariant(),
                VehicleType = ins.VehicleType.ToString(),
                InsuranceCompany = ins.InsuranceCompany ?? string.Empty,
                InsuranceType = ins.InsuranceType.ToString(),
                RenewDate = ins.RenewDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
                ExpiryDate = ins.ExpiryDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
                Amount = ins.Amount,
                Status = ins.Status.ToString(),
                Remarks = ins.Remarks ?? string.Empty,
                InsuranceNumber = ins.InsuranceNumber ?? string.Empty // <-- Added
            };
        })).ToList();

        var odoRecords = validVehicles.SelectMany(v => v.OdometerLogs.Select(o =>
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(o.VehiclePlate)) o.VehiclePlate = v.VehiclePlate;
            return new
            {
                OdometerId = o.OdometerID,
                Date = o.Date.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
                VehiclePlate = o.VehiclePlate.Trim().ToUpperInvariant(),
                VehicleType = o.VehicleType.ToString(),
                StaffId = o.StaffID,                               // <-- Updated from DrivenBy
                Project = o.Project ?? string.Empty,
                OdometerBefore = o.OdometerBefore,
                CurrentOdometer = o.CurrentOdometer,
                FuelCardNumber = o.FuelCardNumber ?? string.Empty,
                FuelCompany = o.FuelCompany ?? string.Empty,
                StationName = o.StationName ?? string.Empty,
                ReceiptNumber = o.ReceiptNumber ?? string.Empty,
                Amount = o.Amount,
                Remarks = o.Remarks ?? string.Empty
            };
        })).ToList();

        // 2. Define UPSERT queries
        const string sqlMasterUpsert = @"
INSERT INTO vehicle_master (
    vehicle_plate, vin, vehicle_type, brand, model,
    road_tax_expiry, inspection_expiry, current_odometer,
    bought_date, bought_price, sold_date, sold_price,
    status
)
VALUES (
    @VehiclePlate, @Vin, @VehicleType, @Brand, @Model,
    @RoadTaxExpiry, @InspectionExpiry, @CurrentOdometer,
    @BoughtDate, @BoughtPrice, @SoldDate, @SoldPrice,
    @Status
)
ON CONFLICT (vehicle_plate) DO UPDATE SET
    vin               = COALESCE(NULLIF(EXCLUDED.vin, ''), vehicle_master.vin),
    vehicle_type      = EXCLUDED.vehicle_type,
    brand             = COALESCE(NULLIF(EXCLUDED.brand, ''), vehicle_master.brand),
    model             = COALESCE(NULLIF(EXCLUDED.model, ''), vehicle_master.model),
    road_tax_expiry   = EXCLUDED.road_tax_expiry,
    inspection_expiry = EXCLUDED.inspection_expiry,
    current_odometer  = CASE WHEN EXCLUDED.current_odometer > 0 THEN EXCLUDED.current_odometer ELSE vehicle_master.current_odometer END,
    bought_date       = EXCLUDED.bought_date,
    bought_price      = EXCLUDED.bought_price,
    sold_date         = EXCLUDED.sold_date,
    sold_price        = EXCLUDED.sold_price,
    status            = EXCLUDED.status,
    updated_at        = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;";

        const string sqlMaintenanceUpsert = @"
        INSERT INTO vehicle_maintenance (
            maintenance_id, vehicle_plate, service_type, date_in, estimated_return,
            date_completed, odometer_in, odometer_out, repair_cost, workshop_name,
            status, remarks,
            tyre_cost, brake_cost, clutch_cost, service_cost, fuel_pump_cost,
            aircond_cost, wiring_cost, battery_cost, towing_cost, cushion_cost, parts_cost
        )
        VALUES (
            @MaintenanceId, @VehiclePlate, @ServiceType, @DateIn, @EstimatedReturn,
            @DateCompleted, @OdometerIn, @OdometerOut, @RepairCost, @WorkshopName,
            @Status, @Remarks,
            @TyreCost, @BrakeCost, @ClutchCost, @ServiceCost, @FuelPumpCost,
            @AircondCost, @WiringCost, @BatteryCost, @TowingCost, @CushionCost, @PartsCost
        )
        ON CONFLICT (maintenance_id) DO UPDATE SET
            vehicle_plate    = EXCLUDED.vehicle_plate,
            service_type     = EXCLUDED.service_type,
            date_in          = EXCLUDED.date_in,
            estimated_return = EXCLUDED.estimated_return,
            date_completed   = EXCLUDED.date_completed,
            odometer_in      = EXCLUDED.odometer_in,
            odometer_out     = EXCLUDED.odometer_out,
            repair_cost      = EXCLUDED.repair_cost,
            workshop_name    = EXCLUDED.workshop_name,
            status           = EXCLUDED.status,
            remarks          = EXCLUDED.remarks,
            tyre_cost        = EXCLUDED.tyre_cost,
            brake_cost       = EXCLUDED.brake_cost,
            clutch_cost      = EXCLUDED.clutch_cost,
            service_cost     = EXCLUDED.service_cost,
            fuel_pump_cost   = EXCLUDED.fuel_pump_cost,
            aircond_cost     = EXCLUDED.aircond_cost,
            wiring_cost      = EXCLUDED.wiring_cost,
            battery_cost     = EXCLUDED.battery_cost,
            towing_cost      = EXCLUDED.towing_cost,
            cushion_cost     = EXCLUDED.cushion_cost,
            parts_cost       = EXCLUDED.parts_cost,
            updated_at       = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;";

        const string sqlInspectionUpsert = @"
            INSERT INTO vehicle_inspections (
                inspection_id, vehicle_plate, schedule_date_in, date_out,
                expiry_date, amount, status, address, remarks, updated_at
            ) VALUES (
                @InspectionId, @VehiclePlate, @ScheduleDateIn, @DateOut,
                @ExpiryDate, @Amount, @Status, @Address, @Remarks, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            )
            ON CONFLICT (inspection_id) DO UPDATE SET
                vehicle_plate    = EXCLUDED.vehicle_plate,
                schedule_date_in = EXCLUDED.schedule_date_in,
                date_out         = EXCLUDED.date_out,
                expiry_date      = EXCLUDED.expiry_date,
                amount           = EXCLUDED.amount,
                status           = EXCLUDED.status,
                address          = EXCLUDED.address,
                remarks          = EXCLUDED.remarks,
                updated_at       = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;";

        const string sqlRoadTaxUpsert = @"
INSERT INTO vehicle_road_taxes (
    road_tax_id, vehicle_plate, vehicle_type, renew_date, expiry_date, amount, status, remarks, updated_at
) VALUES (
    @RoadTaxId, @VehiclePlate, @VehicleType, @RenewDate, @ExpiryDate, @Amount, @Status, @Remarks, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
)
ON CONFLICT (road_tax_id) DO UPDATE SET
    vehicle_plate = EXCLUDED.vehicle_plate,
    vehicle_type  = EXCLUDED.vehicle_type,
    renew_date    = EXCLUDED.renew_date,
    expiry_date   = EXCLUDED.expiry_date,
    amount        = EXCLUDED.amount,
    status        = EXCLUDED.status,
    remarks       = EXCLUDED.remarks,
    updated_at    = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;";

        const string sqlPermitUpsert = @"
INSERT INTO vehicle_permits (
    permit_id, vehicle_plate, vehicle_type, permit_type, renew_date, expiry_date, amount, status, remarks, updated_at
) VALUES (
    @PermitId, @VehiclePlate, @VehicleType, @PermitType, @RenewDate, @ExpiryDate, @Amount, @Status, @Remarks, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
)
ON CONFLICT (permit_id) DO UPDATE SET
    vehicle_plate = EXCLUDED.vehicle_plate,
    vehicle_type  = EXCLUDED.vehicle_type,
    permit_type   = EXCLUDED.permit_type,
    renew_date    = EXCLUDED.renew_date,
    expiry_date   = EXCLUDED.expiry_date,
    amount        = EXCLUDED.amount,
    status        = EXCLUDED.status,
    remarks       = EXCLUDED.remarks,
    updated_at    = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;";

        const string sqlInsuranceUpsert = @"
INSERT INTO vehicle_insurances (
    insurance_id, vehicle_plate, vehicle_type, insurance_company, insurance_type, 
    renew_date, expiry_date, amount, status, remarks, updated_at, insurance_number
) VALUES (
    @InsuranceId, @VehiclePlate, @VehicleType, @InsuranceCompany, @InsuranceType, 
    @RenewDate, @ExpiryDate, @Amount, @Status, @Remarks, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, @InsuranceNumber
)
ON CONFLICT (insurance_id) DO UPDATE SET
    vehicle_plate     = EXCLUDED.vehicle_plate,
    vehicle_type      = EXCLUDED.vehicle_type,
    insurance_company = EXCLUDED.insurance_company,
    insurance_type    = EXCLUDED.insurance_type,
    renew_date        = EXCLUDED.renew_date,
    expiry_date       = EXCLUDED.expiry_date,
    amount            = EXCLUDED.amount,
    status            = EXCLUDED.status,
    remarks           = EXCLUDED.remarks,
    insurance_number  = EXCLUDED.insurance_number, -- <-- Added
    updated_at        = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;";

        const string sqlOdometerUpsert = @"
INSERT INTO vehicle_odometers (
    odometer_id, date, vehicle_plate, vehicle_type, staff_id, project,
    odometer_before, current_odometer, fuel_card_number, fuel_company,
    station_name, receipt_number, amount, remarks, updated_at
) VALUES (
    @OdometerId, @Date, @VehiclePlate, @VehicleType, @StaffId, @Project,
    @OdometerBefore, @CurrentOdometer, @FuelCardNumber, @FuelCompany,
    @StationName, @ReceiptNumber, @Amount, @Remarks, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
)
ON CONFLICT (odometer_id) DO UPDATE SET
    date             = EXCLUDED.date,
    vehicle_plate    = EXCLUDED.vehicle_plate,
    vehicle_type     = EXCLUDED.vehicle_type,
    staff_id         = EXCLUDED.staff_id,
    project          = EXCLUDED.project,
    odometer_before  = EXCLUDED.odometer_before,
    current_odometer = EXCLUDED.current_odometer,
    fuel_card_number = EXCLUDED.fuel_card_number,
    fuel_company     = EXCLUDED.fuel_company,
    station_name     = EXCLUDED.station_name,
    receipt_number   = EXCLUDED.receipt_number,
    amount           = EXCLUDED.amount,
    remarks          = EXCLUDED.remarks,
    updated_at       = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;";

        // 3. Execute in Transaction
        Db.ExecuteInTransaction((conn, trans) =>
        {
            if (isFullBatch)
            {
                conn.Execute("DELETE FROM vehicle_inspections WHERE vehicle_plate != ALL(@ActivePlates);", new { ActivePlates = activePlates }, trans);
                conn.Execute("DELETE FROM vehicle_road_taxes WHERE vehicle_plate != ALL(@ActivePlates);", new { ActivePlates = activePlates }, trans);
                conn.Execute("DELETE FROM vehicle_permits WHERE vehicle_plate != ALL(@ActivePlates);", new { ActivePlates = activePlates }, trans);
                conn.Execute("DELETE FROM vehicle_insurances WHERE vehicle_plate != ALL(@ActivePlates);", new { ActivePlates = activePlates }, trans);
                conn.Execute("DELETE FROM vehicle_odometers WHERE vehicle_plate != ALL(@ActivePlates);", new { ActivePlates = activePlates }, trans);
                conn.Execute("DELETE FROM vehicle_maintenance WHERE vehicle_plate != ALL(@ActivePlates);", new { ActivePlates = activePlates }, trans);
                conn.Execute("DELETE FROM vehicle_master WHERE vehicle_plate != ALL(@ActivePlates);", new { ActivePlates = activePlates }, trans);
            }

            conn.Execute(sqlMasterUpsert, masterRecords, trans);

            if (maintenanceRecords.Any()) conn.Execute(sqlMaintenanceUpsert, maintenanceRecords, trans);
            if (inspectionRecords.Any()) conn.Execute(sqlInspectionUpsert, inspectionRecords, trans);
            if (roadTaxRecords.Any()) conn.Execute(sqlRoadTaxUpsert, roadTaxRecords, trans);
            if (permitRecords.Any()) conn.Execute(sqlPermitUpsert, permitRecords, trans);
            if (insuranceRecords.Any()) conn.Execute(sqlInsuranceUpsert, insuranceRecords, trans);
            if (odoRecords.Any()) conn.Execute(sqlOdometerUpsert, odoRecords, trans);
        });

#if DEBUG
        var sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.AppendLine("=== POSTGRESQL VEHICLE UPLOAD COMMITTED ===");
        sb.AppendLine($"Committed Vehicle Profiles: {validVehicles.Count}");
        sb.AppendLine($"Timestamp: {DateTime.Now:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss}");
        DebugDialog.ShowLog("[DEBUG] PostgreSQL Upload - Vehicle Summary", sb.ToString());
#endif
    }

    public static void SaveAuxiliaryTables(
        IEnumerable<Workshop> workshops,
        IEnumerable<InsuranceCompanyRecord> insuranceCompanies,
        IEnumerable<FuelCardRecord> fuelCards,
        IEnumerable<string> fuelCompanies)
    {
        var compList = fuelCompanies?
            .Where(n => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(n))
            .Select(n => n.Trim())
            .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            .ToList() ?? new List<string>();

        var workshopList = workshops?.ToList() ?? new List<Workshop>();
        var insuranceList = insuranceCompanies?.ToList() ?? new List<InsuranceCompanyRecord>();
        var cardList = fuelCards?.ToList() ?? new List<FuelCardRecord>();
        var companyNames = fuelCompanies?
            .Where(n => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(n))
            .Select(n => n.Trim())
            .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            .ToArray() ?? Array.Empty<string>();

        Db.ExecuteInTransaction((conn, trans) =>
        {
            // Workshops
            if (workshopList.Count > 0)
            {
                conn.Execute(@"
                    INSERT INTO vehicle_workshops (workshop_id, workshop_name, workshop_address, workshop_owner, primary_contact, secondary_contact)
                    VALUES (@WorkshopID, @WorkshopName, @WorkshopAddress, @WorkshopOwner, @PrimaryContact, @SecondaryContact)
                    ON CONFLICT (workshop_id) DO UPDATE SET
                        workshop_name = EXCLUDED.workshop_name,
                        workshop_address = EXCLUDED.workshop_address,
                        workshop_owner = EXCLUDED.workshop_owner,
                        primary_contact = EXCLUDED.primary_contact,
                        secondary_contact = EXCLUDED.secondary_contact,
                        updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;", workshopList, trans);
            }

            // Insurance Companies
            if (insuranceList.Count > 0)
            {
                conn.Execute(@"
                    INSERT INTO vehicle_insurance_companies (insurance_company, company_contact_number, company_address, insurance_agent, agent_contact_number)
                    VALUES (@InsuranceCompany, @CompanyContactNumber, @CompanyAddress, @InsuranceAgent, @AgentContactNumber)
                    ON CONFLICT (insurance_company) DO UPDATE SET
                        company_contact_number = EXCLUDED.company_contact_number,
                        company_address = EXCLUDED.company_address,
                        insurance_agent = EXCLUDED.insurance_agent,
                        agent_contact_number = EXCLUDED.agent_contact_number,
                        updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;", insuranceList, trans);
            }

            // Fuel Companies Lookup - Sync
            if (compList.Count > 0)
            {
                // A. Delete only companies removed from Excel that are NO LONGER referenced by any fuel card
                const string sqlReconcile = @"
                    DELETE FROM lookup_fuel_companies 
                    WHERE fuel_company_name != ALL(@ActiveNames);";

                conn.Execute(sqlReconcile, new { ActiveNames = companyNames }, transaction: trans);

                // B. Insert newly added companies
                const string sqlUpsert = @"
                    INSERT INTO lookup_fuel_companies (fuel_company_name)
                    VALUES (@Name)
                    ON CONFLICT (fuel_company_name) DO NOTHING;";

                var payload = companyNames.Select(n => new { Name = n });
                conn.Execute(sqlUpsert, payload, transaction: trans);
            }
            else
            {
                // If the table was deliberately wiped completely
                conn.Execute("DELETE FROM lookup_fuel_companies;", transaction: trans);
            }

            // Fuel Cards
            if (cardList.Count > 0)
            {
                conn.Execute(@"
                    INSERT INTO vehicle_fuel_cards (fuel_card_number, fuel_company_name, card_owner_id, card_owner_name)
                    VALUES (@FuelCardNumber, @FuelCompanyName, @CardOwnerID, @CardOwnerName)
                    ON CONFLICT (fuel_card_number) DO UPDATE SET
                        fuel_company_name = EXCLUDED.fuel_company_name,
                        card_owner_id = EXCLUDED.card_owner_id,
                        card_owner_name = EXCLUDED.card_owner_name,
                        updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;", cardList, trans);
            }
        });
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 3. STAFF MANAGER PERSISTENCE
    // =========================================================================
    public static void PersistStaff(IList<StaffRecord> staffList, bool isFullBatch)
    {
        var staffItems = staffList.Where(s => s.StaffID > 0).ToList();
        if (!staffItems.Any()) return;

        var activeIds = staffItems.Select(s => s.StaffID).ToArray();

        var masterRecords = staffItems.Select(s => new
        {
            StaffId = s.StaffID,
            StaffName = s.StaffDetails.StaffName ?? string.Empty,
            ShortName = s.StaffDetails.StaffShortName ?? string.Empty,
            Department = s.StaffDetails.Department ?? "Worker",
            Nric = s.StaffDetails.NRIC,
            Passport = s.StaffDetails.Passport ?? string.Empty,
            Phone = s.StaffDetails.Phone ?? string.Empty,
            JoinDate = s.StaffDetails.JoinDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
            ResignDate = s.StaffDetails.ResignDate.HasValue ? (DateTime?)s.StaffDetails.ResignDate.Value.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue) : null,
            ContractStatus = s.StaffDetails.ContractStatus.ToString()
        }).ToList();

        const string sqlMasterUpsert = @"
        INSERT INTO staff_master (
            staff_id, staff_name, short_name, department, nric, passport, phone, join_date, resign_date, contract_status
        )
        VALUES (
            @StaffId, @StaffName, @ShortName, @Department, @Nric, @Passport, @Phone, @JoinDate, @ResignDate, @ContractStatus
        )
        ON CONFLICT (staff_id) DO UPDATE SET
            staff_name      = EXCLUDED.staff_name,
            short_name      = EXCLUDED.short_name,
            department      = EXCLUDED.department,
            nric            = EXCLUDED.nric,
            passport        = EXCLUDED.passport,
            phone           = EXCLUDED.phone,
            join_date       = EXCLUDED.join_date,
            resign_date     = EXCLUDED.resign_date,
            contract_status = EXCLUDED.contract_status,
            updated_at      = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;";

        Db.ExecuteInTransaction((conn, trans) =>
        {
            if (isFullBatch)
            {
                conn.Execute("DELETE FROM staff_vehicle_licenses WHERE staff_id != ALL(@ActiveIds);", new { ActiveIds = activeIds }, trans);
                conn.Execute("DELETE FROM staff_skill_licenses WHERE staff_id != ALL(@ActiveIds);", new { ActiveIds = activeIds }, trans);
                conn.Execute("DELETE FROM staff_master WHERE staff_id != ALL(@ActiveIds);", new { ActiveIds = activeIds }, trans);
            }

            conn.Execute(sqlMasterUpsert, masterRecords, trans);
        });

        // Child Licenses
        var allVLicenses = staffItems.SelectMany(s => s.VehicleLicenses.Select(l => { if (l.StaffID == 0) l.StaffID = s.StaffID; return l; })).ToList();
        PersistStaffVehicleLicenses(allVLicenses);

        var allSLicenses = staffItems.SelectMany(s => s.SkillLicenses.Select(l => { if (l.StaffID == 0) l.StaffID = s.StaffID; return l; })).ToList();
        PersistStaffSkillLicenses(allSLicenses);
    }

    private static void PersistStaffVehicleLicenses(IEnumerable<VehicleLicense> licenses)
    {
        var items = licenses.Where(l => l.StaffID > 0 && l.VehicleLicenseClass != VehicleLicenseClass.None).Select(l => new
        {
            VehicleLicenseId = l.VehicleLicenseID,
            StaffId = l.StaffID,
            VehicleLicenseClass = l.VehicleLicenseClass.ToString(),
            RenewDate = l.RenewDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
            ExpiryDate = l.ExpiryDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
            Status = l.VehicleLicenseStatus.ToString(),
            Cost = l.Cost
        }).ToList();

        if (!items.Any()) return;

        Db.ExecuteTransaction(@"
            INSERT INTO staff_vehicle_licenses (vehicle_license_id, staff_id, vehicle_license_class, renew_date, expiry_date, status, cost)
            VALUES (
                CASE WHEN @VehicleLicenseId > 0 THEN @VehicleLicenseId ELSE nextval('staff_vehicle_licenses_vehicle_license_id_seq') END,
                @StaffId, @VehicleLicenseClass, @RenewDate, @ExpiryDate, @Status, @Cost
            )
            ON CONFLICT (staff_id, vehicle_license_class) DO UPDATE SET
                renew_date  = EXCLUDED.renew_date,
                expiry_date = EXCLUDED.expiry_date,
                status      = EXCLUDED.status,
                cost        = EXCLUDED.cost,
                updated_at  = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;", items);
    }

    private static void PersistStaffSkillLicenses(IEnumerable<SkillLicense> licenses)
    {
        var items = licenses.Where(s => s.StaffID > 0 && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.SkillLicenseType)).Select(s => new
        {
            SkillLicenseId = s.SkillLicenseID,
            StaffId = s.StaffID,
            SkillLicenseType = s.SkillLicenseType.Trim(),
            RenewDate = s.RenewDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
            ExpiryDate = s.ExpiryDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
            Status = s.SkillLicenseStatus.ToString(),
            Cost = s.Cost
        }).ToList();

        if (!items.Any()) return;

        Db.ExecuteTransaction(@"
            INSERT INTO staff_skill_licenses (skill_license_id, staff_id, skill_license_type, renew_date, expiry_date, status, cost)
            VALUES (
                CASE WHEN @SkillLicenseId > 0 THEN @SkillLicenseId ELSE nextval('staff_skill_licenses_skill_license_id_seq') END,
                @StaffId, @SkillLicenseType, @RenewDate, @ExpiryDate, @Status, @Cost
            )
            ON CONFLICT (staff_id, skill_license_type) DO UPDATE SET
                renew_date  = EXCLUDED.renew_date,
                expiry_date = EXCLUDED.expiry_date,
                status      = EXCLUDED.status,
                cost        = EXCLUDED.cost,
                updated_at  = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;", items);
    }

    private static void PersistSkillLicenseTypes(IEnumerable<SkillLicenseType> types)
    {
        var names = types.Where(t => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(t.SkillLicenseName)).Select(t => t.SkillLicenseName.Trim()).Distinct().ToList();
        Db.ExecuteInTransaction((conn, trans) =>
        {
            conn.Execute("DELETE FROM lookup_skill_license_types WHERE skill_license_type != ALL(@Names);", new { Names = names.ToArray() }, trans);
            conn.Execute(@"
                INSERT INTO lookup_skill_license_types (skill_license_type, description)
                VALUES (@SkillLicenseType, '')
                ON CONFLICT (skill_license_type) DO NOTHING;", names.Select(n => new { SkillLicenseType = n }), trans);
        });
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 4. PROJECT & COMPANY PERSISTENCE
    // =========================================================================
    public static void PersistProjects(IList<ProjectRecord> projects, bool isFullBatch)
    {
        var items = projects.Where(p => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(p.ProjectID)).ToList();
        if (!items.Any()) return;

        var activeIds = items.Select(p => p.ProjectID.Trim()).ToArray();
        var records = items.Select(p => new
        {
            ProjectId = p.ProjectID.Trim(),
            ProjectName = p.ProjectDetails.ProjectName ?? string.Empty,
            ProjectType = p.ProjectDetails.ProjectType.ToString(),
            CompanyName = p.ProjectDetails.CompanyName ?? string.Empty,
            StartDate = p.ProjectDetails.StartDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue),
            TargetEndDate = p.ProjectDetails.TargetEndDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue)
        }).ToList();

        Db.ExecuteInTransaction((conn, trans) =>
        {
            if (isFullBatch)
            {
                conn.Execute("DELETE FROM project_master WHERE project_id != ALL(@ActiveIds);", new { ActiveIds = activeIds }, trans);
            }

            conn.Execute(@"
                INSERT INTO project_master (project_id, project_name, project_type, company_name, start_date, target_end_date)
                VALUES (@ProjectId, @ProjectName, @ProjectType, @CompanyName, @StartDate, @TargetEndDate)
                ON CONFLICT (project_id) DO UPDATE SET
                    project_name    = EXCLUDED.project_name,
                    project_type    = CASE WHEN EXCLUDED.project_type != 'None' THEN EXCLUDED.project_type ELSE project_master.project_type END,
                    company_name    = EXCLUDED.company_name,
                    start_date      = EXCLUDED.start_date,
                    target_end_date = EXCLUDED.target_end_date,
                    updated_at      = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;", records, trans);
        });
    }

    public static void PersistCompanies(IList<CompanyRecord> companies, bool isFullBatch)
    {
        var items = companies.Where(c => c.CompanyID > 0).ToList();
        if (!items.Any()) return;

        var activeIds = items.Select(c => c.CompanyID).ToArray();
        var records = items.Select(c => new
        {
            CompanyId = c.CompanyID,
            LegalCompanyName = c.LegalCompanyName ?? string.Empty,
            ShortCompanyName = c.ShortCompanyName ?? string.Empty
        }).ToList();

        Db.ExecuteInTransaction((conn, trans) =>
        {
            if (isFullBatch)
            {
                conn.Execute("DELETE FROM company_master WHERE company_id != ALL(@ActiveIds);", new { ActiveIds = activeIds }, trans);
            }

            conn.Execute(@"
                INSERT INTO company_master (company_id, legal_company_name, short_company_name)
                VALUES (@CompanyId, @LegalCompanyName, @ShortCompanyName)
                ON CONFLICT (company_id) DO UPDATE SET
                    legal_company_name = EXCLUDED.legal_company_name,
                    short_company_name = EXCLUDED.short_company_name,
                    updated_at         = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;", records, trans);
        });
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Database;

using System;

public static class ServerEnvironment
{
#if DEBUG
    private static bool _simulateOffline = false;
#else
    private static readonly bool _simulateOffline = false;
#endif

    public static bool SimulateOffline
    {
        get => _simulateOffline;
        set
        {
#if DEBUG
            _simulateOffline = value;
#else
            throw new InvalidOperationException("Offline simulation is disabled in Release builds.");
#endif

        }
    }

    public static bool IsSimulatedOffline()
    {
#if DEBUG
        return SimulateOffline;
#else
        return false;
#endif
    }

    public static void ToggleSimulatedOffline(ExcelDna.Integration.CustomUI.IRibbonUI? ribbonUi, string controlId, bool pressed)
    {
#if DEBUG
        SimulateOffline = pressed;
        ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl(controlId);
        System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show(
            pressed
                ? "Server is now SIMULATED OFFLINE.\nAll PostgreSQL database operations will fail immediately to test offline local stashing."
                : "Server is now ONLINE.\nNormal PostgreSQL database connectivity restored.",
            "Debug Server Mode",
            System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons.OK,
            pressed ? System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon.Warning : System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon.Information
        );
#endif
    }

    public static void GuardConnection()
    {
        if (SimulateOffline)
        {
            throw new TimeoutException("[Simulated Offline Mode Active] Connection to PostgreSQL server blocked by user switch.");
        }
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Erp;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;

public static class ErpExporter
{
    public static void ExportToFile(
        string filePath,
        IEnumerable<string> headers,
        IEnumerable<IEnumerable<object?>> rows,
        char delimiter = ',',
        Encoding? encoding = null)
    {
        string content = BuildDelimitedContent(headers, rows, delimiter);
        string? directory = Path.GetDirectoryName(filePath);
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(directory) && !Directory.Exists(directory))
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        }
        File.WriteAllText(filePath, content, encoding ?? Encoding.UTF8);
    }

    public static void ExportEntitiesToFile<T>(
        string filePath,
        IEnumerable<string> headers,
        IEnumerable<T> records,
        Func<T, IEnumerable<object?>> rowMapper,
        char delimiter = ',',
        Encoding? encoding = null)
    {
        var rows = new List<IEnumerable<object?>>();
        foreach (var record in records)
        {
            rows.Add(rowMapper(record));
        }
        ExportToFile(filePath, headers, rows, delimiter, encoding);
    }

    public static string BuildDelimitedContent(
        IEnumerable<string> headers,
        IEnumerable<IEnumerable<object?>> rows,
        char delimiter = ',')
    {
        var sb = new StringBuilder();

        sb.AppendLine(string.Join(delimiter, headers.Select(h => ErpHelper.EscapeDelimitedCell(h, delimiter))));

        foreach (var row in rows)
        {
            sb.AppendLine(string.Join(delimiter, row.Select(cell => ErpHelper.EscapeDelimitedCell(cell, delimiter))));
        }

        return sb.ToString();
    }
}

FILE: Core\Integration\Erp\Erp.Helper.cs

namespace SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Erp;

using System;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

public static class ErpHelper
{
    private static readonly string[] DateFormats =
    {
        "dd/MM/yyyy",
        "d/M/yyyy",
        "dd-MM-yyyy",
        "d-M-yyyy",
        "yyyy-MM-dd",
        "yyyy/MM/dd"
    };

    /// <summary>
    /// Normalizes header strings by stripping whitespace, underscores, dots, and hyphens to allow tolerant lookups.
    /// Internal to prevent Excel-DNA UDF registration collisions.
    /// </summary>
    internal static string NormalizeHeader(string header)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(header)) return string.Empty;
        return Regex.Replace(header.Trim(), @"[\s_\-\.]+", string.Empty).ToUpperInvariant();
    }

    public static string[] SplitDelimitedLine(string line, char delimiter = ',')
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(line)) return Array.Empty<string>();
        var pattern = $"{delimiter}(?=(?:[^\"]*\"[^\"]*\")*[^\"]*$)";
        return Regex.Split(line, pattern)
                    .Select(col => col.Trim().Trim('"').Replace("\"\"", "\""))
                    .ToArray();
    }

    public static string EscapeDelimitedCell(object? value, char delimiter = ',')
    {
        if (value is null) return string.Empty;

        string stringVal = value switch
        {
            DateOnly d => d.ToString("dd/MM/yyyy", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
            DateTime dt => dt.ToString("dd/MM/yyyy", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
            decimal dec => dec.ToString("F2", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
            double dbl => dbl.ToString("F2", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
            float flt => flt.ToString("F2", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture),
            _ => value.ToString() ?? string.Empty
        };

        if (stringVal.Contains(delimiter) || stringVal.Contains('"') || stringVal.Contains('\n') || stringVal.Contains('\r'))
        {
            return $"\"{stringVal.Replace("\"", "\"\"")}\"";
        }
        return stringVal;
    }

    public static DateOnly? ParseDateOnly(string value)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value)) return null;

        string cleaned = value.Replace("#", string.Empty).Trim();
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cleaned)) return null;

        if (double.TryParse(cleaned, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double oaDate) && oaDate > 30000 && oaDate < 80000)
        {
            return DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.FromOADate(oaDate));
        }

        if (DateOnly.TryParseExact(cleaned, DateFormats, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DateTimeStyles.None, out var date))
            return date;

        if (DateOnly.TryParse(cleaned, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DateTimeStyles.None, out date))
            return date;

        if (DateTime.TryParse(cleaned, out var dt))
            return DateOnly.FromDateTime(dt);

        return null;
    }

    public static decimal ParseDecimal(string value, decimal defaultValue = 0.00m)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value)) return defaultValue;
        return decimal.TryParse(value.Trim(), NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out decimal res)
            ? res
            : defaultValue;
    }

    public static long ParseLong(string value, long defaultValue = 0)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value)) return defaultValue;
        string clean = Regex.Replace(value.Trim(), @"[^\d\-]", string.Empty);
        return long.TryParse(clean, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out long res)
            ? res
            : defaultValue;
    }
}

FILE: Core\Integration\Erp\Erp.Importer.cs

namespace SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Erp;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using ExcelDna.Integration;

public class ERPTableDocument
{
    public List<string> Headers { get; }
    public List<ERPTableRow> Rows { get; }
    private readonly Dictionary<string, int> _headerMap = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

    public ERPTableDocument(List<string> headers, List<ERPTableRow> rows)
    {
        Headers = headers;
        Rows = rows;
        for (int i = 0; i < headers.Count; i++)
        {
            string clean = ErpHelper.NormalizeHeader(headers[i]);
            if (!_headerMap.ContainsKey(clean))
            {
                _headerMap[clean] = i;
            }
        }
    }

    public int GetColumnIndex(string columnName)
    {
        string target = ErpHelper.NormalizeHeader(columnName);
        return _headerMap.TryGetValue(target, out int index) ? index : -1;
    }

    public bool HasColumn(string columnName) => GetColumnIndex(columnName) >= 0;
}

public class ERPTableRow
{
    private readonly string[] _cells;
    private readonly ERPTableDocument _owner;

    public ERPTableRow(string[] cells, ERPTableDocument owner)
    {
        _cells = cells;
        _owner = owner;
    }

    public string GetString(string columnName, string defaultValue = "")
    {
        int idx = _owner.GetColumnIndex(columnName);
        if (idx < 0 || idx >= _cells.Length) return defaultValue;
        return _cells[idx].Trim();
    }

    public long GetLong(string columnName, long defaultValue = 0) =>
        ErpHelper.ParseLong(GetString(columnName), defaultValue);

    public decimal GetDecimal(string columnName, decimal defaultValue = 0.00m) =>
        ErpHelper.ParseDecimal(GetString(columnName), defaultValue);

    public DateOnly? GetDateOnly(string columnName) =>
        ErpHelper.ParseDateOnly(GetString(columnName));

    public bool GetBool(string columnName, bool defaultValue = false)
    {
        string val = GetString(columnName);
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(val)) return defaultValue;
        if (bool.TryParse(val, out bool b)) return b;
        if (val.Equals("1") || val.Equals("YES", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return true;
        if (val.Equals("0") || val.Equals("NO", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) return false;
        return defaultValue;
    }
}

public static class ErpImporter
{
    public static ERPTableDocument LoadFromFile(string filePath, char delimiter = ',')
    {
        if (!File.Exists(filePath))
            throw new FileNotFoundException($"ERP import file not found at: {filePath}");

        string ext = Path.GetExtension(filePath).ToLowerInvariant();
        if (ext is ".xlsx" or ".xls")
        {
            return LoadFromExcelSpreadsheet(filePath);
        }

        var lines = File.ReadAllLines(filePath);
        return LoadFromLines(lines, delimiter);
    }

    private static ERPTableDocument LoadFromExcelSpreadsheet(string filePath)
    {
        dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
        dynamic? wb = null;
        try
        {
            wb = xlApp.Workbooks.Open(filePath, ReadOnly: true, AddToMru: false);
            dynamic ws = wb.Sheets[1];
            dynamic usedRange = ws.UsedRange;
            object[,] data = usedRange.Value2;

            if (data == null)
                return new ERPTableDocument(new List<string>(), new List<ERPTableRow>());

            int rowCount = data.GetLength(0);
            int colCount = data.GetLength(1);

            if (rowCount < 1)
                return new ERPTableDocument(new List<string>(), new List<ERPTableRow>());

            var headers = new List<string>();
            for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
            {
                headers.Add(data[1, c]?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty);
            }

            var rows = new List<ERPTableRow>();
            var doc = new ERPTableDocument(headers, rows);

            for (int r = 2; r <= rowCount; r++)
            {
                var cells = new string[colCount];
                bool hasValue = false;
                for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                {
                    string cellVal = data[r, c]?.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
                    cells[c - 1] = cellVal;
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cellVal)) hasValue = true;
                }
                if (hasValue)
                {
                    rows.Add(new ERPTableRow(cells, doc));
                }
            }

            return doc;
        }
        finally
        {
            wb?.Close(SaveChanges: false);
        }
    }

    private static ERPTableDocument LoadFromLines(string[] lines, char delimiter)
    {
        var validLines = lines.Where(l => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(l)).ToList();
        if (validLines.Count == 0)
            return new ERPTableDocument(new List<string>(), new List<ERPTableRow>());

        var headers = ErpHelper.SplitDelimitedLine(validLines[0], delimiter).ToList();
        var rows = new List<ERPTableRow>();
        var document = new ERPTableDocument(headers, rows);

        for (int i = 1; i < validLines.Count; i++)
        {
            var cells = ErpHelper.SplitDelimitedLine(validLines[i], delimiter);
            rows.Add(new ERPTableRow(cells, document));
        }

        return document;
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Erp;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Drawing.Drawing2D;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows.Forms;

public record ImportSkippedRecord
{
    public int ExcelRowNumber { get; init; }
    public string Identifier { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string NameOrTitle { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public string Reason { get; init; } = string.Empty;
}

public class ImportSummaryReport
{
    public string SourceSystemName { get; init; } = "ERP";
    public string EntityLabel { get; init; } = "Record";
    public int SuccessfullyImportedCount { get; init; } = 0;
    public int FilteredExclusionCount { get; init; } = 0;
    public string FilterRuleDescription { get; init; } = string.Empty;
    public List<ImportSkippedRecord> SkippedErrors { get; init; } = new();
}

public partial class ImportResultDialog : Form
{
    private readonly ImportSummaryReport _report;

    public ImportResultDialog(ImportSummaryReport report, string fileName)
    {
        _report = report;
        Text = $"{report.SourceSystemName} Import Summary";
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterScreen;
        FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
        MaximizeBox = false;
        MinimizeBox = false;
        AutoScaleMode = AutoScaleMode.Dpi;
        AutoSize = true;
        AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink;
        Padding = new Padding(24, 20, 24, 20);
        BackColor = Color.White;

        bool hasErrors = _report.SkippedErrors.Count > 0;

        var layout = new TableLayoutPanel
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            ColumnCount = 2,
            AutoSize = true,
            AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink
        };
        layout.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        layout.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100f));

        var pnlIndicator = new Panel
        {
            Size = new Size(36, 36),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 2, 16, 0)
        };

        pnlIndicator.Paint += (s, e) =>
        {
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            Color iconColor = hasErrors ? Color.FromArgb(217, 119, 6) : Color.FromArgb(16, 149, 193);
            using var brush = new SolidBrush(iconColor);
            e.Graphics.FillEllipse(brush, 2, 2, 32, 32);

            using var pen = new Pen(Color.White, 2.5f);
            if (!hasErrors)
            {
                e.Graphics.DrawLines(pen, new Point[] { new(10, 18), new(16, 24), new(26, 12) });
            }
            else
            {
                e.Graphics.DrawLine(pen, 18, 10, 18, 20);
                e.Graphics.FillEllipse(Brushes.White, 16.5f, 23.5f, 3, 3);
            }
        };

        var pnlContent = new TableLayoutPanel
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            ColumnCount = 1,
            AutoSize = true,
            AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink
        };

        var lblTitle = new Label
        {
            Text = hasErrors ? "Import Completed with Advisories" : "Import Completed Successfully",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 11.5f, FontStyle.Bold),
            ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(30, 41, 59),
            AutoSize = true,
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 8)
        };

        var sbMessage = new StringBuilder();
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fileName))
        {
            sbMessage.AppendLine($"Source: {fileName}");
            sbMessage.AppendLine();
        }

        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_report.FilterRuleDescription))
        {
            sbMessage.AppendLine($"  Filter Rule: {_report.FilterRuleDescription}");
        }

        if (_report.FilteredExclusionCount > 0)
        {
            sbMessage.AppendLine($"  Excluded by Filter: {_report.FilteredExclusionCount} record(s).");
        }

        sbMessage.AppendLine($"  Successfully Imported to Table: {_report.SuccessfullyImportedCount} {_report.EntityLabel}.");
        sbMessage.AppendLine($"  Skipped Entries (Errors): {_report.SkippedErrors.Count} record(s).");
        sbMessage.AppendLine();
        sbMessage.AppendLine("Review the entries in Excel and click 'Save to System' when ready.");

        var lblMessage = new Label
        {
            Text = sbMessage.ToString(),
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9.5f),
            ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(71, 85, 105),
            AutoSize = true,
            MaximumSize = new Size(460, 0),
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 18)
        };

        var flowButtons = new FlowLayoutPanel
        {
            FlowDirection = FlowDirection.RightToLeft,
            AutoSize = true,
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            Margin = Padding.Empty
        };

        var btnOk = new Button
        {
            Text = "OK",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f),
            AutoSize = true,
            Padding = new Padding(16, 6, 16, 6),
            BackColor = Color.FromArgb(241, 245, 249),
            FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
            DialogResult = DialogResult.OK
        };
        btnOk.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 1;
        btnOk.FlatAppearance.BorderColor = Color.FromArgb(203, 213, 225);
        btnOk.Click += (_, _) => Close();
        flowButtons.Controls.Add(btnOk);

        if (hasErrors)
        {
            var btnViewErrors = new Button
            {
                Text = $"View Skipped Errors ({_report.SkippedErrors.Count})...",
                Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f, FontStyle.Bold),
                AutoSize = true,
                Padding = new Padding(14, 6, 14, 6),
                ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(185, 28, 28),
                BackColor = Color.FromArgb(254, 242, 242),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat
            };
            btnViewErrors.FlatAppearance.BorderColor = Color.FromArgb(252, 165, 165);
            btnViewErrors.Click += (_, _) => ShowDetailedErrors();
            flowButtons.Controls.Add(btnViewErrors);
        }

        pnlContent.Controls.Add(lblTitle);
        pnlContent.Controls.Add(lblMessage);
        pnlContent.Controls.Add(flowButtons);

        layout.Controls.Add(pnlIndicator, 0, 0);
        layout.Controls.Add(pnlContent, 1, 0);
        Controls.Add(layout);
    }

    private void ShowDetailedErrors()
    {
        using var errForm = new Form
        {
            Text = $"{_report.SourceSystemName} - Skipped Entries Report",
            Size = new Size(680, 440),
            StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent,
            FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.Sizable,
            MinimizeBox = false,
            MaximizeBox = true
        };

        var txtLog = new TextBox
        {
            Multiline = true,
            ReadOnly = true,
            ScrollBars = ScrollBars.Both,
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            Font = new Font("Consolas", 9.5f),
            BackColor = Color.White
        };

        var sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.AppendLine("==========================================================================");
        sb.AppendLine($" SKIPPED ENTRIES REPORT ({_report.SkippedErrors.Count} total)");
        sb.AppendLine("==========================================================================");
        sb.AppendLine();

        foreach (var err in _report.SkippedErrors)
        {
            sb.AppendLine($"[Excel Row {err.ExcelRowNumber}]");
            sb.AppendLine($"    Identifier : {err.Identifier}");
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(err.NameOrTitle))
                sb.AppendLine($"    Name/Title : {err.NameOrTitle}");
            sb.AppendLine($"    Reason     : {err.Reason}");
            sb.AppendLine("--------------------------------------------------------------------------");
        }

        txtLog.Text = sb.ToString();
        errForm.Controls.Add(txtLog);
        errForm.ShowDialog(this);
    }
}

FILE: Core\ThumbprintSync\DeviceSyncManager.cs

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Sockets;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using ExcelDna.Integration.CustomUI;
using Excel = Microsoft.Office.Interop.Excel;

namespace SKSB_App.ThumbprintSync
{
    public class TerminalUser
    {
        public string EnrollNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public int Privilege { get; set; }
        public string CardNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    }

    [ComVisible(true)]
    public class DeviceSyncController : ExcelRibbon
    {
        public override string GetCustomUI(string ribbonId)
        {
            return @"
            <customUI xmlns='http://schemas.microsoft.com/office/2009/07/customui'>
              <ribbon>
                <tabs>
                  <tab id='tabAllocation' label='Work Allocation'>
                    <group id='grpDevice' label='Terminal Direct Sync'>
                      <button id='btnImportTerminal' 
                              label='Pull Users From Reader' 
                              size='large' 
                              imageMso='ContactAddNew' 
                              onAction='OnPullUsersClicked' />
                    </group>
                  </tab>
                </tabs>
              </ribbon>
            </customUI>";
        }

        public void OnPullUsersClicked(IRibbonControl control)
        {
            Task.Run(async () =>
            {
                const string ip = "192.168.0.110";
                const int port = 4370;

                try
                {
                    List<TerminalUser> users = await FetchEnrolledUsersAsync(ip, port);

                    if (users == null || users.Count == 0)
                    {
                        MessageBox.Show($"Connected to reader {ip}, but 0 enrolled user records were parsed from memory.",
                                        "Reader Query Result", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                        return;
                    }

                    // Dispatch to Excel main thread
                    ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            PopulateEmployeesTable(users);
                            MessageBox.Show($"Successfully imported {users.Count} employees directly from reader ({ip}).",
                                            "Sync Successful", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                        }
                        catch (Exception renderEx)
                        {
                            MessageBox.Show($"Error writing to sheet: {renderEx.Message}\n\n{renderEx.StackTrace}",
                                            "Excel Render Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                        }
                    });
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show($"Connection failed:\n\n{ex.Message}\n\n({ex.GetType().Name})",
                                    "Terminal Communication Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                }
            });
        }

        private async Task<List<TerminalUser>> private async Task<List<TerminalUser>> FetchEnrolledUsersAsync(string ip, int port, int commKey = 0)
        {
            return await Task.Run(() =>
            {
                // Locate ZkBridge.exe relative to the running XLL add-in
                string addinDirectory = Path.GetDirectoryName(ExcelDnaUtil.XllPath);
                string bridgeExePath = Path.Combine(addinDirectory, "ZkBridge.exe");

                if (!File.Exists(bridgeExePath))
                {
                    // Fallback check current directory
                    bridgeExePath = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "ZkBridge.exe");
                    if (!File.Exists(bridgeExePath))
                    {
                        throw new FileNotFoundException($"Cannot locate ZkBridge.exe at:\n{bridgeExePath}");
                    }
                }

                var startInfo = new ProcessStartInfo
                {
                    FileName = bridgeExePath,
                    Arguments = $"{ip} {port} {commKey}",
                    UseShellExecute = false,
                    RedirectStandardOutput = true,
                    RedirectStandardError = true,
                    CreateNoWindow = true
                };

                using (var process = Process.Start(startInfo))
                {
                    string output = process.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd();
                    string error = process.StandardError.ReadToEnd();
                    process.WaitForExit(15000);

                    if (process.ExitCode != 0 || !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(error))
                    {
                        throw new Exception($"Bridge Error (Code {process.ExitCode}): {error}");
                    }

                    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(output))
                    {
                        return new List<TerminalUser>();
                    }

                    var serializer = new JavaScriptSerializer();
                    return serializer.Deserialize<List<TerminalUser>>(output);
                }
            });
        }

        private byte[] CreateZkPacket(ushort command, ushort sessionId, ushort replyNumber, byte[] data)
        {
            byte[] packet = new byte[8 + data.Length];
            BitConverter.GetBytes(command).CopyTo(packet, 0);
            BitConverter.GetBytes((ushort)0).CopyTo(packet, 2);
            BitConverter.GetBytes(sessionId).CopyTo(packet, 4);
            BitConverter.GetBytes(replyNumber).CopyTo(packet, 6);
            if (data.Length > 0) data.CopyTo(packet, 8);

            int chk = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < packet.Length; i += 2)
            {
                if (i == 2) continue;
                ushort val = (i + 1 < packet.Length) ? BitConverter.ToUInt16(packet, i) : packet[i];
                chk += val;
                if (chk > 0xFFFF) chk = (chk & 0xFFFF) + 1;
            }
            ushort checksum = (ushort)(~chk & 0xFFFF);
            BitConverter.GetBytes(checksum).CopyTo(packet, 2);

            return packet;
        }

        private byte[] MakeAuthPayload(int commKey, ushort sessionId)
        {
            int k = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < 32; i++)
            {
                if ((commKey & (1 << i)) > 0)
                    k = (k << 1 | 1);
                else
                    k = (k << 1);
            }
            k += sessionId;

            byte[] keyBytes = BitConverter.GetBytes(k);
            byte[] encrypted = new byte[4];
            encrypted[0] = (byte)(keyBytes[0] ^ 0x50);
            encrypted[1] = (byte)(keyBytes[1] ^ 0x4F);
            encrypted[2] = (byte)(keyBytes[2] ^ 0x53);
            encrypted[3] = (byte)(keyBytes[3] ^ 0x4F);

            return encrypted;
        }

        private void PopulateEmployeesTable(List<TerminalUser> users)
        {
            Excel.Application app = (Excel.Application)ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            Excel.Workbook wb = app.ActiveWorkbook ?? app.Workbooks.Add();

            Excel.Worksheet ws;
            try
            {
                ws = (Excel.Worksheet)wb.Sheets["Employees"];
            }
            catch
            {
                ws = (Excel.Worksheet)wb.Sheets.Add();
                ws.Name = "Employees";
            }

            ws.Cells.Clear();

            string[] headers = { "EnrollNumber", "Employee Name", "Privilege", "Card Number", "Source of Truth" };
            for (int c = 0; c < headers.Length; c++)
            {
                ws.Cells[1, c + 1] = headers[c];
            }

            object[,] data = new object[users.Count, headers.Length];
            for (int i = 0; i < users.Count; i++)
            {
                data[i, 0] = users[i].EnrollNumber;
                data[i, 1] = users[i].Name;
                data[i, 2] = users[i].Privilege == 14 ? "Admin" : "User";
                data[i, 3] = users[i].CardNumber;
                data[i, 4] = "Terminal (192.168.0.110)";
            }

            Excel.Range dataRange = ws.Range[ws.Cells[2, 1], ws.Cells[users.Count + 1, headers.Length]];
            dataRange.Value2 = data;

            Excel.Range fullTableRange = ws.Range[ws.Cells[1, 1], ws.Cells[users.Count + 1, headers.Length]];
            try
            {
                Excel.ListObject table = ws.ListObjects.Add(
                    Excel.XlListObjectSourceType.xlSrcRange,
                    fullTableRange,
                    Type.Missing,
                    Excel.XlYesNoGuess.xlYes
                );
                table.Name = "Table_Employees";
                table.TableStyle = "TableStyleMedium9";
            }
            catch
            {
                // Table already formatted
            }

            ws.Columns.AutoFit();
            ((Excel.Range)ws.Columns[1]).HorizontalAlignment = Excel.XlHAlign.xlHAlignCenter;
            ((Excel.Range)ws.Columns[3]).HorizontalAlignment = Excel.XlHAlign.xlHAlignCenter;
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using ExcelDna.Integration.CustomUI;

namespace SKSB_App.ThumbprintSync
{
    [ComVisible(true)]
    public class VigilanceRibbonController : ExcelRibbon
    {
        // 1. Injects a minimal tab and button into Excel's top ribbon
        public override string GetCustomUI(string ribbonId)
        {
            return @"
            <customUI xmlns='http://schemas.microsoft.com/office/2009/07/customui'>
              <ribbon>
                <tabs>
                  <tab id='tabAllocation' label='Work Allocation'>
                    <group id='grpDevice' label='Terminal Sync'>
                      <button id='btnSync' 
                              label='Sync to Reader' 
                              size='large' 
                              imageMso='AccessRefreshAllLists' 
                              onAction='OnSyncClicked' />
                    </group>
                  </tab>
                </tabs>
              </ribbon>
            </customUI>";
        }

        // 2. The callback triggered by clicking the button
        public void OnSyncClicked(IRibbonControl control)
        {
            // Run on background thread to keep Excel UI responsive
            Task.Run(async () =>
            {
                try
                {
                    const string deviceIp = "192.168.0.110";

                    // Call your extraction & socket logic here
                    await Task.Delay(500); // Simulated network latency

                    MessageBox.Show($"Successfully dispatched allocation to {deviceIp}.",
                                    "Vigilance Sync",
                                    MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                                    MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show($"Sync error: {ex.Message}",
                                    "Error",
                                    MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                                    MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                }
            });
        }
    }

    // Optional: Expose custom formula for checking device status directly in a cell
    public static class DeviceFormulas
    {
        [ExcelFunction(Description = "Pings the Vigilance reader status")]
        public static string PING_READER(string ipAddress)
        {
            return $"Ready @ {ipAddress}";
        }
    }
}

    Road_Reinstatement,

    Telco_Infra,

    Cancelled,
    Expired,
    None

// =========================================================================

[ExcelTable("AllProjectsTable")]
[ExcelTable("ProjectsTable")]
public record ProjectRecord
{
    [DataList("ProjectIDList")]
    [ExcelColumn("Project ID", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true, Transform = TransformKind.UpperCase)]
    public string ProjectID { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [DataDetail]
    public ProjectDetails ProjectDetails { get; set; } = new();
}

public record ProjectDetails
{
    [DataList("ProjectNameList")]
    [ExcelColumn("Project Name", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true)]
    public string ProjectName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Project Type", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule("ProjectTypeList", IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.Literal, literalValue: "Civil_Works")]
    public ProjectType ProjectType { get; set; } = ProjectType.Civil_Works;

    [ExcelColumn("Company Name", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule("CompaniesTable[Short Company Name]", "CompanyNameList", IsRequired = true)]
    public string CompanyName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Start Date", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.Today)]
    public DateOnly StartDate { get; set; } = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today);

    [ExcelColumn("Target End Date", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.DateOffsetDays, referenceHeader: "Start Date", offset: 365)]
    public DateOnly TargetEndDate { get; set; } = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today.AddDays(365));

    [ExcelColumn("Actual End Date")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = false)]
    public DateOnly? ActualEndDate { get; set; }

    [ExcelColumn("Status")]
    [ColumnRule("ProjectStatusList", IsRequired = false)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.Literal, literalValue: "Active")]
    [ColumnStateGuard(1, StateConditionKind.ColumnNotEmpty, "Completed", "Active", "Actual End Date")]
    [ColumnStateGuard(2, StateConditionKind.DateExpired, "Expired", "Active", "Target End Date")]
    public ProjectStatus Status { get; set; } = ProjectStatus.Active;
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.Types;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types.Attributes;

// =========================================================================
// 1. STAFF DOMAIN ENUMS (Generates Lookups Named Ranges)
// =========================================================================

public enum Department
{
    Worker,
    Driver,
    Supervisor,
    Foreman,
    Operator,
    Mechanic,
    Admin,
    Management,
    Office,
    None
}

public enum ContractStatus
{
    Active,
    Suspended,
    Resigned,
    Terminated,
    On_Leave,
    None
}

public enum VehicleLicenseClass
{
    None,
    B2,
    B,
    D,
    DA,
    E,
    E1,
    E2,
    GDL_D,
    GDL_E,
    GDL_E_Full,
    GDL,
    PSV,
    Forklift,
    H,
    I
}

public enum VehicleLicenseStatus
{
    Valid,
    Invalid,
    Suspended
}

{
    Valid,
    Invalid,
    Suspended

// =========================================================================
// 2. ROOT STAFF RECORD & MASTER TABLE ("AllStaffsTable")
// =========================================================================

[ExcelTable("AllStaffsTable")]
public record StaffRecord
{
    [DataList("StaffIDList")]
    [ExcelColumn("Staff ID", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.AutoIncrement)]
    public long StaffID { get; set; } = 0;

    [DataDetail]
    public StaffDetails StaffDetails { get; set; } = new();

    // Child Collections
    public List<VehicleLicense> VehicleLicenses { get; set; } = new();
    public List<SkillLicense> SkillLicenses { get; set; } = new();

    public ContractStatus OverallStatus => StaffDetails.ContractStatus;

    public ContractStatus EvaluateContractStatus(DateOnly asOfDate)
    {
        if (StaffDetails.ResignDate.HasValue && StaffDetails.ResignDate.Value <= asOfDate)
            return ContractStatus.Resigned;

        return StaffDetails.ContractStatus == ContractStatus.None
            ? ContractStatus.Active
            : StaffDetails.ContractStatus;
    }
}

public record StaffDetails
{
    // Restored on StaffDetails for backward compatibility with Importer and Sync commands
    public long StaffID { get; set; } = 0;

    [DataList("StaffNameList")]
    [ExcelColumn("Staff Name", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true, Transform = TransformKind.Trim)]
    public string StaffName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [DataList("StaffShortNameList")]
    [ExcelColumn("Short Name")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = false, IsWarningOnly = true, Transform = TransformKind.ShortNameFirstLast)]
    public string StaffShortName
    {
        get
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_staffShortName)) return _staffShortName;

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(StaffName)) return string.Empty;

            var parts = StaffName.Trim().Split(new[] { ' ' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
            if (parts.Length <= 1) return StaffName.Trim();

            return $"{parts[0]} {parts[^1]}";
        }
        set => _staffShortName = value;
    }
    private string _staffShortName = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Department", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule("DepartmentList", IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.Literal, literalValue: "Worker")]
    public string Department { get; set; } = "Worker";

    // Restored as long for backward compatibility with legacy importer/database mapping
    [ExcelColumn("IC Number")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = false)]
    public long NRIC { get; set; } = 0;

    [ExcelColumn("Passport Number")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = false)]
    public string Passport { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Phone Number")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = false)]
    public string Phone { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Join Date", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.Today)]
    public DateOnly JoinDate { get; set; } = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today);

    [ExcelColumn("Resign Date")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = false)]
    public DateOnly? ResignDate { get; set; } = null;

    [ExcelColumn("Contract Status", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule("ContractStatusList", IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.Literal, literalValue: "Active")]
    [ColumnStateGuard(1, StateConditionKind.ColumnNotEmpty, "Resigned", "Active", "Resign Date")]
    public ContractStatus ContractStatus
    {
        get => GetContractStatusOn(DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today));
        set => _contractStatus = value;
    }
    private ContractStatus _contractStatus = ContractStatus.Active;

    public ContractStatus GetContractStatusOn(DateOnly targetDate)
    {
        if (ResignDate.HasValue && targetDate >= ResignDate.Value)
            return ContractStatus.Resigned;

        if (targetDate < JoinDate)
            return ContractStatus.Resigned;

        if (_contractStatus == ContractStatus.Suspended)
            return ContractStatus.Suspended;

        return ContractStatus.Active;
    }

    public bool IsActiveOn(DateOnly targetDate) => GetContractStatusOn(targetDate) == ContractStatus.Active;
}

[ExcelTable("VehicleLicensesTable")]
public record VehicleLicense
{
    [ExcelColumn("Vehicle License ID", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.AutoIncrement)]
    public long VehicleLicenseID { get; set; } = 0;

    [ExcelColumn("Staff ID", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule("AllStaffsTable[Staff ID]", IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnSync("Staff Name", "AllStaffsTable[Staff ID]", "AllStaffsTable[Staff Name]")]
    public long StaffID { get; set; } = 0;

    [ExcelColumn("Staff Name")]
    [ColumnRule("AllStaffsTable[Staff Name]", IsRequired = false, IsWarningOnly = true)]
    [ColumnSync("Staff ID", "AllStaffsTable[Staff Name]", "AllStaffsTable[Staff ID]")]
    public string StaffName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Vehicle License Class", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule("VehicleLicenseClassList", IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.Literal, literalValue: "D")]
    public VehicleLicenseClass VehicleLicenseClass { get; set; } = VehicleLicenseClass.D;

    [ExcelColumn("Renew Date", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.Today)]
    public DateOnly RenewDate
    {
        get => _renewDate;
        set => _renewDate = value;
    }
    private DateOnly _renewDate = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today);

    [ExcelColumn("Expiry Date", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.DateOffsetDays, referenceHeader: "Renew Date", offset: 365)]
    public DateOnly ExpiryDate
    {
        get => _expiryDate ?? _renewDate.AddYears(1);
        set => _expiryDate = value;
    }
    private DateOnly? _expiryDate;

    [ExcelColumn("Status", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule("VehicleLicenseStatusList", IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.Literal, literalValue: "Valid")]
    [ColumnStateGuard(1, StateConditionKind.DateExpired, "Invalid", "Valid", "Expiry Date")]
    public VehicleLicenseStatus VehicleLicenseStatus
    {
        get
        {
            if (_status == VehicleLicenseStatus.Suspended)
                return VehicleLicenseStatus.Suspended;

            return DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today) > ExpiryDate
                ? VehicleLicenseStatus.Invalid
                : _status;
        }
        set => _status = value;
    }
    private VehicleLicenseStatus _status = VehicleLicenseStatus.Valid;

    [ExcelColumn("Cost")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = false, AllowNonNegativeZero = true)]
    public decimal Cost { get; set; } = 0.00m;
}

{
    None, Car, Hilux, Van, Lorry, Tipper_Lorry, Roro_Lorry, Crane_Lorry,
    Skylift, Backhoe, Excavator, Roller, Compressor, Generator, Motorcycle, Machinery
}

public enum VehicleStatus
{
    Active, In_Shop, Expired, Reserved, Decommissioned, Suspended, None
}

public enum ServiceType
{
    Regular_Service, Breakdown_Repair, Tyre_Service, Battery_Replacement,
    Aircond_Service, Bodywork_Paint, Puspakom_Prep, Accident_Repair, Other, None
}

    Scheduled, In_Progress, Completed, Cancelled, None
}

    SPAN_Permit, Bomba_Compliance, JKR_JPJ_Special, Other

    Active, Expired, Cancelled, None
