// 2. ROOT VEHICLE RECORD & MASTER TABLE ("AllVehiclesTable")
// =========================================================================

[ExcelTable("AllVehiclesTable")]
public record VehicleRecord
{
    [DataList("VehiclePlateList")]
    [ExcelColumn("Vehicle Plate")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true, Transform = TransformKind.UpperCase)]
    public string VehiclePlate { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [DataDetail]
    public VehicleDetail VehicleDetail { get; set; } = new();

    [ExcelColumn("Status")]
    [ColumnRule("VehicleStatusList", IsWarningOnly = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.Literal, literalValue: "Active")]
    [ColumnStateGuard(1, StateConditionKind.ColumnNotEmpty, "Decommissioned", "Active", "Sold Date", DisallowForcedValueWhenFalse = true)]
    [ColumnStateGuard(2, StateConditionKind.DateExpired, "Expired", "Active", "Road Tax Expiry", "Inspection Expiry", "Insurance Expiry", "Permit Expiry")]
    public VehicleStatus VehicleStatus { get; set; } = VehicleStatus.Active;

    public List<Maintenance> Maintenances { get; set; } = new();
    public List<VehiclePermit> Permits { get; set; } = new();
    public List<RoadTax> RoadTaxes { get; set; } = new();
    public List<Inspection> Inspections { get; set; } = new();
    public List<VehicleInsurance> Insurances { get; set; } = new();
    public List<OdometerLog> OdometerLogs { get; set; } = new();

    public List<OdometerLog> Odometers
    {
        get => OdometerLogs;
        set => OdometerLogs = value;
    }

    public VehicleStatus EvaluateStatus(DateOnly asOfDate)
    {
        // 1. Sold Date reached -> Decommissioned
        if (VehicleDetail.SoldDate.HasValue && VehicleDetail.SoldDate.Value <= asOfDate)
            return VehicleStatus.Decommissioned;

        // 2. Active maintenance ticket -> In_Shop
        bool inWorkshop = Maintenances.Any(m =>
            m.MaintenanceStatus == MaintenanceStatus.In_Progress ||
            (m.DateIn <= asOfDate && (!m.DateCompleted.HasValue || m.DateCompleted.Value > asOfDate)));

        if (inWorkshop)
            return VehicleStatus.In_Shop;

        // 3. Compliance expiration -> Expired
        bool roadTaxExpired = VehicleDetail.RoadTaxExpiry.HasValue && VehicleDetail.RoadTaxExpiry.Value < asOfDate;
        bool inspectionExpired = VehicleDetail.InspectionExpiry.HasValue && VehicleDetail.InspectionExpiry.Value < asOfDate;
        bool insuranceExpired = VehicleDetail.InsuranceExpiry.HasValue && VehicleDetail.InsuranceExpiry.Value < asOfDate;
        bool permitExpired = VehicleDetail.PermitExpiry.HasValue && VehicleDetail.PermitExpiry.Value < asOfDate;

        if (roadTaxExpired || inspectionExpired || insuranceExpired || permitExpired)
            return VehicleStatus.Expired;

        // 4. Preserve Manual Administrative Overrides
        if (VehicleStatus == VehicleStatus.Suspended)
            return VehicleStatus.Suspended;

        if (VehicleStatus == VehicleStatus.Reserved)
            return VehicleStatus.Reserved;

        return VehicleStatus.Active;
    }
}

    [ColumnSync("Insurance Company", "InsuranceCompaniesTable[Company Contact Number]", "InsuranceCompaniesTable[Insurance Company]")]
    [ColumnSync("Insurance Agent", "InsuranceCompaniesTable[Company Contact Number]", "InsuranceCompaniesTable[Insurance Agent]")]
    public string CompanyContactNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    // 3. Unmapped Helper Column (UI / Display only)
    [JsonIgnore]
    [ExcelColumn("Insurance Agent")]
    [ColumnRule("InsuranceCompaniesTable[Insurance Agent]", IsRequired = false, IsWarningOnly = true)]
    [ColumnSync("Insurance Company", "InsuranceCompaniesTable[Insurance Agent]", "InsuranceCompaniesTable[Insurance Company]")]
    [ColumnSync("Company Contact Number", "InsuranceCompaniesTable[Insurance Agent]", "InsuranceCompaniesTable[Company Contact Number]")]
    public string InsuranceAgent { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Insurance Type")]
    [ColumnRule("InsuranceTypeList", IsRequired = false)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.Literal, literalValue: "Comprehensive")]
    public InsuranceType InsuranceType { get; set; } = InsuranceType.Comprehensive;

    [ExcelColumn("Renewal Date", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.Today)]
    public DateOnly RenewDate { get; set; } = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today);

    [ExcelColumn("Expiry Date", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.DateOffsetDays, referenceHeader: "Renewal Date", offset: 365)]
    [ColumnDateGreaterThan("Renewal Date")]
    public DateOnly ExpiryDate { get; set; } = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today.AddDays(365));

    [ExcelColumn("Cost")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = false, AllowNonNegativeZero = true)]
    public decimal Amount { get; set; } = 0.00m;

    [ExcelColumn("Status", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule("InsuranceStatusList", IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.Literal, literalValue: "Active")]
    [ColumnStateGuard(1, StateConditionKind.DateExpired, "Expired", "Active", "Expiry Date")]
    public InsuranceStatus Status { get; set; } = InsuranceStatus.Active;

    [ExcelColumn("Remarks")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = false)]
    public string Remarks { get; set; } = string.Empty;
}

[ExcelTable("OdometersTable")]
public record OdometerLog
{
    [ExcelColumn("Fuel Transaction ID", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.AutoIncrement)]
    public long OdometerID { get; set; }

    [ExcelColumn("Date", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnDefault(DefaultKind.Today)]
    public DateOnly Date { get; set; } = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today);

    [ExcelColumn("Vehicle Plate", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule("AllVehiclesTable[Vehicle Plate]", IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnSync("Vehicle Type", "AllVehiclesTable[Vehicle Plate]", "AllVehiclesTable[Vehicle Type]")]
    public string VehiclePlate { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [JsonIgnore] // 1. Prevents duplicate saves to DB / JSON snapshots
    [ExcelColumn("Vehicle Type")] // 2. Maps the property to the Excel table column
    [ColumnRule("VehicleTypeList", IsRequired = false, IsWarningOnly = true)]
    [ColumnSync("Vehicle Plate", "AllVehiclesTable[Vehicle Type]", "AllVehiclesTable[Vehicle Plate]")] // 3. Two-way sync
    public VehicleType VehicleType { get; set; } = VehicleType.None;

    // 1. Stored Primary Foreign Key
    [ExcelColumn("Staff ID")]
    [ColumnRule("StaffIDList", IsRequired = false, IsWarningOnly = true)]
    [ColumnSync("Staff Name", "StaffIDList", "StaffShortNameList")]
    public long StaffID { get; set; } = 0;

    // 2. Unmapped Helper Column (Two-Way Auto-Evaluated in Excel)
    [JsonIgnore]
    [ExcelColumn("Staff Name")]
    [ColumnRule("StaffShortNameList", IsRequired = false, IsWarningOnly = true)]
    [ColumnSync("Staff ID", "StaffShortNameList", "StaffIDList")]
    public string StaffName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Project")]
    [ColumnRule("ProjectIDList", IsRequired = false, IsWarningOnly = true)]
    public string Project { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Odometer Before")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = false, AllowNonNegativeZero = true)]
    public int OdometerBefore { get; set; } = 0;

    [ExcelColumn("Current Odometer")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true, AllowNonNegativeZero = true)]
    public int CurrentOdometer { get; set; } = 0;

    [ExcelColumn("Fuel Card Number")]
    [ColumnRule("FuelCardsTable[Fuel Card Number]", IsRequired = false, IsWarningOnly = true)]
    [ColumnSync("Fuel Company", "FuelCardsTable[Fuel Card Number]", "FuelCardsTable[Fuel Company Name]")]
    public string FuelCardNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Fuel Company")]
    [ColumnRule("FuelCompanyNamesTable[Fuel Company Name]", IsRequired = false, IsWarningOnly = true)]
    public string FuelCompany { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Station Name")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = false)]
    public string StationName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Receipt Number")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = false)]
    public string ReceiptNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Transaction Amount")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = false, AllowNonNegativeZero = true)]
    public decimal Amount { get; set; } = 0.00m;

    [ExcelColumn("Remarks")]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = false)]
    public string Remarks { get; set; } = string.Empty;
}

[ExcelTable("FuelCardsTable")]
public record FuelCardRecord
{
    [DataList("FuelCardNumberList")]
    [ExcelColumn("Fuel Card Number", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true)]
    public string FuelCardNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Fuel Company Names")]
    [ColumnRule("FuelCompanyNamesTable[Fuel Company Name]", IsRequired = true)]
    public string FuelCompanyName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [ExcelColumn("Card Owner ID", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule("AllVehiclesTable[Vehicle Plate]", "StaffIDList", IsRequired = true)]
    [ColumnSync("Card Owner Name", "AllVehiclesTable[Vehicle Plate]", "AllVehiclesTable[Brand] + ' ' + AllVehiclesTable[Model]")]
    [ColumnSync("Card Owner Name", "StaffIDList", "StaffShortNameList")]
    public string CardOwnerID { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [JsonIgnore]
    [ExcelColumn("Card Owner Name")]
    [ColumnRule("AllVehiclesTable[Brand] + ' ' + AllVehiclesTable[Model]", "StaffShortNameList", IsRequired = false, IsWarningOnly = true)]
    // Two-way sync: Picking the name looks up the ID/Plate and fills Card Owner ID
    [ColumnSync("Card Owner ID", "AllVehiclesTable[Brand] + ' ' + AllVehiclesTable[Model]", "AllVehiclesTable[Vehicle Plate]")]
    [ColumnSync("Card Owner ID", "StaffShortNameList", "StaffIDList")]
    public string CardOwnerName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
}

[ExcelTable("FuelCompanyNamesTable")]
public record FuelCompanyNameRecord
{
    [DataList("FuelCompanyNameList")]
    [ExcelColumn("Fuel Company Names", isRequired: true)]
    [ColumnRule(IsRequired = true)]
    public string FuelCompanyName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.Types.Attributes;

using System;

[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Property, AllowMultiple = false)]
public sealed class ColumnFilterAttribute : Attribute
{
    public string HeaderName { get; }
    public string SourceList { get; }

    /// <summary>
    /// Creates a UI-only filter column that does not map to database storage.
    /// Used to narrow down options in a primary mapped column (e.g., picking a Vehicle Type to filter Vehicle Plates).
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="headerName">The column header name in Excel.</param>
    /// <param name="sourceList">The Named Range to populate the dropdown (e.g., "VehicleTypeList").</param>
    public ColumnFilterAttribute(string headerName, string sourceList)
    {
        HeaderName = headerName;
        SourceList = sourceList;
    }
}

FILE: Core\Types\Attributes\Attributes.ColumnRules.cs.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.Core.Types.Attributes;

using System;

/// <summary>
/// Binds a C# record/class to one or more Excel ListObject table names.
/// </summary>
[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Class, AllowMultiple = true, Inherited = true)]
public sealed class ExcelTableAttribute : Attribute
{
    public string TableName { get; }

    public ExcelTableAttribute(string tableName)
    {
        TableName = tableName;
    }
}

public enum DefaultKind
{
    None,
    Literal,            // Uses LiteralValue (e.g. "Active", "0")
    Today,              // DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today)
    DateOffsetDays,     // ReferenceHeader date + Offset days (Fallback: Today + Offset)
    DateOffsetMonths,   // ReferenceHeader date + Offset months (Fallback: Today + Offset)
    AutoIncrement       // Max(existing column integer values) + 1
}

public enum StateConditionKind
{
    None,
    DateExpired,        // True if ANY ReferenceHeaders date < Today
    ColumnNotEmpty,     // True if ANY ReferenceHeaders cell is not blank (e.g. Sold Date != "")
    ColumnEquals,       // True if ReferenceHeaders cell equals ExpectedValue
    DateGreaterThan
}

public enum TransformKind
{
    None,
    Trim,
    UpperCase,
    ShortNameFirstLast, // First word + Last word
    ShortNameThreeWords // 3-word name formatting rule
}

/// <summary>
/// Primary column rule governing dropdown sources, required status, and text transformation.
/// </summary>
[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Property, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = true)]
public sealed class ColumnRuleAttribute : Attribute
{
    /// <summary>
    /// One or more Defined Names (e.g. "VehicleTypeList") or "TableName[ColumnHeader]" sources.
    /// Multiple sources are stacked in sections (Section 0 at top, Section 1 below, etc.).
    /// </summary>
    public string[] Sources { get; }
    public bool IsRequired { get; set; } = true;
    public bool IsWarningOnly { get; set; } = false;
    public bool IsMultiSelect { get; set; } = false;
    public bool AllowNonNegativeZero { get; set; } = true;
    public TransformKind Transform { get; set; } = TransformKind.None;

    public ColumnRuleAttribute(params string[] sources)
    {
        Sources = sources ?? Array.Empty<string>();
    }
}

/// <summary>
/// Defines automatic adjacent/target column lookup when this column is edited or selected.
/// AllowMultiple = true supports fallback chains (e.g. check Vehicles first, then Staff)
/// and multi-column fills (e.g. fill both Workshop Address and Workshop Contact).
/// </summary>
[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Property, AllowMultiple = true, Inherited = true)]
public sealed class ColumnSyncAttribute : Attribute
{
    public string TargetHeader { get; }
    public string LookupSource { get; }
    public string ResultSource { get; }

    public ColumnSyncAttribute(string targetHeader, string lookupSource, string resultSource)
    {
        TargetHeader = targetHeader;
        LookupSource = lookupSource;
        ResultSource = resultSource;
    }
}

/// <summary>
/// Defines how an empty cell in an active row is automatically defaulted.
/// </summary>
[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Property, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = true)]
public sealed class ColumnDefaultAttribute : Attribute
{
    public DefaultKind Kind { get; }
    public string? LiteralValue { get; set; }
    public string? ReferenceHeader { get; set; }
    public int Offset { get; set; }

    public ColumnDefaultAttribute(
        DefaultKind kind,
        string? literalValue = null,
        string? referenceHeader = null,
        int offset = 0)
    {
        Kind = kind;
        LiteralValue = literalValue;
        ReferenceHeader = referenceHeader;
        Offset = offset;
    }
}

/// <summary>
/// Self-healing state guard that forces or forbids specific dropdown values based on other columns in the same row.
/// Evaluated in order of Priority (lowest number first).
/// </summary>
[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Property, AllowMultiple = true, Inherited = true)]
public sealed class ColumnStateGuardAttribute : Attribute
{
    public int Priority { get; }
    public StateConditionKind Condition { get; }
    public string[] ReferenceHeaders { get; }
    public string ForcedValueWhenTrue { get; }
    public bool DisallowForcedValueWhenFalse { get; set; } = true;
    public string FallbackValueWhenFalse { get; set; } = "Active";
    public string? ExpectedValue { get; set; }
    public string? SkipIfCurrentEquals { get; set; }

    public ColumnStateGuardAttribute(
        int priority,
        StateConditionKind condition,
        string forcedValueWhenTrue,
        string fallbackValueWhenFalse = "Active",
        params string[] referenceHeaders)
    {
        Priority = priority;
        Condition = condition;
        ForcedValueWhenTrue = forcedValueWhenTrue;
        FallbackValueWhenFalse = fallbackValueWhenFalse;
        ReferenceHeaders = referenceHeaders ?? Array.Empty<string>();
    }
}

/// <summary>
/// Enforces that a date column must be strictly greater (later) than a referenced date column.
/// </summary>
[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Property, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = true)]
public sealed class ColumnDateGreaterThanAttribute : Attribute
{
    public string ReferenceHeader { get; }

    public ColumnDateGreaterThanAttribute(string referenceHeader)
    {
        ReferenceHeader = referenceHeader;
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Core.Types.Attributes;

using System;

/// <summary>
/// Marks a model property to be exposed as an Excel Defined Name list (e.g. [PropertyName]List) on the 'Data' tab.
/// </summary>
[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Property, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = true)]
public sealed class DataListAttribute : Attribute
{
    public string? CustomListName { get; }

    public DataListAttribute(string? customListName = null)
    {
        CustomListName = customListName;
    }
}

/// <summary>
/// Marks a child record (e.g. StaffDetails, ProjectDetails, VehicleDetail)
/// so the reflection scanner inspects its inner [DataList] properties.
/// </summary>
[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Property, AllowMultiple = false, Inherited = true)]
public sealed class DataDetailAttribute : Attribute
{
}

namespace SKSB_App.Launcher
{
    public sealed class AppVersion
    {
        public string Version { get; set; } = "1.0.0";
        public DateTime LastUpdatedUtc { get; set; } = DateTime.UtcNow;
        public string Environment { get; set; } = "Production";

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional hash map for file integrity and granular incremental diffs.
        /// Key: Relative file path (e.g., "AddIns/Portal-AddIn64.xll")
        /// Value: SHA256 checksum
        /// </summary>
        public Dictionary<string, string> FileHashes { get; set; } = new();
    }

namespace SKSB_App.Launcher;

using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Windows.Forms;

public partial class InstallationProgressForm : Form
{
    private readonly Label _lblTitle;
    private readonly Label _lblStatus;
    private readonly ProgressBar _progressBar;

    public InstallationProgressForm()
    {
        Text = "SKSB Operations Hub Setup";
        Size = new Size(440, 150);
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterScreen;
        FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
        MaximizeBox = false;
        MinimizeBox = false;
        ControlBox = false;
        TopMost = true;
        BackColor = Color.White;

        _lblTitle = new Label
        {
            Text = "Setting up SKSB App...",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 10.5f, FontStyle.Bold),
            ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(30, 41, 59),
            Location = new Point(24, 16),
            AutoSize = true
        };

        _lblStatus = new Label
        {
            Text = "Connecting to server...",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f, FontStyle.Regular),
            ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(100, 116, 139),
            Location = new Point(25, 44),
            AutoSize = true
        };

        _progressBar = new ProgressBar
        {
            Location = new Point(26, 72),
            Size = new Size(372, 18),
            Style = ProgressBarStyle.Marquee,
            MarqueeAnimationSpeed = 30
        };

        Controls.Add(_lblTitle);
        Controls.Add(_lblStatus);
        Controls.Add(_progressBar);
    }

    public void UpdateStatus(string message)
    {
        if (IsDisposed || !IsHandleCreated) return;

        if (InvokeRequired)
        {
            BeginInvoke(new Action(() => UpdateStatus(message)));
            return;
        }

        _lblStatus.Text = message;
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Launcher;

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.IO.Compression;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Text.Json.Serialization;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Storage;

public class LauncherPackageManifest
{
    [JsonPropertyName("version")]
    public string Version { get; set; } = "1.0.0";

    [JsonPropertyName("lastUpdatedUtc")]
    public DateTime LastUpdatedUtc { get; set; } = DateTime.UtcNow;

    [JsonPropertyName("environment")]
    public string Environment { get; set; } = "Production";
}

static class Program
{
    private const string ServerHost = "http://192.168.0.20:5000";
    private static readonly HttpClient Http = new() { Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(25) };

    private static readonly string RootPath = FolderRegistry.SKSB_App.GetPath();
    private static readonly string TemplatesFolder = FolderRegistry.Templates.GetPath();
    private static readonly string AddInsFolder = FolderRegistry.AddIns.GetPath();
    private static readonly string VersionFilePath = Path.Combine(RootPath, "version.json");
    private static readonly string PortalExePath = Path.Combine(RootPath, "Portal.exe");

    private const string LauncherMutexName = "Global\\SKSB_App_SingleGateway_Mutex";

    [STAThread]
    static void Main()
    {
        // 1. Guard against duplicate running launcher processes
        using var mutex = new Mutex(true, LauncherMutexName, out bool isInitialInstance);
        if (!isInitialInstance) return;

        Application.SetHighDpiMode(HighDpiMode.PerMonitorV2);
        Application.EnableVisualStyles();
        Application.SetCompatibleTextRenderingDefault(false);

        try
        {
            EnsureDirectoryHierarchy();

            // 2. Detection Gate: Check if initial installation is needed
            bool isFirstInstall = !File.Exists(PortalExePath) || !File.Exists(VersionFilePath);

            if (isFirstInstall)
            {
                var progressForm = new InstallationProgressForm();
                progressForm.Shown += async (s, e) =>
                {
                    try
                    {
                        await RunFullInstallationAsync(progressForm);
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex)
                    {
                        MessageBox.Show($"Installation failed: {ex.Message}", "Installation Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                    }
                    finally
                    {
                        progressForm.Close();
                    }
                };
                Application.Run(progressForm);
            }
            else
            {
                // Smart incremental update check
                CheckAndApplyIncrementalUpdate();
            }

            // 3. Launch the Portal application
            LaunchOperationsPortal();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                $"Fatal startup failure in SKSB_App:\n\n{ex.Message}",
                "Gateway Error",
                MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                MessageBoxIcon.Error
            );
        }
    }

    private static void EnsureDirectoryHierarchy()
    {
        FolderRegistry.SKSB_App.EnsureExists();
        FolderRegistry.Templates.EnsureExists();
        FolderRegistry.AddIns.EnsureExists();
        FolderRegistry.Data.EnsureExists();
    }

    private static async Task RunFullInstallationAsync(InstallationProgressForm progress)
    {
        progress.UpdateStatus("Connecting to SKSB server (192.168.0.20)...");

        KillExistingProcesses();

        var (downloaded, error) = await DownloadAndExtractFromServerAsync(progress);

        if (!downloaded)
        {
            progress.UpdateStatus("Server unreachable. Resolving local dependencies...");
            CopyLocalFolderArtifacts();
        }

        if (!File.Exists(VersionFilePath))
        {
            var defaultVer = new LauncherPackageManifest { Version = "1.0.0", LastUpdatedUtc = DateTime.UtcNow };
            WriteVersionManifest(defaultVer);
        }

        progress.UpdateStatus("Ready!");
        await Task.Delay(400);
    }

    private static void CheckAndApplyIncrementalUpdate()
    {
        LauncherPackageManifest? remoteVersion = null;
        var localVersion = ReadVersionManifest();

        try
        {
            string endpoint = $"{ServerHost}/api/update/version";
            string manifestJson = Http.GetStringAsync(endpoint).GetAwaiter().GetResult();
            remoteVersion = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<LauncherPackageManifest>(manifestJson, new JsonSerializerOptions
            {
                PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true
            });
        }
        catch
        {
            // Server offline: silently continue to launch installed local files
            return;
        }

        if (remoteVersion != null && localVersion != null && IsNewerVersion(remoteVersion.Version, localVersion.Version))
        {
            // Check if Excel is currently running on the workstation
            bool isExcelRunning = Process.GetProcessesByName("excel").Length > 0;

            if (isExcelRunning)
            {
                DialogResult choice;
                using (var dummyForm = new Form { TopMost = true })
                {
                    string message =
                        $"A new system update (v{remoteVersion.Version}) is available.\n\n" +
                        "IMPORTANT: Excel is currently open. Installing this update requires closing Excel so file locks can be released.\n\n" +
                        "Please save any open work in your spreadsheets before continuing.\n\n" +
                        "Would you like to close Excel and install the update now?";

                    choice = MessageBox.Show(
                        dummyForm,
                        message,
                        "System Update - Excel Open",
                        MessageBoxButtons.YesNo,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Warning
                    );
                }

                // If user chooses 'No', skip updating for now so their unsaved work is preserved
                if (choice != DialogResult.Yes)
                {
                    return;
                }
            }

            // Execute the update
            var updateForm = new InstallationProgressForm();
            updateForm.Shown += async (s, e) =>
            {
                try
                {
                    KillExistingProcesses();
                    updateForm.UpdateStatus($"Updating system to v{remoteVersion.Version}...");

                    var (applied, err) = await DownloadAndExtractFromServerAsync(updateForm);
                    if (applied)
                    {
                        WriteVersionManifest(remoteVersion);
                        updateForm.UpdateStatus("Update applied successfully!");
                        await Task.Delay(300);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[AutoUpdate Failed]: {err}");
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[AutoUpdate Exception]: {ex.Message}");
                }
                finally
                {
                    updateForm.Close();
                }
            };

            Application.Run(updateForm);
        }
    }

    private static async Task<(bool Success, string Error)> DownloadAndExtractFromServerAsync(InstallationProgressForm? progress)
    {
        string tempZip = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"SKSB_Setup_{Guid.NewGuid():N}.zip");
        string tempExtract = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), $"SKSB_Unpack_{Guid.NewGuid():N}");

        try
        {
            progress?.UpdateStatus("Downloading update package...");
            string packageUrl = $"{ServerHost}/api/update/download";

            using var response = await Http.GetAsync(packageUrl, HttpCompletionOption.ResponseHeadersRead);
            if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode)
            {
                return (false, $"Server returned HTTP {response.StatusCode}");
            }

            await using (var fs = new FileStream(tempZip, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.None, 81920, useAsync: true))
            {
                await response.Content.CopyToAsync(fs);
            }

            progress?.UpdateStatus("Unpacking modules and Excel add-ins...");
            await Task.Run(() => ZipFile.ExtractToDirectory(tempZip, tempExtract, overwriteFiles: true));

            progress?.UpdateStatus("Configuring application environment...");
            await Task.Run(() => DispatchFilesToDestination(tempExtract));

            return (true, string.Empty);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            return (false, ex.Message);
        }
        finally
        {
            try { if (File.Exists(tempZip)) File.Delete(tempZip); } catch { }
            try { if (Directory.Exists(tempExtract)) Directory.Delete(tempExtract, recursive: true); } catch { }
        }
    }

    private static void DispatchFilesToDestination(string sourceFolder)
    {
        foreach (string file in Directory.GetFiles(sourceFolder, "*.*", SearchOption.AllDirectories))
        {
            string fileName = Path.GetFileName(file);
            string ext = Path.GetExtension(file).ToLowerInvariant();

            string destinationPath = ext switch
            {
                ".xltx" or ".xltm" => Path.Combine(TemplatesFolder, fileName),
                ".xll" or ".dna" => Path.Combine(AddInsFolder, fileName),
                _ => Path.Combine(RootPath, fileName)
            };

            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
            {
                try
                {
                    File.Copy(file, destinationPath, overwrite: true);
                    break;
                }
                catch when (i < 2)
                {
                    Thread.Sleep(200);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private static void CopyLocalFolderArtifacts()
    {
        string baseDir = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory;
        foreach (string file in Directory.GetFiles(baseDir, "*.*"))
        {
            string name = Path.GetFileName(file);
            string ext = Path.GetExtension(file).ToLowerInvariant();

            if (ext is ".exe" or ".dll" or ".json")
            {
                try { File.Copy(file, Path.Combine(RootPath, name), overwrite: true); } catch { }
            }
        }
    }

    private static void KillExistingProcesses()
    {
        try
        {
            foreach (var p in Process.GetProcessesByName("Portal"))
            {
                try { p.Kill(); p.WaitForExit(1000); } catch { }
            }
            foreach (var p in Process.GetProcessesByName("excel"))
            {
                try { p.Kill(); p.WaitForExit(1000); } catch { }
            }
        }
        catch { }
    }

    private static void LaunchOperationsPortal()
    {
#if DEBUG
        string localDebugPortal = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(
            AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory,
            @"..\..\..\..\Portal\bin\Debug\net8.0-windows\Portal.exe"
        ));
        if (!File.Exists(localDebugPortal))
        {
            localDebugPortal = Path.Combine(FolderRegistry.SKSB_App.GetPath(), "Portal", "bin", "Debug", "net8.0-windows", "Portal.exe");
        }
        string portalExe = File.Exists(localDebugPortal) ? localDebugPortal : PortalExePath;
#else
        string portalExe = PortalExePath;
        if (!File.Exists(portalExe))
        {
            string localFallback = Path.Combine(AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory, "Portal.exe");
            portalExe = File.Exists(localFallback) ? localFallback : portalExe;
        }
#endif

        if (!File.Exists(portalExe))
        {
            MessageBox.Show(
                $"Operations Portal was not found at:\n{portalExe}\n\n" +
                $"Please verify server deployment or run the installer again.",
                "Application Missing",
                MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                MessageBoxIcon.Error
            );
            return;
        }

        Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo
        {
            FileName = portalExe,
            WorkingDirectory = Path.GetDirectoryName(portalExe) ?? RootPath,
            UseShellExecute = true
        });
    }

    private static LauncherPackageManifest? ReadVersionManifest()
    {
        if (!File.Exists(VersionFilePath)) return null;
        try
        {
            string json = File.ReadAllText(VersionFilePath);
            return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<LauncherPackageManifest>(json);
        }
        catch { return null; }
    }

    private static void WriteVersionManifest(LauncherPackageManifest ver)
    {
        try
        {
            string json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(ver, new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true });
            File.WriteAllText(VersionFilePath, json);
        }
        catch { }
    }

    private static bool IsNewerVersion(string remote, string local)
    {
        if (Version.TryParse(remote, out var r) && Version.TryParse(local, out var l))
            return r > l;
        return !string.Equals(remote, local, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Portal;

using System;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Drawing.Drawing2D;
using System.Drawing.Text;
using System.IO;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Storage;

public partial class PortalForm : Form
{
    private readonly string _currentVersion;

    public PortalForm()
    {
        _currentVersion = ResolveAppVersion();

#if DEBUG
        Text = $"SKSB Operations Portal (Debug) - v{_currentVersion}";
#else
        Text = $"SKSB Operations Portal - v{_currentVersion}";
#endif
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterScreen;
        FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedSingle;
        MaximizeBox = false;
        BackColor = Color.FromArgb(248, 250, 252);

        // Enable automated DPI scaling
        AutoScaleMode = AutoScaleMode.Dpi;
        Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9f, FontStyle.Regular);
        AutoSize = true;
        AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink;
        Padding = new Padding(24, 20, 24, 20);

        InitializeLayout();
    }

    private void InitializeLayout()
    {
        var layout = new TableLayoutPanel
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
            ColumnCount = 1,
            AutoSize = true,
            AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink,
            Margin = new Padding(0),
            Padding = new Padding(0)
        };
        layout.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100f));

        // Header Panel: Title + Version Badge
        var headerPanel = new FlowLayoutPanel
        {
            FlowDirection = FlowDirection.LeftToRight,
            AutoSize = true,
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 4)
        };

        var lblHeader = new Label
        {
#if DEBUG
            Text = "Operations Hub (Debug)",
#else
            Text = "Operations Hub",
#endif
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 16f, FontStyle.Bold),
            ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(15, 23, 42),
            AutoSize = true,
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 8, 0)
        };

        var lblVersion = new Label
        {
            Text = $"v{_currentVersion}",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 8.5f, FontStyle.Bold),
            ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(79, 70, 229),
            BackColor = Color.FromArgb(238, 242, 255),
            Padding = new Padding(6, 2, 6, 2),
            AutoSize = true,
            Margin = new Padding(0, 6, 0, 0)
        };

        headerPanel.Controls.Add(lblHeader);
        headerPanel.Controls.Add(lblVersion);

        var lblSubheader = new Label
        {
            Text = "Select an operational module to launch in Excel:",
            Font = new Font("Segoe UI", 9.5f, FontStyle.Regular),
            ForeColor = Color.FromArgb(100, 116, 139),
            AutoSize = true,
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 16)
        };

        // Module Buttons wired to ModuleRegistry matching current architecture
        var btnScheduler = CreateModuleButton(
            "Daily Scheduler",
            "Biometric logs, shifts & job site allocations",
            () => PortalRouter.Launch(ModuleRegistry.DailyScheduler));

        var btnProject = CreateModuleButton(
            "Project Manager",

        var btnStaff = CreateModuleButton(

            () => PortalRouter.Launch(ModuleRegistry.StaffManager));

        var btnVehicle = CreateModuleButton(
            "Vehicle Manager",
            "Fleet tracking, Puspakom, road tax & workshop repairs",
            () => PortalRouter.Launch(ModuleRegistry.VehicleManager));

        // Footer: Runtime Status
        var lblFooter = new Label

        layout.Controls.Add(headerPanel);

        layout.Controls.Add(btnStaff);
        layout.Controls.Add(btnVehicle);
        layout.Controls.Add(lblFooter);

        Controls.Add(layout);
    }

    private Button CreateModuleButton(string title, string subtitle, Action onClick)
    {
        var btn = new Button
        {
            Width = 360,
            Height = 64,
            BackColor = Color.White,
            FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
            Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 10)
        };
        btn.FlatAppearance.BorderColor = Color.FromArgb(226, 232, 240);
        btn.FlatAppearance.MouseOverBackColor = Color.FromArgb(241, 245, 249);
        btn.Paint += (s, e) =>
        {
            e.Graphics.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            e.Graphics.TextRenderingHint = TextRenderingHint.ClearTypeGridFit;

            using (var titleFont = new Font("Segoe UI", 10.5f, FontStyle.Bold))
            using (var titleBrush = new SolidBrush(Color.FromArgb(30, 41, 59)))
            {
                e.Graphics.DrawString(title, titleFont, titleBrush, 14, 8);
            }

            using (var subFont = new Font("Segoe UI", 8.5f, FontStyle.Regular))
            using (var subBrush = new SolidBrush(Color.FromArgb(100, 116, 139)))
            {
                e.Graphics.DrawString(subtitle, subFont, subBrush, 14, 32);
            }
        };
        btn.Click += (s, e) => onClick();
        return btn;
    }

    private static string ResolveAppVersion()
    {
        try
        {
            // 1. Check version.json in FolderRegistry.SKSB_App
            string versionFile = Path.Combine(FolderRegistry.SKSB_App.GetPath(), "version.json");
            if (File.Exists(versionFile))
            {
                string json = File.ReadAllText(versionFile);
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(json);
                if (doc.RootElement.TryGetProperty("version", out var prop))
                {
                    string? ver = prop.GetString();
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ver)) return ver.Trim().TrimStart('v');
                }
            }

            // 2. Check version.json adjacent to Portal.exe
            string localVersionFile = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "version.json");
            if (File.Exists(localVersionFile))
            {
                string json = File.ReadAllText(localVersionFile);
                using var doc = JsonDocument.Parse(json);
                if (doc.RootElement.TryGetProperty("version", out var prop))
                {
                    string? ver = prop.GetString();
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ver)) return ver.Trim().TrimStart('v');
                }
            }
        }
        catch { }

        // 3. Fallback to Assembly Version
        try
        {
            var asm = Assembly.GetEntryAssembly() ?? Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
            var ver = asm.GetName().Version;
            if (ver != null) return $"{ver.Major}.{ver.Minor}.{ver.Build}";
        }
        catch { }

        return "1.0.0";
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.Portal;

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Threading;
using System.Windows.Forms;

static class Program
{
    private const string PortalMutexName = "Global\\SKSB_App_Portal_SingleInstance_Mutex";

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    private static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr hWnd);

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    private static extern bool ShowWindow(IntPtr hWnd, int nCmdShow);

    private const int SW_RESTORE = 9;

    [STAThread]
    static void Main()
    {
        using var mutex = new Mutex(true, PortalMutexName, out bool isInitialInstance);
        if (!isInitialInstance)
        {
            FocusExistingPortal();
            return;
        }

        Application.SetHighDpiMode(HighDpiMode.PerMonitorV2);
        Application.EnableVisualStyles();
        Application.SetCompatibleTextRenderingDefault(false);
        Application.Run(new PortalForm());
    }

    private static void FocusExistingPortal()
    {
        Process current = Process.GetCurrentProcess();
        foreach (Process process in Process.GetProcessesByName(current.ProcessName))
        {
            if (process.Id != current.Id && process.MainWindowHandle != IntPtr.Zero)
            {
                ShowWindow(process.MainWindowHandle, SW_RESTORE);
                SetForegroundWindow(process.MainWindowHandle);
                break;
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: Portal\Router.cs

namespace SKSB_App.Portal;

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.IO;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Environment;
using SKSB_App.Core.Bootstrap.Storage;

public static class PortalRouter
{
    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    private static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr hWnd);

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    private static extern bool ShowWindow(IntPtr hWnd, int nCmdShow);

    [DllImport("oleaut32.dll", PreserveSig = false)]
    private static extern void GetActiveObject(
        ref Guid rclsid,
        IntPtr pvReserved,
        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.IUnknown)] out object ppunk
    );

    [DllImport("ole32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, PreserveSig = false)]
    private static extern void CLSIDFromProgID(
        [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPWStr)] string progId,
        out Guid clsid
    );

    private delegate bool EnumWindowsProc(IntPtr hWnd, IntPtr lParam);

    [DllImport("user32.dll")]
    [return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Bool)]
    private static extern bool EnumWindows(EnumWindowsProc lpEnumFunc, IntPtr lParam);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Auto, SetLastError = true)]
    private static extern int GetWindowText(IntPtr hWnd, StringBuilder lpString, int nMaxCount);

    [DllImport("user32.dll", SetLastError = true, CharSet = CharSet.Auto)]
    private static extern int GetWindowTextLength(IntPtr hWnd);

    private const int SW_RESTORE = 9;

    public static void Launch(TemplateAddInPair pair)
    {
        string excelPath = ExcelRegistry.ExcelPath;
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(excelPath) || !File.Exists(excelPath))
        {
            MessageBox.Show("Excel installation could not be detected on this workstation.",
                            "Missing Excel", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            return;
        }

        try
        {
            string templateFileName = pair.Template();
            string templateBaseName = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(templateFileName);

            // 1. Check if an Excel window with this specific workbook is already open
            if (TryFocusExistingWorkbook(templateBaseName))
            {
                return; // Module already active and brought to front
            }

            // 2. Resolve Template & Target Add-In paths
            string templatePath = ResolveTemplatePath(templateFileName);
            string addInPath = TemplateAddInPair.ResolveAddInPath(pair.AddIn());

            // 3. Resolve Core Add-In path so it loads alongside every manager
            string coreAddInName = ExcelRegistry.OfficeBitness.Equals(Bitness.Win32)
                ? "Core-AddIn.xll"
                : "Core-AddIn64.xll";
            string coreAddInPath = TemplateAddInPair.ResolveAddInPath(coreAddInName);

            if (!File.Exists(templatePath))
                throw new FileNotFoundException($"Template file not found at:\n{templatePath}", templatePath);
            if (!File.Exists(addInPath))
                throw new FileNotFoundException($"Module Add-in not found at:\n{addInPath}", addInPath);
            if (!File.Exists(coreAddInPath))
                throw new FileNotFoundException($"Core Add-in not found at:\n{coreAddInPath}", coreAddInPath);

            // 4. Use /x to force an isolated Excel process, preventing add-in cross-contamination
            var startInfo = new ProcessStartInfo
            {
                FileName = excelPath,
                Arguments = $"/x /t \"{templatePath}\" \"{coreAddInPath}\" \"{addInPath}\"",
                UseShellExecute = true
            };

            Process.Start(startInfo);
        }
        catch (FileNotFoundException fnf)
        {
            MessageBox.Show($"File missing:\n{fnf.FileName}",
                            "Launch Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show($"Failed to dispatch module:\n{ex.Message}",
                            "Execution Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
        }
    }

    private static string ResolveTemplatePath(string templateFileName)
    {
#if DEBUG
        // 1. Check Codes Root: Documents\codes\SKSB_App\Templates\<file>
        string codesTemplates = Path.Combine(FolderRegistry.SKSB_App.GetPath(), "Templates", templateFileName);
        if (File.Exists(codesTemplates)) return codesTemplates;

        // 2. Check explicit MyDocuments\codes\SKSB_App\Templates
        string directCodes = Path.Combine(
            Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.MyDocuments),
            "codes", "SKSB_App", "Templates", templateFileName);
        if (File.Exists(directCodes)) return directCodes;

        // 3. Check AppContext.BaseDirectory\Templates
        string binTemplates = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "Templates", templateFileName);
        if (File.Exists(binTemplates)) return binTemplates;

        return codesTemplates;
#else
        // Release: Always Documents\SKSB_App\Templates\<file>
        return FolderRegistry.Templates.GetFilePath(templateFileName, mustExist: true);
#endif
    }

    private static bool TryFocusExistingWorkbook(string targetWorkbookName)
    {
        try
        {
            object? xlAppObj = GetActiveObjectHelper("Excel.Application");
            if (xlAppObj != null)
            {
                dynamic xlApp = xlAppObj;
                foreach (dynamic wb in xlApp.Workbooks)
                {
                    string wbName = wb.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                    if (wbName.IndexOf(targetWorkbookName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                    {
                        wb.Activate();
                        xlApp.Visible = true;
                        IntPtr hwnd = new IntPtr((int)xlApp.Hwnd);
                        if (hwnd != IntPtr.Zero)
                        {
                            ShowWindow(hwnd, SW_RESTORE);
                            SetForegroundWindow(hwnd);
                            return true;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        catch { }

        IntPtr foundHwnd = IntPtr.Zero;
        EnumWindows((hWnd, lParam) =>
        {
            int length = GetWindowTextLength(hWnd);
            if (length > 0)
            {
                var sb = new StringBuilder(length + 1);
                GetWindowText(hWnd, sb, sb.Capacity);
                string title = sb.ToString();

                if (title.IndexOf("Excel", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0 &&
                    title.IndexOf(targetWorkbookName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                {
                    foundHwnd = hWnd;
                    return false;
                }
            }
            return true;
        }, IntPtr.Zero);

        if (foundHwnd != IntPtr.Zero)
        {
            ShowWindow(foundHwnd, SW_RESTORE);
            SetForegroundWindow(foundHwnd);
            return true;
        }

        return false;
    }

    private static object? GetActiveObjectHelper(string progId)
    {
        try
        {
            CLSIDFromProgID(progId, out Guid clsid);
            GetActiveObject(ref clsid, IntPtr.Zero, out object obj);
            return obj;
        }
        catch
        {
            return null;
        }
    }
}

FILE: ProjectManager\ProjectManager.Events.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.ProjectManager;

using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.FileManager;
using SKSB_App.Core.FileManager.Structure;
using SKSB_App.Core.TemplateEditor;
using SKSB_App.Core.ServerManager;
using SKSB_App.Core.Services;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookHelper;
using SKSB_App.Core.DialogBoxes;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Windows.Forms;

public class Events : IExcelAddIn
{
    private delegate void WorkbookBeforeCloseDelegate(dynamic wb, ref bool cancel);
    private static dynamic? _appEvents;
    private static WorkbookBeforeCloseDelegate? _closeHandler;

    public void AutoOpen()
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            HookApplicationClose();

            // 1. Sync live database entities (Companies) to the 'Data' sheet
            SyncProjectLookupsData();

            // 2. Startup reconciliation with conflict detection
            double nowSerial = Convert.ToDouble(XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlfNow));
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnTime, nowSerial + (1.0 / 86400.0), "DelayedAutoLoadMacro");

            // 3. Hotkeys
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^s", "OnHotKeySave");
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^+s", "OnHotKeySave");
        });
    }

    public void AutoClose()
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            UnhookApplicationClose();
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^s");
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^+s");
        });
    }

    public static void SyncProjectLookupsData()
    {
        // Pushes short/legal company names into 'Data' sheet -> Generates =CompanyNamesList in Name Manager
        DataToExcel.SyncData<CompanyRecord, string>("CompanyName", c =>
            !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(c.ShortCompanyName) ? c.ShortCompanyName : c.LegalCompanyName);
    }

    private static void HookApplicationClose()
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            _appEvents = xlApp;
            _closeHandler = new WorkbookBeforeCloseDelegate(OnWorkbookBeforeClose);
            xlApp.WorkbookBeforeClose += _closeHandler;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[Events] Failed to hook WorkbookBeforeClose: {ex.Message}");
        }
    }

    private static void UnhookApplicationClose()
    {
        try
        {
            if (_appEvents != null && _closeHandler != null)
            {
                _appEvents.WorkbookBeforeClose -= _closeHandler;
                _closeHandler = null;
                _appEvents = null;
            }
        }
        catch { }
    }

    private static void OnWorkbookBeforeClose(dynamic wb, ref bool cancel)
    {
        try
        {
            if (WorkbookSession.IsMasterTemplateFile() || WorkbookSession.IsTemplateEditMode) return;

            while (true)
            {
                // Attempt standard silent server save
                bool saved = ServerSyncCommands.SaveProjectsSilent();

                if (saved)
                {
                    // Success: Clear any offline stash if it existed
                    OfflineSaveServices.ClearPendingOfflineSnapshot<List<ProjectRecord>>();
                    wb.Saved = true;
                    return;
                }

                // Failed: Show 3-way conflict dialog
                using var dialog = new ExitSyncConflict(
                    "Project Manager",
                    "Could not persist records to the PostgreSQL server (connection timeout or schema conflict)."
                );
                dialog.ShowDialog();

                switch (dialog.Resolution)
                {
                    case ExitResolution.Retry:
                        continue;

                    case ExitResolution.SaveLocallyAndQuit:
                        var (currentProjects, _) = SheetMapper.ExtractProjectRecords();
                        if (currentProjects != null && currentProjects.Count > 0)
                        {
                            OfflineSaveServices.SaveOfflineSnapshot(currentProjects);
                            MessageBox.Show(
                                "Your project records have been safely saved to local disk.\n\n" +
                                "The system will automatically detect this file and prompt to commit it to the server when you next start.",
                                "Saved Offline",
                                MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                                MessageBoxIcon.Information
                            );
                        }

                        wb.Saved = true;
                        return;

                    case ExitResolution.Cancel:
                    default:
                        cancel = true;
                        return;
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[OnWorkbookBeforeClose] Error: {ex.Message}");
        }
    }

    [ExcelCommand(Name = "DelayedAutoLoadMacro")]
    public static void DelayedAutoLoadMacro()
    {
        try
        {
            if (WorkbookSession.IsMasterTemplateFile() || WorkbookSession.IsTemplateEditMode)
            {
                WorkbookSession.ShowStatusConfirmation("Design Mode: Auto-load bypassed.");
                return;
            }

            // -------------------------------------------------------------
            // STARTUP RECONCILIATION: Check for pending offline file
            // -------------------------------------------------------------
            if (OfflineSaveServices.HasPendingOfflineSnapshot<List<ProjectRecord>>())
            {
                var pendingRecords = OfflineSaveServices.LoadPendingOfflineSnapshot<List<ProjectRecord>>();
                DateTime localOfflineTime = File.GetLastWriteTime(
                    Folder.Data.GetFilePath("List`1_PendingUpload.json")
                );

                DateTime? serverLastUpdate = OfflineSaveServices.GetServerLatestTimestamp("project_master");
                bool canCommit = true;

                if (serverLastUpdate.HasValue && serverLastUpdate.Value > localOfflineTime)
                {
                    var conflictChoice = MessageBox.Show(
                        $"[Synchronization Conflict Detected]\n\n" +
                        $"• Your offline saved file: {localOfflineTime:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss}\n" +
                        $"• Newer server version:  {serverLastUpdate.Value:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss}\n\n" +
                        "The server was updated after your offline save.\n\n" +
                        "Do you still want to COMMIT your offline changes to the server?\n" +
                        "• Yes: Overwrite server records with your local offline version.\n" +
                        "• No: Discard local offline changes and load the latest server copy.",
                        "Data Version Conflict",
                        MessageBoxButtons.YesNo,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Question
                    );

                    canCommit = (conflictChoice == DialogResult.Yes);
                }

                if (canCommit && pendingRecords != null && pendingRecords.Count > 0)
                {
                    var (success, count, _) = ServerSyncCommands.SaveProjectsAsync(pendingRecords)
                                                                .GetAwaiter()
                                                                .GetResult();
                    if (success)
                    {
                        OfflineSaveServices.ClearPendingOfflineSnapshot<List<ProjectRecord>>();
                        var companies = ServerSyncCommands.FetchCompanyRecords() ?? new List<CompanyRecord>();
                        SheetMapper.PopulateWorksheet(pendingRecords, companies);
                        WorkbookSession.ShowStatusConfirmation($"Committed {count} offline project records to server.");
                        return;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    OfflineSaveServices.ClearPendingOfflineSnapshot<List<ProjectRecord>>();
                }
            }

            // Standard operational load from PostgreSQL
            if (ServerSyncCommands.LoadProjectsSilent())
            {
                WorkbookSession.ShowStatusConfirmation("Project records loaded from server.");
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[DelayedAutoLoad] Error: {ex.Message}");
        }
    }

    [ExcelCommand(Name = "OnHotKeySave")]
    public static void OnHotKeySave()
    {
        if (WorkbookSession.IsMasterTemplateFile() || WorkbookSession.IsTemplateEditMode)
        {
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcSave);
            return;
        }

        bool success = ServerSyncCommands.SaveProjectsSilent();
        if (success)
        {
            OfflineSaveServices.ClearPendingOfflineSnapshot<List<ProjectRecord>>();
            MessageBox.Show("Save completed successfully.", "SKSB Project Manager", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
        }
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.ProjectManager;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using ExcelDna.Integration.CustomUI;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;

[ComVisible(true)]
public class RibbonController : ExcelRibbon
{
    private static IRibbonUI? _ribbonUi;

    public override string GetCustomUI(string ribbonId)
    {
        return @"
        <customUI xmlns='http://schemas.microsoft.com/office/2009/07/customui' onLoad='OnRibbonLoad'>
          <ribbon>
            <tabs>
              <tab id='tabProjectManager' label='Project Manager'>
                <group id='grpProjectWorkbookHelper' label='Project Database Sync'>
                  <button id='btnSaveProjects' 
                          label='Save to System' 
                          size='large' 
                          imageMso='SaveObject' 
                          onAction='OnSaveClicked' />
                  <button id='btnLoadProjects' 
                          label='Load from System' 
                          size='large' 
                          imageMso='Refresh' 
                          onAction='OnLoadClicked' />
                  <button id='btnSyncLookups' 
                          label='Sync Lookups' 
                          size='large' 
                          imageMso='GroupCustomAttributes' 
                          onAction='OnSyncLookupsClicked' />
                </group>
              </tab>
            </tabs>
          </ribbon>
        </customUI>";
    }

    public void OnRibbonLoad(IRibbonUI ribbon) => _ribbonUi = ribbon;

    public void OnSyncLookupsClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        try
        {
            Events.SyncProjectLookupsData();
            MessageBox.Show(
                "Project lookups refreshed successfully.\n\n• Lookups: Enums managed centrally in Core\n• Data: Live company entities updated in 'Data' sheet",
                "Lookups Synced",
                MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                MessageBoxIcon.Information
            );
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show($"Sync failed: {ex.Message}", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
        }
    }

    public void OnSaveClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        // 1. MAIN UI THREAD: Read Excel cells safely via C-API
        var (projectList, companyList) = SheetMapper.ExtractProjectRecords();
        if (projectList == null || projectList.Count == 0)
        {
            return;
        }

        // 2. BACKGROUND STAFF: Offload disk serialization and PostgreSQL WorkbookHelper
        Task.Run(async () =>
        {
            try
            {
                // Save Companies first if any were modified
                if (companyList != null && companyList.Count > 0)
                {
                    await ServerSyncCommands.SaveCompaniesAsync(companyList);
                }

                var (success, count, path) = await ServerSyncCommands.SaveProjectsAsync(projectList);
                if (success)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        $"Successfully saved {count} project record(s).\n\n• Storage: {path}",
                        "Save Success",
                        MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Information
                    );
                }
                else
                {
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        "Failed to persist projects to PostgreSQL or local storage.",
                        "WorkbookHelper Error",
                        MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Warning
                    );
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"Save Error: {ex.Message}", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
        });
    }

    public void OnLoadClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        var records = ServerSyncCommands.FetchProjectRecords();
        var companies = ServerSyncCommands.FetchCompanyRecords() ?? new List<CompanyRecord>();

        if (records == null || records.Count == 0)
        {
            MessageBox.Show("No project records found in storage.", "Load Projects", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
            return;
        }

        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            SheetMapper.PopulateWorksheet(records, companies);
            MessageBox.Show($"Successfully loaded {records.Count} project record(s).", "Load Success", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
        });
    }
}

FILE: ProjectManager\ProjectManager.ServerSyncCommands.cs

namespace SKSB_App.ProjectManager;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using SKSB_App.Core.FileManager;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;

public static class ServerSyncCommands
{
    public static bool SaveProjectsSilent()
    {
        try
        {
            var (projectList, companyList) = SheetMapper.ExtractProjectRecords();
            if (projectList == null || projectList.Count == 0)
            {
                return false;
            }

            if (companyList != null && companyList.Count > 0)
            {
                OfflineSaveServices.SaveAsync(companyList, writeToDatabase: true).GetAwaiter().GetResult();
            }

            string snapshotPath = OfflineSaveServices.SaveAsync(projectList, writeToDatabase: true)
                                          .GetAwaiter()
                                          .GetResult();

            return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(snapshotPath);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[ServerSyncCommands] Silent Save Error: {ex.Message}");
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static bool LoadProjectsSilent()
    {
        try
        {
            var projectList = OfflineSaveServices.Load<List<ProjectRecord>>();
            var companyList = OfflineSaveServices.Load<List<CompanyRecord>>() ?? new List<CompanyRecord>();

            if (projectList != null && projectList.Count > 0)
            {
                SheetMapper.PopulateWorksheet(projectList, companyList);
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[ServerSyncCommands] Silent Load Error: {ex.Message}");
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static async Task<(bool Success, int Count, string Path)> SaveProjectsAsync(List<ProjectRecord> projectList)
    {
        if (projectList == null || projectList.Count == 0) return (false, 0, string.Empty);

        string path = await OfflineSaveServices.SaveAsync(projectList, writeToDatabase: true);
        return (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path), projectList.Count, path);
    }

    public static async Task<bool> SaveCompaniesAsync(List<CompanyRecord> companyList)
    {
        if (companyList == null || companyList.Count == 0) return false;
        string path = await OfflineSaveServices.SaveAsync(companyList, writeToDatabase: true);
        return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(path);
    }

    public static List<ProjectRecord>? FetchProjectRecords()
    {
        return OfflineSaveServices.Load<List<ProjectRecord>>();
    }

    public static List<CompanyRecord>? FetchCompanyRecords()
    {
        return OfflineSaveServices.Load<List<CompanyRecord>>();
    }
}

FILE: ProjectManager\ProjectManager.SheetMapper.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.ProjectManager;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookHelper;

public static class SheetMapper
{
    private const int ProjectColumnSpan = 6;  // Columns A to F
    private const int CompanyColumnSpan = 3;  // Columns A to C

    private static readonly string[] AllProjectSheetAliases = { "All Projects", "All_Projects", "Project_Master", "Projects" };
    private static readonly string[] CompanySheetAliases = { "Companies", "Company", "Company_Master" };

    public static (List<ProjectRecord> Projects, List<CompanyRecord> Companies) ExtractProjectRecords()
    {
        var projectResult = new List<ProjectRecord>();
        var companyResult = new List<CompanyRecord>();
        var validationErrors = new List<string>();

        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // 1. EXTRACT COMPANIES (Sheet: Companies, Columns A to C)
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        string compSheet = WorkbookSession.ResolveSheetName(CompanySheetAliases, "Companies");
        IntPtr compSheetId = WorkbookSession.GetSheetId(compSheet);

        var compRef = compSheetId != IntPtr.Zero
            ? new ExcelReference(1, 500, 0, CompanyColumnSpan - 1, compSheetId)
            : new ExcelReference(1, 500, 0, CompanyColumnSpan - 1);

        if (compRef.GetValue() is object[,] compGrid)
        {
            int rows = compGrid.GetLength(0);
            for (int r = 0; r < rows; r++)
            {
                object rawId = compGrid[r, 0];
                if (rawId == null || rawId is ExcelEmpty || !long.TryParse(rawId.ToString(), out long compId) || compId <= 0)
                    continue;

                string legalName = CleanCellString(compGrid[r, 1]);
                string shortName = CleanCellString(compGrid[r, 2]);

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(legalName) && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(shortName))
                    continue;

                companyResult.Add(new CompanyRecord
                {
                    CompanyID = compId,
                    LegalCompanyName = legalName,
                    ShortCompanyName = shortName
                });
            }
        }

        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // 2. EXTRACT PROJECTS (Sheet: All Projects, Columns A to F)
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        string projSheet = WorkbookSession.ResolveSheetName(AllProjectSheetAliases, "All Projects");
        IntPtr projSheetId = WorkbookSession.GetSheetId(projSheet);

        var projRef = projSheetId != IntPtr.Zero
            ? new ExcelReference(1, 1000, 0, ProjectColumnSpan - 1, projSheetId)
            : new ExcelReference(1, 1000, 0, ProjectColumnSpan - 1);

        if (projRef.GetValue() is object[,] projGrid)
        {
            int rows = projGrid.GetLength(0);
            for (int r = 0; r < rows; r++)
            {
                // Col A (0): Project Type
                string typeStr = CleanCellString(projGrid[r, 0]);
                // Col B (1): Project ID (e.g. PRJ-001 or IJ-102)
                string projectId = CleanCellString(projGrid[r, 1]);
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(projectId))
                    continue;

                // Col C (2): Project Name
                string projectName = CleanCellString(projGrid[r, 2]);
                // Col D (3): Company
                string companyName = CleanCellString(projGrid[r, 3]);
                // Col E (4): Start Date
                DateOnly startDate = ParseExcelDate(projGrid[r, 4]);
                // Col F (5): End Date
                DateOnly endDate = ParseExcelDate(projGrid[r, 5]);

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(projectName))
                {
                    validationErrors.Add($"• Row {r + 2} [All Projects]: Project '{projectId}' is missing a Project Name.");
                    continue;
                }

                ProjectType pType = Enum.TryParse(typeStr, true, out ProjectType parsedType)
                    ? parsedType
                    : ProjectType.None;

                if (startDate == default) startDate = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today);
                if (endDate == default) endDate = startDate.AddDays(30);

                var record = new ProjectRecord
                {
                    ProjectID = projectId,
                    ProjectDetails = new ProjectDetails
                    {
                        ProjectID = projectId,
                        ProjectType = pType,
                        ProjectName = projectName,
                        CompanyName = companyName,
                        StartDate = startDate,
                        TargetEndDate = endDate,
                        Status = ProjectStatus.Active
                    }
                };

                projectResult.Add(record);
            }
        }

        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // PRE-SAVE VALIDATION GATEWAY: Prevent saving invalid rows
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        if (validationErrors.Count > 0)
        {
            var sb = new StringBuilder();
            sb.AppendLine("Cannot save to system due to validation errors:\n");
            foreach (var err in validationErrors) sb.AppendLine(err);
            sb.AppendLine("\nPlease correct the entries and try saving again.");

            MessageBox.Show(sb.ToString(), "Pre-Save Validation Failed", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
            return (new List<ProjectRecord>(), new List<CompanyRecord>());
        }

        return (projectResult, companyResult);
    }

    public static void PopulateWorksheet(List<ProjectRecord> projectList, List<CompanyRecord> companyList)
    {
        string targetProj = WorkbookSession.ResolveSheetName(AllProjectSheetAliases, "All Projects");
        string targetComp = WorkbookSession.ResolveSheetName(CompanySheetAliases, "Companies");

        object initialActiveSheet = XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlfGetDocument, 1);

        try
        {
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcEcho, false);

            // 1. POPULATE ALL PROJECTS (Columns A to F)
            if (projectList != null && projectList.Count > 0)
            {
                XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcWorkbookSelect, new object[] { targetProj });
                var clearProjRef = new ExcelReference(1, 1000, 0, ProjectColumnSpan - 1);
                clearProjRef.SetValue(new object[1000, ProjectColumnSpan]);

                int pCount = projectList.Count;
                object[,] projOutput = new object[pCount, ProjectColumnSpan];

                for (int i = 0; i < pCount; i++)
                {
                    var p = projectList[i];
                    projOutput[i, 0] = p.ProjectDetails.ProjectType == ProjectType.None ? string.Empty : p.ProjectDetails.ProjectType.ToString();
                    projOutput[i, 1] = p.ProjectID;
                    projOutput[i, 2] = p.ProjectDetails.ProjectName;
                    projOutput[i, 3] = p.ProjectDetails.CompanyName;
                    projOutput[i, 4] = p.ProjectDetails.StartDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue).ToOADate();
                    projOutput[i, 5] = p.ProjectDetails.TargetEndDate.ToDateTime(TimeOnly.MinValue).ToOADate();
                }

                var writeProjRef = new ExcelReference(1, pCount, 0, ProjectColumnSpan - 1);
                writeProjRef.SetValue(projOutput);
            }

            // 2. POPULATE COMPANIES (Columns A to C)
            if (companyList != null && companyList.Count > 0)
            {
                XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcWorkbookSelect, new object[] { targetComp });
                var clearCompRef = new ExcelReference(1, 500, 0, CompanyColumnSpan - 1);
                clearCompRef.SetValue(new object[500, CompanyColumnSpan]);

                int cCount = companyList.Count;
                object[,] compOutput = new object[cCount, CompanyColumnSpan];

                for (int i = 0; i < cCount; i++)
                {
                    var c = companyList[i];
                    compOutput[i, 0] = c.CompanyID;
                    compOutput[i, 1] = c.LegalCompanyName;
                    compOutput[i, 2] = c.ShortCompanyName;
                }

                var writeCompRef = new ExcelReference(1, cCount, 0, CompanyColumnSpan - 1);
                writeCompRef.SetValue(compOutput);
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            if (initialActiveSheet != null)
            {
                string sheetStr = initialActiveSheet.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                int bIdx = sheetStr.LastIndexOf(']');
                string restoreName = bIdx >= 0 ? sheetStr.Substring(bIdx + 1) : sheetStr;

                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(restoreName))
                {
                    try { XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcWorkbookSelect, new object[] { restoreName }); } catch { }
                }
            }

            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcEcho, true);
        }
    }

    private static string CleanCellString(object? cell) =>
        (cell == null || cell is ExcelEmpty || cell is ExcelMissing || cell is ExcelError)
            ? string.Empty
            : cell.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;

    private static DateOnly ParseExcelDate(object? cell)
    {
        if (cell is double d) return DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.FromOADate(d));
        if (DateTime.TryParse(CleanCellString(cell), out DateTime dt)) return DateOnly.FromDateTime(dt);
        return default;
    }
}

FILE: ScheduleManager\ScheduleManager.Events.cs
