namespace SKSB_App.ScheduleManager;

using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.DialogBoxes;
using SKSB_App.Core.FileManager;
using SKSB_App.Core.FileManager.Structure;
using SKSB_App.Core.ServerManager;
using SKSB_App.Core.Services;
using SKSB_App.Core.TemplateEditor;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookHelper;

public class Events : IExcelAddIn
{
    private delegate void WorkbookBeforeCloseDelegate(dynamic wb, ref bool cancel);
    private static dynamic? _appEvents;
    private static WorkbookBeforeCloseDelegate? _closeHandler;

    public void AutoOpen()
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            HookApplicationClose();

            // 1. Sync Core static enums into 'Lookups' tab
            ToExcel.Sync(
                typeof(Shifts),
                typeof(Days),
                typeof(AttendanceStatus),
                typeof(BiometricStatus),
                typeof(VehicleType)
            );

            // 2. Sync dynamic master entities (Staff, Vehicle, Project) into 'Data' tab
            SyncScheduleLookupsData();

            // 3. Delayed startup macro for auto-load & offline reconciliation
            double nowSerial = Convert.ToDouble(XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlfNow));
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnTime, nowSerial + (1.0 / 86400.0), "DelayedAutoLoadScheduleMacro");

            // 4. Hotkeys (Ctrl+S and Ctrl+Shift+S)
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^s", "OnHotKeyScheduleSave");
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^+s", "OnHotKeyScheduleSave");
        });
    }

    public void AutoClose()
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            UnhookApplicationClose();
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^s");
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^+s");
        });
    }

    public static void SyncScheduleLookupsData()
    {
        // Spawns 'Data' sheet and registers defined names
        DataToExcel.SyncData<StaffRecord, long>("StaffID", s => s.StaffID);
        DataToExcel.SyncData<StaffRecord, string>("StaffName", s => s.StaffDetails.StaffName);
        DataToExcel.SyncData<VehicleRecord, string>("VehiclePlate", v => v.VehiclePlate);
        DataToExcel.SyncData<ProjectRecord, string>("ProjectID", p => p.ProjectID);
    }

    private static void HookApplicationClose()
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            _appEvents = xlApp;
            _closeHandler = new WorkbookBeforeCloseDelegate(OnWorkbookBeforeClose);
            xlApp.WorkbookBeforeClose += _closeHandler;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[ScheduleManager.Events] Hook error: {ex.Message}");
        }
    }

    private static void UnhookApplicationClose()
    {
        try
        {
            if (_appEvents != null && _closeHandler != null)
            {
                _appEvents.WorkbookBeforeClose -= _closeHandler;
                _closeHandler = null;
                _appEvents = null;
            }
        }
        catch { }
    }

    private static void OnWorkbookBeforeClose(dynamic wb, ref bool cancel)
    {
        try
        {
            if (WorkbookSession.IsMasterTemplateFile() || WorkbookSession.IsTemplateEditMode) return;

            while (true)
            {
                // Attempt standard silent server save
                bool saved = ServerSyncCommands.SaveScheduleSilent(RibbonController.CurrentScheduleDate);

                if (saved)
                {
                    OfflineSaveServices.ClearPendingOfflineSnapshot<ScheduleRecord>();
                    wb.Saved = true;
                    return;
                }

                // Failed: Show 3-way conflict dialog
                using var dialog = new ExitSyncConflict(
                    "Schedule Manager",
                    $"Could not persist the schedule for {RibbonController.CurrentScheduleDate:yyyy-MM-dd} to the PostgreSQL server."
                );
                dialog.ShowDialog();

                switch (dialog.Resolution)
                {
                    case ExitResolution.Retry:
                        continue;

                    case ExitResolution.SaveLocallyAndQuit:
                        var currentSchedule = SheetMapper.ExtractScheduleRecord(RibbonController.CurrentScheduleDate);
                        if (currentSchedule != null)
                        {
                            OfflineSaveServices.SaveOfflineSnapshot(currentSchedule);
                            MessageBox.Show(
                                $"Schedule for {RibbonController.CurrentScheduleDate:yyyy-MM-dd} saved locally to offline staging.",
                                "Saved Offline",
                                MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                                MessageBoxIcon.Information
                            );
                        }
                        wb.Saved = true;
                        return;

                    case ExitResolution.Cancel:
                    default:
                        cancel = true;
                        return;
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[ScheduleManager.Events] OnWorkbookBeforeClose error: {ex.Message}");
        }
    }

    [ExcelCommand(Name = "DelayedAutoLoadScheduleMacro")]
    public static void DelayedAutoLoadScheduleMacro()
    {
        try
        {
            if (WorkbookSession.IsMasterTemplateFile() || WorkbookSession.IsTemplateEditMode)
            {
                WorkbookSession.ShowStatusConfirmation("Design Mode: Auto-load bypassed.");
                return;
            }

            // -------------------------------------------------------------
            // STARTUP RECONCILIATION: Check for pending offline file
            // -------------------------------------------------------------
            if (OfflineSaveServices.HasPendingOfflineSnapshot<ScheduleRecord>())
            {
                var pendingSchedule = OfflineSaveServices.LoadPendingOfflineSnapshot<ScheduleRecord>();
                DateTime localOfflineTime = File.GetLastWriteTime(
                    Folder.Data.GetFilePath("ScheduleRecord_PendingUpload.json")
                );

                DateTime? serverLastUpdate = OfflineSaveServices.GetServerLatestTimestamp("daily_schedule");
                bool canCommit = true;

                if (serverLastUpdate.HasValue && serverLastUpdate.Value > localOfflineTime)
                {
                    var conflictChoice = MessageBox.Show(
                        $"[Synchronization Conflict Detected]\n\n" +
                        $"• Your offline saved schedule: {localOfflineTime:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss}\n" +
                        $"• Newer server version:         {serverLastUpdate.Value:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss}\n\n" +
                        "Do you still want to COMMIT your offline changes to the server?\n" +
                        "• Yes: Overwrite server schedule with your local version.\n" +
                        "• No: Discard local changes and load latest server copy.",
                        "Data Version Conflict",
                        MessageBoxButtons.YesNo,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Question
                    );
                    canCommit = (conflictChoice == DialogResult.Yes);
                }

                if (canCommit && pendingSchedule != null)
                {
                    var (success, _) = ServerSyncCommands.SaveScheduleAsync(pendingSchedule)
                                                         .GetAwaiter()
                                                         .GetResult();
                    if (success)
                    {
                        OfflineSaveServices.ClearPendingOfflineSnapshot<ScheduleRecord>();
                        RibbonController.SetScheduleDate(pendingSchedule.DailyScheduleDate);
                        SheetMapper.PopulateScheduleSheets(pendingSchedule);
                        WorkbookSession.ShowStatusConfirmation($"Committed offline schedule for {pendingSchedule.DailyScheduleDate} to server.");
                        return;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    OfflineSaveServices.ClearPendingOfflineSnapshot<ScheduleRecord>();
                }
            }

            // Standard operational load for the current date
            if (ServerSyncCommands.LoadScheduleSilent(RibbonController.CurrentScheduleDate))
            {
                WorkbookSession.ShowStatusConfirmation($"Schedule for {RibbonController.CurrentScheduleDate:yyyy-MM-dd} loaded from server.");
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[ScheduleManager.Events] DelayedAutoLoad error: {ex.Message}");
        }
    }

    [ExcelCommand(Name = "OnHotKeyScheduleSave")]
    public static void OnHotKeyScheduleSave()
    {
        if (WorkbookSession.IsMasterTemplateFile() || WorkbookSession.IsTemplateEditMode)
        {
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcSave);
            return;
        }

        bool success = ServerSyncCommands.SaveScheduleSilent(RibbonController.CurrentScheduleDate);
        if (success)
        {
            OfflineSaveServices.ClearPendingOfflineSnapshot<ScheduleRecord>();
            MessageBox.Show(
                $"Schedule for {RibbonController.CurrentScheduleDate:yyyy-MM-dd} saved successfully.",
                "SKSB Schedule Manager",
                MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                MessageBoxIcon.Information
            );
        }
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.ScheduleManager;

using ExcelDna.Integration;
using ExcelDna.Integration.CustomUI;
using SKSB_App.Core.FileManager;
using SKSB_App.Core.Services;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookHelper;
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Forms;

[ComVisible(true)]
public class RibbonController : ExcelRibbon
{
    private static IRibbonUI? _ribbonUi;
    public static DateOnly CurrentScheduleDate { get; private set; } = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today);

    public static void SetScheduleDate(DateOnly newDate)
    {
        CurrentScheduleDate = newDate;
        _ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl("ebScheduleDate");
    }

    public override string GetCustomUI(string ribbonId)
    {
        return @"
        <customUI xmlns='http://schemas.microsoft.com/office/2009/07/customui' onLoad='OnRibbonLoad'>
          <ribbon>
            <tabs>
              <tab id='tabScheduleManager' label='Schedule Manager'>
                <group id='grpScheduleDate' label='Schedule Date'>
                  <editBox id='ebScheduleDate' 
                           label='Date (YYYY-MM-DD):' 
                           sizeString='2026-10-14'
                           getText='GetScheduleDateText' 
                           onChange='OnScheduleDateChanged' />
                  <button id='btnLoadDay' 
                          label='Load from System' 
                          size='large' 
                          imageMso='Refresh' 
                          onAction='OnLoadClicked' />
                  <button id='btnSaveDay' 
                          label='Save to System' 
                          size='large' 
                          imageMso='SaveObject' 
                          onAction='OnSaveClicked' />
                  <button id='btnSyncLookups' 
                          label='Sync Lookups' 
                          size='large' 
                          imageMso='GroupCustomAttributes' 
                          onAction='OnSyncLookupsClicked' />
                </group>
              </tab>
            </tabs>
          </ribbon>
        </customUI>";
    }

    public void OnRibbonLoad(IRibbonUI ribbon) => _ribbonUi = ribbon;

    public string GetScheduleDateText(IRibbonControl control) => CurrentScheduleDate.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd");

    public void OnScheduleDateChanged(IRibbonControl control, string text)
    {
        if (DateOnly.TryParse(text, out DateOnly newDate))
        {
            CurrentScheduleDate = newDate;
            WorkbookSession.ShowStatusConfirmation($"Switched to Date: {newDate:yyyy-MM-dd}");
            ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() => OnLoadClicked(control));
        }
        else
        {
            MessageBox.Show("Please enter a valid date formatted as YYYY-MM-DD.", "Invalid Date", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
            _ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl("ebScheduleDate");
        }
    }

    public void OnSyncLookupsClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        try
        {
            Events.SyncScheduleLookupsData();
            MessageBox.Show(
                "Schedule lookup data refreshed successfully.\n\n• Lookups: Synced core enums\n• Data: Live Staff, Vehicle, and Project identifiers refreshed",
                "Lookups Synced",
                MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                MessageBoxIcon.Information
            );
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show($"Lookup sync failed: {ex.Message}", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
        }
    }

    public void OnSaveClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        // 1. MAIN UI THREAD: Synchronous read of Excel cells via C-API with validation gate
        var schedule = SheetMapper.ExtractScheduleRecord(CurrentScheduleDate);
        if (schedule == null)
        {
            return; // Pre-save validation failed or no data
        }

        // 2. BACKGROUND STAFF: Offload serialization and PostgreSQL WorkbookHelper
        Task.Run(async () =>
        {
            try
            {
                var (success, path) = await ServerSyncCommands.SaveScheduleAsync(schedule);
                if (success)
                {
                    OfflineSaveServices.ClearPendingOfflineSnapshot<ScheduleRecord>();
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        $"Successfully saved schedule for {schedule.DailyScheduleDate:yyyy-MM-dd}.\n\n• Storage: {path}",
                        "Save Success",
                        MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Information
                    );
                }
                else
                {
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        "Failed to persist schedule to database or local storage.",
                        "WorkbookHelper Error",
                        MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Warning
                    );
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"Save Error: {ex.Message}", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
        });
    }

    public void OnLoadClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        var record = ServerSyncCommands.FetchSchedule(CurrentScheduleDate);
        if (record == null)
        {
            MessageBox.Show($"No saved schedule found for {CurrentScheduleDate:yyyy-MM-dd}.", "Load Schedule", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
            return;
        }

        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            SheetMapper.PopulateScheduleSheets(record);
            WorkbookSession.ShowStatusConfirmation($"Loaded schedule for {CurrentScheduleDate:yyyy-MM-dd}");
        });
    }
}

FILE: ScheduleManager\ScheduleManager.ServerSyncCommand.cs

namespace SKSB_App.ScheduleManager;

using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using SKSB_App.Core.FileManager;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;

public static class ServerSyncCommands
{
    public static bool SaveScheduleSilent(DateOnly scheduleDate)
    {
        try
        {
            var schedule = SheetMapper.ExtractScheduleRecord(scheduleDate);
            if (schedule == null)
            {
                return false;
            }

            string snapshotPath = OfflineSaveServices.SaveAsync(schedule, writeToDatabase: true)
                                          .GetAwaiter()
                                          .GetResult();

            return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(snapshotPath);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[ScheduleManager.ServerSyncCommands] Silent Save Error: {ex.Message}");
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static bool LoadScheduleSilent(DateOnly scheduleDate)
    {
        try
        {
            var schedule = OfflineSaveServices.Load<ScheduleRecord>();
            if (schedule != null && schedule.DailyScheduleDate == scheduleDate)
            {
                SheetMapper.PopulateScheduleSheets(schedule);
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[ScheduleManager.ServerSyncCommands] Silent Load Error: {ex.Message}");
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static async Task<(bool Success, string Path)> SaveScheduleAsync(ScheduleRecord schedule)
    {
        if (schedule == null) return (false, string.Empty);

        string path = await OfflineSaveServices.SaveAsync(schedule, writeToDatabase: true);
        return (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path), path);
    }

    public static ScheduleRecord? FetchSchedule(DateOnly scheduleDate)
    {
        var record = OfflineSaveServices.Load<ScheduleRecord>();
        return record?.DailyScheduleDate == scheduleDate ? record : null;
    }
}
`n
============================================================
FILE: ScheduleManager\ScheduleManager.SheetMapper.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.ScheduleManager;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookHelper;

public static class SheetMapper
{
    // Sheet Aliases
    private static readonly string[] ScheduleAliases = { "Daily Schedule", "Daily_Schedule", "Schedule" };
    private static readonly string[] AttendanceAliases = { "Daily Attendance", "Daily_Attendance", "Attendance" };
    private static readonly string[] PreferenceAliases = { "Preference", "Staff Preference", "Preferences" };

    public static ScheduleRecord? ExtractScheduleRecord(DateOnly scheduleDate)
    {
        var record = new ScheduleRecord { DailyScheduleDate = scheduleDate };
        var validationErrors = new List<string>();

        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // 1. INGEST PROJECTS (Sheet: "Daily Schedule")
        // Columns: Project ID (A) | Shift (B) | Staffs CSV (C) | Vehicles CSV (D) | Remarks (E)
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        string schedSheet = WorkbookSession.ResolveSheetName(ScheduleAliases, "Daily Schedule");
        IntPtr schedId = WorkbookSession.GetSheetId(schedSheet);
        var schedRef = schedId != IntPtr.Zero
            ? new ExcelReference(1, 200, 0, 4, schedId)
            : new ExcelReference(1, 200, 0, 4);

        if (schedRef.GetValue() is object[,] schedGrid)
        {
            int rows = schedGrid.GetLength(0);
            for (int r = 0; r < rows; r++)
            {
                string projId = CleanCellString(schedGrid[r, 0]);
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(projId)) continue;

                string shiftStr = CleanCellString(schedGrid[r, 1]);
                string staffsCsv = CleanCellString(schedGrid[r, 2]);
                string vehiclesCsv = CleanCellString(schedGrid[r, 3]);
                string remarks = CleanCellString(schedGrid[r, 4]);

                var staffList = staffsCsv.Split(',', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries | StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries).ToList();
                var vehicleList = vehiclesCsv.Split(',', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries | StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries).ToList();

                if (staffList.Count == 0)
                {
                    validationErrors.Add($"• Row {r + 2} [Daily Schedule]: Project '{projId}' has no assigned staffs.");
                }

                record.Projects.Add(new DailyProjectSchedule
                {
                    ProjectID = projId,
                    Shift = Enum.TryParse(shiftStr, true, out Shifts s) ? s : Shifts.Morning,
                    Staffs = staffList,
                    Vehicles = vehicleList,
                    Remarks = remarks
                });
            }
        }

        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // 2. INGEST ATTENDANCE (Sheet: "Daily Attendance")
        // Col A: Staff ID | B: Name (Skip) | C: Skill (Skip) | D: Leave (Skip) | E: ClockIn | F: ClockOut | G: Biometric | H: Override | I: Reason | J: AttendanceState | K: IsAllocated
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        string attSheet = WorkbookSession.ResolveSheetName(AttendanceAliases, "Daily Attendance");
        IntPtr attId = WorkbookSession.GetSheetId(attSheet);
        var attRef = attId != IntPtr.Zero
            ? new ExcelReference(1, 500, 0, 10, attId)
            : new ExcelReference(1, 500, 0, 10);

        if (attRef.GetValue() is object[,] attGrid)
        {
            int rows = attGrid.GetLength(0);
            for (int r = 0; r < rows; r++)
            {
                object rawStaffId = attGrid[r, 0];
                if (rawStaffId == null || rawStaffId is ExcelEmpty || !long.TryParse(rawStaffId.ToString(), out long staffId) || staffId <= 0)
                    continue;

                TimeOnly clockIn = ParseTime(attGrid[r, 4]);
                TimeOnly clockOut = ParseTime(attGrid[r, 5]);
                string bioStr = CleanCellString(attGrid[r, 6]);
                string overrideStr = CleanCellString(attGrid[r, 7]);
                string reason = CleanCellString(attGrid[r, 8]);
                string attStateStr = CleanCellString(attGrid[r, 9]);
                bool.TryParse(CleanCellString(attGrid[r, 10]), out bool isAllocated);

                AttendanceStatus manualOverride = Enum.TryParse(overrideStr, true, out AttendanceStatus mo) ? mo : AttendanceStatus.Absence;
                if (manualOverride != AttendanceStatus.Absence && string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(reason))
                {
                    validationErrors.Add($"• Row {r + 2} [Daily Attendance]: Staff ID {staffId} has a manual override without an override reason.");
                }

                record.StaffAttendance.Add(new DailyStaffAttendance
                {
                    DailyScheduleDate = scheduleDate,
                    StaffID = staffId,
                    ClockInTime = clockIn,
                    ClockOutTime = clockOut,
                    BiometricStatus = Enum.TryParse(bioStr, true, out BiometricStatus b) ? b : BiometricStatus.None,
                    ManualOverride = manualOverride,
                    OverrideReason = reason,
                    AttendanceStatus = Enum.TryParse(attStateStr, true, out AttendanceStatus ast) ? ast : AttendanceStatus.Absence,
                    IsAllocated = isAllocated
                });
            }
        }

        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        // PRE-SAVE VALIDATION GATEWAY: Halt WorkbookHelper if data is dirty
        // -------------------------------------------------------------
        if (validationErrors.Count > 0)
        {
            var sb = new StringBuilder();
            sb.AppendLine("Cannot save schedule to system due to validation errors:\n");
            foreach (var err in validationErrors) sb.AppendLine(err);
            sb.AppendLine("\nPlease correct the entries and try saving again.");

            MessageBox.Show(sb.ToString(), "Pre-Save Validation Failed", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
            return null;
        }

        return record;
    }

    public static void PopulateScheduleSheets(ScheduleRecord? record)
    {
        if (record == null) return;

        object initialActiveSheet = XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlfGetDocument, 1);

        try
        {
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcEcho, false);

            // 1. Populate Daily Schedule Tab
            string schedSheet = WorkbookSession.ResolveSheetName(ScheduleAliases, "Daily Schedule");
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcWorkbookSelect, new object[] { schedSheet });
            WorkbookSession.ClearActiveSheetDataRows(1, 200, 0, 4);

            if (record.Projects.Count > 0)
            {
                object[,] schedOut = new object[record.Projects.Count, 5];
                for (int i = 0; i < record.Projects.Count; i++)
                {
                    var p = record.Projects[i];
                    schedOut[i, 0] = p.ProjectID;
                    schedOut[i, 1] = p.Shift.ToString();
                    schedOut[i, 2] = string.Join(", ", p.Staffs);
                    schedOut[i, 3] = string.Join(", ", p.Vehicles);
                    schedOut[i, 4] = p.Remarks;
                }
                new ExcelReference(1, record.Projects.Count, 0, 4).SetValue(schedOut);
            }

            // 2. Populate Attendance Tab (Formula columns index 1, 2, 3 left blank to let Excel formulas evaluate)
            string attSheet = WorkbookSession.ResolveSheetName(AttendanceAliases, "Daily Attendance");
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcWorkbookSelect, new object[] { attSheet });
            WorkbookSession.ClearActiveSheetDataRows(1, 500, 0, 10);

            if (record.StaffAttendance.Count > 0)
            {
                object[,] attOut = new object[record.StaffAttendance.Count, 11];
                for (int i = 0; i < record.StaffAttendance.Count; i++)
                {
                    var a = record.StaffAttendance[i];
                    attOut[i, 0] = a.StaffID;
                    // attOut[i, 1] -> Name (Formula Lookup)
                    // attOut[i, 2] -> Primary Skill (Formula Lookup)
                    // attOut[i, 3] -> Leave Status (Formula Lookup)
                    attOut[i, 4] = a.ClockInTime == TimeOnly.MinValue ? string.Empty : a.ClockInTime.ToString("HH:mm");
                    attOut[i, 5] = a.ClockOutTime == TimeOnly.MaxValue ? string.Empty : a.ClockOutTime.ToString("HH:mm");
                    attOut[i, 6] = a.BiometricStatus.ToString();
                    attOut[i, 7] = a.ManualOverride.ToString();
                    attOut[i, 8] = a.OverrideReason;
                    attOut[i, 9] = a.AttendanceStatus.ToString();
                    attOut[i, 10] = a.IsAllocated;
                }
                new ExcelReference(1, record.StaffAttendance.Count, 0, 10).SetValue(attOut);
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            if (initialActiveSheet != null)
            {
                string sheetStr = initialActiveSheet.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                int bIdx = sheetStr.LastIndexOf(']');
                string restoreName = bIdx >= 0 ? sheetStr.Substring(bIdx + 1) : sheetStr;

                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(restoreName))
                {
                    try { XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcWorkbookSelect, new object[] { restoreName }); } catch { }
                }
            }
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcEcho, true);
        }
    }

    private static string CleanCellString(object? cell) =>
        (cell == null || cell is ExcelEmpty || cell is ExcelMissing || cell is ExcelError)
            ? string.Empty
            : cell.ToString()?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;

    private static TimeOnly ParseTime(object cell)
    {
        string text = CleanCellString(cell);
        return TimeOnly.TryParse(text, out TimeOnly t) ? t : TimeOnly.MinValue;
    }
}

FILE: StaffManager\StaffManager.Events.cs

namespace SKSB_App.StaffManager;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DateFilter;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Entry;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sync;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Entry;

public class Events : IExcelAddIn
{
    private static dynamic? _xlAppRoot;
    private static bool _isExiting = false;
    private static bool _isInternalSaving = false;

    private static readonly EventsAutoOpen _coreEvents = new();

    private delegate void WorkbookBeforeCloseDelegate(dynamic wb, ref bool cancel);
    private delegate void WorkbookBeforeSaveDelegate(dynamic wb, bool saveAsUi, ref bool cancel);
    private static WorkbookBeforeCloseDelegate? _beforeCloseHandler;
    private static WorkbookBeforeSaveDelegate? _beforeSaveHandler;

    public static readonly SaveContract<List<StaffRecord>> StaffSyncDescriptor = new()
    {
        ModuleName = "Staff Manager",
        ServerTableName = "staff_master",
        ExtractAndValidate = () => SheetMapper.ExtractStaffRecordsWithDiagnostics(),
        IsDatasetEmpty = list => list == null || list.Count == 0,
        SaveToServer = list => (ServerSyncCommands.SaveStaffSilent(list, out string err), err),
        PopulateWorksheet = list => SheetMapper.PopulateWorksheet(list),
        FetchFromServer = () => ServerSyncCommands.FetchStaffRecords(),
        SyncLookups = isInteractive => SyncStaffLookupsData(isInteractive),
        ValidateOfflinePayload = list => list != null && list.Count > 0 && list.TrueForAll(s => s.StaffID > 0),
        FormatConflictSummary = (local, server) =>
        {
            int localVLic = local.Sum(s => s.VehicleLicenses?.Count ?? 0);
            int localSLic = local.Sum(s => s.SkillLicenses?.Count ?? 0);
            int serverVLic = server.Sum(s => s.VehicleLicenses?.Count ?? 0);
            int serverSLic = server.Sum(s => s.SkillLicenses?.Count ?? 0);

            return $"  Local Offline : {local.Count} staff(s), {localVLic} vehicle license(s), {localSLic} skill license(s)\n" +
                   $"  Server Copy   : {server.Count} staff(s), {serverVLic} vehicle license(s), {serverSLic} skill license(s)";
        }
    };

    public void AutoOpen()
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            _coreEvents.AutoOpen();

            HookApplicationEvents();
            Arrow.EnsureExcelEventsHooked();

            double nowSerial = Convert.ToDouble(XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlfNow));
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnTime, nowSerial + (1.0 / 86400.0), "DelayedAutoLoadMacro");

            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^s", "OnHotKeySave");
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^+s", "OnHotKeySave");
        });
    }

    public void AutoClose()
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            _coreEvents.AutoClose();
            Arrow.Teardown();
            UnhookApplicationEvents();
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^s");
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^+s");
        });
    }

    public static void SyncStaffLookupsData(bool isInteractive = false)
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                // 1. Sync Staff Manager Enums to 'Lookups' sheet
                EnumList.Sync(
                    typeof(Department),
                    typeof(ContractStatus),
                    typeof(VehicleLicenseClass),
                    typeof(VehicleLicenseStatus),
                    typeof(SkillLicenseStatus),
                    typeof(DateFilterScope)
                );

                // 2. Sync external Vehicle Plates to 'Data' sheet
                DataList.SyncProperty<VehicleRecord, string>(
                    v => v.VehiclePlate,
                    customListName: "VehiclePlateList"
                );

                // 3. Populate and sync SkillLicenseTypesTable & SkillLicenseTypeList
                SheetMapper.PopulateSkillLicenseTypesTable(showDialogIfFailed: isInteractive);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[SyncStaffLookupsData Warning] {ex.Message}");
            }
        });
    }

    private static void HookApplicationEvents()
    {
        try
        {
            if (_xlAppRoot != null) return;
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            _xlAppRoot = xlApp;

            dynamic ourWorkbook = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;

            _beforeCloseHandler = new WorkbookBeforeCloseDelegate((dynamic wb, ref bool cancel) =>
            {
                try
                {
                    if (ourWorkbook != null && wb != null && wb != ourWorkbook)
                    {
                        wb.Saved = true;
                        cancel = false;
                        return;
                    }
                }
                catch { }

                SaveEntry.InterceptClose(
                    (object)wb,
                    ref cancel,
                    StaffSyncDescriptor,
                    ref _isExiting,
                    new Action(UnhookApplicationEvents)
                );
            });
            _xlAppRoot.WorkbookBeforeClose += _beforeCloseHandler;

            _beforeSaveHandler = new WorkbookBeforeSaveDelegate((dynamic wb, bool saveAsUi, ref bool cancel) =>
            {
                try
                {
                    if (ourWorkbook != null && wb != null && wb != ourWorkbook) return;
                }
                catch { }

                if (_isInternalSaving || SheetOperations.IsMasterTemplateFile() || SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode) return;

                cancel = true;
                ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() => SaveEntry.Save(StaffSyncDescriptor));
            });
            _xlAppRoot.WorkbookBeforeSave += _beforeSaveHandler;
        }
        catch { }
    }

    private static void UnhookApplicationEvents()
    {
        try
        {
            if (_xlAppRoot != null)
            {
                if (_beforeCloseHandler != null) _xlAppRoot.WorkbookBeforeClose -= _beforeCloseHandler;
                if (_beforeSaveHandler != null) _xlAppRoot.WorkbookBeforeSave -= _beforeSaveHandler;
                _beforeCloseHandler = null;
                _beforeSaveHandler = null;
                _xlAppRoot = null;
            }
        }
        catch { }
    }

    [ExcelCommand(Name = "OnHotKeySave")]
    public static void OnHotKeySave()
    {
        if (SheetOperations.IsMasterTemplateFile() || SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode)
        {
            _isInternalSaving = true;
            try { XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcSave); } finally { _isInternalSaving = false; }
            return;
        }

        SaveEntry.Save(StaffSyncDescriptor);
    }

    [ExcelCommand(Name = "DelayedAutoLoadMacro")]
    public static void DelayedAutoLoadMacro()
    {
        dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
        try
        {
            if (xlApp.ActiveWorkbook != null && !SheetOperations.IsMasterTemplateFile())
            {
                xlApp.ActiveWindow.Caption = "Staff_Manager";
            }
        }
        catch { }

        OfflineSave.HandleStartup(StaffSyncDescriptor);

        dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
        if (wb != null && !SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode)
        {
            foreach (dynamic sheet in wb.Worksheets)
            {
                string sName = sheet.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
                if (sName.Equals(SheetOperations.LookupsSheetName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                    sName.Equals(SheetOperations.DataSheetName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                {
                    continue;
                }
                SheetProtections.ApplyStyleDrivenProtection(sheet);
            }

            SheetOperations.SetAuxiliarySheetsVisibility(visible: false);
        }
    }
}

using ExcelDna.Integration;

public static class Guard
{
    public static bool IsStaffWorkbook(dynamic? wb = null)
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic targetWb = wb ?? xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (targetWb == null) return false;

            // Check for signature sheet and signature ListObject table
            dynamic ws = targetWb.Sheets["All Staffs"];
            if (ws != null && ws.ListObjects["AllStaffsTable"] != null)
            {
                return true;
            }
        }
        catch { }
        return false;
    }
}

FILE: StaffManager\StaffManager.Importer.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.StaffManager;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Erp;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.RuleEngine;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types.Attributes;

public static class StaffImportConfig
{
    public const string DefaultDepartment = "Worker";

    private static readonly Dictionary<string, string> DepartmentMappings = new(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
    {
        { "PROJECT", "Worker" },
        { "OFFICE", "Office" },
        { "ADMIN", "Office" },
        { "MANAGEMENT", "Office" }
    };

    public static string MapDepartment(string? rawDepartment)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rawDepartment))
            return DefaultDepartment;

        string clean = rawDepartment.Trim();
        if (DepartmentMappings.TryGetValue(clean, out string? targetDept) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(targetDept))
        {
            return targetDept;
        }

        return clean;
    }
}

public class StaffImportResult
{
    public List<StaffRecord> ImportedStaffs { get; set; } = new();
    public List<ImportSkippedRecord> SkippedErrors { get; set; } = new();
    public int TotalRowsProcessed { get; set; } = 0;

    public ImportSummaryReport ToGenericReport() => new()
    {
        SourceSystemName = "SQL Payroll",
        EntityLabel = "staff(s)",
        SuccessfullyImportedCount = ImportedStaffs.Count,
        FilteredExclusionCount = 0,
        FilterRuleDescription = "Mapped departments: PROJECT -> Worker, OFFICE/ADMIN -> Office.",
        SkippedErrors = SkippedErrors
    };
}

public static class Importer
{
    public static StaffImportResult ImportStaffs(string filePath)
    {
        var doc = ErpImporter.LoadFromFile(filePath);
        var result = new StaffImportResult();

        for (int r = 0; r < doc.Rows.Count; r++)
        {
            var row = doc.Rows[r];
            int excelRowNum = r + 2;
            result.TotalRowsProcessed++;

            string rawCode = row.GetString("Code");
            string name = row.GetString("Name")?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
            string rawDept = row.GetString("Department");

            string mappedDept = StaffImportConfig.MapDepartment(rawDept);

            // 1. Validate Staff ID / Code
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rawCode))
            {
                result.SkippedErrors.Add(new ImportSkippedRecord
                {
                    ExcelRowNumber = excelRowNum,
                    Identifier = "[Empty]",
                    NameOrTitle = name,
                    Reason = "Missing Staff Code / ID."
                });
                continue;
            }

            long staffId = row.GetLong("Code");
            if (staffId <= 0 && (!long.TryParse(rawCode, out staffId) || staffId <= 0))
            {
                result.SkippedErrors.Add(new ImportSkippedRecord
                {
                    ExcelRowNumber = excelRowNum,
                    Identifier = rawCode,
                    NameOrTitle = name,
                    Reason = $"Invalid non-numeric Staff Code '{rawCode}'."
                });
                continue;
            }

            // 2. Validate Name
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(name))
            {
                result.SkippedErrors.Add(new ImportSkippedRecord
                {
                    ExcelRowNumber = excelRowNum,
                    Identifier = rawCode,
                    NameOrTitle = "[Empty]",
                    Reason = $"Staff ID {staffId} has no name specified."
                });
                continue;
            }

            string shortName = RuleEvaluator.ApplyTextTransform(name, TransformKind.ShortNameFirstLast);

            // 3. Validate Employment Dates
            DateOnly? rawJoinDate = row.GetDateOnly("Join Date") ?? row.GetDateOnly("JoinDate");
            DateOnly? rawResignDate = row.GetDateOnly("Resign") ?? row.GetDateOnly("Resign Date") ?? row.GetDateOnly("ResignDate");

            if (!rawJoinDate.HasValue && rawResignDate.HasValue)
            {
                result.SkippedErrors.Add(new ImportSkippedRecord
                {
                    ExcelRowNumber = excelRowNum,
                    Identifier = rawCode,
                    NameOrTitle = name,
                    Reason = $"Staff is marked resigned ({rawResignDate.Value:dd/MM/yyyy}) but has no Join Date recorded. Skipped from active operations."
                });
                continue;
            }

            DateOnly joinDate = rawJoinDate ?? DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today);

            // 4. Contact & Identity
            long nric = 0;
            string rawIc = row.GetString("New IC");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rawIc)) rawIc = row.GetString("NewIC");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rawIc)) rawIc = row.GetString("ICNo");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rawIc)) rawIc = row.GetString("IC");
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rawIc))
            {
                string cleanIc = Regex.Replace(rawIc, @"[^\d]", string.Empty);
                long.TryParse(cleanIc, out nric);
            }

            string passport = row.GetString("Passport");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(passport)) passport = row.GetString("PassportNo");

            string phone = row.GetString("Mobile");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(phone)) phone = row.GetString("Handphone");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(phone)) phone = row.GetString("Phone");

            ContractStatus initialStatus = (rawResignDate.HasValue && rawResignDate.Value <= DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today))
                ? ContractStatus.Resigned
                : ContractStatus.Active;

            var staff = new StaffRecord
            {
                StaffID = staffId,
                StaffDetails = new StaffDetails
                {
                    StaffID = staffId,
                    StaffName = name,
                    StaffShortName = shortName,
                    Department = mappedDept,
                    NRIC = nric,
                    Passport = passport,
                    Phone = phone,
                    JoinDate = joinDate,
                    ResignDate = rawResignDate,
                    ContractStatus = initialStatus
                },
                VehicleLicenses = new List<VehicleLicense>(),
                SkillLicenses = new List<SkillLicense>()
            };

            result.ImportedStaffs.Add(staff);
        }

        return result;
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.StaffManager;

using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using ExcelDna.Integration.CustomUI;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Database;
using SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Erp;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.TableRowManager;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Entry;

[ComVisible(true)]
public class RibbonController : ExcelRibbon
{
    private static IRibbonUI? _ribbonUi;

    public override string GetCustomUI(string ribbonId)
    {
#if DEBUG
        string debugGroupXml = @"
                <group id='grpDebugControls' label='Debug Controls'>
                  <toggleButton id='btnToggleOffline' 
                                label='Simulate Server Offline' 
                                size='large' 
                                imageMso='AccessSetOfflineStatus' 
                                getPressed='GetSimulateOfflinePressed'
                                onAction='OnToggleOfflineClicked' />
                  <toggleButton id='btnToggleMasterDebug'
                                label='Debug Logs Active'
                                size='large'
                                imageMso='TraceDependents'
                                getPressed='GetMasterDebugPressed'
                                onAction='OnToggleMasterDebugClicked' />
                </group>";
#else
        string debugGroupXml = string.Empty;
#endif

        return $@"
        <customUI xmlns='http://schemas.microsoft.com/office/2009/07/customui' onLoad='OnRibbonLoad'>
          <ribbon>
            <tabs>
              <tab id='tabStaffManager' label='Staff Manager'>
                <group id='grpStaffPersistence' label='Staff Database Sync'>
                  <button id='btnLoadStaff' 
                          label='Load from System' 
                          size='large' 
                          imageMso='Refresh' 
                          screentip='Load Staff Records'
                          supertip='Fetches all staff master records and licenses from PostgreSQL or offline storage.'
                          onAction='OnLoadClicked' />
                  <button id='btnSaveStaff' 
                          label='Save to System' 
                          size='large' 
                          imageMso='FileSave' 
                          screentip='Save Staff Records'
                          supertip='Validates and commits employee profiles and licenses.'
                          onAction='OnSaveClicked' />
                </group>

                <group id='grpErpIntegration' label='SQL Payroll Integration'>
                  <button id='btnImportStaffs' 
                          label='Import from&#10;SQL Payroll' 
                          size='large' 
                          imageMso='ImportExcel' 
                          screentip='Import from SQL Payroll'
                          supertip='Imports employee records from an exported SQL Payroll Excel or CSV file while retaining existing licenses.'
                          onAction='OnImportStaffsClicked' />
                </group>

                <!-- ROW MANAGEMENT GROUP -->
<group id='grpRowDirection' label='Row Management'>
  <toggleButton id='btnToggleInsertTop' 
                label='Insert at Top' 
                size='large' 
                imageMso='TableInsertRowsAbove' 
                screentip='Toggle Top/Bottom Auto-Insert'
                supertip='When enabled, typing in the first data row creates a new row at the top. When disabled, rows are appended at the bottom.'
                getPressed='GetInsertAtTopPressed'
                onAction='OnToggleInsertAtTopClicked' />
  <button id='btnCleanEmptyRows'
          label='Trim Empty Rows'
          size='large'
          imageMso='TableDeleteRows'
          screentip='Trim Redundant Empty Rows'
          supertip='Scans all tables on the active sheet and trims extra blank rows, leaving exactly one empty entry row.'
          onAction='OnCleanEmptyRowsClicked' />
  <button id='btnCleanDuplicateRows'
          label='Remove Duplicates'
          size='large'
          imageMso='RemoveDuplicates'
          screentip='Remove Duplicate Rows'
          supertip='Scans all tables on the active sheet and removes identical duplicate rows, keeping the first occurrence.'
          onAction='OnCleanDuplicateRowsClicked' />
  <button id='btnDeleteTableRow'
          label='Delete Row'
          size='large'
          imageMso='TableRowsDelete'
          screentip='Delete Row'
          supertip='Deletes selected row(s) in absolute master tables. Deletion is blocked on additive transaction tables.'
          onAction='OnDeleteTableRowClicked' />
<toggleButton id='btnToggleTotalRow' 
              label='Total Row' 
              size='large' 
              imageMso='AutoSum' 
              screentip='Toggle Total Row'
              supertip='Shows or hides the summary total row at the bottom of the active table.'
              getPressed='GetTotalRowPressed' 
              onAction='OnToggleTotalRowClicked' />
</group>

                {debugGroupXml}
              </tab>
            </tabs>
          </ribbon>
        </customUI>";
    }

    public void OnRibbonLoad(IRibbonUI ribbon) => _ribbonUi = ribbon;

    public void OnLoadClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        try
        {
            Events.SyncStaffLookupsData(isInteractive: false);

            var records = ServerSyncCommands.FetchStaffRecords();
            if (records == null || records.Count == 0)
            {
                MessageBox.Show("No staff records found in database or local snapshot.",
                    "Load Staffs", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
                return;
            }

            ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
            {
                SheetMapper.PopulateWorksheet(records);
            });
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show($"Failed loading staff records:\n{ex.Message}", "Load Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
        }
    }

    public void OnSaveClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            SaveEntry.Save(Events.StaffSyncDescriptor);
        });
    }

    public void OnImportStaffsClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        try
        {
            using var openFileDialog = new OpenFileDialog
            {
                Title = "Select SQL Payroll Export File (Employee.xlsx)",
                Filter = "Excel Files (*.xlsx;*.xls)|*.xlsx;*.xls|CSV Files (*.csv)|*.csv|All Files (*.*)|*.*",
                FilterIndex = 1,
                RestoreDirectory = true,
                CheckFileExists = true,
                Multiselect = false
            };

            if (openFileDialog.ShowDialog() != DialogResult.OK)
                return;

            string selectedFilePath = openFileDialog.FileName;
            string fileNameOnly = Path.GetFileName(selectedFilePath);

            var importResult = Importer.ImportStaffs(selectedFilePath);

            if (importResult.ImportedStaffs.Count == 0 && importResult.SkippedErrors.Count == 0)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    "No operational staff records were found in the selected file.\n\n" +
                    "Ensure the file contains the Code, Name, Handphone/Mobile, and Department columns.",
                    "Import Results",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                    MessageBoxIcon.Warning
                );
                return;
            }

            ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
            {
                var (existingStaffs, _, _) = SheetMapper.ExtractStaffRecordsWithDiagnostics();

                var cleanStaffs = TableRowManager.FilterDuplicates(
                    incomingRecords: importResult.ImportedStaffs,
                    keySelector: s => $"{s.StaffID}",
                    displaySelector: s => $"Staff ID {s.StaffID} ({s.StaffDetails.StaffName})",
                    entityLabel: "Staff Profile",
                    duplicateWarnings: out var duplicateNotices,
                    existingRecords: existingStaffs
                );

                if (cleanStaffs == null)
                {
                    SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation("Staff import cancelled by user.");
                    return;
                }

                if (cleanStaffs.Count > 0)
                {
                    var existingLookup = (existingStaffs ?? new List<StaffRecord>())
                        .Where(w => w.StaffID > 0)
                        .GroupBy(w => w.StaffID)
                        .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.First());

                    foreach (var imported in cleanStaffs)
                    {
                        if (existingLookup.TryGetValue(imported.StaffID, out var current))
                        {
                            imported.VehicleLicenses = current.VehicleLicenses;
                            imported.SkillLicenses = current.SkillLicenses;
                        }
                    }

                    SheetMapper.PopulateWorksheet(cleanStaffs);
                }

                using var summaryDialog = new ImportResultDialog(importResult.ToGenericReport(), fileNameOnly);
                summaryDialog.ShowDialog();
            });
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show($"Import failed: {ex.Message}", "Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
        }
    }

    public bool GetInsertAtTopPressed(IRibbonControl control)
        => TableRowManager.IsInsertAtTopEnabled;

    public void OnToggleInsertAtTopClicked(IRibbonControl control, bool isPressed)
    {
        TableRowManager.IsInsertAtTopEnabled = isPressed;
        _ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl(control.Id);

        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
                dynamic ws = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook?.ActiveSheet;
                if (ws != null)
                {
                    TableRowManager.CleanActiveSheetTables(ws);
                }
            }
            catch { }

            SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation(
                isPressed
                    ? "Insert at TOP active: Spawns at top, redundant bottom rows trimmed."
                    : "Insert at BOTTOM active: Spawns at bottom, redundant top rows trimmed."
            );
        });
    }

    public void OnCleanEmptyRowsClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
                dynamic ws = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook?.ActiveSheet;
                if (ws == null) return;

                int tablesCleaned = TableRowManager.CleanActiveSheetTables(ws);
                SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation(
                    $"Cleaned empty rows across {tablesCleaned} table(s). Exactly 1 empty row retained."
                );
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"Failed cleaning empty rows:\n{ex.Message}", "Row Cleanup Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
        });
    }

    public void OnCleanDuplicateRowsClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
                dynamic ws = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook?.ActiveSheet;
                if (ws == null) return;

                bool origUpdating = true;
                try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }

                int totalDuplicatesRemoved = 0;
                var warnings = new System.Collections.Generic.List<string>();

                try
                {
                    SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)ws, new Action(() =>
                    {
                        foreach (dynamic table in ws.ListObjects)
                        {
                            totalDuplicatesRemoved += TableRowManager.CleanTableDuplicateRows(table, warnings);
                        }
                    }));
                }
                finally
                {
                    try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
                }

                if (totalDuplicatesRemoved > 0)
                {
                    SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation($"Removed {totalDuplicatesRemoved} duplicate row(s).");
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        $"Successfully removed {totalDuplicatesRemoved} duplicate row(s) from the active sheet.\n\n" +
                        string.Join("\n", warnings),
                        "Duplicates Removed",
                        MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Information
                    );
                }
                else
                {
                    SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation("No duplicate rows found on the active sheet.");
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        "No duplicate rows found in any tables on the active sheet.",
                        "Clean Duplicates",
                        MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Information
                    );
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"Failed to remove duplicate rows:\n{ex.Message}", "Cleanup Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
        });
    }

    public void OnDeleteTableRowClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(TableRowManager.DeleteSelectedRowCommand);
    }

    public bool GetTotalRowPressed(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic table = xlApp.ActiveCell?.ListObject;
            if (table != null)
            {
                return (bool)table.ShowTotals;
            }
        }
        catch { }
        return false;
    }

    public void OnToggleTotalRowClicked(IRibbonControl control, bool isPressed)
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
                dynamic ws = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook?.ActiveSheet;
                if (ws == null) return;

                dynamic table = xlApp.ActiveCell?.ListObject;
                if (table == null && ws.ListObjects.Count > 0)
                {
                    table = ws.ListObjects[1]; // Fallback to first table on sheet
                }
                if (table == null) return;

                SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)ws, new Action(() =>
                {
                    table.ShowTotals = isPressed;

                    if (isPressed)
                    {
                        // 1. MUST unlock total row so native header sorting isn't blocked
                        table.TotalsRowRange.Locked = false;

                        // 2. Configure default calculation for Cost columns
                        int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
                        for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                        {
                            dynamic col = table.ListColumns[c];
                            string header = col.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;

                            if (header.IndexOf("Cost", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0 ||
                                header.IndexOf("Amount", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                            {
                                col.TotalsCalculation = 1; // xlTotalsCalculationSum
                                try { col.Total.NumberFormat = "#,##0.00"; } catch { }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }));

                _ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl(control.Id);
                SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation(isPressed ? "Total row enabled." : "Total row hidden.");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                AppLogger.Error("Failed toggling Total Row", ex);
            }
        });
    }

#if DEBUG
    public bool GetSimulateOfflinePressed(IRibbonControl control)
        => ServerEnvironment.SimulateOffline;

    public void OnToggleOfflineClicked(IRibbonControl control, bool pressed)
    {
        ServerEnvironment.SimulateOffline = pressed;
        _ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl(control.Id);
        MessageBox.Show(
            pressed
                ? "Server is now SIMULATED OFFLINE.\nAll PostgreSQL database operations will fail immediately to test offline local stashing."
                : "Server is now ONLINE.\nNormal PostgreSQL database connectivity restored.",
            "Debug Server Mode",
            MessageBoxButtons.OK,
            pressed ? MessageBoxIcon.Warning : MessageBoxIcon.Information
        );
    }

    public bool GetMasterDebugPressed(IRibbonControl control)
        => DebugDialog.DebugEnabled;

    public void OnToggleMasterDebugClicked(IRibbonControl control, bool pressed)
    {
        DebugDialog.DebugEnabled = pressed;
        _ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl(control.Id);
        MessageBox.Show(
            pressed
                ? "Debug diagnostics are now ENABLED across all operations."
                : "Debug diagnostics are now MUTED.",
            "Debug Log Switch",
            MessageBoxButtons.OK,
            pressed ? MessageBoxIcon.Information : MessageBoxIcon.Warning
        );
    }
#endif
}

============================================================

namespace SKSB_App.StaffManager;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Database;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.RuleEngine;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types.Attributes;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Storage;

public static class ServerSyncCommands
{
    public static bool SaveStaffSilent(List<StaffRecord> staffList, out string errorMessage)
    {
        errorMessage = string.Empty;
        try
        {
            // 1. Persist auxiliary SkillLicenseTypesTable
            var skillTypes = SheetMapper.ExtractSkillLicenseTypes();
            if (skillTypes.Count > 0)
            {
                OfflineSnapshot.Save(skillTypes, writeToDatabase: true);
            }

            if (staffList == null || staffList.Count == 0)
            {
                return true;
            }

            // 2. Persist validated staff records to JSON cache & PostgreSQL
            string snapshotPath = OfflineSnapshot.Save(staffList, writeToDatabase: true);
            return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(snapshotPath);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            errorMessage = ex.Message;
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[ServerSyncCommands] Silent Save Error: {ex.Message}");
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static bool SaveStaffSilent(out string errorMessage)
    {
        errorMessage = string.Empty;
        var (staffList, errors, _) = SheetMapper.ExtractStaffRecordsWithDiagnostics();
        if (errors.Count > 0)
        {
            errorMessage = $"{errors.Count} critical validation error(s) must be resolved.";
            return false;
        }
        return SaveStaffSilent(staffList ?? new List<StaffRecord>(), out errorMessage);
    }

    public static bool LoadStaffSilent()
    {
        try
        {
            var staffList = FetchStaffRecords();
            if (staffList != null && staffList.Count > 0)
            {
                SheetMapper.PopulateWorksheet(staffList);
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[ServerSyncCommands] Silent Load Error: {ex.Message}");
            return false;
        }
    }

    public static async Task<(bool Success, int Count, string Path)> SaveStaffAsync(List<StaffRecord> staffList)
    {
        if (staffList == null || staffList.Count == 0) return (false, 0, string.Empty);

        var skillTypes = SheetMapper.ExtractSkillLicenseTypes();
        if (skillTypes.Count > 0)
        {
            await OfflineSnapshot.SaveAsync(skillTypes, writeToDatabase: true);
        }

        string path = await OfflineSnapshot.SaveAsync(staffList, writeToDatabase: true);
        return (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path), staffList.Count, path);
    }

    public static List<SkillLicenseType> FetchSkillLicenseTypesFromDb()
    {
        try
        {
            if (ServerEnvironment.SimulateOffline)
            {
                return OfflineSnapshot.Load<List<SkillLicenseType>>() ?? new List<SkillLicenseType>();
            }

            var db = new PostgreSqlService();
            const string colSql = @"
                SELECT column_name 
                FROM information_schema.columns 
                WHERE table_name = 'lookup_skill_license_types';";
            var existingCols = db.Query<string>(colSql).ToList();

            string nameCol = existingCols.Any(c => c.Equals("skill_license_type", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                ? "skill_license_type"
                : "license_type";

            string idCol = existingCols.Any(c => c.Equals("skill_license_type_id", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                ? "skill_license_type_id"
                : "type_id";

            string querySql = $@"
                SELECT 
                    {idCol}   AS SkillLicenseTypeID, 
                    {nameCol} AS SkillLicenseName, 
                    COALESCE(description, '') AS Description 
                FROM lookup_skill_license_types 
                ORDER BY {nameCol};";

            var result = db.Query<SkillLicenseType>(querySql)?.ToList() ?? new List<SkillLicenseType>();
            if (result.Count > 0)
            {
                OfflineSnapshot.Save(result, writeToDatabase: false);
            }
            return result;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[FetchSkillLicenseTypesFromDb Error] {ex.Message}");
            return OfflineSnapshot.Load<List<SkillLicenseType>>() ?? new List<SkillLicenseType>();
        }
    }

    public static List<StaffRecord>? FetchStaffRecords()
    {
        using var _ = AppLogger.Scope("StaffManager.FetchStaffRecords");
        try
        {
            if (!ServerEnvironment.SimulateOffline)
            {
                // 1. Pre-fetch and cache SkillLicenseTypes locally
                FetchSkillLicenseTypesFromDb();

                var db = new PostgreSqlService();
                const string sqlStaffs = "SELECT * FROM staff_master ORDER BY staff_id;";
                const string sqlVehicleLicenses = "SELECT * FROM staff_vehicle_licenses ORDER BY vehicle_license_id;";
                const string sqlSkillLicenses = "SELECT * FROM staff_skill_licenses ORDER BY skill_license_id;";

                var rawStaffs = db.Query<dynamic>(sqlStaffs)?.ToList();
                if (rawStaffs != null && rawStaffs.Count > 0)
                {
                    var staffs = new Dictionary<long, StaffRecord>();
                    foreach (var w in rawStaffs)
                    {
                        long staffId = (long)w.staff_id;
                        string fullName = (string)(w.staff_name ?? string.Empty);
                        string shortName = (string)(w.short_name ?? string.Empty);
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(shortName) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fullName))
                        {
                            shortName = RuleEvaluator.ApplyTextTransform(fullName, TransformKind.ShortNameFirstLast);
                        }

                        long parsedNric = 0;
                        if (w.nric != null)
                        {
                            long.TryParse(w.nric.ToString(), out parsedNric);
                        }

                        staffs[staffId] = new StaffRecord
                        {
                            StaffID = staffId,
                            StaffDetails = new StaffDetails
                            {
                                StaffID = staffId,
                                StaffName = fullName,
                                StaffShortName = shortName,
                                Department = (string)(w.department ?? "Worker"),
                                NRIC = parsedNric,
                                Passport = (string)(w.passport ?? string.Empty),
                                Phone = (string)(w.phone ?? string.Empty),
                                JoinDate = w.join_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime((DateTime)w.join_date) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today),
                                ResignDate = w.resign_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime((DateTime)w.resign_date) : null,
                                ContractStatus = Enum.TryParse<ContractStatus>((string)(w.contract_status ?? "Active"), true, out var cs) ? cs : ContractStatus.Active
                            },
                            VehicleLicenses = new List<VehicleLicense>(),
                            SkillLicenses = new List<SkillLicense>()
                        };
                    }

                    var vehicleLicenses = db.Query<dynamic>(sqlVehicleLicenses)?.ToList() ?? new List<dynamic>();
                    foreach (var vl in vehicleLicenses)
                    {
                        long wid = (long)vl.staff_id;
                        if (staffs.TryGetValue(wid, out var staff))
                        {
                            staff.VehicleLicenses.Add(new VehicleLicense
                            {
                                VehicleLicenseID = vl.vehicle_license_id != null ? (long)vl.vehicle_license_id : 0,
                                StaffID = wid,
                                StaffName = staff.StaffDetails.StaffName,
                                VehicleLicenseClass = Enum.TryParse<VehicleLicenseClass>((string)(vl.vehicle_license_class ?? vl.license_type ?? vl.license_class ?? "None"), true, out var lc) ? lc : VehicleLicenseClass.None,
                                RenewDate = vl.renew_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime((DateTime)vl.renew_date) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today),
                                ExpiryDate = vl.expiry_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime((DateTime)vl.expiry_date) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today.AddYears(1)),
                                VehicleLicenseStatus = Enum.TryParse<VehicleLicenseStatus>((string)(vl.status ?? "Valid"), true, out var ls) ? ls : VehicleLicenseStatus.Valid,
                                Cost = vl.cost != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(vl.cost) : 0.00m
                            });
                        }
                    }

                    var skillLicenses = db.Query<dynamic>(sqlSkillLicenses)?.ToList() ?? new List<dynamic>();
                    foreach (var sl in skillLicenses)
                    {
                        long wid = (long)sl.staff_id;
                        if (staffs.TryGetValue(wid, out var staff))
                        {
                            staff.SkillLicenses.Add(new SkillLicense
                            {
                                SkillLicenseID = sl.skill_license_id != null ? (long)sl.skill_license_id : 0,
                                StaffID = wid,
                                StaffName = staff.StaffDetails.StaffName,
                                SkillLicenseType = (string)(sl.skill_license_type ?? sl.license_type ?? string.Empty),
                                RenewDate = sl.renew_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime((DateTime)sl.renew_date) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today),
                                ExpiryDate = sl.expiry_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime((DateTime)sl.expiry_date) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today.AddYears(1)),
                                SkillLicenseStatus = Enum.TryParse<SkillLicenseStatus>((string)(sl.status ?? "Valid"), true, out var ls) ? ls : SkillLicenseStatus.Valid,
                                Cost = sl.cost != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(sl.cost) : 0.00m
                            });
                        }
                    }

                    var assembledList = staffs.Values.ToList();

                    // Cache to local JSON snapshot so VehicleManager and DailyScheduler have fresh staff lookups
                    OfflineSnapshot.Save(assembledList, writeToDatabase: false);

#if DEBUG
                    if (DebugDialog.DebugEnabled)
                    {
                        var sb = new StringBuilder();
                        sb.AppendLine("=== POSTGRESQL DOWNLOAD SUMMARY ===");
                        sb.AppendLine($"Total Staff Profiles   : {assembledList.Count}");
                        sb.AppendLine($"Total Vehicle Licenses : {vehicleLicenses.Count}");
                        sb.AppendLine($"Total Skill Licenses   : {skillLicenses.Count}");
                        sb.AppendLine();
                        foreach (var s in assembledList)
                        {
                            sb.AppendLine($"  [{s.StaffID}] {s.StaffDetails.StaffName} ({s.StaffDetails.StaffShortName}) | Dept: {s.StaffDetails.Department} | Status: {s.StaffDetails.ContractStatus}");
                            if (s.VehicleLicenses.Count > 0)
                            {
                                sb.AppendLine($"      Vehicle Lic: " + string.Join(", ", s.VehicleLicenses.Select(v => $"{v.VehicleLicenseClass} (RM{v.Cost:F2})")));
                            }
                            if (s.SkillLicenses.Count > 0)
                            {
                                sb.AppendLine($"      Skill Lic: " + string.Join(", ", s.SkillLicenses.Select(sk => $"{sk.SkillLicenseType} (RM{sk.Cost:F2})")));
                            }
                        }
                        DebugDialog.ShowLog("[DEBUG] PostgreSQL Download - Staff Summary", sb.ToString());
                    }
#endif
                    return assembledList;
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error("[FetchStaffRecords DB Fallback]", ex);
        }

        return OfflineSnapshot.Load<List<StaffRecord>>();
    }
}
