============================================================
FILE: StaffManager\StaffManager.SheetMapper.cs
============================================================
namespace SKSB_App.StaffManager;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Database;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sync;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.SheetMapper;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Storage;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Validation;

public static class SheetMapper
{
    public const string MasterSheetName = "All Staffs";
    public const string MasterTableName = "AllStaffsTable";

    private static readonly string[] MasterSheetAliases = { "All Staffs", "AllStaffs", "Staff_Master", "Staffs", "All Staff", "All Workers" };
    private static readonly string[] VehicleLicenseAliases = { "Vehicle Licenses", "VehicleLicenses", "Vehicle License", "Staff_Licenses" };
    private static readonly string[] SkillLicenseAliases = { "Skill Licenses", "SkillLicenses", "Skill License", "Staff_Skills" };

    // =========================================================================
    // 1. MASTER EXTRACTION & VALIDATION PIPELINE
    // =========================================================================
    public static (List<StaffRecord>? Data, List<string> Errors, List<string> Warnings) ExtractStaffRecordsWithDiagnostics()
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (wb == null) return (null, new List<string> { "No active workbook found." }, new List<string>());

            dynamic? masterWs = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, MasterSheetAliases, MasterSheetName);
            if (masterWs == null)
            {
                return (null, new List<string> { $"Worksheet '{MasterSheetName}' was not found in workbook." }, new List<string>());
            }

            // 1. Extract Master Staff Records (runs EvaluateAndHealTable automatically inside TableMapper)
            List<StaffRecord> staffs = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<StaffRecord>((object)masterWs, MasterTableName);
            foreach (var s in staffs)
            {
                s.StaffDetails.StaffID = s.StaffID;
            }

            // 2. Extract Child License Collections & Check Foreign Key Integrity
            var childErrors = ExtractChildRecords((object)wb, staffs);

            // 3. Validate All Tables Across All Sheets (AllStaffsTable, VehicleLicensesTable, SkillLicensesTable, SkillLicenseTypesTable)
            var (errors, warnings) = RulesExtractor.ValidateAllWorkbookTables((object)wb);
            errors.AddRange(childErrors);

            // 4. Primary Key & Date Integrity Checks on Master Table
            var idGroups = staffs
                .Where(s => s.StaffID > 0)
                .GroupBy(s => s.StaffID);

            foreach (var group in idGroups)
            {
                if (group.Count() > 1)
                {
                    errors.Add($"  [{MasterSheetName}]: Duplicate Staff ID '{group.Key}' detected {group.Count()} times in '{MasterTableName}'.");
                }
            }

            foreach (var s in staffs)
            {
                if (s.StaffDetails.ResignDate.HasValue && s.StaffDetails.ResignDate.Value < s.StaffDetails.JoinDate)
                {
                    errors.Add($"  [{MasterSheetName}]: Staff ID '{s.StaffID}' ({s.StaffDetails.StaffName}) Resign Date ({s.StaffDetails.ResignDate.Value:dd/MM/yyyy}) cannot be earlier than Join Date ({s.StaffDetails.JoinDate:dd/MM/yyyy}).");
                }
            }

            return (staffs, errors, warnings);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            return (null, new List<string> { $"Failed extracting staff records: {ex.Message}" }, new List<string>());
        }
    }

    public static List<StaffRecord> ExtractStaffRecords()
    {
        var (data, _, _) = ExtractStaffRecordsWithDiagnostics();
        return data ?? new List<StaffRecord>();
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 2. MASTER POPULATE PIPELINE
    // =========================================================================
    public static void PopulateWorksheet(List<StaffRecord> records)
    {
        if (records == null) return;

        var sortedList = records
            .OrderBy(s => s.StaffDetails.ContractStatus == ContractStatus.Resigned ? 1 : 0)
            .ThenBy(s => s.StaffID)
            .ToList();

        dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
        bool origEvents = true;
        bool origUpdating = true;
        int origCalculation = -4105; // xlCalculationAutomatic
        try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }
        try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }
        try { origCalculation = (int)xlApp.Calculation; xlApp.Calculation = -4135; /* xlCalculationManual */ } catch { }

        try
        {
            dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (wb == null) return;

            // 1. Populate Master Table
            dynamic? masterWs = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, MasterSheetAliases, MasterSheetName);
            if (masterWs != null)
            {
                SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)masterWs, new Action(() =>
                {
                    TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<StaffRecord>((object)masterWs, MasterTableName, sortedList);
                }));
            }

            // 2. Populate Child Tables & Auxiliary SkillLicenseTypesTable
            PopulateChildTables((object)wb, sortedList);

#if DEBUG
            ShowPopulateDiagnostics((object)wb, sortedList);
#endif
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[StaffSheetMapper.PopulateWorksheet Error] {ex.Message}");
        }
        finally
        {
            try { xlApp.Calculation = origCalculation; } catch { }
            try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
            try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
        }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 3. CHILD & AUXILIARY TABLE OPERATIONS
    // =========================================================================
    private static List<string> ExtractChildRecords(object wbObj, List<StaffRecord> staffs)
    {
        var childErrors = new List<string>();
        dynamic wb = wbObj;

        var staffMap = staffs
            .Where(s => s.StaffID > 0)
            .GroupBy(s => s.StaffID)
            .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.First());

        // 1. Vehicle Licenses
        dynamic? vWs = ResolveSheetSafely(wbObj, VehicleLicenseAliases, "Vehicle Licenses");
        if (vWs != null)
        {
            try
            {
                List<VehicleLicense> vLicenses = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<VehicleLicense>((object)vWs, "VehicleLicensesTable");
                var seenIds = new HashSet<long>();

                foreach (var lic in vLicenses)
                {
                    if (lic.StaffID <= 0) continue;

                    if (lic.VehicleLicenseID > 0 && !seenIds.Add(lic.VehicleLicenseID))
                    {
                        childErrors.Add($"  [Vehicle Licenses]: Duplicate Vehicle License ID '{lic.VehicleLicenseID}'.");
                    }

                    if (staffMap.TryGetValue(lic.StaffID, out StaffRecord? s) && s != null)
                    {
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(lic.StaffName))
                            lic.StaffName = s.StaffDetails.StaffName;
                        s.VehicleLicenses.Add(lic);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        childErrors.Add($"  [Vehicle Licenses]: Staff ID '{lic.StaffID}' does not exist in '{MasterSheetName}'.");
                    }
                }
            }
            catch { }
        }

        // 2. Skill Licenses
        dynamic? sWs = ResolveSheetSafely(wbObj, SkillLicenseAliases, "Skill Licenses");
        if (sWs != null)
        {
            try
            {
                List<SkillLicense> sLicenses = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<SkillLicense>((object)sWs, "SkillLicensesTable");
                var seenIds = new HashSet<long>();

                foreach (var lic in sLicenses)
                {
                    if (lic.StaffID <= 0) continue;

                    if (lic.SkillLicenseID > 0 && !seenIds.Add(lic.SkillLicenseID))
                    {
                        childErrors.Add($"  [Skill Licenses]: Duplicate Skill License ID '{lic.SkillLicenseID}'.");
                    }

                    if (staffMap.TryGetValue(lic.StaffID, out StaffRecord? s) && s != null)
                    {
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(lic.StaffName))
                            lic.StaffName = s.StaffDetails.StaffName;
                        s.SkillLicenses.Add(lic);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        childErrors.Add($"  [Skill Licenses]: Staff ID '{lic.StaffID}' does not exist in '{MasterSheetName}'.");
                    }
                }
            }
            catch { }
        }

        return childErrors;
    }

    private static void PopulateChildTables(object wbObj, List<StaffRecord> records)
    {
        // 1. Vehicle Licenses
        dynamic? vWs = ResolveSheetSafely(wbObj, VehicleLicenseAliases, "Vehicle Licenses");
        if (vWs != null)
        {
            try
            {
                SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)vWs, new Action(() =>
                {
                    var allVLicenses = records.SelectMany(s => s.VehicleLicenses.Select(lic =>
                    {
                        if (lic.StaffID <= 0) lic.StaffID = s.StaffID;
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(lic.StaffName))
                            lic.StaffName = s.StaffDetails.StaffName;
                        return lic;
                    })).ToList();

                    TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<VehicleLicense>((object)vWs, "VehicleLicensesTable", allVLicenses);
                }));
            }
            catch { }
        }

        // 2. Skill Licenses & Side Reference Table (SkillLicenseTypesTable)
        dynamic? sWs = ResolveSheetSafely(wbObj, SkillLicenseAliases, "Skill Licenses");
        if (sWs != null)
        {
            try
            {
                SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)sWs, new Action(() =>
                {
                    var allSLicenses = records.SelectMany(s => s.SkillLicenses.Select(lic =>
                    {
                        if (lic.StaffID <= 0) lic.StaffID = s.StaffID;
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(lic.StaffName))
                            lic.StaffName = s.StaffDetails.StaffName;
                        return lic;
                    })).ToList();

                    TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<SkillLicense>((object)sWs, "SkillLicensesTable", allSLicenses);

                    // Load SkillLicenseTypes from local cache (already refreshed during FetchStaffRecords)
                    var savedTypes = OfflineSnapshot.Load<List<SkillLicenseType>>();
                    if (savedTypes == null || savedTypes.Count == 0)
                    {
                        savedTypes = ServerSyncCommands.FetchSkillLicenseTypesFromDb();
                    }

                    if (savedTypes != null && savedTypes.Count > 0)
                    {
                        TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<SkillLicenseType>((object)sWs, "SkillLicenseTypesTable", savedTypes);
                        var names = savedTypes.Select(t => t.SkillLicenseName).Where(n => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(n)).ToList();
                        DataList.SyncValues("SkillLicenseTypeList", names);
                    }
                }));
            }
            catch { }
        }
    }

    public static void PopulateSkillLicenseTypesTable(bool showDialogIfFailed = false)
    {
        dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
        bool origEvents = true;
        try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }

        try
        {
            dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (wb == null) return;

            dynamic? ws = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, SkillLicenseAliases, "Skill Licenses");
            if (ws == null) return;

            var types = ServerSyncCommands.FetchSkillLicenseTypesFromDb();
            if (types.Count == 0)
            {
                types = OfflineSnapshot.Load<List<SkillLicenseType>>() ?? new List<SkillLicenseType>();
            }

            if (types.Count > 0)
            {
                SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)ws, new Action(() =>
                {
                    TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<SkillLicenseType>((object)ws, "SkillLicenseTypesTable", types);
                }));

                var names = types.Select(t => t.SkillLicenseName).Where(n => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(n)).ToList();
                DataList.SyncValues("SkillLicenseTypeList", names);
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            if (showDialogIfFailed)
                System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox.Show($"Failed populating skill license types: {ex.Message}");
        }
        finally
        {
            try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
        }
    }

    public static List<SkillLicenseType> ExtractSkillLicenseTypes()
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (wb == null) return new List<SkillLicenseType>();

            dynamic? sWs = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, SkillLicenseAliases, "Skill Licenses");
            if (sWs == null) return new List<SkillLicenseType>();

            var extracted = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<SkillLicenseType>((object)sWs, "SkillLicenseTypesTable");
            var distinct = extracted
                .Where(t => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(t.SkillLicenseName))
                .Select(t => t.SkillLicenseName.Trim())
                .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                .OrderBy(n => n)
                .Select((name, idx) => new SkillLicenseType
                {
                    SkillLicenseTypeID = idx + 1,
                    SkillLicenseName = name
                })
                .ToList();

            if (distinct.Count > 0)
            {
                DataList.SyncValues("SkillLicenseTypeList", distinct.Select(d => d.SkillLicenseName));
            }

            return distinct;
        }
        catch
        {
            return new List<SkillLicenseType>();
        }
    }

    internal static dynamic? ResolveSheetSafely(object wbObj, string[] aliases, string defaultName)
    {
        dynamic wb = wbObj;
        string resolvedName = SheetOperations.ResolveSheetName(aliases, defaultName);
        try
        {
            dynamic s = wb.Sheets[resolvedName];
            if (s != null) return s;
        }
        catch { }

        foreach (dynamic ws in wb.Sheets)
        {
            string name = ws.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            if (aliases.Any(a => string.Equals(a, name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
            {
                return ws;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

#if DEBUG
    private static void ShowPopulateDiagnostics(object wbObj, List<StaffRecord> records)
    {
        try
        {
            if (!DebugDialog.DebugEnabled) return;

            dynamic wb = wbObj;
            var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            sb.AppendLine("=== STAFF MANAGER POPULATE AUDIT ===");
            sb.AppendLine($"Total Staff Profiles Loaded: {records.Count}");
            sb.AppendLine();

            string[] inspectTables = { "AllStaffsTable", "VehicleLicensesTable", "SkillLicensesTable", "SkillLicenseTypesTable" };
            foreach (var tName in inspectTables)
            {
                dynamic? tbl = null;
                string sName = "Unknown";
                foreach (dynamic ws in wb.Sheets)
                {
                    try { tbl = ws.ListObjects[tName]; if (tbl != null) { sName = ws.Name?.ToString() ?? "Unknown"; break; } } catch { }
                }

                if (tbl != null)
                {
                    int cols = (int)tbl.ListColumns.Count;
                    int rows = tbl.DataBodyRange != null ? (int)tbl.DataBodyRange.Rows.Count : 0;
                    int startCol = (int)tbl.HeaderRowRange.Column;
                    int startRow = (int)tbl.HeaderRowRange.Row;
                    sb.AppendLine($"  [{tName}] on Sheet '{sName}':");
                    sb.AppendLine($"      Dimensions : {rows} data row(s) × {cols} column(s)");
                    sb.AppendLine($"      Coordinates: Row {startRow}, Columns {startCol} to {startCol + cols - 1}");
                    sb.AppendLine();
                }
                else
                {
                    sb.AppendLine($"  [{tName}]: NOT FOUND on any worksheet");
                    sb.AppendLine();
                }
            }

            DebugDialog.ShowLog("Staff Manager - Populate Audit", sb.ToString());
        }
        catch { }
    }
#endif
}

namespace SKSB_App.VehicleManager;

using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Database;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DateFilter;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.DropDown;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Entry;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sync;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Entry;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Storage;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class Events : IExcelAddIn
{
    private static dynamic? _xlAppRoot;
    private static bool _isExiting = false;
    private static bool _isInternalSaving = false;

    private static readonly EventsAutoOpen _coreEvents = new();

    private delegate void WorkbookBeforeCloseDelegate(dynamic wb, ref bool cancel);
    private delegate void WorkbookBeforeSaveDelegate(dynamic wb, bool saveAsUi, ref bool cancel);
    private static WorkbookBeforeCloseDelegate? _beforeCloseHandler;
    private static WorkbookBeforeSaveDelegate? _beforeSaveHandler;

    public static readonly SaveContract<List<VehicleRecord>> VehicleSyncDescriptor = new()
    {
        ModuleName = "Vehicle Manager",
        ServerTableName = "vehicle_master",
        ExtractAndValidate = () => SheetMapper.ExtractVehicleRecordsWithDiagnostics(),
        IsDatasetEmpty = list => list == null || list.Count == 0,
        SaveToServer = list => (ServerSyncCommands.SaveVehiclesSilent(list, out string err), err),
        PopulateWorksheet = list => SheetMapper.PopulateWorksheet(list),
        FetchFromServer = () => ServerSyncCommands.FetchVehicleRecords(),
        SyncLookups = isInteractive => SyncVehicleLookupsData(isInteractive),
        ValidateOfflinePayload = list => list.Count > 0 && list.TrueForAll(v => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v.VehiclePlate))
    };

    public void AutoOpen()
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            _coreEvents.AutoOpen();

            HookApplicationEvents();
            Arrow.EnsureExcelEventsHooked();

            double nowSerial = Convert.ToDouble(XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlfNow));
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnTime, nowSerial + (1.0 / 86400.0), "DelayedAutoLoadMacro");

            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^s", "OnHotKeySave");
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^+s", "OnHotKeySave");
        });
    }

    public void AutoClose()
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            _coreEvents.AutoClose();
            Arrow.Teardown();
            UnhookApplicationEvents();
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^s");
            XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcOnKey, "^+s");
        });
    }

    public static void SyncVehicleLookupsData(bool isInteractive = false)
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                // Sync ONLY the specific enums used across Vehicle Manager sheets
                EnumList.Sync(
                    typeof(DateFilterScope),
                    typeof(VehicleType),
                    typeof(ServiceType),
                    typeof(MaintenanceStatus),
                    typeof(VehicleStatus),
                    typeof(VehiclePermitType),
                    typeof(VehicleType),
                    typeof(InsuranceType)
                );

                // 1. Sync Staff IDs (ordered numerically)
                DataList.SyncProperty<StaffRecord, long>(
                    s => s.StaffID,
                    customListName: "StaffIDList"
                );

                // 2. Push StaffShortName into StaffNameList (with fallback to StaffName if blank)
                DataList.SyncProperty<StaffRecord, string>(
                    s => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s.StaffDetails.StaffShortName)
                        ? s.StaffDetails.StaffShortName
                        : s.StaffDetails.StaffName,
                    customListName: "StaffNameList"
                );

                // Optional: Also sync dedicated StaffShortNameList if needed elsewhere
                DataList.SyncProperty<StaffRecord, string>(
                    s => s.StaffDetails.StaffShortName,
                    customListName: "StaffShortNameList"
                );
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[SyncVehicleLookupsData Warning] {ex.Message}");
            }
        });
    }

    private static void HookApplicationEvents()
    {
        try
        {
            if (_xlAppRoot != null) return;
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            _xlAppRoot = xlApp;

            dynamic ourWorkbook = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;

            _beforeCloseHandler = new WorkbookBeforeCloseDelegate((dynamic wb, ref bool cancel) =>
            {
                if (!Guard.IsVehicleWorkbook(wb))
                {
                    wb.Saved = true;
                    cancel = false;
                    return;
                }

                SaveEntry.InterceptClose(
                    (object)wb,
                    ref cancel,
                    VehicleSyncDescriptor,
                    ref _isExiting,
                    new Action(UnhookApplicationEvents)
                );
            });
            _xlAppRoot.WorkbookBeforeClose += _beforeCloseHandler;

            _beforeSaveHandler = new WorkbookBeforeSaveDelegate((dynamic wb, bool saveAsUi, ref bool cancel) =>
            {
                try
                {
                    if (ourWorkbook != null && wb != null && wb != ourWorkbook) return;
                }
                catch { }

                if (_isInternalSaving || SheetOperations.IsMasterTemplateFile() || SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode) return;

                cancel = true;
                ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() => SaveEntry.Save(VehicleSyncDescriptor));
            });
            _xlAppRoot.WorkbookBeforeSave += _beforeSaveHandler;
        }
        catch { }
    }

    private static void UnhookApplicationEvents()
    {
        try
        {
            if (_xlAppRoot != null)
            {
                if (_beforeCloseHandler != null) _xlAppRoot.WorkbookBeforeClose -= _beforeCloseHandler;
                if (_beforeSaveHandler != null) _xlAppRoot.WorkbookBeforeSave -= _beforeSaveHandler;
                _beforeCloseHandler = null;
                _beforeSaveHandler = null;
                _xlAppRoot = null;
            }
        }
        catch { }
    }

    [ExcelCommand(Name = "OnHotKeySave")]
    public static void OnHotKeySave()
    {
        if (SheetOperations.IsMasterTemplateFile() || SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode)
        {
            _isInternalSaving = true;
            try { XlCall.Excel(XlCall.xlcSave); } finally { _isInternalSaving = false; }
            return;
        }

        SaveEntry.Save(VehicleSyncDescriptor);
    }

    [ExcelCommand(Name = "DelayedAutoLoadMacro")]
    public static void DelayedAutoLoadMacro()
    {
        if (!Guard.IsVehicleWorkbook()) return;

        dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
        try
        {
            if (xlApp.ActiveWorkbook != null && !SheetOperations.IsMasterTemplateFile())
            {
                xlApp.ActiveWindow.Caption = "Vehicle_Manager";
            }
        }
        catch { }

        OfflineSave.HandleStartup(VehicleSyncDescriptor);

        dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
        if (wb != null && !SheetOperations.IsTemplateEditMode)
        {
            foreach (dynamic sheet in wb.Worksheets)
            {
                SheetProtections.ApplyStyleDrivenProtection(sheet);
            }

            SheetOperations.SetAuxiliarySheetsVisibility(visible: false);
        }
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.VehicleManager;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Erp;

public class FuelImportResult
{
    public List<FuelImportRow> ImportedTransactions { get; set; } = new();
    public List<ImportSkippedRecord> SkippedRecords { get; set; } = new();
    public int TotalRowsProcessed { get; set; } = 0;
    public string ProviderDetected { get; set; } = "Unknown";

    public ImportSummaryReport ToGenericReport() => new()
    {
        SourceSystemName = $"{ProviderDetected} Fleet Card",
        EntityLabel = "fuel transaction(s)",
        SuccessfullyImportedCount = ImportedTransactions.Count,
        FilteredExclusionCount = 0,
        FilterRuleDescription = "Mapped against active FuelCardsTable. Auto-derived Odometer Before and Card Owner.",
        SkippedErrors = SkippedRecords
    };
}

public class FuelImportRow
{
    public DateOnly Date { get; set; }
    public string Time { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string CardNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string VehiclePlate { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string VehicleType { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public long StaffID { get; set; }
    public string StaffName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string StationName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public decimal Amount { get; set; }
    public int Odometer { get; set; }
}

public static class FuelImporter
{
    public static FuelImportResult ImportTransactions(
        string filePath,
        Dictionary<string, (string Plate, string Type, long StaffId, string StaffName)> cardRegistry)
    {
        var result = new FuelImportResult();
        var doc = ErpImporter.LoadFromFile(filePath);
        if (doc.Rows.Count == 0)
            return result;

        // Auto-detect Provider based on column headers and card patterns
        bool isShell = doc.HasColumn("Vehicle Registration") || doc.HasColumn("VRN") || doc.HasColumn("Shell Card Number");
        result.ProviderDetected = isShell ? "Shell Fleet Hub" : "Petronas SmartPay";

        for (int r = 0; r < doc.Rows.Count; r++)
        {
            var row = doc.Rows[r];
            int excelRowNum = r + 2;
            result.TotalRowsProcessed++;

            // 1. Extract & Sanitize Card Number
            string rawCard = isShell
                ? (row.GetString("Card Number") ?? row.GetString("Shell Card Number"))
                : row.GetString("Card Number");
            string cleanCard = rawCard.Replace("'", "").Replace(" ", "").Trim();

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cleanCard) || cleanCard.Contains("TRANSACTION") || cleanCard.Contains("UNBILL"))
            {
                continue;
            }

            // 2. Extract Date & Time
            string rawDateTimeStr = isShell
                ? (row.GetString("Transaction Date & Time") ?? row.GetString("Transaction Date") ?? row.GetString("Date"))
                : (row.GetString("Date Time") ?? row.GetString("Date / Time") ?? row.GetString("Date"));

            DateTime parsedDt;
            if (!DateTime.TryParse(rawDateTimeStr, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DateTimeStyles.None, out parsedDt))
            {
                if (!DateTime.TryParse(rawDateTimeStr, out parsedDt))
                {
                    result.SkippedRecords.Add(new ImportSkippedRecord
                    {
                        ExcelRowNumber = excelRowNum,
                        Identifier = cleanCard,
                        NameOrTitle = "Invalid Date",
                        Reason = $"Could not parse transaction date/time '{rawDateTimeStr}'."
                    });
                    continue;
                }
            }

            DateOnly txDate = DateOnly.FromDateTime(parsedDt);
            string txTime = parsedDt.ToString("HH:mm");

            // 3. Extract Amount
            string rawAmount = isShell
                ? (row.GetString("Gross Amount (MYR)") ?? row.GetString("Gross Amount") ?? row.GetString("Amount"))
                : (row.GetString("Transaction Amount (RM)") ?? row.GetString("Amount"));

            decimal.TryParse(rawAmount?.Replace("RM", "").Trim(), NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out decimal amount);

            // 4. Resolve Vehicle & Assigned Staff via FuelCardsTable registry
            string plate = string.Empty;
            string vType = string.Empty;
            long staffId = 0;
            string staffName = string.Empty;

            if (cardRegistry.TryGetValue(cleanCard, out var cardInfo))
            {
                plate = cardInfo.Plate;
                vType = cardInfo.Type;
                staffId = cardInfo.StaffId;
                staffName = cardInfo.StaffName;
            }
            else
            {
                plate = row.GetString("Vehicle Registration") ?? row.GetString("Vehicle No.") ?? row.GetString("VRN");
                staffName = row.GetString("Driver") ?? row.GetString("Driver Name");
            }

            // 5. Extract Station & Odometer
            string station = isShell
                ? (row.GetString("Station Name") ?? row.GetString("Site Name") ?? row.GetString("Location"))
                : (row.GetString("Station Name") ?? row.GetString("Site"));

            string rawOdo = row.GetString("Odometer") ?? row.GetString("Mileage");
            int.TryParse(rawOdo?.Replace(",", "").Trim(), out int odometer);

            result.ImportedTransactions.Add(new FuelImportRow
            {
                Date = txDate,
                Time = txTime,
                CardNumber = cleanCard,
                VehiclePlate = plate,
                VehicleType = vType,
                StaffID = staffId,
                StaffName = staffName,
                StationName = station,
                Amount = amount,
                Odometer = odometer
            });
        }

        return result;
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.VehicleManager;

using ExcelDna.Integration;

public static class Guard
{
    public static bool IsVehicleWorkbook(dynamic? wb = null)
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic targetWb = wb ?? xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (targetWb == null) return false;

            dynamic ws = null;
            try { ws = targetWb.Sheets["All Vehicles"]; } catch { }
            if (ws == null)
            {
                try { ws = targetWb.Sheets["AllVehicles"]; } catch { }
            }
            if (ws == null) return false;

            dynamic table = null;
            try { table = ws.ListObjects["AllVehiclesTable"]; } catch { }
            return table != null;
        }
        catch
        {
            return false;
        }
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.VehicleManager.Importer;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using UglyToad.PdfPig;
using UglyToad.PdfPig.Content;

public enum FuelProvider
{
    Unknown,
    Petronas,
    Shell
}

public sealed class FuelTransactionRecord
{
    public DateOnly Date { get; set; }
    public string Time { get; set; } = "00:00";
    public string VehiclePlate { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string VehicleType { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public long Odometer { get; set; }
    public string CardNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public long StaffID { get; set; }
    public string StaffName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string StationName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public decimal Amount { get; set; }
    public string ReceiptNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string ProductDescription { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public decimal Litres { get; set; }
    public FuelProvider Provider { get; set; }
}

public sealed class FuelImportResult
{
    public FuelProvider Provider { get; set; } = FuelProvider.Unknown;
    public string SourceFileName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public List<FuelTransactionRecord> NewTransactions { get; } = new();
    public List<FuelTransactionRecord> DuplicatesSkipped { get; } = new();
    public List<string> Warnings { get; } = new();
    public decimal TotalImportedAmount => NewTransactions.Sum(t => t.Amount);
    public int TotalParsedCount => NewTransactions.Count + DuplicatesSkipped.Count;
}

public static class FuelImporter
{
    private static readonly Regex PlateRegex = new(@"\b([A-Z]{1,3})\s*(\d{1,4})\s*([A-Z]?)\b", RegexOptions.Compiled);

    public static FuelImportResult ProcessFile(
        string filePath,
        HashSet<string> existingTransactionSignatures,
        Dictionary<string, (string FullCard, string OwnerName, long OwnerId)> cardRegistry,
        Dictionary<string, string> vehicleTypeRegistry,
        Dictionary<string, long> staffRegistry)
    {
        var result = new FuelImportResult
        {
            SourceFileName = Path.GetFileName(filePath)
        };

        if (!File.Exists(filePath))
        {
            result.Warnings.Add($"File '{filePath}' does not exist.");
            return result;
        }

        string ext = Path.GetExtension(filePath).ToLowerInvariant();
        List<FuelTransactionRecord> rawRecords;

        if (ext == ".pdf")
        {
            rawRecords = ExtractFromPdf(filePath, result);
        }
        else if (ext == ".xlsx" || ext == ".xls" || ext == ".csv")
        {
            rawRecords = ExtractFromSpreadsheet(filePath, result);
        }
        else
        {
            result.Warnings.Add($"Unsupported file format '{ext}'. Please select a PDF, Excel, or CSV statement.");
            return result;
        }

        foreach (var rec in rawRecords)
        {
            // 1. Reconcile Card Number (especially for masked Shell cards e.g. 7077841******000208)
            rec.CardNumber = ResolveFullCardNumber(rec.CardNumber, rec.VehiclePlate, cardRegistry);

            // 2. Reconcile Vehicle Type from master registry if blank
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rec.VehicleType) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rec.VehiclePlate))
            {
                string normPlate = NormalizePlate(rec.VehiclePlate);
                if (vehicleTypeRegistry.TryGetValue(normPlate, out string? vType))
                {
                    rec.VehicleType = vType;
                }
            }

            // 3. Reconcile Staff ID from Driver Name if missing
            if (rec.StaffID <= 0 && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rec.StaffName))
            {
                string normStaff = rec.StaffName.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
                foreach (var (name, id) in staffRegistry)
                {
                    if (name.Trim().ToLowerInvariant().Contains(normStaff) || normStaff.Contains(name.Trim().ToLowerInvariant()))
                    {
                        rec.StaffID = id;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            // 4. Duplicate Check Signature: Date + Time + NormalizedPlate + Amount
            string sig = $"{rec.Date:yyyyMMdd}_{rec.Time}_{NormalizePlate(rec.VehiclePlate)}_{rec.Amount:F2}";
            if (existingTransactionSignatures.Contains(sig))
            {
                result.DuplicatesSkipped.Add(rec);
            }
            else
            {
                result.NewTransactions.Add(rec);
                existingTransactionSignatures.Add(sig);
            }
        }

        return result;
    }

    private static List<FuelTransactionRecord> ExtractFromPdf(string pdfPath, FuelImportResult result)
    {
        var records = new List<FuelTransactionRecord>();
        using var document = PdfDocument.Open(pdfPath);

        var allPagesText = new List<string>();
        foreach (var page in document.GetPages())
        {
            allPagesText.Add(page.Text);
        }

        string fullContent = string.Join("\n", allPagesText);

        // Detect Provider
        if (fullContent.Contains("PETRONAS", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
            fullContent.Contains("smartPAY", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            result.Provider = FuelProvider.Petronas;
            records.AddRange(ParsePetronasPdf(document));
        }
        else if (fullContent.Contains("Shell Malaysia Trading", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                 fullContent.Contains("fleethub.shell.com", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            result.Provider = FuelProvider.Shell;
            records.AddRange(ParseShellPdf(document));
        }
        else
        {
            result.Warnings.Add("Could not identify provider as either Petronas SmartPay or Shell Malaysia.");
        }

        return records;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Parses official Petronas SmartPay e-Statement (STM-*.pdf).
    /// </summary>
    private static List<FuelTransactionRecord> ParsePetronasPdf(PdfDocument document)
    {
        var list = new List<FuelTransactionRecord>();
        string currentVehiclePlate = string.Empty;
        string currentCardNumber = string.Empty;

        var headerCardRegex = new Regex(@"Nombor\s*Kad\s*[:\s]*(\d{16,19})", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
        var headerPlateRegex = new Regex(@"No\.\s*Pendaftaran\s*Kenderaan\s*[:\s]*([A-Z0-9\s]+)", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
        var txRegex = new Regex(
            @"(\d{2}/\d{2}/\d{4})\s+(\d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2})\s+(\d{12})\s+(PSS\s+[^\d]+?)\s+([A-Z\s]+?)\s+(\d{6,10})\s+(Diesel[^\d]+|PRIMAX[^\d]+)\s+([\d\.]+)\s+([\d\.]+)\s+([\d\.]+)\s+([\d\.,]+)\s+([\d\.,]+)",
            RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);

        foreach (var page in document.GetPages())
        {
            string pageText = page.Text;
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(pageText) || !pageText.Contains("LAPORAN ANALISA KENDERAAN"))
                continue;

            var cardMatch = headerCardRegex.Match(pageText);
            if (cardMatch.Success) currentCardNumber = cardMatch.Groups[1].Value.Trim();

            var plateMatch = headerPlateRegex.Match(pageText);
            if (plateMatch.Success)
            {
                string rawP = plateMatch.Groups[1].Value.Trim();
                int newlineIdx = rawP.IndexOfAny(new[] { '\r', '\n' });
                if (newlineIdx > 0) rawP = rawP.Substring(0, newlineIdx).Trim();
                currentVehiclePlate = FormatPlate(rawP);
            }

            var lines = pageText.Split(new[] { '\r', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
            foreach (var line in lines)
            {
                var match = txRegex.Match(line);
                if (!match.Success) continue;

                if (!DateOnly.TryParseExact(match.Groups[1].Value, "dd/MM/yyyy", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DateTimeStyles.None, out var txDate))
                    continue;

                string timeFull = match.Groups[2].Value; // "HH:mm:ss"
                string timeClean = timeFull.Length >= 5 ? timeFull.Substring(0, 5) : timeFull;
                string station = match.Groups[4].Value.Trim();
                string driver = match.Groups[5].Value.Trim();
                long.TryParse(match.Groups[6].Value, out long odo);
                string product = match.Groups[7].Value.Trim();
                decimal.TryParse(match.Groups[8].Value, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out decimal qty);
                string netAmountStr = match.Groups[12].Value.Replace(",", "");
                decimal.TryParse(netAmountStr, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out decimal netAmount);

                // Handle Spare cards where driver name field contains the actual vehicle plate
                string finalPlate = currentVehiclePlate;
                if ((finalPlate.Equals("SPARE", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(finalPlate)) &&
                    PlateRegex.IsMatch(driver))
                {
                    finalPlate = FormatPlate(driver);
                }

                list.Add(new FuelTransactionRecord
                {
                    Date = txDate,
                    Time = timeClean,
                    VehiclePlate = finalPlate,
                    CardNumber = currentCardNumber,
                    StaffName = driver,
                    StationName = station,
                    Odometer = odo,
                    Litres = qty,
                    ProductDescription = product,
                    Amount = netAmount,
                    ReceiptNumber = match.Groups[3].Value,
                    Provider = FuelProvider.Petronas
                });
            }
        }

        return list;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Parses official Shell Malaysia Trading e-Invoice (Invoice_*.pdf).
    /// </summary>
    private static List<FuelTransactionRecord> ParseShellPdf(PdfDocument document)
    {
        var list = new List<FuelTransactionRecord>();
        string currentCard = string.Empty;
        string currentDriver = string.Empty;
        string currentPlate = string.Empty;

        var cardHeaderRegex = new Regex(@"\b(7077\d{3}\*{6}\d{6})\s+([A-Z\s]+?)\s+([A-Z]{1,3}\s*\d{1,4}\s*[A-Z]?)\b");
        var txRowRegex = new Regex(
            @"(\d{2}/\d{2}/\d{4})\s+(\d{2}:\d{2})\s*(?:\d+)?\s*(SHELL\s+[A-Z0-9\s\.,/\-]+?)\s+(\d{5,8})\s+(\d+)\s+(\d{2})\s+([\d\.]+)\s*Lit\s+([\d\.]+)\s+([\d\.,]+)(?:\s+([\-\d\.,]+)\s+([\-\d\.,]+))?",
            RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);

        foreach (var page in document.GetPages())
        {
            string pageText = page.Text;
            if (!pageText.Contains("TRANSACTION DETAILS") && !pageText.Contains("BUTIRAN TRANSAKSI"))
                continue;

            var lines = pageText.Split(new[] { '\r', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
            foreach (var line in lines)
            {
                var cardMatch = cardHeaderRegex.Match(line);
                if (cardMatch.Success)
                {
                    currentCard = cardMatch.Groups[1].Value.Trim();
                    currentDriver = cardMatch.Groups[2].Value.Trim();
                    currentPlate = FormatPlate(cardMatch.Groups[3].Value.Trim());
                    continue;
                }

                var txMatch = txRowRegex.Match(line);
                if (!txMatch.Success) continue;

                if (!DateOnly.TryParseExact(txMatch.Groups[1].Value, "dd/MM/yyyy", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DateTimeStyles.None, out var txDate))
                    continue;

                string time = txMatch.Groups[2].Value;
                string station = CleanStationName(txMatch.Groups[3].Value);
                string receipt = txMatch.Groups[4].Value;
                long.TryParse(txMatch.Groups[5].Value, out long odo);
                decimal.TryParse(txMatch.Groups[7].Value, NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out decimal qty);
                decimal.TryParse(txMatch.Groups[9].Value.Replace(",", ""), NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out decimal grossAmount);

                decimal subsidyAmount = 0m;
                if (txMatch.Groups[10].Success && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txMatch.Groups[10].Value))
                {
                    decimal.TryParse(txMatch.Groups[10].Value.Replace(",", ""), NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out subsidyAmount);
                }

                // Net company cost after government subsidy credit
                decimal netAmount = grossAmount + (subsidyAmount < 0 ? subsidyAmount : 0m);

                list.Add(new FuelTransactionRecord
                {
                    Date = txDate,
                    Time = time,
                    VehiclePlate = currentPlate,
                    CardNumber = currentCard,
                    StaffName = currentDriver,
                    StationName = station,
                    Odometer = odo,
                    Litres = qty,
                    Amount = netAmount,
                    ReceiptNumber = receipt,
                    ProductDescription = $"Shell Code {txMatch.Groups[6].Value}",
                    Provider = FuelProvider.Shell
                });
            }
        }

        return list;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Fallback for portal spreadsheet downloads (.xlsx, .xls, .csv).
    /// </summary>
    private static List<FuelTransactionRecord> ExtractFromSpreadsheet(string path, FuelImportResult result)
    {
        var records = new List<FuelTransactionRecord>();
        var lines = File.ReadAllLines(path);
        if (lines.Length == 0) return records;

        // Sniff CSV or delimited text
        char delim = lines[0].Contains('\t') ? '\t' : ',';
        int headerRow = -1;
        var colMap = new Dictionary<string, int>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        for (int i = 0; i < Math.Min(15, lines.Length); i++)
        {
            var cols = lines[i].Split(delim);
            for (int c = 0; c < cols.Length; c++)
            {
                string h = cols[c].Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
                if (h.Contains("date") || h.Contains("card") || h.Contains("amount"))
                {
                    headerRow = i;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (headerRow >= 0) break;
        }

        if (headerRow < 0) return records;

        var headerCols = lines[headerRow].Split(delim);
        for (int c = 0; c < headerCols.Length; c++)
        {
            string clean = headerCols[c].Trim().Replace("\"", "").ToLowerInvariant();
            colMap[clean] = c;
        }

        for (int i = headerRow + 1; i < lines.Length; i++)
        {
            string rawLine = lines[i].Trim();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rawLine) || rawLine.Contains("TOTAL")) continue;

            var parts = rawLine.Split(delim);
            string dateStr = GetCol(parts, colMap, "date");
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dateStr)) continue;

            if (DateTime.TryParse(dateStr, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, DateTimeStyles.None, out var dt) ||
                DateTime.TryParse(dateStr, new CultureInfo("en-GB"), DateTimeStyles.None, out dt))
            {
                decimal.TryParse(GetCol(parts, colMap, "amount").Replace(",", ""), NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out decimal amt);
                long.TryParse(GetCol(parts, colMap, "odometer"), out long odo);

                records.Add(new FuelTransactionRecord
                {
                    Date = DateOnly.FromDateTime(dt),
                    Time = dt.ToString("HH:mm"),
                    CardNumber = GetCol(parts, colMap, "card").Replace("'", ""),
                    VehiclePlate = FormatPlate(GetCol(parts, colMap, "vehicle")),
                    StaffName = GetCol(parts, colMap, "driver"),
                    StationName = GetCol(parts, colMap, "station"),
                    Amount = amt,
                    Odometer = odo
                });
            }
        }

        return records;
    }

    private static string ResolveFullCardNumber(
        string rawCard,
        string vehiclePlate,
        Dictionary<string, (string FullCard, string OwnerName, long OwnerId)> cardRegistry)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rawCard)) return string.Empty;
        string clean = rawCard.Replace("'", "").Trim();

        // Direct exact match
        if (cardRegistry.TryGetValue(clean, out var exact))
            return exact.FullCard;

        // Shell Masked Card Pattern (e.g. 7077841******000208)
        if (clean.Contains("*"))
        {
            string prefix = clean.Substring(0, clean.IndexOf('*'));
            string suffix = clean.Substring(clean.LastIndexOf('*') + 1);

            foreach (var (fullNum, val) in cardRegistry)
            {
                if (fullNum.StartsWith(prefix) && fullNum.EndsWith(suffix))
                {
                    return fullNum;
                }
            }
        }

        return clean;
    }

    public static string FormatPlate(string rawPlate)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rawPlate)) return string.Empty;
        string clean = Regex.Replace(rawPlate.Trim(), @"\s+", "");
        var m = PlateRegex.Match(clean);
        if (m.Success)
        {
            return $"{m.Groups[1].Value} {m.Groups[2].Value} {m.Groups[3].Value}".Trim();
        }
        return clean.ToUpperInvariant();
    }

    public static string NormalizePlate(string plate) =>
        Regex.Replace(plate ?? string.Empty, @"[\s_\-]+", "").ToUpperInvariant();

    private static string CleanStationName(string raw)
    {
        string s = Regex.Replace(raw, @"\s+", " ").Trim();
        int slashIdx = s.IndexOf('/');
        if (slashIdx > 0) s = s.Substring(0, slashIdx).Trim();
        return s;
    }

    private static string GetCol(string[] parts, Dictionary<string, int> map, string keyword)
    {
        foreach (var (k, idx) in map)
        {
            if (k.Contains(keyword) && idx < parts.Length)
            {
                return parts[idx].Trim().Replace("\"", "");
            }
        }
        return string.Empty;
    }
}

    public void OnRibbonLoad(IRibbonUI ribbon) => _ribbonUi = ribbon;

    public void OnLoadClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
        bool origUpdating = true;
        try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }

        try
        {
            var serverData = ServerSyncCommands.FetchVehicleRecords();
            if (serverData != null)
            {
                SheetMapper.PopulateWorksheet(serverData);
            }
        }
        finally
        {
            try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
        }
    }

    public void OnSaveClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            SaveEntry.Save(Events.VehicleSyncDescriptor);
        });
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // FUEL STATEMENT IMPORT CALLBACK
    // =========================================================================
    public void OnImportFuelStatementClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        try
        {
            using var openFileDialog = new OpenFileDialog
            {
                Title = "Select PETRONAS SmartPay or Shell Fleet Hub Statement",
                Filter = "All Statements (*.pdf;*.xlsx;*.xls;*.csv)|*.pdf;*.xlsx;*.xls;*.csv|PDF Invoices (*.pdf)|*.pdf|Excel / CSV (*.xlsx;*.xls;*.csv)|*.xlsx;*.xls;*.csv|All Files (*.*)|*.*",
                FilterIndex = 1,
                RestoreDirectory = true,
                CheckFileExists = true,
                Multiselect = false
            };

            if (openFileDialog.ShowDialog() != DialogResult.OK)
                return;

            string selectedFilePath = openFileDialog.FileName;
            string fileNameOnly = Path.GetFileName(selectedFilePath);

            // Queue as Excel Macro so COM worksheet manipulation runs safely on the UI thread
            ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
            {
                var (imported, skipped, totalAmount, provider) = SheetMapper.ImportFuelStatement(selectedFilePath);

                if (imported == 0 && skipped == 0)
                {
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        $"No valid fuel transactions could be parsed from:\n{fileNameOnly}\n\n" +
                        "Ensure the file is an original PDF invoice or portal export from PETRONAS SmartPay or Shell Fleet Hub.",
                        "Import Unsuccessful",
                        MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Warning
                    );
                    return;
                }

                string summary =
                    $"Provider Detected : {provider}\n" +
                    $"Source File       : {fileNameOnly}\n\n" +
                    $"  • New Logs Appended : {imported}\n" +
                    $"  • Duplicates Skipped: {skipped}\n" +
                    $"  • Net Fuel Expense  : RM {totalAmount:N2}\n\n" +
                    "All records have been written to 'OdometersTable' via TableMapper.";

                MessageBox.Show(summary, "Fuel Statement Import Complete", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
            });
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            MessageBox.Show($"Fuel import failed:\n{ex.Message}", "Import Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
        }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // ROW MANAGEMENT CALLBACKS
    // =========================================================================
    public bool GetInsertAtTopPressed(IRibbonControl control)
        => TableRowManager.IsInsertAtTopEnabled;

    public void OnToggleInsertAtTopClicked(IRibbonControl control, bool isPressed)
    {
        TableRowManager.IsInsertAtTopEnabled = isPressed;
        _ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl(control.Id);

        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
                dynamic ws = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook?.ActiveSheet;
                if (ws != null)
                {
                    TableRowManager.CleanActiveSheetTables(ws);
                }
            }
            catch { }

            SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation(
                isPressed
                    ? "Insert at TOP active: Spawns at top, redundant bottom rows trimmed."
                    : "Insert at BOTTOM active: Spawns at bottom, redundant top rows trimmed."
            );
        });
    }

    public void OnCleanEmptyRowsClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
                dynamic ws = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook?.ActiveSheet;
                if (ws == null) return;

                int tablesCleaned = TableRowManager.CleanActiveSheetTables(ws);
                SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation(
                    $"Cleaned empty rows across {tablesCleaned} table(s). Exactly 1 empty row retained."
                );
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"Failed cleaning empty rows:\n{ex.Message}", "Row Cleanup Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
        });
    }

    public void OnCleanDuplicateRowsClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
                dynamic ws = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook?.ActiveSheet;
                if (ws == null) return;

                bool origUpdating = true;
                try { origUpdating = (bool)xlApp.ScreenUpdating; xlApp.ScreenUpdating = false; } catch { }

                int totalDuplicatesRemoved = 0;
                var warnings = new System.Collections.Generic.List<string>();

                try
                {
                    SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)ws, new Action(() =>
                    {
                        foreach (dynamic table in ws.ListObjects)
                        {
                            totalDuplicatesRemoved += TableRowManager.CleanTableDuplicateRows(table, warnings);
                        }
                    }));
                }
                finally
                {
                    try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
                }

                if (totalDuplicatesRemoved > 0)
                {
                    SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation($"Removed {totalDuplicatesRemoved} duplicate row(s).");
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        $"Successfully removed {totalDuplicatesRemoved} duplicate row(s) from the active sheet.\n\n" +
                        string.Join("\n", warnings),
                        "Duplicates Removed",
                        MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Information
                    );
                }
                else
                {
                    SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation("No duplicate rows found on the active sheet.");
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        "No duplicate rows found in any tables on the active sheet.",
                        "Clean Duplicates",
                        MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Information
                    );
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show($"Failed to remove duplicate rows:\n{ex.Message}", "Cleanup Error", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
        });
    }

    public void OnDeleteTableRowClicked(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(TableRowManager.DeleteSelectedRowCommand);
    }

    public bool GetTotalRowPressed(IRibbonControl control)
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic table = xlApp.ActiveCell?.ListObject;
            if (table != null)
            {
                return (bool)table.ShowTotals;
            }
        }
        catch { }
        return false;
    }

    public void OnToggleTotalRowClicked(IRibbonControl control, bool isPressed)
    {
        ExcelAsyncUtil.QueueAsMacro(() =>
        {
            try
            {
                dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
                dynamic ws = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook?.ActiveSheet;
                if (ws == null) return;

                dynamic table = xlApp.ActiveCell?.ListObject;
                if (table == null && ws.ListObjects.Count > 0)
                {
                    table = ws.ListObjects[1]; // Fallback to first table on sheet
                }
                if (table == null) return;

                SheetProtections.ExecuteWithBypass((object)ws, new Action(() =>
                {
                    table.ShowTotals = isPressed;

                    if (isPressed)
                    {
                        // 1. MUST unlock total row so native header sorting isn't blocked
                        table.TotalsRowRange.Locked = false;

                        // 2. Configure default calculation for Cost columns
                        int colCount = (int)table.ListColumns.Count;
                        for (int c = 1; c <= colCount; c++)
                        {
                            dynamic col = table.ListColumns[c];
                            string header = col.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;

                            if (header.IndexOf("Cost", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0 ||
                                header.IndexOf("Amount", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)
                            {
                                col.TotalsCalculation = 1; // xlTotalsCalculationSum
                                try { col.Total.NumberFormat = "#,##0.00"; } catch { }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }));

                _ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl(control.Id);
                SheetOperations.ShowStatusConfirmation(isPressed ? "Total row enabled." : "Total row hidden.");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                AppLogger.Error("Failed toggling Total Row", ex);
            }
        });
    }

#if DEBUG
    public bool GetSimulateOfflinePressed(IRibbonControl control)
        => ServerEnvironment.SimulateOffline;

    public void OnToggleOfflineClicked(IRibbonControl control, bool pressed)
    {
        ServerEnvironment.SimulateOffline = pressed;
        _ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl(control.Id);
        MessageBox.Show(
            pressed
                ? "Server is now SIMULATED OFFLINE.\nAll PostgreSQL database operations will fail immediately to test offline local stashing."
                : "Server is now ONLINE.\nNormal PostgreSQL database connectivity restored.",
            "Debug Server Mode",
            MessageBoxButtons.OK,
            pressed ? MessageBoxIcon.Warning : MessageBoxIcon.Information
        );
    }

    public bool GetMasterDebugPressed(IRibbonControl control)
        => DebugDialog.DebugEnabled;

    public void OnToggleMasterDebugClicked(IRibbonControl control, bool pressed)
    {
        DebugDialog.DebugEnabled = pressed;
        _ribbonUi?.InvalidateControl(control.Id);
        MessageBox.Show(
            pressed
                ? "Debug diagnostics are now ENABLED across all operations."
                : "Debug diagnostics are now MUTED.",
            "Debug Log Switch",
            MessageBoxButtons.OK,
            pressed ? MessageBoxIcon.Information : MessageBoxIcon.Warning
        );
    }
#endif
}
