namespace SKSB_App.VehicleManager;

using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Database;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.SheetMapper;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Storage;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

internal static class ServerSyncCommands
{
    internal static bool SaveVehiclesSilent(List<VehicleRecord> vehicleList, out string errorMessage)
    {
        errorMessage = string.Empty;
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;

            // 1. Extract Auxiliary Tables from active workbook
            var workshops = new List<Workshop>();
            var insuranceCompanies = new List<InsuranceCompanyRecord>();
            var fuelCards = new List<FuelCardRecord>();
            var fuelCompanies = new List<string>();

            if (wb != null)
            {
                dynamic? wsWorkshops = SheetMapper.ResolveSheetSafely(wb, new[] { "Workshops", "Workshop" }, "Workshops");
                if (wsWorkshops != null)
                    workshops = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<Workshop>((object)wsWorkshops, "WorkshopsTable");

                dynamic? wsInsurances = SheetMapper.ResolveSheetSafely(wb, new[] { "Insurances", "Insurance" }, "Insurances");
                if (wsInsurances != null)
                    insuranceCompanies = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<InsuranceCompanyRecord>((object)wsInsurances, "InsuranceCompaniesTable");

                dynamic? wsOdometers = SheetMapper.ResolveSheetSafely(wb, new[] { "Odometers", "Odometer" }, "Odometers");
                if (wsOdometers != null)
                {
                    fuelCards = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<FuelCardRecord>((object)wsOdometers, "FuelCardsTable");

                    // Extract ONLY valid rows inside the ListObject boundary so deleted rows are excluded
                    var companyRecords = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<FuelCompanyNameRecord>((object)wsOdometers, "FuelCompanyNamesTable", "FuelCompaniesTable");
                    fuelCompanies = companyRecords.Select(c => c.FuelCompanyName).Where(n => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(n)).Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase).ToList();
                }
            }

            // 2. Persist to PostgreSQL Server
            ServerDispatcher.PersistVehicles(vehicleList, isFullBatch: true);
            ServerDispatcher.SaveAuxiliaryTables(workshops, insuranceCompanies, fuelCards, fuelCompanies);

            // 3. Persist local offline snapshots
            OfflineSnapshot.Save(vehicleList, writeToDatabase: false);
            OfflineSnapshot.Save(workshops, writeToDatabase: false);
            OfflineSnapshot.Save(insuranceCompanies, writeToDatabase: false);
            OfflineSnapshot.Save(fuelCards, writeToDatabase: false);

            return true;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            errorMessage = ex.Message;
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[ServerSyncCommands] SaveVehiclesSilent Error: {ex.Message}");
            return false;
        }
    }

    internal static List<VehicleRecord> FetchVehicleRecords()
    {
        using var _ = SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics.AppLogger.Scope("VehicleManager.FetchVehicleRecords");

        try
        {
            var db = new PostgreSqlService();

            // 1. Fetch Vehicle Master
            const string sqlMaster = "SELECT * FROM vehicle_master ORDER BY vehicle_plate;";
            var rawVehicles = db.Query<dynamic>(sqlMaster).ToList();
            SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics.AppLogger.Info($"[PostgreSQL Query] vehicle_master returned {rawVehicles.Count} record(s).");

            if (rawVehicles.Any())
            {
                var serverVehicles = new List<VehicleRecord>();

                foreach (var v in rawVehicles)
                {
                    string plate = (string)v.vehicle_plate;

                    var target = new VehicleRecord
                    {
                        VehiclePlate = plate,
                        VehicleStatus = Enum.TryParse<VehicleStatus>((string)v.status, true, out var vs) ? vs : VehicleStatus.Active,
                        VehicleDetail = new VehicleDetail
                        {
                            VIN = (string)(v.vin ?? string.Empty),
                            Type = Enum.TryParse<VehicleType>((string)v.vehicle_type, true, out var vt) ? vt : VehicleType.None,
                            Brand = (string)(v.brand ?? string.Empty),
                            Model = (string)(v.model ?? string.Empty),
                            RoadTaxExpiry = v.road_tax_expiry != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime((DateTime)v.road_tax_expiry) : default,
                            InspectionExpiry = v.inspection_expiry != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime((DateTime)v.inspection_expiry) : default,
                            CurrentOdometer = v.current_odometer != null ? Convert.ToInt32(v.current_odometer) : 0,
                            BoughtDate = v.bought_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime((DateTime)v.bought_date) : null,
                            SoldDate = v.sold_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime((DateTime)v.sold_date) : null,
                            BoughtPrice = v.bought_price != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(v.bought_price) : 0.00m,
                            SoldPrice = v.sold_price != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(v.sold_price) : 0.00m
                        },
                        Maintenances = new List<Maintenance>(),
                        RoadTaxes = new List<RoadTax>(),
                        Inspections = new List<Inspection>(),
                        Permits = new List<VehiclePermit>(),
                        Insurances = new List<VehicleInsurance>(),
                        OdometerLogs = new List<OdometerLog>()
                    };

                    serverVehicles.Add(target);
                }

                var maintMap = serverVehicles.ToDictionary(v => v.VehiclePlate, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

                // 2. Fetch Maintenances
                try
                {
                    const string sqlMaintenance = "SELECT * FROM vehicle_maintenance ORDER BY maintenance_id;";
                    var rawMaintenances = db.Query<dynamic>(sqlMaintenance).ToList();
                    SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics.AppLogger.Info($"[PostgreSQL Query] vehicle_maintenance returned {rawMaintenances.Count} record(s).");

                    foreach (var m in rawMaintenances)
                    {
                        string mPlate = (string)m.vehicle_plate;
                        if (maintMap.TryGetValue(mPlate, out var parentVehicle))
                        {
                            Enum.TryParse<ServiceType>((string)(m.service_type ?? "None"), true, out var sType);
                            Enum.TryParse<MaintenanceStatus>((string)(m.status ?? "In_Progress"), true, out var mStatus);

                            parentVehicle.Maintenances.Add(new Maintenance
                            {
                                MaintenanceID = Convert.ToInt64(m.maintenance_id),
                                VehiclePlate = mPlate,
                                ServiceType = sType,
                                DateIn = m.date_in != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime(Convert.ToDateTime(m.date_in)) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today),
                                EstimatedReturn = m.estimated_return != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime(Convert.ToDateTime(m.estimated_return)) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today),
                                DateCompleted = m.date_completed != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime(Convert.ToDateTime(m.date_completed)) : null,
                                OdometerIn = m.odometer_in != null ? Convert.ToInt32(m.odometer_in) : 0,
                                OdometerOut = m.odometer_out != null ? Convert.ToInt32(m.odometer_out) : 0,
                                RepairCost = m.repair_cost != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(m.repair_cost) : 0.00m,
                                WorkshopName = m.workshop_name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty,
                                MaintenanceStatus = mStatus,
                                Remarks = m.remarks?.ToString() ?? string.Empty,

                                // ✅ Load all 11 breakdown costs:
                                TyreCost = m.tyre_cost != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(m.tyre_cost) : 0.00m,
                                BrakeCost = m.brake_cost != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(m.brake_cost) : 0.00m,
                                ClutchCost = m.clutch_cost != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(m.clutch_cost) : 0.00m,
                                ServiceCost = m.service_cost != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(m.service_cost) : 0.00m,
                                FuelPumpCost = m.fuel_pump_cost != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(m.fuel_pump_cost) : 0.00m,
                                AircondCost = m.aircond_cost != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(m.aircond_cost) : 0.00m,
                                WiringCost = m.wiring_cost != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(m.wiring_cost) : 0.00m,
                                BatteryCost = m.battery_cost != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(m.battery_cost) : 0.00m,
                                TowingCost = m.towing_cost != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(m.towing_cost) : 0.00m,
                                CushionCost = m.cushion_cost != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(m.cushion_cost) : 0.00m,
                                PartsCost = m.parts_cost != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(m.parts_cost) : 0.00m
                            });
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception exMaint)
                {
                    SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics.AppLogger.Error("[PostgreSQL Query] vehicle_maintenance failed", exMaint);
                }

                // 3. Fetch Road Taxes
                try
                {
                    const string sqlRt = "SELECT * FROM vehicle_road_taxes ORDER BY road_tax_id;";
                    var rawRt = db.Query<dynamic>(sqlRt).ToList();
                    foreach (var r in rawRt)
                    {
                        string p = (string)r.vehicle_plate;
                        if (maintMap.TryGetValue(p, out var pv))
                        {
                            Enum.TryParse<RoadTaxStatus>((string)(r.status ?? "Active"), true, out var rStatus);
                            pv.RoadTaxes.Add(new RoadTax
                            {
                                RoadTaxID = Convert.ToInt64(r.road_tax_id),
                                VehiclePlate = p,
                                RenewDate = r.renew_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime(Convert.ToDateTime(r.renew_date)) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today),
                                ExpiryDate = r.expiry_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime(Convert.ToDateTime(r.expiry_date)) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today.AddYears(1)),
                                Amount = r.amount != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(r.amount) : 0.00m,
                                Status = rStatus,
                                Remarks = r.remarks?.ToString() ?? string.Empty
                            });
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception exRt) { AppLogger.Error("[PostgreSQL Query] vehicle_road_taxes failed", exRt); }

                // 4. Fetch Inspections
                try
                {
                    const string sqlInsp = "SELECT * FROM vehicle_inspections ORDER BY inspection_id;";
                    var rawInsp = db.Query<dynamic>(sqlInsp).ToList();
                    foreach (var i in rawInsp)
                    {
                        string p = (string)i.vehicle_plate;
                        if (maintMap.TryGetValue(p, out var pv))
                        {
                            Enum.TryParse<InspectionStatus>((string)(i.status ?? "Scheduled"), true, out var iStatus);
                            pv.Inspections.Add(new Inspection
                            {
                                InspectionID = Convert.ToInt64(i.inspection_id),
                                VehiclePlate = p,
                                ScheduleDateIn = i.schedule_date_in != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime(Convert.ToDateTime(i.schedule_date_in)) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today),
                                DateOut = i.date_out != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime(Convert.ToDateTime(i.date_out)) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today),
                                ExpiryDate = i.expiry_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime(Convert.ToDateTime(i.expiry_date)) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today.AddMonths(6)),
                                Amount = i.amount != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(i.amount) : 0.00m,
                                Status = iStatus,
                                Address = i.address?.ToString() ?? string.Empty,
                                Remarks = i.remarks?.ToString() ?? string.Empty
                            });
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception exInsp) { AppLogger.Error("[PostgreSQL Query] vehicle_inspections failed", exInsp); }

                // 5. Fetch Permits
                try
                {
                    const string sqlPermits = "SELECT * FROM vehicle_permits ORDER BY permit_id;";
                    var rawPermits = db.Query<dynamic>(sqlPermits).ToList();
                    foreach (var pRec in rawPermits)
                    {
                        string p = (string)pRec.vehicle_plate;
                        if (maintMap.TryGetValue(p, out var pv))
                        {
                            Enum.TryParse<VehiclePermitType>((string)(pRec.permit_type ?? "APAD_Carrier_A"), true, out var pType);
                            Enum.TryParse<VehiclePermitStatus>((string)(pRec.status ?? "Active"), true, out var pStatus);
                            pv.Permits.Add(new VehiclePermit
                            {
                                PermitID = Convert.ToInt64(pRec.permit_id),
                                VehiclePlate = p,
                                PermitType = pType,
                                RenewDate = pRec.renew_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime(Convert.ToDateTime(pRec.renew_date)) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today),
                                ExpiryDate = pRec.expiry_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime(Convert.ToDateTime(pRec.expiry_date)) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today.AddYears(1)),
                                Amount = pRec.amount != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(pRec.amount) : 0.00m,
                                Status = pStatus,
                                Remarks = pRec.remarks?.ToString() ?? string.Empty
                            });
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception exPermits) { AppLogger.Error("[PostgreSQL Query] vehicle_permits failed", exPermits); }

                // 6. Fetch Insurances
                try
                {
                    const string sqlIns = "SELECT * FROM vehicle_insurances ORDER BY insurance_id;";
                    var rawIns = db.Query<dynamic>(sqlIns).ToList();
                    foreach (var ins in rawIns)
                    {
                        string p = (string)ins.vehicle_plate;
                        if (maintMap.TryGetValue(p, out var pv))
                        {
                            Enum.TryParse<InsuranceType>((string)(ins.insurance_type ?? "Comprehensive"), true, out var insType);
                            Enum.TryParse<InsuranceStatus>((string)(ins.status ?? "Active"), true, out var insStatus);
                            pv.Insurances.Add(new VehicleInsurance
                            {
                                InsuranceID = Convert.ToInt64(ins.insurance_id),
                                VehiclePlate = p,
                                InsuranceCompany = ins.insurance_company?.ToString() ?? string.Empty,
                                InsuranceType = insType,
                                RenewDate = ins.renew_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime(Convert.ToDateTime(ins.renew_date)) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today),
                                ExpiryDate = ins.expiry_date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime(Convert.ToDateTime(ins.expiry_date)) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today.AddYears(1)),
                                Amount = ins.amount != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(ins.amount) : 0.00m,
                                Status = insStatus,
                                Remarks = ins.remarks?.ToString() ?? string.Empty,
                                InsuranceNumber = ins.insurance_number?.ToString() ?? string.Empty // <-- Added
                            });
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception exIns) { AppLogger.Error("[PostgreSQL Query] vehicle_insurances failed", exIns); }

                // 7. Fetch Odometers
                try
                {
                    const string sqlOdo = "SELECT * FROM vehicle_odometers ORDER BY odometer_id;";
                    var rawOdo = db.Query<dynamic>(sqlOdo).ToList();
                    foreach (var o in rawOdo)
                    {
                        string p = (string)o.vehicle_plate;
                        if (maintMap.TryGetValue(p, out var pv))
                        {
                            // Map staff_id cleanly (supports both legacy driven_by fallback and new staff_id)
                            long sId = 0;
                            if (o.staff_id != null)
                            {
                                long.TryParse(o.staff_id.ToString(), out sId);
                            }
                            else if (o.driven_by != null)
                            {
                                long.TryParse(o.driven_by.ToString(), out sId);
                            }

                            pv.OdometerLogs.Add(new OdometerLog
                            {
                                OdometerID = Convert.ToInt64(o.odometer_id),
                                Date = o.date != null ? DateOnly.FromDateTime(Convert.ToDateTime(o.date)) : DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Today),
                                VehiclePlate = p,
                                StaffID = sId,
                                Project = o.project?.ToString() ?? string.Empty,
                                OdometerBefore = o.odometer_before != null ? Convert.ToInt32(o.odometer_before) : 0,
                                CurrentOdometer = o.current_odometer != null ? Convert.ToInt32(o.current_odometer) : 0,
                                FuelCardNumber = o.fuel_card_number?.ToString() ?? string.Empty,
                                FuelCompany = o.fuel_company?.ToString() ?? string.Empty,
                                StationName = o.station_name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty,
                                ReceiptNumber = o.receipt_number?.ToString() ?? string.Empty,
                                Amount = o.amount != null ? Convert.ToDecimal(o.amount) : 0.00m,
                                Remarks = o.remarks?.ToString() ?? string.Empty
                            });
                        }
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception exOdo) { AppLogger.Error("[PostgreSQL Query] vehicle_odometers failed", exOdo); }

                // Summary of child records
                int totalRoadTaxes = serverVehicles.Sum(v => v.RoadTaxes.Count);
                int totalInspections = serverVehicles.Sum(v => v.Inspections.Count);
                int totalPermits = serverVehicles.Sum(v => v.Permits.Count);
                int totalInsurances = serverVehicles.Sum(v => v.Insurances.Count);
                int totalOdometers = serverVehicles.Sum(v => v.OdometerLogs.Count);

                SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics.AppLogger.Info(
                    $"[Server Assembly Summary] Vehicles={serverVehicles.Count}, Maintenances={serverVehicles.Sum(v => v.Maintenances.Count)}, " +
                    $"RoadTaxes={totalRoadTaxes}, Inspections={totalInspections}, Permits={totalPermits}, Insurances={totalInsurances}, Odometers={totalOdometers}");

                try
                {
                    OfflineSnapshot.Save(serverVehicles, writeToDatabase: false);
                }
                catch { }

                return serverVehicles;
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics.AppLogger.Error("FetchVehicleRecords server query failed", ex);
        }

        var fallback = OfflineSnapshot.Load<List<VehicleRecord>>() ?? new List<VehicleRecord>();
        SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics.AppLogger.Warn($"[Server Offline Fallback] Loaded {fallback.Count} vehicle records from local offline snapshot.");
        return fallback;
    }

    internal static List<Workshop> FetchWorkshops()
    {
        try
        {
            var db = new PostgreSqlService();
            const string sql = @"
                SELECT 
                    workshop_id AS WorkshopID, 
                    workshop_name AS WorkshopName, 
                    workshop_address AS WorkshopAddress, 
                    workshop_owner AS WorkshopOwner, 
                    primary_contact AS PrimaryContact, 
                    secondary_contact AS SecondaryContact 
                FROM vehicle_workshops 
                ORDER BY workshop_id;";
            var list = db.Query<Workshop>(sql).ToList();
            if (list.Any()) return list;
        }
        catch { }

        return OfflineSnapshot.Load<List<Workshop>>() ?? new List<Workshop>();
    }

    internal static List<InsuranceCompanyRecord> FetchInsuranceCompanies()
    {
        try
        {
            var db = new PostgreSqlService();
            const string sql = @"
                SELECT 
                    insurance_company AS InsuranceCompany, 
                    company_contact_number AS CompanyContactNumber, 
                    company_address AS CompanyAddress, 
                    insurance_agent AS InsuranceAgent, 
                    agent_contact_number AS AgentContactNumber 
                FROM vehicle_insurance_companies 
                ORDER BY insurance_company;";
            var list = db.Query<InsuranceCompanyRecord>(sql).ToList();
            if (list.Any()) return list;
        }
        catch { }

        return OfflineSnapshot.Load<List<InsuranceCompanyRecord>>() ?? new List<InsuranceCompanyRecord>();
    }

    internal static List<FuelCardRecord> FetchFuelCards()
    {
        try
        {
            var db = new PostgreSqlService();
            const string sql = @"
                SELECT 
                    fuel_card_number AS FuelCardNumber, 
                    fuel_company_name AS FuelCompanyName, 
                    card_owner_id AS CardOwnerID, 
                    card_owner_name AS CardOwnerName 
                FROM vehicle_fuel_cards 
                ORDER BY fuel_card_number;";
            var list = db.Query<FuelCardRecord>(sql).ToList();
            if (list.Any()) return list;
        }
        catch { }

        return OfflineSnapshot.Load<List<FuelCardRecord>>() ?? new List<FuelCardRecord>();
    }

    internal static List<string> FetchFuelCompanies()
    {
        try
        {
            var db = new PostgreSqlService();
            const string sql = "SELECT fuel_company_name FROM lookup_fuel_companies ORDER BY fuel_company_name;";
            var list = db.Query<string>(sql).Where(s => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(s)).Distinct().ToList();
            return list; // Return exact records from database (even if empty)
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[FetchFuelCompanies DB Error] {ex.Message}");
        }

        var cached = OfflineSnapshot.Load<List<FuelCompanyNameRecord>>();
        return cached?.Select(c => c.FuelCompanyName).Where(n => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(n)).ToList() ?? new List<string>();
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.VehicleManager;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.Integration.Database;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.SheetMapper;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Storage;

internal static partial class SheetMapper
{
    private static (List<string> Errors, List<string> Warnings) ExtractAndPersistAuxiliaryTables(object wbObj)
    {
        var errors = new List<string>();
        var warnings = new List<string>();
        dynamic wb = wbObj;

        List<Workshop> workshops = new();
        dynamic? wsWorkshop = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, WorkshopAliases, "Workshops");
        if (wsWorkshop != null)
        {
            workshops = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<Workshop>((object)wsWorkshop, "WorkshopsTable");
        }

        List<InsuranceCompanyRecord> insuranceCompanies = new();
        dynamic? wsIns = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, InsuranceAliases, "Insurances");
        if (wsIns != null)
        {
            insuranceCompanies = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<InsuranceCompanyRecord>((object)wsIns, "InsuranceCompaniesTable");
        }

        List<FuelCardRecord> fuelCards = new();
        List<FuelCompanyNameRecord> fuelCompanyRecords = new();
        dynamic? wsOdo = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, OdometerAliases, "Odometers");
        if (wsOdo != null)
        {
            fuelCards = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<FuelCardRecord>((object)wsOdo, "FuelCardsTable");
            fuelCompanyRecords = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<FuelCompanyNameRecord>((object)wsOdo, "FuelCompanyNamesTable", "FuelCompaniesTable");
        }

        var fuelCompanies = fuelCompanyRecords
            .Select(c => c.FuelCompanyName)
            .Where(n => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(n))
            .Select(n => n.Trim())
            .Distinct(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            .ToList();

        // Relational Check: Warn and auto-restore fuel company if assigned to an active card
        var activeFuelCompanySet = new HashSet<string>(
            fuelCompanies.Where(n => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(n)).Select(n => n.Trim()),
            StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase
        );

        var restoredCompanies = new HashSet<string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        foreach (var card in fuelCards)
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(card.FuelCompanyName))
            {
                string compName = card.FuelCompanyName.Trim();
                if (!activeFuelCompanySet.Contains(compName))
                {
                    if (restoredCompanies.Add(compName))
                    {
                        warnings.Add($"Fuel company '{compName}' is still assigned to Fuel Card '{card.FuelCardNumber}'. Deletion was reverted and '{compName}' has been automatically restored.");
                        fuelCompanies.Add(compName);
                        activeFuelCompanySet.Add(compName);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Restore onto sheet if changes occurred
        if (restoredCompanies.Count > 0 && wsOdo != null)
        {
            SheetMapperHelper.SyncReferenceTable((object)wb, "FuelCompanyNamesTable", "", fuelCompanies);
        }

        // Persist Auxiliary Tables
        try
        {
            OfflineSnapshot.Save(workshops, writeToDatabase: false);
            OfflineSnapshot.Save(insuranceCompanies, writeToDatabase: false);
            OfflineSnapshot.Save(fuelCards, writeToDatabase: false);
            OfflineSnapshot.Save(fuelCompanyRecords, writeToDatabase: false);

            ServerDispatcher.SaveAuxiliaryTables(
                workshops,
                insuranceCompanies,
                fuelCards,
                fuelCompanies
            );
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            AppLogger.Error("Failed persisting auxiliary tables to database", ex);
            errors.Add($"Failed saving auxiliary tables to database: {ex.Message}");
        }

        return (errors, warnings);
    }

    private static void PopulateAuxiliaryTables(object wbObj)
    {
        dynamic wb = wbObj;

        // 1. WorkshopsTable
        dynamic? wsWorkshop = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, WorkshopAliases, "Workshops");
        if (wsWorkshop != null)
        {
            try { wsWorkshop.Unprotect("SKSB_Internal_Guard"); } catch { }
            var workshops = ServerSyncCommands.FetchWorkshops();
            TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<Workshop>((object)wsWorkshop, "WorkshopsTable", workshops);
            SheetProtections.ApplyStyleDrivenProtection(wsWorkshop);
        }

        // 2. InsuranceCompaniesTable
        dynamic? wsIns = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, InsuranceAliases, "Insurances");
        if (wsIns != null)
        {
            try { wsIns.Unprotect("SKSB_Internal_Guard"); } catch { }
            var insuranceCompanies = ServerSyncCommands.FetchInsuranceCompanies();
            TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<InsuranceCompanyRecord>((object)wsIns, "InsuranceCompaniesTable", insuranceCompanies);
            SheetProtections.ApplyStyleDrivenProtection(wsIns);
        }

        // 3. FuelCardsTable & FuelCompanyNamesTable
        dynamic? wsOdo = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, OdometerAliases, "Odometers");
        if (wsOdo != null)
        {
            try { wsOdo.Unprotect("SKSB_Internal_Guard"); } catch { }
            var fuelCards = ServerSyncCommands.FetchFuelCards();
            TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<FuelCardRecord>((object)wsOdo, "FuelCardsTable", fuelCards);

            var fuelCompanies = ServerSyncCommands.FetchFuelCompanies();
            SheetMapperHelper.SyncReferenceTable((object)wb, "FuelCompanyNamesTable", "", fuelCompanies);
            SheetProtections.ApplyStyleDrivenProtection(wsOdo);
        }
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.VehicleManager;

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.SheetMapper;

internal static partial class SheetMapper
{
    private static void ExtractChildRecords(object wbObj, List<VehicleRecord> vehicles)
    {
        dynamic wb = wbObj;
        var vehicleMap = vehicles
            .Where(v => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v.VehiclePlate))
            .GroupBy(v => v.VehiclePlate, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
            .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.First(), StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        // 1. Maintenances
        dynamic? wsMaint = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, MaintenanceAliases, "Maintenances");
        if (wsMaint != null)
        {
            List<Maintenance> maintenances = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<Maintenance>((object)wsMaint, "MaintenancesTable");
            foreach (var m in maintenances)
            {
                if (vehicleMap.TryGetValue(m.VehiclePlate, out VehicleRecord? v) && v != null)
                    v.Maintenances.Add(m);
            }
        }

        // 2. Road Taxes
        dynamic? wsRt = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, RoadTaxAliases, "Road Taxes");
        if (wsRt != null)
        {
            List<RoadTax> roadTaxes = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<RoadTax>((object)wsRt, "RoadTaxesTable");
            foreach (var rt in roadTaxes)
            {
                if (vehicleMap.TryGetValue(rt.VehiclePlate, out VehicleRecord? v) && v != null)
                    v.RoadTaxes.Add(rt);
            }
        }

        // 3. Inspections
        dynamic? wsInsp = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, InspectionAliases, "Inspections");
        if (wsInsp != null)
        {
            List<Inspection> inspections = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<Inspection>((object)wsInsp, "InspectionsTable");
            foreach (var insp in inspections)
            {
                if (vehicleMap.TryGetValue(insp.VehiclePlate, out VehicleRecord? v) && v != null)
                    v.Inspections.Add(insp);
            }
        }

        // 4. Permits
        dynamic? wsPermit = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, PermitAliases, "Permits");
        if (wsPermit != null)
        {
            List<VehiclePermit> permits = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<VehiclePermit>((object)wsPermit, "PermitsTable");
            foreach (var p in permits)
            {
                if (vehicleMap.TryGetValue(p.VehiclePlate, out VehicleRecord? v) && v != null)
                    v.Permits.Add(p);
            }
        }

        // 5. Insurances
        dynamic? wsIns = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, InsuranceAliases, "Insurances");
        if (wsIns != null)
        {
            List<VehicleInsurance> insurances = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<VehicleInsurance>((object)wsIns, "InsurancesTable");
            foreach (var ins in insurances)
            {
                if (vehicleMap.TryGetValue(ins.VehiclePlate, out VehicleRecord? v) && v != null)
                    v.Insurances.Add(ins);
            }
        }

        // 6. OdometersTable
        dynamic? wsOdo = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, OdometerAliases, "Odometers");
        if (wsOdo != null)
        {
            List<OdometerLog> odoLogs = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<OdometerLog>((object)wsOdo, "OdometersTable");
            foreach (var o in odoLogs)
            {
                if (vehicleMap.TryGetValue(o.VehiclePlate, out VehicleRecord? v) && v != null)
                    v.OdometerLogs.Add(o);
            }
        }
    }

    private static void PopulateChildTables(object wbObj, List<VehicleRecord> records)
    {
        dynamic wb = wbObj;

        // 1. MaintenancesTable
        dynamic? wsMaint = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, MaintenanceAliases, "Maintenances");
        if (wsMaint != null)
        {
            try { wsMaint.Unprotect("SKSB_Internal_Guard"); } catch { }
            var allMaintenances = records.SelectMany(v => v.Maintenances.Select(m =>
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(m.VehiclePlate)) m.VehiclePlate = v.VehiclePlate;
                return m;
            })).ToList();

            TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<Maintenance>((object)wsMaint, allMaintenances, "MaintenancesTable");
            SheetProtections.ApplyStyleDrivenProtection(wsMaint);
        }

        // 2. RoadTaxesTable
        dynamic? wsRt = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, RoadTaxAliases, "Road Taxes");
        if (wsRt != null)
        {
            try { wsRt.Unprotect("SKSB_Internal_Guard"); } catch { }
            var allRoadTaxes = records.SelectMany(v => v.RoadTaxes.Select(rt =>
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rt.VehiclePlate)) rt.VehiclePlate = v.VehiclePlate;
                return rt;
            })).ToList();

            TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<RoadTax>((object)wsRt, allRoadTaxes, "RoadTaxesTable");
            SheetProtections.ApplyStyleDrivenProtection(wsRt);
        }

        // 3. InspectionsTable
        dynamic? wsInsp = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, InspectionAliases, "Inspections");
        if (wsInsp != null)
        {
            try { wsInsp.Unprotect("SKSB_Internal_Guard"); } catch { }
            var allInspections = records.SelectMany(v => v.Inspections.Select(insp =>
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(insp.VehiclePlate)) insp.VehiclePlate = v.VehiclePlate;
                return insp;
            })).ToList();

            TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<Inspection>((object)wsInsp, allInspections, "InspectionsTable");
            SheetProtections.ApplyStyleDrivenProtection(wsInsp);
        }

        // 4. PermitsTable
        dynamic? wsPermit = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, PermitAliases, "Permits");
        if (wsPermit != null)
        {
            try { wsPermit.Unprotect("SKSB_Internal_Guard"); } catch { }
            var allPermits = records.SelectMany(v => v.Permits.Select(p =>
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(p.VehiclePlate)) p.VehiclePlate = v.VehiclePlate;
                return p;
            })).ToList();

            TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<VehiclePermit>((object)wsPermit, allPermits, "PermitsTable");
            SheetProtections.ApplyStyleDrivenProtection(wsPermit);
        }

        // 5. InsurancesTable
        dynamic? wsIns = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, InsuranceAliases, "Insurances");
        if (wsIns != null)
        {
            try { wsIns.Unprotect("SKSB_Internal_Guard"); } catch { }
            var allInsurances = records.SelectMany(v => v.Insurances.Select(ins =>
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ins.VehiclePlate)) ins.VehiclePlate = v.VehiclePlate;
                return ins;
            })).ToList();

            TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<VehicleInsurance>((object)wsIns, allInsurances, "InsurancesTable");
            SheetProtections.ApplyStyleDrivenProtection(wsIns);
        }

        // 6. OdometersTable
        dynamic? wsOdo = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, OdometerAliases, "Odometers");
        if (wsOdo != null)
        {
            try { wsOdo.Unprotect("SKSB_Internal_Guard"); } catch { }
            var allOdoLogs = records.SelectMany(v => v.OdometerLogs.Select(o =>
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(o.VehiclePlate)) o.VehiclePlate = v.VehiclePlate;
                return o;
            })).ToList();

            TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<OdometerLog>((object)wsOdo, allOdoLogs, "OdometersTable");
            SheetProtections.ApplyStyleDrivenProtection(wsOdo);
        }
    }
}

namespace SKSB_App.VehicleManager;

using ExcelDna.Integration;
using SKSB_App.Core.Diagnostics;
using SKSB_App.Core.RuntimeUI.RuleEngine;
using SKSB_App.Core.Types;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookOpen.Sheet;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.SheetMapper;
using SKSB_App.Core.WorkbookSave.Validation;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;

internal static partial class SheetMapper
{
    public const string MasterSheetName = "All Vehicles";
    public const string MasterTableName = "AllVehiclesTable";

    private static readonly string[] MasterSheetAliases = { "All Vehicles", "AllVehicles", "Vehicle_Master", "Vehicles" };
    private static readonly string[] MaintenanceAliases = { "Maintenances", "Maintenance", "Vehicle_Maintenance" };
    private static readonly string[] WorkshopAliases = { "Workshops", "Workshop" };
    private static readonly string[] RoadTaxAliases = { "Road Taxes", "RoadTaxes", "Road Tax" };
    private static readonly string[] InspectionAliases = { "Inspections", "Inspection" };
    private static readonly string[] PermitAliases = { "Permits", "Permit" };
    private static readonly string[] InsuranceAliases = { "Insurances", "Insurance" };
    private static readonly string[] OdometerAliases = { "Odometers", "Odometer", "Fuel Logs", "Fuel" };

    // =========================================================================
    // 1. MASTER EXTRACTION PIPELINE
    // =========================================================================
    internal static (List<VehicleRecord>? Data, List<string> Errors, List<string> Warnings) ExtractVehicleRecordsWithDiagnostics()
    {
        try
        {
            dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
            dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (wb == null) return (null, new List<string> { "No active workbook found." }, new List<string>());

            dynamic? masterWs = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, MasterSheetAliases, MasterSheetName);
            if (masterWs == null)
            {
                return (null, new List<string> { $"Worksheet '{MasterSheetName}' was not found in workbook." }, new List<string>());
            }

            // 1. Extract Master Vehicle Records
            List<VehicleRecord> vehicles = TableMapper.ExtractFromTable<VehicleRecord>((object)masterWs, MasterTableName);

            // 2. Extract Child Collections
            ExtractChildRecords((object)wb, vehicles);

            // 3. Extract & Validate Auxiliary Tables
            var (auxErrors, auxWarnings) = ExtractAndPersistAuxiliaryTables((object)wb);

            // 4. Validate All Tables Across All Sheets
            var (errors, warnings) = RulesExtractor.ValidateAllWorkbookTables((object)wb);
            errors.AddRange(auxErrors);
            warnings.AddRange(auxWarnings);

            // 5. Primary Key Integrity Check
            var plateGroups = vehicles
                .Where(v => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(v.VehiclePlate))
                .GroupBy(v => v.VehiclePlate, StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            foreach (var group in plateGroups)
            {
                if (group.Count() > 1)
                {
                    errors.Add($"Duplicate Vehicle Plate '{group.Key}' detected {group.Count()} times in master table.");
                }
            }

            return (vehicles, errors, warnings);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            return (null, new List<string> { $"Failed extracting vehicle records: {ex.Message}" }, new List<string>());
        }
    }

    internal static List<VehicleRecord> ExtractVehicleRecords()
    {
        var (data, _, _) = ExtractVehicleRecordsWithDiagnostics();
        return data ?? new List<VehicleRecord>();
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 2. MASTER POPULATE PIPELINE
    // =========================================================================
    internal static void PopulateWorksheet(List<VehicleRecord> records)
    {
        if (records == null) return;

        dynamic xlApp = ExcelDnaUtil.Application;
        bool origEvents = true;
        bool origUpdating = true;
        try { origEvents = (bool)xlApp.EnableEvents; xlApp.EnableEvents = false; } catch { }

        try
        {
            dynamic wb = xlApp.ActiveWorkbook;
            if (wb == null) return;

            // 1. Populate Master Table
            dynamic? masterWs = ResolveSheetSafely((object)wb, MasterSheetAliases, MasterSheetName);
            if (masterWs != null)
            {
                try { masterWs.Unprotect("SKSB_Internal_Guard"); } catch { }

                if (masterWs.Cells[1, 2].Value2 == null)
                    masterWs.Cells[1, 2].Value2 = DateTime.Today.ToOADate();

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(masterWs.Cells[1, 3].Value2?.ToString()))
                    masterWs.Cells[1, 3].Value2 = "All_Time";

                TableMapper.PopulateTableSafe<VehicleRecord>((object)masterWs, MasterTableName, records);
                SheetProtections.ApplyStyleDrivenProtection(masterWs);
            }

            // 2. Populate Child Tables
            PopulateChildTables((object)wb, records);

            // 3. Populate Auxiliary Tables
            PopulateAuxiliaryTables((object)wb);

            try
            {
                xlApp.CalculateFull();
            }
            catch (Exception exCalc)
            {
                System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[PopulateWorksheet Calculation Warning]: {exCalc.Message}");
            }

            string[] tableEvalOrder = new[]
        {
            "AllVehiclesTable",
            "WorkshopsTable",
            "InsuranceCompaniesTable",
            "FuelCardsTable",
            "MaintenancesTable",
            "RoadTaxesTable",
            "InspectionsTable",
            "PermitsTable",
            "InsurancesTable",
            "OdometersTable",
            "AllVehiclesTable"
        };

            foreach (string tName in tableEvalOrder)
            {
                foreach (dynamic sheet in wb.Worksheets)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        dynamic tbl = sheet.ListObjects[tName];
                        if (tbl != null)
                        {
                            RuleEvaluator.EvaluateAndHealTable(sheet, tbl);
                            break; // Found the table, move to the next one
                        }
                    }
                    catch { } // Table not on this specific sheet, loop continues
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[VehicleSheetMapper.PopulateWorksheet Error] {ex.Message}");
        }
        finally
        {
            try { xlApp.EnableEvents = origEvents; } catch { }
            try { xlApp.ScreenUpdating = origUpdating; } catch { }
        }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // 3. SHARED UTILITIES
    // =========================================================================
    internal static dynamic? ResolveSheetSafely(object wbObj, string[] aliases, string defaultName)
    {
        dynamic wb = wbObj;
        string resolvedName = SheetOperations.ResolveSheetName(aliases, defaultName);
        try
        {
            dynamic s = wb.Sheets[resolvedName];
            if (s != null) return s;
        }
        catch { }

        foreach (dynamic ws in wb.Sheets)
        {
            string name = ws.Name?.ToString() ?? string.Empty;
            if (aliases.Any(a => string.Equals(a, name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
            {
                return ws;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    internal static string NormalizePlate(string plate) =>
        Regex.Replace(plate ?? string.Empty, @"[\s_\-]+", "").ToUpperInvariant();

    internal static string FormatPlate(string raw)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(raw)) return string.Empty;
        string clean = NormalizePlate(raw);
        var m = Regex.Match(clean, @"^([A-Z]{1,3})(\d{1,4})([A-Z]?)$");
        if (m.Success)
        {
            return $"{m.Groups[1].Value} {m.Groups[2].Value} {m.Groups[3].Value}".Trim();
        }
        return clean;
    }

    internal static T GetProp<T>(object target, params string[] propNames)
    {
        if (target == null) return default!;
        Type type = target.GetType();
        foreach (var name in propNames)
        {
            var p = type.GetProperty(name, BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.IgnoreCase);
            if (p != null)
            {
                object? val = p.GetValue(target);
                if (val != null)
                {
                    if (val is T directVal) return directVal;
                    Type tType = Nullable.GetUnderlyingType(typeof(T)) ?? typeof(T);
                    if (tType == typeof(DateOnly) && val is DateTime dtVal)
                        return (T)(object)DateOnly.FromDateTime(dtVal);
                    try { return (T)Convert.ChangeType(val, tType); }
                    catch { }
                }
            }
        }
        return default!;
    }

    internal static void SetProp(object target, string propName, object? value)
    {
        if (target == null || value == null) return;
        var p = target.GetType().GetProperty(propName, BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.IgnoreCase);
        if (p != null && p.CanWrite)
        {
            try
            {
                Type uType = Nullable.GetUnderlyingType(p.PropertyType) ?? p.PropertyType;
                if (uType == typeof(DateOnly) && value is DateTime dt)
                    p.SetValue(target, DateOnly.FromDateTime(dt));
                else if (uType == typeof(DateOnly) && value is DateOnly dOnly)
                    p.SetValue(target, dOnly);
                else if (uType == typeof(TimeOnly) && value is string st && TimeOnly.TryParse(st, out var to))
                    p.SetValue(target, to);
                else if (uType.IsEnum)
                {
                    if (Enum.TryParse(uType, value.ToString(), true, out var ev))
                        p.SetValue(target, ev);
                }
                else if (uType.IsInstanceOfType(value))
                    p.SetValue(target, value);
                else
                {
                    p.SetValue(target, Convert.ChangeType(value, uType));
                }
            }
            catch { }
        }
    }

#if DEBUG
    private static void ShowPopulateDiagnostics(object wbObj, List<VehicleRecord> records)
    {
        try
        {
            if (!DebugDialog.DebugEnabled) return;

            dynamic wb = wbObj;
            var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            sb.AppendLine("=== VEHICLE MANAGER POPULATE AUDIT ===");
            sb.AppendLine($"Total Vehicle Profiles Loaded: {records.Count}");
            sb.AppendLine();

            string[] inspectTables =
            {
                "AllVehiclesTable", "MaintenancesTable", "WorkshopsTable", "RoadTaxesTable",
                "InspectionsTable", "PermitsTable", "InsurancesTable", "InsuranceCompaniesTable",
                "OdometersTable", "FuelCardsTable", "FuelCompanyNamesTable"
            };

            foreach (var tName in inspectTables)
            {
                dynamic? tbl = null;
                string sName = "Unknown";
                foreach (dynamic ws in wb.Sheets)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        tbl = ws.ListObjects[tName];
                        if (tbl != null) { sName = ws.Name?.ToString() ?? "Unknown"; break; }
                    }
                    catch { }
                }

                if (tbl != null)
                {
                    int cols = (int)tbl.ListColumns.Count;
                    int rows = tbl.DataBodyRange != null ? (int)tbl.DataBodyRange.Rows.Count : 0;
                    int startCol = (int)tbl.HeaderRowRange.Column;
                    int startRow = (int)tbl.HeaderRowRange.Row;
                    sb.AppendLine($"  [{tName}] on Sheet '{sName}':");
                    sb.AppendLine($"      Dimensions : {rows} data row(s) × {cols} column(s)");
                    sb.AppendLine($"      Coordinates: Row {startRow}, Columns {startCol} to {startCol + cols - 1}");
                    sb.AppendLine();
                }
                else
                {
                    sb.AppendLine($"  [{tName}]: NOT FOUND on any worksheet");
                    sb.AppendLine();
                }
            }

            DebugDialog.ShowLog("Vehicle Manager - Populate Audit", sb.ToString());
        }
        catch { }
    }
#endif
}

FILE: VehicleManager\VehicleManager.SheetMapper.FuelImport.cs
